### [1] [GHySSeg: Real-Time Geometric Ground and Road Segmentation for Hybrid Solid-State LiDAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21507)
*Martín Palos,Fernando García*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出纯几何方法GHySSeg，从单帧混合固态LiDAR数据中分割地面与可行驶道路，无需训练和GPU，并发布首个公开基准HySoLiD-Drive。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合固态LiDAR数据无环、密集、杂乱且集中于前视视野，与经典360度旋转LiDAR的有序环列结构不同，导致传统几何方法失效，而学习型方法计算重、难认证且仅基于旋转LiDAR数据训练。

Method: GHySSeg逐条追踪扫描曲线，利用高度阶跃和距离回折两种几何特征进行分割；同时构建HySoLiD-Drive基准，包含两车真实驾驶记录的100帧人工标注和7000帧无标注数据。

Result: 在HySoLiD-Drive上，GHySSeg在地面和道路分割上均超越几何与学习型最优基线，道路IoU达0.907、地面IoU达0.896，精度超0.96，单核实时运行约22毫秒每帧。

Conclusion: 混合固态LiDAR的密集逐曲线几何可被纯几何方法有效利用，GHySSeg以实时、无需训练和GPU的方式弥合了产业传感器与自动驾驶研究之间的差距。

Abstract: Hybrid solid-state LiDAR sensors are making their way into production vehicles, yet the data they produce differs structurally from that of the established spinning mechanical sensors behind today's autonomous-driving datasets: it is ring-free, dense, cluttered, and concentrated in a frontal field of view. The classical geometric methods that autonomous vehicles rely on for drivable space computation and navigation assume the ordered ring-and-column structure of a 360-degree spinning LiDAR and degrade or fail on this data, while learned alternatives are computationally heavy, hard to certify, and trained almost exclusively on spinning-LiDAR data. We demonstrate that the same dense, per-curve geometry is instead an opportunity to close the divide between the industry's preferred sensors and autonomous-driving research. We present GHySSeg, a purely geometric method that segments ground and drivable road from a single hybrid solid-state LiDAR frame by tracing each scan curve and reading two geometric signatures, a step in height and a fold-back in range, with no training and no graphics processing unit. To evaluate it, we release HySoLiD-Drive, the first public benchmark dedicated to ground and road segmentation on an automotive hybrid solid-state LiDAR, comprising 100 manually labeled and 7000 unlabeled frames recorded by two vehicles in real driving. On this benchmark, GHySSeg surpasses both geometric and learned state-of-the-art baselines across ground and road segmentation, reaching an intersection-over-union of 0.907 for road and 0.896 for ground at over 0.96 precision, while running in real time at about 22 milliseconds per frame on a single core.

</details>


### [2] [ScaleVLA: Scaling Vision-Language-Action Models to 552 Billion Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21607)
*Ruixi Yu,Dairu Liu,Xuchuan Chen,Zekun Qi,Zekai Li,Zhuo Cao,Luyang Niu,Yingsheng Zhang,Sikai Liang,Ke Fan,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出ScaleVLA，将552B参数的DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash视觉语言模型与可训练动作DiT连接，系统比较2B至552B的VLM在共享训练设置下的机器人控制表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿VLM（如GPT-6 Astra）展现机器人控制潜力，但多数VLA策略仍依赖较小的VLM骨干，未能充分利用大规模预训练知识；作者探究前沿规模表征能否为机器人学习提供更强基础。

Method: 构建ScaleVLA，将552B DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash骨干连接到可训练动作DiT，并在统一训练设置下对2B至552B的VLM进行系统对比，评估样本效率、泛化能力和精确操作三项基本能力。

Result: 样本效率上，仅10次示范即达87.8%，超过用50次示范训练的2B骨干（85.6%）；泛化上，LIBERO-PRO成功率达45.1%，较小骨干最高仅31.3%；精确操作上，真实世界充电器插入成功率从4/10提升至9/10，增益集中于接触敏感对齐；尽管规模庞大，每动作块中位延迟仅216 ms。

Conclusion: 前沿VLM不仅可作为推理智能体，还能作为高效且可泛化机器人策略的强大表征基础。

Abstract: Frontier Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as GPT-6 Astra have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in robot control, suggesting that broad pretraining provides knowledge valuable for physical action. Yet most Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies still rely on much smaller VLM backbones, leaving this potential largely untapped. We ask whether frontier-scale representations can provide a stronger foundation for robot learning. We introduce ScaleVLA, which connects a 552B DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash backbone to a trainable action DiT, and systematically compare VLMs from 2B to 552B under a shared training setup. We evaluate three fundamental capabilities of robot foundation models. In sample efficiency, just 10 demonstrations outperform a 2B backbone trained with 50 (87.8% vs. 85.6%). In generalization, LIBERO-PRO success reaches 45.1%, compared with at most 31.3% for smaller backbones. In precise manipulation, real-world charger insertion improves from 4/10 to 9/10 successes, with gains concentrated in contact-sensitive alignment. Despite its scale, ScaleVLA achieves 216 ms median latency per action chunk. Our findings suggest that frontier VLMs can serve not only as reasoning agents, but also as powerful representational foundations for efficient and generalizable robot policies.

</details>


### [3] [SADL: On the Demand-Supply Geometry of Representation Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21596)
*Junhan Sun,Yigui Feng,Mingzhe Liu,Haoyan Xu,Lei Ye,Ziqi Han,Hanru Bai,Yanzhao Zhang,Hao Zhao,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在JEPA中，投影器输出约束无法单独防止表征坍缩；作者发现坍缩源于编码器初始即被输入间共享分量主导，并提出SADL直接约束编码器，使坍缩率从50%降至0%，显著提升有效秩与预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA中即便投影器输出被良好约束，表征仍会坍缩；现有正则化加在投影器之后，无法纠正编码器中由输入共享分量主导的问题，表征差异逐渐缩小直至坍缩。

Method: 提出SADL（供需损失）：保留原训练目标，直接比较投影器输出所需的表征差异（需求）与编码器能保留并传递给投影器的差异（供给），仅在供给不足时对编码器施加约束；可与不同需求正则器配合，不引入辅助网络、额外可训练参数或架构改动。

Result: 在单任务及四任务联合训练的LeWM与INTACT实验中，SADL将十个随机种子下的坍缩率从50%降至0%；训练一个epoch后编码器有效秩为基线的3.4–6.4倍，潜空间预测误差降至1/20–1/303；损失权重跨数量级无需调参仍稳健；配合多种需求正则器时INTACT无搜索控制平均成功率超95%；在四个图像数据集上一致提升精度与随机种子稳定性。

Conclusion: 使正则器所需求的表征差异与编码器所能供给的差异相匹配，可在控制与图像分类任务中实现更快、更稳定的表征学习。

Abstract: In Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), a well-constrained projector output does not by itself prevent representation collapse. We find that, from initialization, the encoder representation is dominated by a component shared by different inputs. A batch-normalized projector removes this component, so regularization after the projector cannot correct it, and the differences between representations shrink until they collapse. We propose SADL (Supply And Demand Loss), which keeps the original training objective and constrains the encoder directly when needed. SADL compares the representation differences that the projector output demands with the differences the encoder can retain and pass on to the projector, and adds a constraint on the encoder only when supply falls short of demand. It works with different Demand Regularizers and introduces no auxiliary networks, additional trainable parameters, or architectural changes. In single-task and jointly trained four-task LeWorldModel (LeWM) and INTACT experiments, SADL lowers the collapse rate over ten random seeds from 50% to 0%. After one epoch of training, encoder effective rank is 3.4-6.4 times that of the corresponding baseline without SADL, and latent prediction error falls to 1/20-1/303 of it. Without additional tuning, SADL stays robust as its loss weight spans orders of magnitude, and with several Demand Regularizers INTACT exceeds 95% mean search-free control success. On four image datasets, SADL also consistently improves accuracy and stability across random seeds. These results show that matching the representation differences a regularizer demands with the differences the encoder can supply leads to faster and more stable representation learning in control and image classification.

</details>


### [4] [Safe Physical Interaction of All-Electric Heavy-Duty Robotic Manipulators via Control Barrier Functions and Variable Impedance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21309)
*Amir Hossein Barjini,Mohammad Bahari,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种安全感知物理交互架构，将可变阻抗控制（VIC）与控制屏障函数（CBF）叠加于虚拟分解控制（VDC）框架，用于电动重型机械臂在受限空间中的安全交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全电动重型机械臂需在隧道壁、设备等结构附近作业，同时保持大负载所需力能力；仅精确运动控制无法满足安全与交互需求。

Method: 在VDC框架上结合VIC与CBF：VIC根据预测接近度和接触风险连续调整任务空间刚度与阻尼；关节速度CBF滤波器强制执行笛卡尔工作空间边界及闭链升降/倾斜机构的物理行程限制；利用关节速度跟踪误差实测界收紧CBF不等式以补偿内环VDC/EMLA动态的非理想实现；对比VDC、VIC+VDC、CBF+VDC、VIC+CBF+VDC四种配置。

Result: VIC使环境交互力峰值和RMS分别降低92.59%和96.33%；两种鲁棒CBF配置均保持正的工作空间与EMLA安全裕度；VIC+CBF+VDC较CBF+VDC将CBF干预指标降低10.30%。

Conclusion: VIC与CBF结合可在保持安全约束的同时显著降低交互力并减少CBF干预，适用于EMLA驱动重型机械臂的受限空间安全作业。

Abstract: All-electric heavy-duty robotic manipulators are increasingly expected to operate close to tunnel walls, equipment, and other structures while retaining the force capability required for large-payload tasks. Accurate motion control alone does not address the resulting safety and interaction requirements. This paper develops a safety-aware physical-interaction architecture that combines variable impedance control (VIC) with control barrier functions (CBFs) on top of an established virtual decomposition control (VDC) framework for electromechanical linear actuator (EMLA)-driven manipulators. VIC continuously adapts the task-space stiffness and damping according to predicted proximity and contact risk, whereas a joint-velocity CBF filter enforces Cartesian workspace boundaries and the physical stroke limits induced by the closed-chain lift and tilt mechanisms. To account for nonideal realization of the filtered velocity by the inner VDC/EMLA dynamics, the CBF inequalities are tightened using a measured bound on the joint-velocity tracking error. Four configurations, namely VDC, VIC+VDC, CBF+VDC, and VIC+CBF+VDC, are evaluated under workspace, actuator-limit, and physical-interaction scenarios. VIC reduces the peak and RMS environment-interaction forces by 92.59% and 96.33%, respectively; both robust CBF configurations maintain positive workspace and EMLA safety margins, while VIC+CBF+VDC reduces the CBF intervention metric by 10.30% compared with CBF+VDC.

</details>


### [5] [Hybrid Optimization-Generative Motion Planning for Multiagent Affine Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21264)
*Zhonggang Li,Hongyu Zhou,Raj Thilak Rajan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合优化-生成框架，用于仿射编队的多智能体运动规划，通过约束最大后验估计解耦多智能体目标，利用预训练单智能体扩散先验，并交织投影梯度下降严格满足仿射几何约束与安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编队控制器严重依赖先验提供的高质量且动态可行的参考轨迹，而传统基于约束优化的编队运动规划在高维联合轨迹上计算困难，因此需要更高效的规划方法。

Method: 将规划任务建模为约束最大后验估计，解耦多智能体目标以利用预训练的单智能体扩散先验（保证动态可行性、避障和平滑性），并将生成过程与投影梯度下降交织，严格施加仿射几何约束和安全保证。

Result: 仿真表明该框架能生成高度平滑、无碰撞且易于常见分布式控制器跟踪的计划，并在多项指标上优于最先进的规划器。

Conclusion: 该混合优化-生成框架有效解决了仿射编队运动规划的计算挑战，在平滑性、安全性和可跟踪性方面表现优越。

Abstract: Formation maneuver control enables a multiagent system to cooperatively navigate through constrained environments in collective motion. While various formation controllers exist, they rely heavily on high-quality and dynamically feasible reference trajectories provided a priori, making formation motion planning (FMP) essential. To address the computational challenges for high-dimensional joint trajectories faced by conventional constrained optimization-based solutions, this paper introduces a hybrid optimization-generative framework with a focus on affine formations, which provide the necessary spatial flexibility. By formulating the planning task as a constrained Maximum-a-Posteriori estimation, we decouple multiagent objectives to leverage pre-trained single-agent diffusion priors for dynamic feasibility, obstacle avoidance, and smoothness. We then interleave the generative process with projected gradient descent (PGD) to strictly enforce affine geometric constraints with safety guarantees. Simulations demonstrate that our framework generates highly smooth, collision-free plans that are readily trackable by common distributed controllers and outperforms state-of-the-art planners in various metrics.

</details>


### [6] [ThinkWAM: Rethinking Latent Futures for Action Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21258)
*Haoyun Tang,Baqiao Deng,Enjin Zhou,Songwen Tian,Keyao Xu,Bowen Jing,Kun Wang,Ke Ma,Zhandong Mei,Huibin Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ThinkWAM提出一种潜在世界动作模型，通过“提议-再思考-精炼”循环在动作执行前建立双向反馈，利用被动作块校正的未来潜在表示提升最终动作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有世界动作模型生成的初始动作块可能有缺陷，而未来预测若不以此为条件，就无法反映动作将对视觉表示造成的变化，从而难以在动作执行前进行有效精炼。

Method: 策略先输出完整动作块，该提议通过一个学习到的残差校正多时域未来潜在表示，校正后的潜在表示再注入同一动作头的第二遍以生成可执行动作块；循环由预测编码启发的可供性引导观察-动作记忆和近精细远粗糙的多时域预测方案支撑，且全部预测保持在潜在空间。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到98.70%成功率，LIBERO-Plus上84.76%（未经OOD微调），RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Random上85.75%/65.85%，RoboCasa24上65.17%，三个真实世界OOD条件下平均成功率64.4%；每个动作块两次推理平均延迟为LIBERO 153.48 ms、RoboTwin 164.61 ms、Galaxea A1Z 101.2 ms；消融显示再思考与可供性引导带来互补增益，诊断表明由提议条件化的校正同时改变预测未来与动作轨迹。

Conclusion: 提议条件化的未来表示校正能够改善最终动作，验证了在执行前通过潜在空间双向反馈进行动作精炼的有效性。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple action generation with predictions of future states. An initial action chunk may be flawed. A future prediction that is not conditioned on the chunk may fail to reflect the changes that the proposed actions would induce in visual representations, limiting its usefulness for refinement before execution. We introduce ThinkWAM, a latent WAM that establishes bidirectional feedback before execution through a proposal-rethink-refine loop: the policy first proposes a complete action chunk, the proposal corrects multi-horizon future latents through a learned residual, and the corrected latents are injected into a second pass of the same action head, which generates the executable chunk. An affordance-guided observation-action memory and a near-fine, far-coarse multi-horizon prediction scheme, both motivated by predictive coding, support this loop, and all prediction stays in latent space. ThinkWAM achieves 98.70% success on LIBERO, 84.76% on LIBERO-Plus without OOD fine-tuning, 85.75%/65.85% on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean/Random, and 65.17% on RoboCasa24. ThinkWAM achieves 64.4% mean success across three real-world OOD conditions. The mean two-pass inference latency per action chunk is 153.48 ms on LIBERO, 164.61 ms on RoboTwin, and 101.2 ms on the Galaxea A1Z. Ablations show complementary gains from rethinking and affordance guidance. Diagnostics indicate that proposal-conditioned corrections change both predicted futures and action trajectories, showing that the corrected future representations improve the final action.

</details>


### [7] [BoBDeLaN: Bounded-Bandwidth Context Adaptation for Deep Lagrangian Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21209)
*Lucas Schulze,Sucheth Shenoy,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BoBDeLaN将收缩且增益有界的前馈递归平衡网络上下文编码器与稳定线性滤波结合，为上下文感知的Deep Lagrangian Networks提供频率加权扰动敏感度界，并在模型预测控制中验证其降低高频敏感度、预测误差和运动抖动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文感知的Deep Lagrangian Networks虽能通过测量历史推断潜在上下文并保持拉格朗日结构，但该结构本身不能约束对测量扰动的敏感度，不确定负载和未建模动态仍限制预测精度。

Method: 提出BoBDeLaN，将收缩、增益有界的Recurrent Equilibrium Network上下文编码器与稳定线性滤波相结合，以塑造跨频率的扰动敏感度；理论保证递归初始化指数遗忘，并在上下文动态模型Lipschitz连续假设下给出有限时域、频率加权的上下文扰动界及残差力矩预测界；在仿真和真实硬件操作任务中，以低通实现嵌入模型预测控制评估，并在真实机器人数据上做频率敏感度分析。

Result: 与使用相同输入滤波的LSTM编码器相比，BoBDeLaN表现出更低的高频上下文敏感度、更低的残差力矩预测误差和更平滑的运动，且在注入扰动下性能退化更小。

Conclusion: BoBDeLaN通过结构化的上下文编码与滤波设计，在保持拉格朗日结构的同时有效约束扰动敏感度，提升了上下文感知物理模型在机器人控制中的鲁棒性和预测精度。

Abstract: Learned physics-based models provide structure for robot control, but uncertain payloads and unmodeled dynamics limit their predictive accuracy. Context-aware Deep Lagrangian Networks enable adaptation through a latent context inferred from measurement histories while retaining Lagrangian structure for each fixed context. However, this structure alone does not constrain sensitivity to measurement disturbances. We introduce BoBDeLaN, which combines a contracting, gain-bounded Recurrent Equilibrium Network context encoder with stable linear filtering to shape disturbance sensitivity across frequencies. This architecture guarantees exponential forgetting of the recurrent initialization and admits a finite-horizon, frequency-weighted bound on context perturbations, which extends to residual torque prediction under a Lipschitz continuity assumption on the context-conditioned dynamics model. We evaluate a low-pass realization within model predictive control on simulated and real hardware manipulation tasks, together with frequency-sensitivity analysis on real-robot data. Compared with an LSTM encoder using the same input filter, BoBDeLaN exhibits reduced high-frequency context sensitivity, lower residual torque prediction error, and smoother motion, with less degradation under injected disturbances.

</details>


### [8] [Cooperative Mobile Manipulation using Learned Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21198)
*Saray Bakker,Wendelin Böhmer,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CALDS框架，将单智能体学习到的动力学系统适配为多移动机械臂的协同执行，实现全身避碰并保持任务行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同移动操作需要机器人在保证全身避碰的同时协调运动并保留任务相关行为，而现有单智能体学习行为难以直接用于多机协同。

Method: 基于从演示中学习的个体动力学系统（DS），CALDS包含两个关键组件：协同策略选择（识别兼容的学习策略组合）和时间适配（在保持原行为空间结构与收敛性质的同时确保无碰撞协同）。

Result: 在辅助穿衣、餐厅清洁与上菜、开抽屉与放置物体三个场景中评估，定量结果表明该方法提升了任务成功率，实现了安全协调的同步执行，并保持了对演示行为的保真度。

Conclusion: CALDS能够有效复用单智能体学习行为实现移动机械臂的安全协同操作，在提升任务成功率的同时保持行为保真性。

Abstract: Cooperative mobile manipulation requires robots to coordinate their motions while ensuring whole-body collision avoidance and preserving task-relevant behaviors. We propose CALDS (Cooperative Adaptation of Learned Dynamical Systems), a framework that enables mobile manipulators to adapt learned single-agent behaviors for coordinated execution. Building on individual dynamical systems (DSs) learned from demonstrations, CALDS enables cooperative adaptation through two key components: Cooperative Policy Selection, which identifies compatible combinations of learned policies, and Temporal Adaptation, which ensures collision-free cooperation while preserving the spatial structure and convergence properties of the original behaviors. We evaluate CALDS across three diverse scenarios: assistive dressing, restaurant cleaning and serving, and drawer opening and object placement. Quantitative results demonstrate its effectiveness in improving task success and enabling safe, coordinated simultaneous execution while maintaining fidelity to demonstrated behaviors.

</details>


### [9] [Polygraph: Large-Scale Multi-Session Trajectory Optimization using Pose Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21163)
*Petar Lukovic,Tian Yi Lim,Marc Pollefeys,Pablo Speciale,Hermann Blum,Zuria Bauer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Polygraph 是一种在线多会话轨迹优化流水线，逐个关键帧和空间簇处理，无需构建3D地图，通过聚类、跨会话检索与验证、两级共识门控和增量/批量优化实现全局一致轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模多会话轨迹优化方法多为离线处理，要求所有会话预先可用并固定一个参考帧，无法满足在线增量场景需求。

Method: 将关键帧分组为局部簇；簇闭合时通过学习图像检索与其他会话匹配，用稀疏特征匹配、单目度量深度和鲁棒位姿估计验证候选对；采用两级共识门控仅在独立证据佐证时接纳跨会话边到共享位姿图；使用廉价增量求解保持图最新，并周期性鲁棒批量求解剔除离群值。

Result: 在立体室外驾驶、HoloLens/iPad室内遍历、Boston Dynamics Spot腿式室内运动及大规模室外车辆与手持路线等场景评估；与最接近的先前工作在同一GPU模型上比较，Polygraph运行更快、提交更少回环边，并在精度上持平或更优。

Conclusion: Polygraph 实现在线、增量的大规模多会话轨迹优化，无需3D地图，在多种平台和场景下优于或媲美现有方法。

Abstract: Robots, vehicles, and wearable devices often traverse the same environment many times. Each pass produces its own trajectory from onboard visual or visual-inertial odometry. These trajectories are locally accurate but drift over time and live in unrelated coordinate frames. Turning many such independent trajectories into one globally consistent set is the large-scale multi-session trajectory optimization problem. Existing solutions largely work offline. All sessions must be available up front, one session is fixed as the reference frame, and a single fused map is then optimized. This paper presents Polygraph, a multi-session trajectory optimization pipeline that processes sessions online, one keyframe and one spatial cluster at a time, and never builds a 3D map. Keyframes are grouped into local clusters. When a cluster closes, it is matched against every other session through learned image retrieval. Candidate pairs are verified with sparse feature matching, monocular metric depth, and robust pose estimation. A two-tier consensus gate admits a cross-session edge into the shared pose graph only once independent evidence corroborates it. A cheap incremental solve keeps the graph current, and a periodic robust batch solve prunes outliers. We evaluate Polygraph on stereo outdoor driving, HoloLens and iPad indoor traversals, legged indoor locomotion with a Boston Dynamics Spot, and large-scale outdoor vehicle and handheld routes. Against the closest prior work on the same GPU model, using its reported timings, Polygraph runs faster, commits fewer loop-closure edges, and matches or improves on accuracy.

</details>


### [10] [WinDEX: Metric 3D Hand Motion Reconstruction in the Wild for Dexterous Teleoperation and Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21143)
*Hongyu Ma,Hairong Qu,Shiqi Zhao,Yongsong Yang,Kai Shi,Jiahao Ai,Xin You,Peng Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WinDEX提出低成本大规模野外人体操作数据采集与重建系统，实现高精度实时手部动作重建并用于机器人学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体操作数据广泛用于训练机器人模仿人类灵巧性，但低成本、大规模野外采集仍困难；现有方法在几何度量尺度重建、真实世界泛化和推理速度上存在局限。

Method: 集成四项技术：(1) 几何-时间优化从野外第一视角立体视频生成度量3D手部标注，结合视觉-惯性SLAM实现世界坐标系重建；(2) WinDEX-Net端到端鱼眼原生网络，融合标定立体、局部细节和时间上下文，并采用模型辅助标注与再训练的数据-模型飞轮；(3) 蒸馏模型支持离线重建与因果流式推理，服务强化学习和遥操作；(4) 低成本CyberCap立体惯性采集设备，构建WinDEX-Hand数据集（1000小时训练标注及留出的人标注野外汇2D集和独立光学动捕评测集）。

Result: 离线重建43.61 FPS，因果流式171 FPS；留出数据上离线重建达当前最优：野外重投影误差14.5像素，结合视觉惯性里程计世界坐标系关节误差37.29 mm；强化学习策略任务成功率分别为使用WiLoR和HaWoR运动参考的7.6倍和3.6倍，并实现实时臂-手遥操作。

Conclusion: WinDEX实现了低成本野外大规模手部操作数据采集、高精度实时重建与机器人学习应用，在精度、速度和任务成功率上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Human manipulation data are widely used to train robots for human-like dexterity. However, low-cost, large-scale collection in the wild remains challenging. Existing methods remain limited in geometrically grounded metric-scale reconstruction, real-world generalization and inference speed. We present WinDEX, integrating (1) geometric-temporal optimization for metric 3D hand annotation from in-the-wild egocentric stereo videos, combined with visual-inertial SLAM for world-frame reconstruction; (2) WinDEX-Net, an end-to-end, fisheye-native network combining calibrated stereo, local detail and temporal context, with a data-model flywheel of model-assisted annotation and retraining; (3) distilled models for offline reconstruction and causal streaming, supporting reinforcement learning and teleoperation; and (4) low-cost CyberCap stereo-inertial capture, forming the WinDEX-Hand dataset with 1,000 hours of training annotations and held-out human-annotated in-the-wild 2D and independent optical motion-capture evaluation sets. Offline reconstruction runs at 43.61 FPS and causal streaming at 171 FPS. On held-out data, offline reconstruction achieves state-of-the-art accuracy: 14.5 pixels in-the-wild reprojection error and, with visual-inertial odometry, 37.29 mm world-frame joint error. We achieve 7.6 and 3.6 times the task success rates of reinforcement learning policies trained with WiLoR and HaWoR motion references, respectively, and demonstrate real-time arm-hand teleoperation. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [11] [ReuseMem: One Memory, Multiple Goals in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21115)
*Yoonseok Kang,Dayeon Jung,Seunghoon Jeon,Wonjib Kim,Dongchul Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReuseMem学习将单一视觉记忆复用于多种后续操作目标，通过检索互补证据并结合当前场景，在仿真和物理任务中显著提升目标完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要利用先前观察到的经验应对新请求，但同一经验可能支持不同用途（如识别物体或归位），而这些用途在观察时未知。

Method: 独立于未来目标记录视觉特征；针对每个请求检索互补证据；通过轻量动作接口与当前场景结合；检索由候选证据对专家动作预测的支持程度监督；重复特征仅存储一次，动作生成中不变的内存计算被复用。

Result: 在四个仿真任务和两个物理场景中验证了通用记录的复用；在匹配动作适应下，相比统一内存，联合目标完成率提升24.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 一个视觉记忆可以随场景变化支持多种后续操作目标。

Abstract: Robots that work alongside people should be able to use what they have seen when new requests arise. The same experience may later support identifying an object or returning it to its original location, although these uses were unknown during observation. ReuseMem learns to use one visual memory for multiple later manipulation goals. It records visual features independently of future goals, retrieves complementary evidence for each request, and combines it with the current scene through a lightweight action interface. Retrieval is supervised by how well candidate evidence supports expert-action prediction across different current states, linking memory selection to its use in manipulation. Repeated visual features are stored once, and memory computations that remain unchanged during action generation are reused. Experiments on four simulation tasks and two physical scenarios demonstrate reuse of a common recording across different requests. With matched action adaptation, ReuseMem improves joint goal completion on the benchmark tasks by 24.5 percentage points over uniform memory. These findings show that one memory can support multiple later manipulation goals as the scene changes.

</details>


### [12] [ForceHarness: Turning Force into Action and Physics for Frozen Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21099)
*Ke Sun,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Yike Zhang,Yanzhe Chen,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceHarness让仅通过API访问的冻结多模态模型无需演示或微调即可控制接触力，在真实机械臂上将GPT-6 Astra零样本成功率从3.3%提升至90.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像无法反映接触力，而多数操作任务依赖力；将力引入仅API的冻结模型面临两个挑战：接触变化远快于API模型决策速度，且冻结模型无法理解原始力流。

Method: 提出ForceHarness：先通过腕部传感器或仅关节力矩校准手部力；用自终止力原语在模型下层闭合力控制环并在接触事件时返回；再用物理翻译层将力轨迹转为带不确定性的物理量作为工具结果。另构建含隐藏物理变量的七任务MuJoCo基准HiddenPhys用于评估。

Result: 真实无腕力传感器的机械臂上，零样本成功率从3.3%升至90.0%；仿真中ForceBench提升17.5个百分点，HiddenPhys提升34.3个百分点，超过基于演示训练的力感知策略，在HiddenPhys上匹配手写专家控制器；消融显示小模型在给定物理量时决策显著优于原始力值。

Conclusion: ForceHarness使冻结多模态模型无需演示或微调即可有效利用接触力完成操作任务，且物理量形式的力反馈比原始力流更适合小模型推理。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal models can now control robots zero-shot from images, yet many manipulation tasks depend on contact forces that images cannot show. Bringing force to an API-only frozen model raises two challenges. First, contact evolves much faster than an API model can decide, so the system must close the loop on force beneath the model. Second, a frozen model cannot interpret raw force streams, so force must be returned in a form the model can reason about. Therefore, we present ForceHarness, a harness that lets frozen models use force without demonstrations or finetuning. After calibrating hand force from a wrist sensor or joint torques alone, ForceHarness addresses the first challenge with self-terminating force primitives that regulate force on their own and return at a contact event. To address the second challenge, a physics translation layer turns each force trace into physical quantities with uncertainty as the tool result. To evaluate ForceHarness against expert force controllers and learned policies under identical conditions, we also build HiddenPhys, a seven-task MuJoCo benchmark with hidden physical variables. On a real arm without a wrist force sensor, ForceHarness raises the zero-shot success rate of GPT-6 Astra from 3.3% to 90.0%. In simulation, it adds 17.5 percentage points on the public ForceBench suite and 34.3 percentage points on HiddenPhys, surpassing force-aware policies trained on demonstrations on both benchmarks and matching hand-written expert controllers on HiddenPhys. Ablations further show that smaller models make significantly better decisions when given physical quantities rather than raw force values.

</details>


### [13] [CoopDistill: Equilibrium-Guided Multi-Agent Policy Distillation for Cooperative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21069)
*Shiyu Fang,Sheng Zhao,Jiarui Zhang,Chengkai Xu,Xiaocong Zhao,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoopDistill框架，将势博弈与多智能体强化学习结合，通过策略蒸馏提升协同驾驶的安全性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化方法固定路径限制空间灵活性，MARL协调学习与安全执行困难，两者集成面临探索效率、经验分配和执行可靠性挑战。

Method: 以势博弈提供固定路径纵向参考，残差MARL学习纵向加速度、冲突接近时机和横向路径修正；采用硬案例课程聚焦未解决交互；比较式回滚选择器评估联合后果后选择执行动作。

Result: 在汇入、环岛和交叉口场景、四个联网自动驾驶渗透率下，相比势博弈成功率提升2.1–27.1个百分点，延误降低7.3–27.3%，未观察到碰撞。

Conclusion: CoopDistill有效融合势博弈与MARL，在保证安全的同时显著提升协同驾驶的成功率和效率。

Abstract: Cooperative driving extends autonomous driving from individual vehicle decisions to joint maneuvers that improve traffic safety and efficiency. Optimization-based methods impose safety constraints but often fix paths to simplify multi-vehicle planning, limiting spatial flexibility. Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) explores alternatives through interaction, but coordinated learning and safe execution remain challenging. Their integration nevertheless faces challenges in exploration efficiency, experience allocation, and execution reliability. To address these challenges, this paper proposes CoopDistill, an equilibrium-guided multi-agent policy distillation framework. A potential-game (PG) formulation provides a fixed-path longitudinal reference, around which a residual MARL policy learns longitudinal acceleration, conflict-approach timing, and lateral path corrections. A hard-case curriculum concentrates experience on interactions that PG leaves unresolved or substantially delayed. A comparative rollout selector evaluates the joint consequences of learned corrections against PG before choosing the executed action. Experiments in merging, roundabout, and intersection scenarios compare CoopDistill with six baselines at four connected automated vehicle penetration levels. Relative to PG, success improves by 2.1--27.1 percentage points and delay decreases by 7.3--27.3\%, with no observed collisions. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [14] [WiTac: Wrist-Informed Tactile Representations for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21057)
*Haotian He,Yiyang Chen,Congsheng Xu,Wenchao Yue,Mingze Ye,Shuai Chen,Yi Yang,Yaping Li,Haonan Wang,Weinan E,Wenzhao Lian,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WiTac 是一种在动作生成前学习的预训练触觉编码器，将触觉信号、手部运动和腕部视图深度融合为统一的接触表征，用于条件化 VLA 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同触觉接触因手、物体与目标构型不同需要不同修正；将触觉与腕部视觉分别输入不如在策略注入前融合，能更好把触觉特征置于视觉语境中，得到更准确的接触表征。

Method: 基于近300小时 SharpaDex 数据预训练 WiTac 编码器，深度融合触觉信号、手部运动与腕部视图形成全面的接触表征，再将其作为条件输入下游 VLA 策略（GR00T N1.7 与 π0.5）。

Result: 在四项接触丰富的操作任务上，相比仅视觉基座策略平均成功率分别提升28.8%和15.0%；相比无语腕部触觉编码器分别高出18.8%和7.5%。离线分析显示引入腕部视觉可获得更准确的接触描述、更强的任务-接触联合判别力、在触觉噪声下更高的特征稳定性，以及从相似接触中更清晰的动作方向读出。

Conclusion: 在策略注入前融合触觉与腕部视觉的预训练接触表征能显著提升接触丰富操作任务的策略性能与鲁棒性，优于仅视觉或无语腕部触觉的方案。

Abstract: Similar tactile contacts can call for different corrections depending on the configuration of the hand, object and target. Rather than supplying touch and wrist vision separately, fusing them before policy injection better grounds tactile features in visual context, yielding more accurate contact representations. We introduce Wrist-Informed Tactile Representations (WiTac), a pretrained encoder that learns such representations before action generation. Pretrained on nearly 300 hours of SharpaDex data, WiTac deeply fuses tactile signals with hand motion and wrist views into a comprehensive contact representation. Conditioning downstream VLA policies (GR00T N1.7 and $\pi_{0.5}$) on WiTac substantially improves task success over vision-only base policies across four contact-rich manipulation tasks (+28.8% and +15.0% on average), and further outperforms a wrist-free tactile encoder by 18.8% and 7.5%, respectively. Offline analyses further show that incorporating wrist vision yields more accurate contact descriptors, stronger joint task-contact discrimination, greater feature stability under tactile noise, and clearer action-direction readout from similar this http URL page: this https URL .

</details>


### [15] [FlowRow: Learning Navigation Behaviors in Agricultural Environments with Long-Horizon Goal-Conditioned Flow Matching Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21035)
*Utkarsh Bajpai,Cédric Pradalier,Stéphanie Aravecchia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowRow 是一种基于流匹配的局部规划器，将半结构化农业环境导航建模为目标导向任务，并提出了 RowNavBench 基准；在 900 个仿真回合中达到 87% 成功率，远超最强生成式行为克隆基线的 44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型方法能实现稳健的行内导航，但跨行导航通常依赖对田地几何的先验知识来指定和调试行间转换动作，无法在行间任意切换，限制了自主监测与干预。

Method: 将果园/葡萄园导航形式化为目标导向导航任务：以相对机器人的长时程目标表达意图，结合深度感知，用流匹配建模 SE(2) 路径分布作为局部规划器（FlowRow）；并在 Isaac Sim 中构建 RowNavBench 基准，覆盖葡萄园、柠檬园和苹果园共 900 回合，变化行长、行株距、植株位置与杂草生长。

Result: FlowRow 成功率为 87%，而在相同演示数据上重训的最强生成式行为克隆基线仅为 44%。

Conclusion: 基于流匹配的目标导向局部规划能有效覆盖行内行驶、行转向与跳行、行间穿越等多样农业导航行为，且无需显式田地几何先验；作者将随论文接收发布基准、数据与代码。

Abstract: Autonomous monitoring and intervention in semi-structured agricultural environments, such as orchards and vineyards, require mobile robots to navigate to locations across diverse field configurations. This entails performing navigation behaviors including in-row driving, row turning and skipping, and inter-row traversal. Recent learning-based approaches have enabled robust in-row navigation from onboard perception, but navigation across rows typically requires a priori knowledge of field geometry to specify and tune row-transition maneuvers, preventing arbitrary transitions between rows. We address this challenge by framing navigation in these environments as a goal-directed navigation task, where a long-horizon goal is expressed relative to the robot and a planner generates local paths based on the goal and the robot's perceptual observations. We present FlowRow, a flow-matching-based local planner which models the distribution of diverse agricultural navigation behaviors as $\mathrm{SE}(2)$ paths using the long-horizon goal and depth perception. To enable systematic evaluation, we introduce RowNavBench, a navigation benchmark for semi-structured agricultural environments developed in Isaac Sim, comprising 900 episodes across grape vineyards, lemon orchards, and apple orchards with varying row lengths, row and plant spacings, plant positions, and weed growth. FlowRow achieves 87% success, compared to 44% for the strongest generative behavior cloning baseline retrained on the same demonstrations. We will release our benchmark, data, and code upon acceptance.

</details>


### [16] [Inverse Kinematics in Endoscopic Scenarios using MoveIt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20978)
*Marc Bestmann,Timon Engelke,Rebecca Rodeck,Gerko Wende*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对受限场景下MoveIt逆运动学求解器性能不足的问题，对现有求解器进行基准测试并比较多种实用改进方法，发现将IK问题拆分为两部分效果最佳，在内窥镜水箱场景中实现100毫秒内100%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆运动学对机器人应用至关重要，但基于MoveIt的标准求解器在受限场景中表现不佳，需要评估并改进现有求解器性能。

Method: 对现有求解器进行基准测试，并比较多种提升求解器性能的实用修改方案，其中包括将逆运动学问题拆分为两部分。

Result: 将IK问题拆分为两部分取得最佳性能，在内窥镜水箱场景中于100毫秒内达到100%成功率。

Conclusion: 问题拆分是提升受限场景下逆运动学求解性能的有效策略，可实现快速且高成功率的求解。

Abstract: Inverse kinematics are critical in many robotic applications, yet standard MoveIt-based solvers struggle in confined scenarios. This paper provides a benchmark of the available solvers and compares multiple practical modifications for improving the performance of the solvers. Splitting the IK problem into two parts results in the best performance, leading to a 100% success rate under 100 ms in an endoscopic tank scenario.

</details>


### [17] [ROSE: A Shared Cross-Embodiment Facial-Motion Latent Space for Humanoid Robot Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20950)
*Yongji Fu,Man Cao,Yi Zhou,Fei Lin,Mark F. Hansen,Guanqun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROSE框架通过仿生硬件、跨具身运动表示学习和多模态运动生成，实现紧凑人形机器人的自然全脸动态表情。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统仅实现有限唇动或脚本化表情，且学习系统常经渲染-反演路径引入延迟和伪影，难以可靠迁移到物理机器人。

Method: 硬件采用柔性肌腱鞘传动结构，将34个执行器集成到19.5厘米仿生头部；算法上ROSE-VAE从人类、数字人和机器人观测中学习身份无关的共享面部运动潜空间，ROSE-Flow直接将文本、音频或其组合映射到该空间轨迹，ROSE-ACT再将轨迹转换为受约束的执行器指令。

Result: 系统展示了高密度仿生硬件与共享可执行面部运动表示能够联合统一跨具身学习、多模态生成和物理机器人表情。

Conclusion: ROSE通过硬件与算法协同设计，实现了紧凑人形机器人自然、连贯的全脸动态表情生成与物理执行。

Abstract: Natural, coherent full-face dynamic expressions are central to making humanoid robots socially approachable, yet existing systems commonly realize only limited lip motion or scripted expressions. The limitation is both physical and algorithmic. A compact head leaves little room for densely packed, independently controllable actuation; meanwhile, learned systems often generate an avatar video, landmarks, or facial parameters and then infer actuator commands through visual retargeting. This render-and-invert path adds latency and artifacts, and it does not reliably transfer generated behavior to a physical robot. We present ROSE (RObot facial expression through Shared motion Embeddings), a framework that integrates biomimetic hardware, cross-embodiment motion representation learning, and multimodal motion generation. On the hardware side, we build a flexible tendon-sheath transmission structure that integrates 34 actuators into a 19.5 cm biomimetic head. By decoupling actuator placement from facial action points, it enables coordinated full-face expression within a compact volume. On the algorithmic side, ROSE-VAE learns an identity-independent shared facial-motion latent space from human, digital-human, and robot observations. ROSE-Flow directly maps text, audio, or their combination to trajectories in this motion space without using video as a generator input. ROSE-ACT then converts these trajectories into constrained actuator commands. Our system demonstrates how high-density biomimetic hardware and a shared executable facial-motion representation can jointly unify cross-embodiment learning, multimodal generation, and physical robot expression.

</details>


### [18] [TacBridge: Learning Unified Tactile Representations through Cross-Sensor Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20939)
*Yongji Fu,Yijiong Lin,Yi Zhou,Yi Qi,Guanqun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TacBridge框架，通过跨传感器对应关系从多传感器触觉视频中学习统一表示，解决光学触觉传感器因成像差异导致表示难以迁移的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同光学触觉传感器（GelSight、DIGIT、GelSlim、TacTip等）在成像几何、背景、光照、分辨率和形变响应上存在差异，导致同一接触事件产生传感器相关观测，限制了表示迁移与多传感器数据联合使用。

Method: 采用多传感器掩码触觉视频预训练学习跨传感器时空表示；随后利用匹配接触事件的成对源/目标视频，将同一掩码模型联合训练于同传感器与跨传感器重建任务。

Result: 跨传感器翻译与下游感知实验表明，所学触觉表示具有跨传感器泛化能力和任务可迁移性。

Conclusion: TacBridge通过跨传感器对应关系学习统一触觉表示，可有效缓解传感器差异带来的表示迁移障碍，支持多传感器数据的联合利用。

Abstract: Optical tactile sensors record tactile videos that capture local deformation and its temporal evolution during contact, supporting robot manipulation, force estimation, and tactile representation learning. Differences in imaging geometry, background appearance, illumination, resolution, and deformation response lead to sensor-dependent observations of the same contact across GelSight, DIGIT, GelSlim, TacTip, and other optical tactile sensors. This variability limits representation transfer and the joint use of multi-sensor data. We introduce TacBridge, a framework for learning unified representations from multi-sensor tactile videos through cross-sensor correspondence. Specifically, multi-sensor masked tactile-video pre-training learns spatiotemporal representations across sensors. We then train the same masked model jointly on same-sensor and cross-sensor reconstruction, using paired source and target videos of matched contact events. Experiments on cross-sensor translation and downstream perception demonstrate the cross-sensor generalization and task transferability of the learned tactile representations.

</details>


### [19] [Transferable Neural CBFs via Generalized Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20929)
*Nishant Jayesh Bhave,Parthib Khound*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究能否通过将状态表示为相对于每个障碍物的坐标，复用一个神经控制障碍函数（CBF）跨越不同障碍物配置，并提出广义闭包条件与提升定理来保证迁移的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CBF依赖手工设计，对欠驱动车辆困难且安全集过于保守；神经CBF可从数据学习更具信息量的障碍函数，但通常只针对单一固定障碍物配置验证，难以复用。

Method: 将状态表达为相对于各障碍物的坐标，引入广义闭包条件，把相对坐标可能丢失的自身演化信息表示为残差；给出提升定理，将已验证的相对坐标障碍函数迁移到一族障碍物中的每一个；并用秩检验判断仅凭对称性无法证明迁移的情形。

Result: 在双积分器、平面飞行器和12状态四旋翼上验证：未通过秩检验的坐标导致障碍物穿透或悬停附近障碍条件失效；机体坐标系对四旋翼精确满足闭包条件，所得障碍函数无需重新训练即可迁移到新地图，并在部署与快速启动实验中有效。

Conclusion: 一个神经障碍函数可在障碍物族间复用，前提是相对坐标满足广义闭包条件；秩检验可识别对称性不足的情形，机体坐标系对四旋翼是合适选择。

Abstract: Classical control barrier functions (CBFs) provide safety constraints for controllers, but designing them by hand is difficult for underactuated vehicles and the resulting safe sets can be overly conservative. Neural CBFs can learn more informative barriers from data, yet a learned barrier is usually verified only for one fixed obstacle configuration. We study whether one neural barrier can be reused across environments by expressing the state relative to each obstacle. The key gap is that these relative coordinates may discard information needed to predict their own evolution. We give a generalized closure condition that represents this missing information as a residual and a lifting theorem that transfers a verified relative-coordinate barrier to every obstacle in a family. A rank test also identifies when symmetry alone cannot justify the transfer. We validate the conditions on a double integrator, a planar aircraft, and a 12-state quadrotor. Coordinates that fail the test lead to obstacle penetration or to a barrier condition that fails near hover, whereas body-frame coordinates close exactly for the quadrotor. The resulting barrier transfers to new maps without retraining and is effective in the reported deployment and fast-start experiments.

</details>


### [20] [Driving the Style: Velocity-Conditioned Whole-Body Control from Motion Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20888)
*Murad Dawood,Oezge Drama Berkenkamp,Antonio Gonzales Marin,Nutan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅用全身跟踪奖励训练、但将 actor 限制为紧凑运动命令与机器人观测的人形机器人策略，无需运行时参考轨迹或教师蒸馏，即可在仿真和 Unitree G1 实机上复现演示风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在矛盾：对抗模仿能提供风格信号但难以迁移到硬件；全身跟踪强化学习部署可靠但依赖未来参考轨迹，且运动偏离训练数据时易失败。目标是让策略仅凭速度命令和本体观测复现演示的全身风格，同时保持可部署性。

Method: 用全身跟踪奖励训练人形策略，参考运动仅监督奖励和特权 critic，不向 actor 提供参考关节轨迹，也不需要教师蒸馏；actor 输入仅为紧凑的运动命令和机器人观测。

Result: 在单条随机行走运动上训练，并用留出运动的速度曲线命令：仿真成功率至少98%，Unitree G1 实机全部试验完成；留出慢速行走时，全身跟踪成功率降至2%、命令条件蒸馏为56%，两者实机均失败；训练运动上关节位置误差保持在全身跟踪的8%以内，风格覆盖度高于 AMP，而 AMP 在无安全吊带时实机跌倒。

Conclusion: 将演示风格通过跟踪奖励编码进以命令为条件的策略，可兼得风格复现与实机可靠部署，避免运行时参考轨迹和教师蒸馏的依赖。

Abstract: Achieving human-like behavior on humanoid robots has advanced rapidly by leveraging motion-capture demonstrations rather than hand-crafted cost functions. Adversarial imitation supplies a style signal that lets a policy fulfill a task while adhering to a desired style, but is difficult to transfer to hardware. Whole-body tracking through reinforcement learning instead deploys reliably and imitates demonstrations faithfully, but the resulting policies are conditioned on the future reference, so they require a reference trajectory at runtime and can fail when the motion differs from the training data. We train a humanoid policy directly with whole-body tracking rewards while restricting its actor to compact locomotion commands and robot observations. Reference motion supervises the reward and privileged critic, without supplying reference joint trajectories to the actor or requiring teacher distillation. The demonstrated style is thereby encoded in the policy through the tracking reward, so the robot reproduces the whole-body style under commanded velocities. Trained on a single random-walk motion and commanded with velocity profiles from held-out motions, our policy reaches at least 98\% success in simulation and completes every trial on the Unitree G1. On held-out slow walking, whole-body tracking drops to 2\% success and command-conditioned distillation to 56\%, and both fail on the real robot. On the trained motions, the joint position error of our policy stays within 8\% of whole-body tracking, and it has a higher style coverage than adversarial motion priors (AMP), which fell on the real robot without a safety crane.

</details>


### [21] [QTOP: Scaling Humanoid Motion Tracking with Quantile Critics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20791)
*Markus Peschl,Dana Kianfar,Daniel Dijkman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出QTOP方法，通过状态-参考对条件化的分布优势估计提升人类动作跟踪强化学习的样本效率，并在仿真和真实场景中验证其性能及缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类动作跟踪强化学习需同时扩展GPU和数据集规模才能高保真泛化到未见动作，但多样动作训练引入类似多任务强化学习的学习干扰，使训练本质上是计算受限的，需提升样本效率。

Method: 提出分位数跟踪优化（QTOP），引入以状态和参考对为条件的分布优势估计，改进样本效率；在多个数据集上与同策略和异策略强化学习基线对比，并建立QTOP的缩放规律。

Result: QTOP在仿真和真实世界跟踪场景中于多个数据集上比基线提升最高10%；缩放规律显示，除可预测缩放外，在中等GPU小时区间内数据集大小对性能影响极小。

Conclusion: QTOP通过分布优势估计有效缓解学习干扰并提升样本效率，且其缩放行为表明中等计算预算下数据集规模并非主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Learning human motion tracking policies through reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a core capability that underpins a majority of recent progress in humanoid whole-body control. However, achieving generalization to unseen motions at high fidelity requires jointly scaling training to thousands of GPU and dataset hours. This work hypothesizes that motion tracking is inherently compute-bound because training on a diverse set of motions introduces learning interference, similar to multi-task RL. To tackle this, we propose Quantile Tracking Optimization (QTOP), which improves sample efficiency by introducing a distributional advantage estimate conditioned on state and reference pairs. QTOP outperforms on and off-policy RL baselines both in simulation and real-world tracking scenarios on multiple datasets by up to 10\%. Furthermore, we establish a scaling law for QTOP and find that, aside from scaling predictably, dataset size has minimal impact on performance for moderately sized GPU hour regimes.

</details>


### [22] [Non-Imaging Optical Tactile Sensors for Robotics: Transduction Principles, System Integration, and Robotic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20770)
*Hao Wu,Yu Feng,Haotian Guo,Huichan Zhao,Changsheng Wu,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 综述建立机械载荷传递、光学编码、读出与触觉推理的统一框架，提出三项核心考量：可辨识性、系统级覆盖与反馈时序、分层证据评估，并给出校准迁移与任务验证的优先方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非成像光学触觉传感器架构多样，但其有效性取决于能否将接触变量与耦合的机械及环境效应可靠区分，并及时输出信息指导机器人动作，缺乏统一评估框架。

Method: 构建连接机械载荷传递、光学编码、读出与触觉推理到机器人任务需求的统一框架；系统考察反射式与波导器件、微/纳米光纤、光纤光栅、干涉与谐振结构及机械发光复合材料。

Result: 提炼三项考量：高光学灵敏度仅在目标变量于相关接触与校准条件下可辨识时才有意义；空间覆盖与反馈时序须在系统层面评估，计入复用、采集、解调与推理；器件表征、离线识别与闭环机器人性能是不同层级证据，需不同评价标准；代表性研究展示其对力估计、接触定位、滑移校正与分布式交互的影响。

Conclusion: 提出校准迁移、可靠接口、可扩展读出与基于任务的验证等优先方向，为机器人非成像光学触觉架构的选择与开发提供依据。

Abstract: Non-imaging optical tactile sensors encode contact through changes in optical intensity, wavelength, spectra, or photon counts, offering diverse architectures for robotic touch. Their usefulness depends on whether contact variables can be reliably distinguished from coupled mechanical and environmental effects, and whether the resulting information can be delivered in time to guide action. This review develops a unified framework linking mechanical load transmission, optical encoding, readout, and tactile inference to robotic task requirements. It examines reflective and waveguide-based devices, micro/nanofibers, fiber gratings, interferometric and resonant structures, and mechanoluminescent composites. Three considerations emerge across these approaches. First, high optical sensitivity supports accurate tactile estimation only when target variables remain identifiable under the relevant contact and calibration conditions. Second, spatial coverage and feedback timing must be assessed at the system level, accounting for multiplexing, acquisition, demodulation, and inference. Third, device characterization, offline recognition, and closed-loop robotic performance provide distinct levels of evidence and require different evaluation criteria. Representative studies illustrate how these considerations shape force estimation, contact localization, slip correction and distributed interaction. This review concludes with priorities for calibration transfer, reliable interfaces, scalable readout, and task-based validation, providing a basis for selecting and developing non-imaging optical tactile architectures for robotics.

</details>


### [23] [AVD: A Multi-Sensor Teleoperated Wheelchair Platform with Safety-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20720)
*Namai Chandra,Madhur Thareja,Boby George*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AVD低成本远程操作辅助移动架构，统一感知、通信、嵌入式控制与远程监控，并以安全分层和实测传感器特性验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动轮椅主要依赖手动摇杆，严重运动与视觉障碍者难以独立移动，需要一种可靠、低成本且安全的远程操作方案。

Method: 构建完全采用商用硬件的AVD平台；控制权限采用静态层级，手动急停和乘员摇杆优先于远程命令，车载感知层仅有停止权限；计算设计划分主功能与安全功能并定义心跳驱动的安全状态转换；对感知栈进行实测表征，包括逐通道采集延迟、2025次扫描的LiDAR退化实验（五种目标配置及水雾、粉尘、孔径遮挡控制）和至47度静态坡度姿态测量。

Result: 最差环境条件汇总均值仍保留76.6%的清晰基线前向扇区产出，无扫描完全丢失该扇区；记录到一次振动导致惯性单元掉线而LiDAR与相机记录继续，体现通道互补性；形成有文档的原型和明确验证路径。

Conclusion: AVD为可部署轮椅实现提供了安全导向、低成本且经实测验证的远程操作架构与验证路线。

Abstract: Electric wheelchairs predominantly rely on manual joystick interfaces, which present critical barriers to independent mobility for individuals with severe motor and visual impairments. This paper presents AVD (Autonomous Vehicle Drive), a dependable teleoperation architecture for assistive mobility that unifies perception, communication, embedded control, and remote supervision in a single low-cost platform built entirely from commercially available hardware. The central engineering contribution is a safety-oriented design framework: control authority follows a static hierarchy, a manual emergency stop and the occupant's joystick pre-empt remote commands, and the on-board sensing layer has stop-only authority. The compute design partitions primary and safety functions and specifies a heartbeat-driven safe-state transition for the fault cases considered. The sensing stack this framework depends on is characterized against measured data: per-channel acquisition latency, a 2,025-scan LiDAR degradation campaign spanning five target configurations and a control under water mist, airborne dust, and direct aperture occlusion, and attitude measurements across static inclines to 47 degrees. The lowest pooled environmental-condition mean retained 76.6% of the clear-baseline forward-sector yield, and no scan lost the sector completely. A logged vibration-induced inertial dropout occurred while LiDAR and camera logging continued, illustrating complementary channel behavior. The result is a documented prototype and an explicit validation path for a deployable wheelchair implementation.

</details>


### [24] [ACiT: Proprioceptive-Prior Action Chunking with Inverted Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20719)
*Haixiang Wu,Zhihao Guan,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACiT，以本体感受历史关节耦合为先验条件、视觉为后校准，通过倒置Transformer实现动作分块预测，在多个基准和真实任务上提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略将本体感受序列与高维视觉等同融合，导致本体感受信号被淹没，丢失了关节间强耦合这一关键动作决定结构。

Method: 受神经感觉运动环路启发，设计三个模块：倒置状态编码用逐关节token和关节间自注意力构建本体感受先验；状态-视觉接地让关节token查询视觉证据，使视觉仅用于目标与校准；倒置动作分块独立解码逐关节轨迹。

Result: ACiT在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界大多数任务上提升成功率，在需要动态与协调的任务上增益最大；同一条件设计迁移至VLA骨干pi_0-ACiT后获得显著提升并具有更好的执行效率。

Conclusion: 以历史关节耦合为主要条件、视觉为后校准的融合设计优于等权融合，能有效保留本体感受动态先验并提升策略性能与效率。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies condition action prediction on visual and proprioceptive observations. While historical proprioceptive sequences carry rich dynamic priors, current methods treat them as equal to high-dimensional vision, causing the proprioceptive signal to be overwhelmed. To identify what is lost in equal-conditioning fusion, we analyze proprioceptive data and find that strong inter-joint coupling is the key action-determining structure. This finding aligns with the neural sensorimotor loop, in which proprioception first forms a body-centered prior and vision is later calibrated via parietal remapping. Inspired by this, we propose ACiT, Proprioceptive-Prior Action Chunking with Inverted Transformers, which reinforces historical joint-coupling as the primary condition with vision as a later calibration. This design is instantiated through three modules: Inverted State Encoding builds a proprioceptive prior via per-joint tokens and inter-joint self-attention. State-Visual Grounding lets joint tokens query visual evidence, making vision goal- and calibration-only. Inverted Action Chunking decodes per-joint trajectories independently. ACiT improves success rates on most LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world tasks, with the largest gains on those that require dynamics and coordination. The same conditioning design transferred to the VLA backbone pi_0-ACiT yields significant gains with better execution efficiency.

</details>


### [25] [TaF-Foundation: Wrench-Grounded Cross-Sensor Tactile Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20718)
*Jiaming Jiang,Yuzhe Huang,Baokun Yang,Hao Liang,Li Gao,Haoran Guo,Pei Lin,Shengcheng Luo,Chenxi Xiao,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以六维力/力矩（wrench）为物理锚点的触觉表征方法，统一异构触觉传感器并支持跨硬件迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉对接触密集操作至关重要，但不同传感器的物理机制与观测格式各异，阻碍跨硬件迁移；由于物理接触受牛顿力学支配，可用统一的wrench作为共享物理参考。

Method: 构建采集平台，从4类传感原理的24个传感器收集超1亿对触觉观测与wrench测量；框架结合传感器特定输入适配与共享编码器，通过重建wrench学习，将不同传感器观测映射到基于接触物理的共享连续特征空间；提出TaF-Policy，融合触觉特征、视觉观测与机器人状态生成动作块。

Result: 核心基准上单帧力RMSE降低17.1%，跨传感器检索R@1提升13.11个百分点；TaF-Policy在3个仿真任务平均成功率51.33%（最强基线47.33%），4个真实任务46.25%（最强基线23.75%）。

Conclusion: 以wrench为共同物理参考可实现无需触觉—触觉配对的跨传感器共享监督，学到的表征在力估计、跨传感器检索、少样本适配和机器人操作中均优于基线。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for contact-rich manipulation, yet heterogeneous sensing mechanisms and observation formats hinder cross-hardware transfer. Since physical contact is governed by Newtonian mechanics, we ground tactile representations in six-dimensional wrenches, establishing a common physical reference across sensing mechanisms. Specifically, we pair tactile observations from all sensors with wrench measurements, enabling shared physical supervision without requiring direct tactile-to-tactile pairing between sensors. To support learning at scale, we develop a dedicated acquisition platform and collect over 100 million paired tactile observations and wrench measurements from 24 sensors spanning four families of sensing principles. Building on this dataset, our framework combines sensor-specific input adaptation with a shared tactile encoder. By learning to reconstruct wrenches, the encoder maps observations from different sensors into a shared, continuous feature space grounded in contact physics. To leverage these representations for manipulation, we introduce TaF-Policy, which integrates tactile features with visual observations and robot states to generate action chunks. We evaluate the learned representations through wrench estimation, cross-sensor retrieval, few-shot adaptation to unseen sensors, and robotic manipulation. On the core benchmark, our method reduces single-frame force RMSE by 17.1% and improves cross-sensor retrieval R@1 by 13.11 percentage points over the strongest baseline. In manipulation experiments, TaF-Policy achieves average success rates of 51.33% across three simulated tasks and 46.25% across four real-world tasks, compared with 47.33% and 23.75%, respectively, for the strongest baselines evaluated on the same task sets. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [26] [SG-ODOM: Event-Driven Proprioceptive State Estimation with Stability-Gated Contact Updates for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20710)
*Francesco Iotti,Chiyun Noh,Franco Angelini,Manolo Garabini,Ayoung Kim,Maurice Fallon,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG-ODOM是一种使用稳定性门控接触选择来调度运动学更新的本体感知里程计框架，仅在足部达到稳定支撑条件后才接受接触进行状态估计，从而提升足式机器人状态估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有本体感知状态估计将惯性传感与腿部编码器运动学融合，但运动学修正假设支撑相足部静止；实际触地（尤其冲击和负载转移阶段）不一定提供静止参考，同一接触相内反复应用运动学修正会引入冗余或不可靠信息。

Method: 提出SG-ODOM框架：物理触地后，稳定性门在足部达到足够稳定的支撑条件时才接受该接触用于状态估计；经校验的触地事件触发估计器修正，额外超时更新限制无中断IMU传播的最大时长。

Result: 在四足和人形多种足式平台、多个序列上与代表性先进本体感知估计器对比，SG-ODOM精度具竞争力或更优；在GrandTour数据集户外序列上，相对位姿误差（RPE）较基线估计器最多降低25%。

Conclusion: 通过稳定性门控接触选择调度运动学更新，可避免不可靠接触信息，提升足式机器人本体感知里程计的估计精度。

Abstract: Reliable proprioceptive state estimation for legged robots fuses inertial sensing with kinematic sensing from leg encoders. The latter assumes a foot is stationary during the stance phase; however, physical foot touchdown does not necessarily provide a stationary reference for odometry, particularly during impact and load transfer. Repeatedly applying kinematic corrections throughout the same contact phase can introduce redundant or unreliable information. We present SG-ODOM, a proprioceptive odometry framework that uses stability-gated contact selection to schedule kinematic updates. Following physical touchdown, a stability gate accepts the contact for state estimation only after the foot reaches a sufficiently stable support condition. The resulting validated touchdown event triggers an estimator correction, while additional timeout updates bound the maximum duration of uninterrupted IMU propagation. The framework is evaluated on multiple legged platforms, both quadrupedal and humanoid robots, against representative state-of-the-art proprioceptive estimators. Across the evaluated sequences, SG-ODOM demonstrates competitive or superior estimation accuracy, reducing Relative Pose Error (RPE) by up to 25% compared to the baseline estimator on outdoor sequences from the GrandTour dataset. Source code: this https URL . Video: this https URL

</details>

Abstract: The authors present a prototype of an electric scooter that uses computer vision to determine driving lanes and automatically modify vehicle configuration. Scooters steer on sidewalks, bike paths, or car lanes. The government imposes speed limits on the lanes in which electric scooters drive. Featuring computer vision, electric scooters can determine driving lanes and automatically modify vehicle configuration. However, such systems' safety is still a concern, and research into such vehicle systems' safety and security aspects is proceeding. First, we will present our vehicular setup. Then, we will present our implemented scooter with safety constraints. Finally, we present our security prototype for such an electric scooter.

*Xinyi Yu,Yunshuang Li,Daniel Seita,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了首个大规模单臂全身抓取数据集WAG（170万关节构型，793个物体），并提出了ARMFUL管线，结合扩散模型与运动规划器，在仿真和真实Franka机械臂上实现了大型物体的全身抓取与举升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常大型或重型物体（如储物箱、篮子、椅子）无法被夹爪直接抓取，但单臂可通过环绕支撑来拾取。此类环绕运动难以遥操作，且现有数据集缺乏全身拾取数据，导致学习困难。

Method: 在关节空间直接采样抓取构型，通过仿真中的挤压和举升原语执行，并在主动摇晃和外部力下进行压力测试，构建了包含170万关节构型、793个物体的WAG数据集；开发了ARMFUL管线，使用在WAG上训练的扩散模型从物体点云生成全身抓取，并配合运动规划器无碰撞到达后执行相同的挤压-举升流程。

Result: 在仿真中，ARMFUL在训练物体上达到85.4%的举升成功率，在未见物体上达到69.9%；在真实Franka机械臂上对10个宜家物体进行100次试验，成功举升70次。

Conclusion: WAG数据集和ARMFUL管线有效解决了单臂全身抓取大型物体的难题，在仿真和真实环境中均表现出较高的成功率，为全身抓取学习提供了数据和方法基础。

Abstract: Many everyday objects, such as storage boxes, baskets, and chairs, are too large or heavy for a gripper to grasp, yet a single arm can still pick them up by wrapping around and supporting them. Such grasps are hard to learn: wrapping motions are difficult to teleoperate, and existing datasets contain little whole-arm pickup data. We introduce WAG (Whole-Arm Grasp Dataset), the first large-scale dataset for single-arm pickup of large objects, with 1.7~million joint configurations across 793 objects. We sample configurations directly in joint space, execute each through squeeze and lift primitives in simulation, and stress-test survivors under active shaking and external forces. To demonstrate the utility of WAG and accomplish the whole-arm pickup task, we develop ARMFUL, a whole-arm pickup pipeline that pairs a diffusion model trained on WAG, generating whole-arm grasps from the object's point cloud, with a motion planner that reaches them collision-free before the same squeeze-and-lift execution. In simulation, ARMFUL reaches 85.4\% lift success on training and 69.9\% on held-out objects, and it lifts the object in 70 of 100 trials on a physical Franka arm across 10 IKEA objects.

</details>


### [29] [Learning and Verifying Progress: Closed-Loop Agentic Planning with Progress-Enhanced Execution for Long-Horizon Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20676)
*Kangjie Zhou,Sukai Wang,Yuhan Xiao,Qinghang Liu,Zhejia Wen,ChunXuan Jiao,Wei Shan,Guanghui Ren,Maoqing Yao,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeaP-VLN提出一种计划-执行-验证-恢复循环的视觉语言导航系统，通过进度验证器和两阶段课程训练提升长时程导航的鲁棒性与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程VLN中，执行器在部署时面临策略导致的状态偏移，且停止信号不能可靠指示子任务完成，局部错误会跨子任务传播。

Method: 系统耦合进度感知执行与独立进度验证：显式使用进度验证器在推进前验证子任务完成并在路线偏离时触发双阶段恢复；隐式通过联合训练执行器进行进度推理与动作预测。采用两阶段进度增强课程：先在专家演示上行为克隆，再在演示、专家动作纠正和进度推理监督上联合训练，并引入策略生成轨迹暴露子任务内漂移与子任务间状态偏移。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上，无需额外导航数据集训练的单目方法中取得最先进成功率；真实世界实验验证了长时程导航有效性。

Conclusion: LeaP-VLN通过进度感知执行与独立验证的闭环机制，有效缓解策略诱导状态偏移和停止信号不可靠问题，提升长时程VLN性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon vision-language navigation (VLN) requires agents to track instruction progress and recover from local errors before they propagate across subtasks. However, executors trained on expert trajectories encounter policy-induced state shifts during deployment, and their stop signals do not reliably indicate subtask completion. We introduce Learning and Verifying Progress for VLN (LeaP-VLN), an agentic VLN system that couples progress-aware execution with independent progress verification through plan--execute--verify--recover cycles. LeaP-VLN leverages progress both explicitly, through a progress verifier that verifies subtask completion before the agent advances and triggers dual-phase recovery upon route deviation, and implicitly, by co-training the executor on progress reasoning and action prediction to enhance the executor's progress awareness and corrective control. To adapt the executor to this closed-loop interface, we propose a two-stage progress-enhanced curriculum: behavior cloning on expert demonstrations, followed by joint training on demonstrations, expert action corrections, and progress-reasoning supervision. Policy-generated trajectories expose the executor to both intra-subtask drift and inter-subtask state shifts, aligning training with the deviations encountered during closed-loop execution. Extensive experiments on R2R-CE and RxR-CE demonstrate state-of-the-art success rates among monocular methods trained without additional navigation datasets, while real-world experiments further validate the effectiveness of LeaP-VLN in long-horizon navigation.

</details>


### [30] [JEPTERA: A Platform Agnostic JEPA-Assisted Model Predictive Planner for Urban UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20593)
*Gibran Fathoni Belva,Mansur Arief,Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPTERA 集成 JEPA、MPC、PID 与安全层，用于无人机城市导航；在 180 次仿真中完整架构成功率最高（30 次完成 28 次），并可跨 Gazebo/ROS 2、MuJoCo、CARLA 移植。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机城市导航需同时考虑环境几何、飞行器动力学与动态障碍，现有方法难以兼顾预测、控制与安全校验，且规划核心与仿真接口耦合影响可移植性。

Method: 提出 JEPTERA 架构：JEPA 预测无人机对候选指令的运动响应以辅助 MPC 选动作，PID 控制执行，安全层基于占据空间与预测障碍运动校验轨迹；通过仿真适配器与平台 YAML 配置分离规划核心与仿真接口。开展消融实验，在三个 gym 场景下评估六种配置共 180 次运行，并做多城市地图路线测试及 Gazebo/ROS 2、MuJoCo、CARLA 集成测试。

Result: 完整架构取得最高总体成功率，30 次运行完成 28 次；在三张城市地图上完成一条路线且无碰撞；在不修改核心规划逻辑的情况下于 Gazebo/ROS 2、MuJoCo 和 CARLA 中运行。

Conclusion: 结果表明该集成架构用于仿真城市无人机导航具有可行性，且具备跨仿真接口的可移植性。

Abstract: Urban navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) requires planning that accounts for environmental geometry, vehicle dynamics, and moving obstacles. This paper presents JEPTERA, a navigation architecture that integrates a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), Model Predictive Control (MPC), PID control, and a safety layer. JEPA predicts the UAV's motion response to candidate commands for MPC action selection, while the safety layer checks trajectories against occupied space and predicted obstacle motion. Simulator adapters and platform-specific YAML configurations separate the planning core from simulator interfaces. An ablation study evaluated six configurations across three gym scenarios, totaling 180 runs. The complete architecture achieved the highest aggregate success rate, completing 28 of 30 runs. Additional tests completed one route across three city maps without collisions. Separate integration tests demonstrated operation in Gazebo/ROS 2, MuJoCo, and CARLA without modifying the core planning logic. These results support the feasibility of the integrated architecture for simulated urban UAV navigation and its portability across simulator interfaces.

</details>


### [31] [Stability Principle Inherent in Wheel Gait of Planar X-shaped Walker Generated Using Constant Torque Drive and Mechanical Stoppers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20587)
*Fumihiko Asano,Cong Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究髋关节机械限位器对平面X形步行机器人轮式步态稳定性的影响，通过分阶段线性化分析状态误差传递矩阵，揭示机械约束产生整体渐近稳定性的机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知髋/膝关节机械限位器能生成稳定被动动态步行，但机械约束如何增强整体步态稳定性的理论研究不足。

Method: 设计带髋关节机械限位器的平面X形步行机器人，在斜坡上以恒定扭矩驱动；将单步运动分为四个阶段，基于线性化模型推导各阶段状态误差传递函数矩阵的近似解析解，并用最大奇异值等指标分析状态误差范数的增减。

Result: 数值仿真表明，两个阶段的状态误差范数增加（不稳定），另外两个阶段减少（稳定），整体呈现渐近稳定性；机器人仅靠前倾并利用限位器约束至目标冲击姿态即可生成高稳定轮式步态。

Conclusion: 髋关节机械限位器通过分阶段约束与误差修正机制，使轮式步态整体渐近稳定，为机械约束增强步态稳定性提供了理论解释。

Abstract: Since the late 19th century when the first walking toys were developed, it has been known that mechanical stoppers at the hip joint are crucial for generating stable passive dynamic walking. Recent research on passive-dynamic and limit-cycle walkers has also confirmed that mechanical stoppers at the hip and knee joints are effective for generating stable walking motion, but theoretical research on how this mechanical constraint enhances the overall gait stability remains insufficient. This paper introduces a planar X-shaped walker equipped with mechanical stoppers at the hip joint, and investigates the effect of the mechanical constraint on the stability of the wheel gait generated by constant torque drive on a downslope. By simply falling forward while using the stoppers to constrain itself to the target impact posture, the robot can generate a highly stable wheel gait. We divide the motion of one step into four phases, derive approximate analytical solutions for the state error transition function matrix in each phase using a linearized model, and analyze the increase or decrease in the state error norm using metrics such as its maximum singular value. Numerical simulations demonstrate that while two phases are unstable in terms of the increase in the state error norm, the remaining two phases are stable, resulting in overall asymptotic stability.

</details>


### [32] [ExtremeDrive: A Real-World Dataset and Benchmark for Critical Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20574)
*Xinhan Li,Yuhong Jiang,Shiyue Zhao,Junzhi Zhang,Chengkun He,Ruihai Ma,Shijie Zhao,Wenxuan Cheng,Jinbo Lv,Tao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ExtremeDrive真实世界数据集与基准，覆盖安全关键场景与极端环境，实验表明加入关键驾驶数据可降低轨迹预测误差并提升关键成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集和基准多聚焦常规驾驶，缺乏覆盖安全关键场景与极端环境条件的真实数据。

Method: 构建ExtremeDrive数据集，从大量真实驾驶记录中筛选589个驾驶场景，包含紧急机动、复杂交通交互及湿滑/积雪等恶劣条件，提供校准对齐的多传感器数据和路结构/交通参与者标注；建立基准，通过模拟车辆执行评估规划轨迹，衡量安全相关指标和驾驶性能。

Result: 在相同训练集规模下，融入关键驾驶数据可降低轨迹预测误差，并提升关键场景中的平均关键成功率。

Conclusion: ExtremeDrive为安全关键场景和极端环境条件下的自动驾驶学习与评估提供了统一资源。

Abstract: Existing autonomous driving datasets and benchmarks provide extensive resources for routine driving, yet real-world data covering safety-critical scenarios and extreme environmental conditions remain limited. To address this gap, we present ExtremeDrive, a real-world dataset and benchmark covering emergency maneuvers, complex traffic interactions, and adverse conditions such as slippery or snow-covered roads, alongside routine driving. The dataset comprises 589 driving scenes curated from a larger pool of real-world driving recordings, with calibrated and aligned multisensor data and reviewed annotations of road structure and traffic participants. We further establish a benchmark to evaluate planned trajectories through simulated vehicle execution, measuring safety-related metrics and driving performance. Experiments at equal training set sizes show that incorporating critical driving data reduces trajectory prediction errors and improves mean critical-success rates in critical scenarios. ExtremeDrive provides a unified resource for learning and evaluating autonomous driving in safety-critical scenarios and extreme environmental conditions.

</details>


### [33] [sqrtVINS-Based 360 Visual-Inertial SLAM Systems: Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge 2026 Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20568)
*Jungwoo Lee,Hogyun Kim,Jiwon Choi,Jinyong Jeong,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报道了2026年Hilti-Trimble SLAM挑战赛的参赛系统，一个仅使用双鱼眼图像流和IMU的360相机视觉惯性SLAM流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对室内长序列中暗区、重复走廊、动态物体和有限几何视差等挑战，构建可复现的鲁棒视觉惯性SLAM系统。

Method: 先对原始图像做低光增强和静态/动态掩码预处理，输入基于SuperPoint特征、ELSED线段和LightGlue匹配的√VINS前端作为局部里程计；再用GTSAM后端进行紧耦合因子图优化，结合SALAD全局描述子和视觉BA，并执行局部到全局的无结构BA、紧正则化的内外参精化以及利用相机间基线和跨视角约束的全局尺度调整。

Result: 在多个室内序列上，所提方法与楼层平面图对齐良好，验证了系统的定位与建图精度。

Conclusion: 该流水线能在给定ROS2工作空间中复现，并有效应对暗区、重复走廊、动态物体和有限视差等室内SLAM难题。

Abstract: This report describes our submission to the 2026 Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge. The system is a 360-camera visual-inertial pipeline that uses only the provided dual-fisheye image streams and IMU measurements. First, raw images are preprocessed with low-light enhancement and static/dynamic masking, and then fed into a $\sqrt{\text{VINS}}$ frontend as the local odometry, with deep learning-based SuperPoint features, ELSED line segments, and LightGlue matching. The resulting trajectory is refined using a GTSAM-based backend that performs tightly-coupled factor graph optimization with SALAD global descriptor and visual bundle adjustment (BA). The backend performs local-to-global structureless BA, tightly regularized intrinsic and extrinsic refinement, and global scale adjustment supported by the known inter-camera baseline and cross-view constraints. The design targets long indoor sequences with dark regions, repetitive corridors, dynamic objects, and limited geometric parallax, while keeping the pipeline reproducible in the provided ROS2 workspace. Fig. 1 demonstrates that our proposed method aligns well with the floorplan across several indoor sequences.

</details>


### [34] [WTR-Drive: World-Task Decomposition with Structured Risk Supervision for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20510)
*Haoyu Xing,Shaobing Ding,Guanglei Zhao,Guanhua Zhao,Nawu Peng,Mingyang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WTR-Drive 是一个基于 Drive-JEPA 的规划器，将共享场景估计与候选轨迹条件推理分离，在 NAVSIM 上提升了 PDMS 和 EPDMS 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有候选轨迹条件场景预测缺乏跨候选轨迹的共同对象级参考；聚合规划分数使违规涉及的对象和时间隐含不明确。

Method: 提出 WTR-Drive：与候选无关的 World Memory 在每轮细化时重读原始视觉特征，估计对象状态、地图结构和未来运动；候选反馈仅更新残差 Task 分支而不回写 World Memory，该分支通过候选特定检索和门控视觉反馈引导细化；最终 Task 读出提供候选条件特征用于评分；从现有候选评分 rollout 中推导结构化风险目标，编码事件存在性、共享对象槽关联及首次违规时间箱。

Result: 在 NAVSIM navtest 划分上，WTR-Drive 在 v1 上达到 94.66 PDMS，在 v2 上达到 88.24 EPDMS，分别比已发表的 Drive-JEPA 值高 0.96 和 0.44 分；候选级分析显示最佳候选分数更高可能与所选轨迹分数更低共存。

Conclusion: 分离共享场景估计与候选条件推理可提升规划性能；候选质量与选择有效性之间存在差距，需关注选择机制。

Abstract: Proposal-conditioned scene predictions can lack a common object-level reference across candidate trajectories. Aggregate planning scores also leave the objects and times associated with violations implicit. We present WTR-Drive (World-Task-Risk Driving), a Drive-JEPA-based planner that separates shared scene estimation from proposal-conditioned reasoning. A proposal-independent World Memory estimates object states, map structure, and future motion by re-reading the original visual features at each refinement round. Proposal feedback updates a residual Task branch without writing back to World Memory. This branch guides refinement through candidate-specific retrieval and gated visual feedback. A final Task readout supplies candidate-conditioned features for scoring. We derive structured risk targets from existing candidate-scoring rollouts without additional simulator calls. For at-fault collision and time-to-collision violations, the targets encode event existence, association with shared object slots, and the first violation time bin. On the NAVSIM navtest split, WTR-Drive achieves 94.66 PDMS on v1 and 88.24 EPDMS on v2 (expert-frame sequential evaluation). These scores are 0.96 and 0.44 points above the published Drive-JEPA values, respectively. Candidate-level analysis shows that higher best-candidate scores can coexist with lower selected-trajectory scores, revealing a gap between candidate quality and selection effectiveness.

</details>


### [35] [Lift, See, Act: Hierarchical Robot Policy Pretraining with 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20481)
*Yiyuan Ge,Changxing Ding,Ziyu Hao,Zijie Zheng,Xiangmin Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LSA是一种三阶段分层机器人策略预训练框架，通过稠密3D表示学习与空间引导，从人类视频中提升策略泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法从人类视频中用3D基础模型估计手部运动轨迹，但仅保留稀疏轨迹作为监督，忽略了轨迹提取过程中产生的丰富细粒度信息，导致缺乏显式的动作相关表示。

Method: 提出LSA框架，包含Lift、See、Act三个阶段：Lift阶段将2D视频观测提升为稠密3D表示，通过自适应对齐策略浅层特征与多个3D基础模型的中间表示；See阶段通过双重引导赋予策略显式几何与交互感知，引入深度图重建帮助理解3D空间布局，并利用手部区域线索显式监督编码器关注任务相关交互热点；Act阶段实现稳健准确的机器人操作。

Result: 在多种仿真和真实世界任务上的大量实验表明，LSA显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 稠密3D表示学习与精确空间引导能够有效从无标注人类视频中预训练机器人策略，提升操作性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Robot policy pretraining based on human videos is crucial for improving the policy's generalization ability. One main challenge for this task is the lack of explicit action-relevant representations in such unlabeled data. Recent works tend to estimate 3D hand motion trajectories from videos using 3D foundation models (3D FMs). However, they keep solely the sparse trajectories as supervision, ignoring the rich, fine-grained information produced during the trajectory extraction process. To address this issue, we propose LSA, a hierarchical robot policy pretraining framework that includes three phases: Lift, See, and Act. Specifically, the Lift phase elevates 2D video observations into dense 3D representations by adaptively aligning features in the policy's shallow layers with the intermediate representations extracted from multiple 3D FMs. Building upon these lifted representations, the See phase equips the policy with explicit geometric and interactive awareness through dual guidance. It introduces depth-map reconstruction to help the model comprehend 3D spatial layouts and utilizes hand region cues to explicitly supervise the encoder's attention toward task-relevant interaction hotspots. Empowered by the dense 3D representation learning and precise spatial guidance, our policy achieves robust and accurate robotic manipulation in the Act phase. Extensive experiments on diverse simulation and real-world tasks demonstrate that LSA significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art approaches. The project page is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [36] [Stochastic Self-Distillation for Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20460)
*Jingru Guo,Alessandra Russo,Joey Bose,Christos Ziakas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-SSD通过将流匹配动作头自蒸馏为随机一步策略，解决流式VLA模型在on-policy强化学习中的高推理延迟和低随机性问题，并在LIBERO上以小幅成功率下降换取效率与后训练增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式VLA模型虽在模仿学习中表现优异，但用on-policy强化学习微调受限：基于组的RL方法（如GRPO）每个rollout步骤需多步ODE积分导致高延迟，且采样器随机性有限使rollout组方差低。

Method: 提出VLA-SSD，将预训练流匹配动作头自蒸馏为随机一步策略，通过让单跳生成器以布朗路径为条件实现；由此导出条件动作密度，支持基于似然比的RL算法进行高效后训练。

Result: 从π0 VLA蒸馏VLA-SSD使动作头前向传递时间减少7.7倍，LIBERO基准成功率相对下降5.5%；但利用VLA-SSD在组生成中增加的样本多样性，用GRPO后训练π0.5 VLA获得20.6%的相对提升。

Conclusion: VLA-SSD有效兼顾推理效率与采样多样性，虽带来小幅性能损失，但显著提升组式强化学习后训练效果，为流式VLA的on-policy RL微调提供可行方案。

Abstract: Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models achieve strong performance in robotics through imitation learning, yet fine-tuning them with on-policy reinforcement learning (RL) is limited. In group-based RL methods like Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), every rollout step requires multi-step ODE integration, and the sampler's limited stochasticity leads to rollout groups with low variance. We propose VLA-SSD, which addresses both high inference latency and limited stochasticity. In particular, our method self-distills a pretrained flow-matching action head into a stochastic one-step policy by conditioning a single-jump generator on a Brownian path. We derive a conditional action density via our stochastic one-step policy, enabling efficient post-training via likelihood-ratio RL algorithms. We show that distilling VLA-SSD from the $\pi_0$ VLA reduces the action head's forward-pass time by a factor of 7.7, at the cost of a 5.5% relative decrease in the success rate on the LIBERO benchmark. However, by leveraging the increased sample diversity of VLA-SSD during group generation, post-training a $\pi_{0.5}$ VLA using GRPO yields a 20.6% relative improvement.

</details>


### [37] [XMimic and XSoma: Learning Humanoid Motion Tracking and Locomotion in Under a Minute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20452)
*Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出XMimic和XSoma，基于SuperMimic方法，利用JAX编译完整训练循环并结合MuJoCo Warp模拟器，在四块GPU上并行化短链计算，实现不到一分钟内学习人形机器人运动跟踪与崎岖地形行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SuperMimic实现因训练迭代为长串行小GPU操作链而无法利用多GPU加速，训练效率低。

Method: 缩短每轮迭代操作链，在最多四块GPU上同时执行各步骤；用JAX编译含MuJoCo Warp模拟器的完整训练循环；XSoma仅将机器人动力学交给世界模型，其余精确计算（含足下地形高度），采用一秒视界且无需critic。

Result: 四块H100上，XMimic用0.86分钟让Unitree G1跟踪全部40个LAFAN1片段，比单GPU的BlinkMimic快3.7倍；XSoma用0.77分钟达到Holosoma为FastSAC设定的跟踪目标，比单GPU的FastSAC快12.3倍，环境步数仅为其八分之一。

Conclusion: 通过缩短串行链并多GPU并行编译训练循环，可大幅加速基于世界模型的人形运动学习，显著减少训练时间和样本需求。

Abstract: We present XMimic and XSoma, which learn humanoid motion tracking and locomotion on rough terrain in under a minute of training. Both follow SuperMimic, which learns a world model of the robot and optimizes the policy with analytic gradients through short rollouts imagined in this model. Existing implementations of this method do not make use of larger GPUs, as every training iteration is a long chain of small GPU operations that run one after another. XMimic and XSoma therefore shorten this chain and run the steps of every iteration at the same time on up to four GPUs. We compile the complete training loop with JAX, including the MuJoCo Warp simulator. For locomotion, XSoma leaves only the dynamics of the robot to the world model and computes everything else exactly, including the terrain height under the feet. It learns with a horizon of one second and without a critic. On four H100 GPUs, XMimic learns to track all 40 LAFAN1 clips with the Unitree G1 in 0.86 minutes, 3.7 times faster than BlinkMimic on one GPU. XSoma reaches the tracking target that Holosoma's tests set for FastSAC in 0.77 minutes, 12.3 times faster than FastSAC on one GPU, while needing only an eighth of its environment steps.

</details>


### [38] [High-Speed Forward and Reverse Tractor-Trailer Driving with Learned Vehicle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20324)
*Aaron Chen,David Shen,Taekyung Kim,Kaleb Ben Naveed,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出学习动力学模型用于拖拉机-挂车控制，并与解析模型对比，实验表明学习模型在跟踪速度和铰接角控制上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拖拉机-挂车系统控制困难，倒车时不稳定，且动力学依赖路面摩擦、悬架系数等难以测量的因素。

Method: 构建学习动力学模型，与基于Fiala轮胎力的降阶解析模型作为基线对比；采用MPPI控制器分别结合学习模型和解析模型，并对比三种额外基线控制器，在四个仿真环境中进行闭环跟踪实验。

Result: 在每个测试设定点下，学习动力学MPPI的平均速度更接近目标值，铰接角RMS更低；在八种倒车配置中有七种完成率更高。

Conclusion: 学习动力学模型结合MPPI控制器在拖拉机-挂车跟踪控制中优于解析模型基线，尤其在倒车场景表现更佳。

Abstract: Tractor-trailer systems are difficult to control, especially unstable during reverse motion, and their dynamics depend on difficult-to-measure factors, including road friction and suspension coefficients. We introduce a learned dynamics model for tractor-trailer control and compare it with a reduced-order dynamics model with Fiala tire forces as an analytic baseline. Specifically, we compare a model predictive path integral (MPPI) controller that uses the learned dynamics model to an MPPI controller that uses the analytic model, as well as to three additional baseline controllers. In the main closed-loop tracking experiments across four simulated environments, MPPI with learned dynamics achieves mean speeds closer to the target magnitudes and lower root-mean-square (RMS) hitch angles than MPPI with the analytic model at every tested setpoint, with higher completion rates in seven of eight reverse configurations.

</details>


### [39] [Ontology-Compiled Control Barrier Functions for Heterogeneous Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20306)
*Peng Wu,Seyede Fatemeh Ghoreishi,Nathan Bastian,Mohsen Imani,Tian Lan,Mahdi Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OntoCBF通过将领域本体编译为控制障碍函数过滤器，在线为异构机器人团队提供安全约束，但多机器人交互中仍存在不可避免的冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人团队的安全过滤器因缺少约束、制动能力高估及个体可满足约束间的冲突而失效。

Method: 构建含类分类、类型化关系、安全公理和每类机器人动力学配置的领域本体，编译为每智能体一个二次规划的控制障碍函数过滤器；用描述逻辑推理在线获取团队约束；证明子类可复用父类制动能力的条件。
