Result: 仿真仓库中设计类过滤器在未见类别下89-100%回合违反安全；OntoCBF在所有场景（含32机器人）中20-70%回合违反安全，均发生在冲突后，中位最深侵入为禁入距离的10-17%；带过滤器训练的多智能体PPO将不可行二次规划率降低18-41%。

Conclusion: 本体推理能扩展安全约束覆盖新类别，但多机器人交互中冲突导致的安全损失不可避免，需结合学习策略减少冲突。

Abstract: Safety filters for heterogeneous robot teams fail because of missing constraints, overestimated braking capabilities and conflicts between individually satisfiable constraints. We present OntoCBF, which compiles a domain ontology with a class taxonomy, typed relations, safety axioms and a dynamics profile per robot class into a control barrier function filter with one quadratic program per agent. Description-logic entailment supplies the constraints of any team online, including those that follow from chains of relations and from classes defined by what a robot carries, so the existing safety axioms cover new robot classes, payload classes and combinations. We prove that a subclass may reuse the braking capability of its parent whenever it can produce every velocity of the parent, a property separate from subsumption. For box-bounded double integrators this property is also necessary, and every loss of safety then begins with a conflict. Once three robots interact, the safe set of pairwise barriers contains states from which a collision is unavoidable, and such states persist in larger teams under any fixed capability reduction. In a simulated warehouse where humans ignore the robots, a filter written for the design classes violates safety in 89-100 % of the episodes with unseen classes. In all scenarios, including one with 32 robots, OntoCBF violates safety in 20-70 % of the episodes, each time after a conflict, with a median deepest intrusion of 10-17 % of the keep-out distance. A multi-agent PPO policy trained with the filter in the loop lowers the rate of infeasible quadratic programs, nearly all of them conflicts, by 18-41 %.

</details>


### [40] [DexFormer 2.0: Towards a Cross-Embodiment Foundation Policy for Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20273)
*Lixin Xu,Ke Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Jie Tan,Senming Tan,Jiaju Yin,Yating Feng,Xiaoyi Lin,Xiyue Dong,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexFormer 2.0 是一个单一强化学习策略，通过规范关节接口、运动树偏置注意力和 Transformer-XL 记忆，实现跨多种手臂-手部组合的灵巧抓取，并能零样本迁移到未见过的组合和手型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取策略通常针对单一手臂-手部配对训练，每新增一个配对就需要新策略，策略数量随手臂和手部数量增长而膨胀；核心问题是能否用单一策略控制多种手臂-手部组合，并迁移到未见过的配对和手型。

Method: 提出 DexFormer 2.0：将每个机器人映射到带有效性掩码的规范关节接口；用受运动学树偏置的注意力编码各关节形态；用 Transformer-XL 记忆从近期交互中推断与具体 embodiment 相关的动力学；在 4 种手臂和 6 种手部组成的 18 个组合上训练。

Result: 在已见组合上达到 89% 稳定成功率，在 6 个留出的手臂-手部配对上达到 51%，优于 GRU 和 DexFormer 1.0 基线；无需微调即可用四种新手型抓取，并可通过重定向用于平行夹爪；消融实验表明记忆、运动学树偏置和更广的配对覆盖均对迁移有贡献；在物理 Flexiv 手臂上，蒸馏学生策略从仿真零样本迁移到两种手（含一个留出配对）。

Conclusion: 单一强化学习策略能够跨多种手臂-手部组合控制灵巧抓取，并迁移到未见过的配对、手型和平行夹爪，说明规范接口、运动学结构先验和记忆机制是实现跨 embodiment 泛化的关键。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping policies are usually trained for one arm paired with one hand. Every new pairing then needs its own policy, and the number of policies grows with every arm and hand added. We ask whether a single policy can control many arm-hand combinations and transfer to pairings and hands it has never seen. This is hard because robots differ in the number, layout, and dynamics of their joints, so their observations and actions share no common interface. We present DexFormer 2.0, a single reinforcement learning policy for cross-embodiment dexterous grasping. It maps every robot to a canonical joint interface with validity masks, encodes each joint's morphology with attention biased by the kinematic tree, and uses a Transformer-XL memory to infer embodiment-dependent dynamics from recent interaction. Trained on 18 combinations of four arms and six hands, it reaches 89% stable success on seen combinations and 51% on six held-out arm-hand pairs, outperforming GRU and DexFormer 1.0 baselines. Without fine-tuning, it also grasps with four new hand types and, through retargeting, with parallel grippers. Ablations show that memory, the kinematic-tree bias, and broader pairing coverage each matter for transfer. On a physical Flexiv arm, distilled students transfer zero-shot from simulation with two hands, including a held-out pair. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [41] [DAPPT: Domain-Aware Policies from Procedural Trajectories for Sim-to-Real Dexterous Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20202)
*Beining Han,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DAPPT框架，在不模拟非刚体材料的情况下，通过跟踪由域参数参数化的程序轨迹并蒸馏为端到端域感知感觉运动策略，实现涉及可变形物体和颗粒的灵巧工具使用操作的仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真到现实强化学习在灵巧操作中依赖精确刚体仿真，涉及可变形物体和颗粒时仿真速度过慢，无法用于大规模强化学习。

Method: 学习域感知强化学习策略以跟踪由域参数参数化的程序轨迹，并将其蒸馏为端到端域感知感觉运动策略；利用显式域表示快速选择可最大化下游任务性能的域参数。

Result: 在六个灵巧操作任务上端到端评估，任务指标提升1.5-3.8倍，并实现零样本迁移到真实世界。

Conclusion: DAPPT能够在不模拟非刚体材料的情况下，有效学习并迁移涉及可变形物体和颗粒的灵巧工具使用操作技能。

Abstract: Sim-to-real RL has achieved impressive results in dexterous manipulation but remains limited to tasks with accurate rigid-body simulation. However, these methods cannot be applied to skills that involve deformable and particles, with which the simulation speed will become too slow for large-scale RL learning. We present DAPPT, a sim-to-real framework for end-to-end dexterous tool-use manipulation skills, e.g., sand scooping, play-dough rod-rolling, without simulating non-rigid materials. Our method learns a domain-aware RL policy by tracking procedural trajectories parameterized by domain parameters, and distills it into an end-to-end domain-aware sensorimotor policy. Furthermore, explicit domain represen-tation enables fast selection of domain parameters that can maximize downstream task performance. We evaluate DAPPT end-to-end on six dexterous manipulation tasks, including sand scooping, play-dough rod-rolling, sand scraping, mouse-wheel scrolling, dispenser pressing, and spray triggering. Our method achieves 1.5-3.8x in task metrics through the best domain parameter and transfers zero-shot to the real world.

</details>


### [42] [Cleanly-DINO: Efficiently Learning Visual Task Spaces for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20137)
*Adrian Röfer,Melika Shekarriz,José Arce y de la Borbolla,Iman Nematollahi,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Cleanly-DINO，从少量演示中学习视觉任务空间，用于清洁等由视觉状态变化定义的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 清洁等任务由视觉状态变化而非物体位姿定义，传统抽象表示不适用，需从少量演示中学习视觉任务空间。

Method: 给定任务相关区域掩码和冻结ViT特征，Cleanly-DINO拟合投影以隔离任务空间，并拟合进度度量；用多样化清洁示例训练。

Result: 模型能定位未完成任务区域并跨视觉域迁移；作为观测编码器和奖励信号提升强化学习效果；在真实机器人擦拭流程中验证了任务执行引导能力。

Conclusion: Cleanly-DINO为视觉状态定义的任务提供有效的少样本任务空间学习与进度估计方法，可服务于机器人学习与真实部署。

Abstract: Learning manipulation tasks from few demonstrations requires abstractions that capture the changes relevant to task completion, such as object poses in rigid-body manipulation. However, tasks such as cleaning are defined by changes in visual state rather than object pose, making the choice of task representation less clear. In this work, we propose Cleanly-DINO to learn visual task spaces from few demonstrations. Given a mask of a task-relevant region and frozen ViT features, Cleanly-DINO fits a projection to isolate a task space and fit a progression metric to it. We train the model on diverse cleaning examples and evaluate its ability to localize incomplete task regions and transfer across visual domains. We also study its utility for robot learning by using it as an observation encoder and reward signal in a reinforcement learning setting. Finally, we deploy our fitted model within a real-world robotic wiping pipeline to evaluate its use in guiding task execution.

</details>


### [43] [EgoSwarm: Learning Decentralized Robot Swarm Intelligence from Human Swarm Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20100)
*Ziqiang Liu,Xitong Chen,Min Li,Yiqun Li,Zhuoping Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EgoSwarm框架，从人类多主体第一人称演示中学习去中心化多机器人协作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习方法依赖个体演示，难以建模智能体动作间依赖，且规模化采集多机器人演示困难；人类第一人称演示可扩展但分布式观测无法直接监督去中心化策略。

Method: 采集超50小时多人第一人称协作数据，提出Swarm Interaction Packet（SIP）与Swarm Interaction Token（SIT）从分布式观测恢复跨智能体交互关系并转为局部监督，提出SwarmVLA将群体行为知识迁移到仅依赖局部观测、局部历史和有限邻居消息的去中心化策略。

Result: SwarmVLA在三类多机器人协作任务上平均成功率达72.2%。

Conclusion: EgoSwarm首次实现从人类群体演示学习去中心化多机器人协作，为规模化通用具身智能提供可行路径。

Abstract: Advancing from individual embodied intelligence to swarm embodied intelligence is a key step toward scalable, general-purpose embodied intelligence. Complex tasks often require multiple agents to divide responsibilities, coordinate handovers, and manipulate shared objects collaboratively. However, existing robot learning methods rely primarily on individual demonstrations, which provide limited supervision for modeling dependencies among agent actions, while collecting multi-robot demonstrations at scale remains difficult. Human egocentric demonstrations offer a scalable alternative, yet distributed observations from multiple participants cannot directly supervise decentralized policies. We introduce EgoSwarm, the first robot learning framework for learning decentralized multi-robot collaboration from human swarm demonstrations. EgoSwarm collects more than 50 hours of collaborative experience using a robot-free multi-person egocentric system. We introduce the Swarm Interaction Packet (SIP) and Swarm Interaction Token (SIT) to recover cross-agent interaction relations from distributed observations and transform this experience into local supervision consistent with deployment-time information constraints. We further propose SwarmVLA, which transfers knowledge of swarm behavior to decentralized policies conditioned on local observations, local history, and limited neighbor messages. SwarmVLA achieves a mean success rate of 72.2% across three categories of multi-robot collaborative tasks. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [44] [MoViC: Real-Time Humanoid Whole-Body Control from Monocular Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19998)
*Yimin Han,Jiahui Zhang,Wanyue Li,Zeren Luo,Yeke Chen,Liu Zhao,Peizhuo Li,Xiaoyu Ji,Shihao Dong,Xuechao Chen,Zhangguo Yu,Annan Tang,Peng Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoViC 提出一种无需可穿戴设备的框架，利用单目 RGB 视频作为人形机器人全身实时模仿的控制信号，结合运动重建、重定向与基于 Transformer 的行为基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时全身模仿需要鲁棒的通用跟踪器与高质量实时重建，同时现有方法依赖可穿戴设备或显式堆叠未来参考帧，导致输入维度大、网络复杂。

Method: 框架包含单目人体运动重建、机器人重定向和 Transformer 行为基础模型；策略输入本体感受历史、单个显式下一步参考和紧凑频域运动表示以提供时序上下文；部署时用外推方法桥接 10Hz 视觉估计与 50Hz 控制器，实现异步视觉处理与策略执行。

Result: 硬件实验展示了高度动态的运动跟踪，以及基于实时单目 RGB 输入的无设备双臂和单臂操作任务遥操作。

Conclusion: MoViC 验证了仅用单目 RGB 视频即可实现人形机器人实时全身模仿与无设备遥操作，且通过频域表示和外推方法降低了输入维度并支持异步执行。

Abstract: Real-time whole-body imitation from monocular video requires both a robust universal tracker and high-quality real-time reconstruction. We present MoViC, a framework that uses live RGB video as a control signal for humanoid robots without wearable tracking devices. The framework combines monocular human motion reconstruction, robot retargeting, and a transformer-based behavior foundation model. The policy receives proprioceptive history, a single explicit next-step reference, and a compact frequency-domain motion representation that supplies temporal context without explicitly stacking future reference frames, which reduces the input dimension and network size. During deployment, our extrapolation method bridges the 10-Hz visual estimation with 50-Hz controller, enabling asynchronous visual processing and policy execution. Hardware experiments further demonstrate highly dynamic motion tracking and device-free teleoperation of bimanual and single-arm manipulation tasks from live monocular RGB input. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [Practice Is Not Free: Measuring the Embodied Cost of Reset-Free Robot Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19979)
*Vedant Telrandhe,Sushil Sharma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出低成本SO-101双臂自复位测试床，显式计量机器人练习中的时间、关节磨损与人工干预成本，并预先注册成本感知练习调度比较方案；51分钟硬件实验显示固定重试上限未减少失败时间占比（36% vs 27%，不显著）且不省时，因失败无聚集性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有练习调度器把每次尝试视为零成本，但实际无人复位场景下每次尝试都消耗墙钟时间、执行器磨损甚至人工注意力，缺乏可测量这些具身成本的开放测试床与成本感知调度框架。

Method: 自建SO-101机械臂运行两个演示训练的ACT策略互相复位，配合视觉成功检测器和记录每次尝试时间、关节行程与人工干预的账本；形式化成本感知练习调度，以扣除具身成本后的能力增益为奖励，并预先指定比较条件；在硬件上运行前两个条件共51分钟。

Result: 固定重试上限未降低失败导致的执行时间占比（36%，可行性规则为27%，差异不显著），也未节省机器人时间；日志显示失败无聚集迹象，失败后立即重试成功率与其他尝试相当；重试规则额外人工时间主要来自方块被击出可达范围；成本感知规则在日志回放中无需手工上限即恢复合理选择；还发现覆盖效应：将演示集中于更小工作区使该区域成功率从8/20提升至10/10。

Conclusion: 练习尝试的具身成本应被显式建模和计量；固定启发式重试规则缺乏收益，而基于测量成本与能力增益的成本感知调度可在回放中自动做出合理决策；所提开放低成本测试床与预注册比较为后续研究提供可复现基础。

Abstract: When a robot practices without human resets, every attempt still costs wall-clock time, actuator wear, and sometimes a person's attention, yet the schedulers that decide what to practice next treat attempts as free. We present an open, low-cost testbed for measuring these costs: a self-built SO-101 arm runs two demonstration-trained ACT policies that reset each other, with a vision-based success detector and a ledger that records the time, joint travel, and human interventions of every attempt. We formulate cost-aware practice scheduling, in which task selection is rewarded by measured competence gain net of embodied cost, and specify in advance the comparison that will test it. Its first two conditions, run on hardware for 51 minutes of robot time, show that a fixed retry limit did not reduce the share of execution time lost to failures (36%, against 27% for a feasibility rule; not significant) and saved no robot time. The logs suggest why: failures showed no sign of clustering, so a retry right after a failure was as likely to succeed as any other attempt. The ledger also shows that most of the retry rule's extra human time came from cubes knocked out of reach rather than from the rule itself, and a cost-aware rule replayed on the logs recovers the sensible choice without a hand-set limit. We also report lessons from building the system, including a coverage effect in which concentrating demonstrations in a smaller workspace raised success there from 8/20 to 10/10.

</details>


### [46] [CRAFT: A Robot-Agent Harness with Scope-Aware Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19952)
*Peng Zhang,Jiawen Wang,Cheng Xue,Jochen Renz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出CRAFT框架，将物理成功与需求满足分开记录，并用记忆、显式规则和学习器的“阶梯”融合来学习局部需求，在仿真中优于单一记忆但仍有适应变化需求的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人虽能移动物体却需学习物体应放在何处；当人类拒绝某次放置时，机器人需判断该纠正仅适用于当前情境还是表达更广泛规则，且不能重新训练机器人技能。

Method: CRAFT框架不重训技能，单独记录物理成功与需求满足；采用范围阶梯：近同情境已判定时用记忆，否则按对先前反馈的预测准确度加权融合记忆、显式规则和规则外学习器的预测。

Result: 受控实验中效用优于仅用纠正记忆；在相机接地的机器人仿真中，阶梯比带持久记忆的语言模型智能体提问更少，但部分智能体错误动作更少且在需求变化后成功率更高；所有执行失败和超时均纳入评估。

Conclusion: 结果支持将具体记忆与更广规则结合，但可靠适应变化需求仍未解决；所有证据均来自带生成需求和模拟反馈的仿真。

Abstract: A robot may know how to move an object yet still need to learn where it belongs. When a person rejects a placement, the robot must decide whether the correction applies only to that situation or expresses a broader rule. We study this problem in CRAFT (Contextual Requirement Adaptation with Frozen Tools), a framework for learning local requirements without retraining robot skills. It records physical success separately from whether an action meets the requirement. Our scope ladder uses memory when an action has already been judged in a nearly identical situation. Elsewhere, it combines memory, explicit rules and a learner for requirements beyond those rules, weighting their predictions by how well they predicted earlier feedback. Controlled studies show gains over correction memory alone in utility, which rewards acceptable actions and charges for mistakes and questions. In camera-grounded robot simulations, the ladder asks fewer questions than language-model agents with persistent memory, but some of those agents take fewer wrong actions and succeed more often after requirements change. All execution failures and timeouts remain in the evaluation. These results support combining specific memories with broader rules, while leaving reliable adaptation to changing requirements unresolved. All evidence comes from simulation with generated requirements and simulated feedback.

</details>


### [47] [From Companion to Guardian: Designing an Emergency Support System Using a Quadruped Robot for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19932)
*Chunyang Meng,Eduardo B. Sandoval,Josh Andres,Francisco Cruz,Mari Velonaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一款基于Unitree Go1四足平台的居家陪伴机器人系统，将日常陪伴与紧急感知响应结合，采用三阶段交互模式和基于规则的升级策略，并通过105次自动化试验和25次物理试验验证了决策规则与升级时序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人独居现象日益普遍，且护理支持人力需求预计大幅增长，家用陪伴机器人需在提供日常陪伴的同时具备紧急支持能力，以弥补人类支持无法即时到位的空缺。

Method: 在Unitree Go1四足平台上构建陪伴机器人系统，采用陪伴、危险感知和紧急三阶段交互模型；基于视觉和语音输入检测到的迹象，通过规则化升级策略判定风险等级并调整等待用户确认安全的时间，更多迹象可缩短等待时间；用户可确认安全、请求帮助、停止运动或重置系统。

Result: 通过105次自动化试验和25次分阶段物理试验验证了决策规则与升级时序；额外视觉证据可缩短等待时间，若等待结束仍未确认安全则系统进入紧急模式。

Conclusion: 该四足陪伴机器人系统能够在提供日常陪伴的同时实现紧急感知与响应，验证了规则化升级策略的可行性与时序有效性，为独居老年人的紧急支持提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As older adults increasingly live alone, emergency support becomes an important function for home companion robots. In Australia, demand for the care and support workforce is projected to nearly double between 2020-21 and 2049-50. Service robots such as robot dogs may support older adults by combining everyday companionship with assistance when human support is not immediately available. We present the design and implementation of a domestic companion-robot system on a Unitree Go1 quadruped platform that combines companionship with emergency awareness and response. The system uses a three-stage interaction model consisting of companion, danger-awareness, and emergency modes. A rule-based escalation policy combines signs detected from visual and speech input to determine the current risk level and adjust how long the robot waits for the user to confirm safety before entering emergency mode. Additional detected signs can shorten this waiting time. Users can confirm safety, request immediate help, stop robot motion, or reset the system. We verified the implemented decision rules and escalation timing through 105 automated trials and 25 staged physical trials. Additional visual evidence shortened the waiting time, and the system entered emergency mode when the waiting time ended without confirmation.

</details>


### [48] [DiffWAM: Learning World Action Model from Consecutive Visual Difference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19904)
*Xinheng Zhang,Haoyu Xiong,Chao Jing,Xiaohan Zhang,Linshan Li,Hongbang Fan,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffWAM 提出一种混合专家框架，将未来状态预测与连续视觉差分预测联合学习，以增强世界表征，在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0 及真实机器人操作任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型从当前观测与指令联合预测未来场景状态和机器人动作，但未来状态中包含大量与任务无关的内容（如静态背景），削弱了模型捕捉任务执行相关变化的能力。

Method: 定义连续视觉差分为相邻观测潜在表征之间的差异，作为时序变化的显式学习信号；构建由 World Expert（预测未来帧）、Action Expert（生成动作）和 Diff Expert（预测轨迹上的潜在差异）组成的 MoE 框架，并使用结构化单向注意力掩码使三个专家仅以当前帧表征为条件且专家间互不关注；推理时可移除 World 与 Diff 专家，不增加额外推理开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin 2.0 基准上达到最优性能，并显著优于 FastWAM；平均成功率分别为 98.8%、92.65% 和 88.3%，相较 FastWAM 分别提升 1.75、1.55 和 15.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过联合学习未来状态预测与连续视觉差分预测，DiffWAM 能获得更聚焦任务变化的 world representation，并在仿真基准和真实操作任务上均优于现有最优方法，且推理阶段无额外开销。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as an important paradigm in robot learning, jointly predicting future scene states and robot actions from current observations and instructions. However, future states entangle task-related changes with large amounts of task-irrelevant content, such as static backgrounds, which weakens the model's ability to capture changes relevant to task execution. To address this, we propose Difference World Action Model (DiffWAM), a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework that enhances world representations by jointly learning future-state prediction and consecutive visual difference prediction. We define consecutive visual differences as differences between the latent representations of consecutive observations, providing an explicit learning signal for temporal variation. The framework comprises a World Expert for future frame prediction, an Action Expert for action generation, and a Diff Expert for predicting these latent differences along the trajectory. A structured unidirectional attention mask conditions the Diff, World, and Action Experts solely on the current-frame representation while blocking attention between experts. This design allows both the World and Diff Experts to be removed during inference, introducing no additional inference overhead. DiffWAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on the LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0 benchmarks and substantially improves over FastWAM on real-world manipulation tasks. Specifically, it attains average success rates of 98.8%, 92.65%, and 88.3%, outperforming the state-of-the-art baseline (i.e., FastWAM) by 1.75, 1.55, and 15.0 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [49] [EasyNav: A Modular Architecture for Real-Time Navigation Across Heterogeneous Robot Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19894)
*Francisco Martín Rico,Francisco Miguel Moreno,Juan Sebastián Cely Gutiérrez,Esther Aguado,Miguel Ángel De Miguel Paraíso,José Miguel Guerrero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出以显式共享导航状态为核心接口的模块化导航架构EasyNav，并在真实机器人实验中显著降低反应延迟与资源占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人导航栈将建图、定位、规划和控制隐式绑定到特定表示与数据流，导致架构难以扩展、难以实验对比、难以进行端到端时序推理；在多种空间表示、传感模态和坐标系并存且以不同速率演化的异构场景中尤为关键。

Method: 设计以显式共享导航状态作为模块间唯一交互接口的导航架构，将建图、定位、规划和控制约束在该共享状态上运行，从而在显式兼容性与时序契约下实现可互换、表示无关的流水线；以开源ROS 2框架EasyNav实现，并通过真实机器人实验验证，包括激光传感室内导航以及GPS与3D LiDAR的大规模室内外多楼层导航。

Result: 在条件匹配的对比中，EasyNav相较Nav2将室内反应延迟从377 ms降至33 ms，室外从71 ms降至8 ms，同时CPU和内存占用更低。

Conclusion: 以显式共享导航状态为中心的架构可提升导航系统的可扩展性与可比较性，并在真实场景中实现更低延迟和更少资源消耗。

Abstract: Most robot navigation stacks implicitly bind mapping, localization, planning, and control to specific representations and data flows, yielding architectures hard to extend, compare experimentally, or reason about end-to-end timing. This is critical in heterogeneous scenarios, where multiple spatial representations, sensing modalities, and coordinate frames coexist and evolve at different rates. This paper presents a navigation architecture centered on an explicit, shared navigation state that serves as the sole interaction interface between modular components. Constraining mapping, localization, planning, and control to operate over this shared state enables interchangeable, representation-agnostic pipelines under explicit compatibility and timing contracts. We realize this architecture as EasyNav, an open source ROS 2 framework, and validate it through real-robot experiments, including indoor navigation with laser sensing and large-scale indoor-outdoor navigation with GPS and 3D LiDAR across multi-level routes. Compared with Nav2 under matched conditions, EasyNav reduces reaction latency from 377 to 33 ms indoors and from 71 to 8 ms outdoors, while also using less CPU and memory.

</details>


### [50] [Drone Navigation for Fast, Safe Emergency Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19818)
*Pranav Nedunghat,Jeffrey Mao,Praveen Merumugumala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种实时计算最小时间轨迹并保证避障的方法，以最小化规划与遍历时间之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机自主救灾需在复杂障碍环境中快速抵达目标，现有时间最优轨迹方法未考虑避障。

Method: 在时间最优轨迹计算中引入避障约束，实时求解最小时间轨迹并保证全程避障。

Result: 以规划时间加遍历时间为指标，示例四旋翼穿越峡谷状障碍时大部分时间接近最大推力。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证避障的同时缩短整体任务时间，适用于时间敏感的应急救援场景。

Abstract: In 2025, there was a total of 358 weather-related disasters worldwide affecting millions of people. For unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to autonomously aid these people, they need to be able to navigate around complex obstacles and reach their destinations to deliver supplies or quickly locate survivors in disaster-stricken areas. Our research addresses this need by providing a method to compute minimum-time trajectories in real time while providing guarantees of obstacle avoidance along the trajectory. Previous approaches treat the problem of computing time-optimal trajectories without considering obstacle avoidance. Our work not only includes obstacle avoidance constraints, but also improves overall mission time for navigation. We define mission time as the sum of planning time and traversal time. In time-sensitive emergencies, rapid trajectory planning is essential to ensure the prompt delivery of critical medical aid. As such, we consider the combined planning and traversal time as the key metric for how quickly the vehicle can reach a survivor. We show a sample mission in Fig. 1 where the quadrotor navigates through multiple canyon-like obstacles to deliver aid; during most of this trajectory, it is applying the near-maximum thrust.

</details>


### [51] [DynLimit: A Simulation Benchmark for Dynamics-Critical Planning and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19785)
*Yang Xiao,Yongzhi Liu,Sunan Zhang,Kang Wang,Guodong Yin,Weichao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynLimit仿真平台将车辆操纵极限实验中的需求、执行和任务完成三个问题分离，并提供任务导向的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在车辆操纵极限附近的实验中，必须区分操纵请求、闭环系统执行以及任务是否完成，现有方法未能清晰分离这三者。

Method: 提出DynLimit仿真平台：场景由道路、摩擦、障碍和参考对象组成；规划器和控制器通过共享观测与控制接口接入；车辆适配器运行无许可证的四轮模型或外部动力学求解器；同步记录用于任务评估、回放和响应检查；平台采用任务导向评估方法，将规定的参考需求与采样的通过集及事件对齐诊断配对。

Result: 在八个任务族、三种摩擦环境和三种参考辅助基线链上执行450次运行（360次标称、18次局部细化、72次规定初始偏移）。方法差异取决于任务族，相同的总通过计数来自不同的通过单元，局部细化缩小了两个采样过渡但未定位连续边界；两个案例比较表明侧滑或轮胎利用率峰值不能替代任务约束。

Conclusion: 在每次报告的运行中，三个视图保持分离：结果可从规定需求追溯到执行响应，再到决定任务完成的任务规则。

Abstract: Experiments near vehicle handling limits must keep three questions apart: what a maneuver requests, what the closed-loop system executes, and whether the task is completed. We present DynLimit, a simulation platform that organizes this process end to end. Scenarios are composed from road, friction, obstacle, and reference objects; planners and controllers attach through a shared observation and control interface; a vehicle adapter runs either a license-free four-wheel model or an external dynamics solver; and synchronized records feed task evaluation, replay, and response inspection. The platform carries a task-oriented evaluation method that pairs a prescribed reference demand with a sampled passing set and event-aligned diagnosis. Across eight task families, three friction environments, and three reference-assisted baseline chains we executed 450 runs: 360 nominal, 18 locally refined, and 72 with prescribed initial offsets. Method differences depend on the task family, equal aggregate pass counts arise from different passing cells, and local refinement narrows two sampled transitions without locating a continuous boundary. Two case comparisons show why sideslip or tire-utilization peaks cannot replace task constraints. The three views therefore stay distinct in every reported run: an outcome can be traced from the prescribed demand, through the executed response, to the task rule that decided it. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [52] [Recovery-Guided Model-Based Diffusion for Safety-Critical Legged Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19775)
*Lei Zheng,Shivam Sood,Mithun Vanniasinghe,Guillaume Sartoretti,Armin Lederer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种恢复引导的基于模型扩散（MBD）框架，将学习到的终端恢复评价器嵌入短时域轨迹优化，实现安全关键四足高速运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短预测时域降低滚动计算成本但安全前瞻有限，严格安全约束又大幅压缩可行轨迹空间，需在安全前瞻与在线计算效率间取得平衡。

Method: 将学习到的终端恢复评价器融入短时域轨迹优化，该评价器近似指定恢复策略滚动下的最坏情况未来间隙裕度；并引入可行性引导的精炼策略，在每个采样阶段结合时域内约束与终端可恢复性，同时保留可行精英样本以缓解采样支撑损失。

Result: 在50组配对的Unitree Go2随机避障试验中，方法实现零碰撞和98%严格间隙成功率，且计算量显著低于长时域MBD基线。

Conclusion: 恢复引导的短时域MBD可在保证安全性的同时大幅降低计算开销，适用于安全关键的高速四足运动规划。

Abstract: Safety-critical high-speed legged locomotion requires motion planners to balance safety foresight with online computational efficiency. Although short prediction horizons reduce rollout costs, they provide limited safety foresight, and strict safety constraints severely restrict the feasible trajectory space. We propose a recovery-guided model-based diffusion (MBD) framework for safety-critical legged locomotion. It incorporates a learned terminal recovery critic into short-horizon trajectory optimization. Specifically, the critic approximates the worst-case future clearance margin along rollouts of a designated recovery policy. We introduce a feasibility-guided refinement strategy that combines within-horizon constraints and terminal recoverability at each sampling stage, while retaining feasible elites to mitigate sampling-support loss. Across 50 paired randomized Unitree Go2 obstacle-avoidance trials, our approach achieves zero collisions and 98% strict-clearance success, while requiring substantially less computation than long-horizon MBD baselines. A project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [53] [M-CROSS: A Multisensor Simulation Dataset for SLAM across Water--Land Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19765)
*Yan Dong,Tianyi Wu,Junyu Yang,Shaoqiang Qiu,Zefeng Yan,Tao Zhang,Bin Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了首个跨水陆连续过渡的多传感器SLAM仿真数据集与基准M-CROSS，并给出过渡感知评价协议，实验表明现有SLAM系统难以在完整水陆过渡中同时保持定位精度与轨迹连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水陆跨域机器人需在陆地、过渡和水下阶段连续定位，但传感器特性与可用性会突变；现有数据集与常规评价指标无法刻画过渡引起的性能退化与失败。

Method: 构建M-CROSS多传感器仿真数据集，提供同步LiDAR、声呐、双目相机、IMU、DVL与深度测量，6DoF位姿真值、逐传感器介质状态标注及介质相关退化模型；提出过渡感知协议，包括轨迹完成率TCR、分段绝对位姿误差PW-APE、过渡终点误差TEE和过渡生存期TSP。

Result: 对6个代表性SLAM系统的实验显示，现有方法无法在全部水陆过渡中同时维持定位精度与轨迹连续性，出现过渡诱导漂移、轨迹不连续和跟踪失败。

Conclusion: M-CROSS填补了跨水陆过渡SLAM评测空白，揭示现有方法的过渡鲁棒性不足，为后续跨介质定位研究提供公开基准。

Abstract: Water--land cross-domain robots require continuous localization across land, transition and underwater phases, where sensor characteristics and availability change abruptly. Existing datasets and conventional metrics are not designed to capture transition-induced degradation and failures. We present \textbf{M-CROSS}, to the best of our knowledge, the first multisensor simulation dataset and benchmark for SLAM across continuous water--land transitions. It provides synchronized LiDAR, sonar, stereo camera, IMU, DVL, and depth measurements; 6DoF pose ground truth; sensor-wise medium-state annotations; and medium-dependent sensor degradation models. We establish a transition-aware protocol comprising the Trajectory Completion Rate (TCR), Piecewise Absolute Pose Error (PW-APE), Transition End Error (TEE), and Transition Survival Period (TSP), which measure trajectory completion, single-domain accuracy, transition-induced error, and localization survival under prolonged partial immersion, respectively. Experiments with 6 representative SLAM systems show that existing methods fail to maintain both localization accuracy and trajectory continuity across the full set of water--land transitions, exhibiting transition-induced drift, trajectory discontinuities, and tracking failures. M-CROSS is publicly available this https URL

</details>


### [54] [Cooperative Transport of Unknown Objects by Distributed Effort Regulation in Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19731)
*Tamzidul Mina,Shyam S. Kannan,Wonse Jo,Shaocheng Luo,Galen B. King,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向未知形状、尺寸、几何、质量及质量分布的物体的分布式多机器人搬运策略，无需任何先验知识，机器人沿物体边界动态确定推动点，并使用无物体模型的航向误差驱动推力调节方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体搬运方法通常需要已知物体模型或机器人数量，且难以适应未知形状、质量分布及外部扰动，因此需要一种无先验知识、可扩展且鲁棒的分布式多机器人搬运策略。

Method: 机器人沿物体边界移动并动态选择可行推动点；提出基于个体与集体航向误差的推力调节方法以促进目标导向运动；当推动点丢失或所需推力超出机器人能力时，机器人沿边界动态重定位；通过简单与复杂形状（含凸与非凸边界）及动态变化质量分布进行验证，并分析机器人分布与初始位姿对搬运时间与路径效率的影响。

Result: 所提系统产生高效物体路径，多次试验中搬运时间一致，对非均匀摩擦和轮滑等外部扰动具有鲁棒性；可扩展性分析表明搬运时间取决于可用推动面数量，而该数量又取决于物体尺寸、形状及机器人间安全距离。

Conclusion: 该分布式无模型多机器人搬运策略在未知物体与动态条件下有效、可扩展且鲁棒，能够实现高效且稳定的物体运输。

Abstract: A distributed multi-robot object transportation strategy is proposed for objects of unknown shape, size, geometry, mass and mass distribution without any prior knowledge about the object or number of robots involved in the transportation process. Robots follow the boundary of the object and dynamically determine viable pushing points. A novel object-model-free heading-error-driven pushing effort regulation method is proposed to promote goal-directed object motion; robots regulate their pushing effort using individual and collective heading errors. Robots dynamically relocate around the object boundary if a pushing point is lost or the required pushing effort exceeds its capability to correct its heading. The proposed method is validated using simple and complex shapes with convex and non-convex boundary regions and dynamically changing weight distribution of objects. Detailed analysis on the effects of robot distribution and initial object pose on the transportation performance in terms of required time and path efficiency is also presented. Scalability analysis revealed that object transportation time is dependent on the number of pushing surfaces available which in turn depends on the object size, shape, and set inter-robot safety distance. The proposed system yielded highly efficient object paths with consistent elapsed transport times over several trials robust to external disturbances such as non-uniform friction between contacting surfaces and robot wheel slip.

</details>


### [55] [AlphaTruck: A Platform for High-Speed Off-Road Autonomy on Unstructured Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19730)
*Rwik Rana,Adam J. Thorpe,Jesse Quattrociocchi,William Ward,Goktug Poyrazoglu,Adam Uccello,Christian Ellis,Rahul Moorthy Mahesh,Yukang Cao,Nathan Tsoi,Garrett Warnell,Volkan Isler,Ufuk Topcu,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlphaTruck平台，一种1/5比例的RC车辆，集成了多模态传感、嵌入式CPU-GPU计算和自主导航栈，用于高速越野自主导航，平衡了移动性、传感、计算、载荷和可维护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野自主导航需要鲁棒的物理平台，现有全尺寸车辆昂贵且基础设施要求高，RC比例车辆则地形通过性和载荷能力有限。因此需要一种介于两者之间、成本效益高且能力强的平台。

Method: 基于1/5比例RC车辆底盘，集成紧凑型多模态传感器、嵌入式CPU-GPU计算和自主导航栈，设计时平衡移动性、丰富传感、板载计算、载荷容量和实际操作与维修五个需求。

Result: AlphaTruck仅使用板载传感和计算，在越野环境中以6-10 m/s的速度实现自主导航。

Conclusion: AlphaTruck可作为研究测试平台和现场可部署平台，适用于调查、搜索救援和农业等商业和国防任务，并能承载任务特定载荷。

Abstract: Progress in autonomous navigation in off-road environments requires not only advances in algorithms, but also robust and capable physical platforms suited to both development and deployment. Today, off-road autonomy is typically developed and tested at one of two extremes of vehicle scale, each with different drawbacks. Full-size vehicles are capable but expensive, require large logistic infrastructure for deployment and maintenance, and expensive when pushed beyond their limits. RC-scale vehicles are small and inexpensive, but limited in the terrain they can traverse and the payload that they can carry, which includes their sensors and compute. We present the AlphaTruck platform, which leverages recent advances in compact sensing and compute to integrate multimodal sensing, embedded CPU-GPU compute, and an onboard autonomy stack for high-speed, off-road navigation, all integrated into a large-scale, 1/5th RC vehicle chassis. AlphaTruck is designed as both a research testbed and a field-deployable platform for commercial and defense missions such as survey, search and rescue, and agriculture, with the capacity to carry task-specific payloads. At 1/5 scale, the autonomy stack, sensors, and batteries all compete for the same mass, power, and cooling budget, so the platform must balance five needs: mobility over rough terrain, rich sensing, onboard computation, payload capacity to carry the autonomy stack while leaving room for task-specific equipment, and practical operation and repair. Thus, AlphaTruck navigates off-road environments autonomously at 6-10 m/s using only onboard sensing and compute.

</details>


### [56] [Sim-to-Real Room-Mediated Object-Goal Navigation with ROS 2 and Nav2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19720)
*Matthew Chua,Adam Scicluna,Gavin Paul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将基于房间中介共现（RPV）的ObjectNav策略部署到Spot和TurtleBot 4真实机器人上，在66次试验中实现0.455原始成功率，比仿真结果低8.4个百分点，并分析了差距来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ObjectNav方法多在仿真中评估，真实部署仅停留在可行性演示层面；本文旨在研究语义先验（RPV）如何泛化到物理环境，并描述从仿真迁移到硬件所需的适配。

Method: 通过机器人无关的ROS 2桥接，将Room-Mediated Co-occurrence策略（基于房间概率向量RPV）部署到Boston Dynamics Spot和Clearpath TurtleBot 4平台；在高校研究生科研设施中，从两个起始位置搜索11类物体，对Spot进行66次试验，并适配传感、定位和动作接口。

Result: Spot上RPV原始成功率为0.455，与仿真报告的0.539相比存在8.4个百分点差距；分析识别出未检测到的玻璃墙、进入禁区、可用深度范围受限、目标尺寸以及房间词典与物理布局不匹配等可能原因。

Conclusion: RPV语义先验可迁移到真实机器人，但真实部署成功率低于仿真，需针对感知、定位与动作接口进行适配，并关注玻璃墙、禁区、深度范围及语义词典匹配等实际因素。

Abstract: Although object-goal navigation (ObjectNav) approaches are usually evaluated in simulation, which offers scale and repeatable environments, real-world deployments are rarely examined beyond feasibility demonstrations. This paper presents the deployment of Room-Mediated Co-occurrence, an ObjectNav policy that reasons over Room Probability Vectors (RPVs), on two robotic platforms (Boston Dynamics Spot and Clearpath Robotics TurtleBot 4) through a robot-agnostic ROS 2 bridge. We study how these semantic priors generalise to physical environments and describe the adaptations required to transfer RPV from simulation to hardware, covering sensing, localisation, and the action interface. Across 66 trials on Spot, searching for 11 object categories from two starting positions in a university postgraduate research facility, RPV achieves a raw Success Rate (SR) of 0.455. Our analysis identifies undetected glass walls, entry into off-limits areas, limited usable depth range, target size, and mismatch between the room lexicon and physical layout as plausible contributors to the 8.4 percentage-point gap between the real-world SR and the reported simulated SR of 0.539. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [57] [Real-Time Air Pollution Monitoring and Tracking via Adaptive Density-Driven Multi-UAV Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19703)
*Julian Martinez,Ruchika Singh,Mohammad Afrazi,Kooktae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应密度驱动控制（D²C）框架，协调多无人机跟踪和绘制动态演化的污染物分布，并在模拟中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确及时的空气污染监测对公共健康和環境管理至关重要，但动态污染羽流的跟踪具有挑战性，现有方法在风速高且变化时容易失效。

Method: 扩展D²C策略，基于预测的污染物传播和动态更新的参考密度引导无人机轨迹；采用传感器驱动的代理羽流更新机制，通过梯度吸引、风平流、样本间排斥和随机扩散传播参考样本；无人机与中央协调器共享局部测量，维护统一参考样本集，实现系统化目标分配和轨迹规划。

Result: 在温和和强风条件下的仿真表明，所提方法在Sinkhorn距离上显著优于最先进基线；在高风速时变风场下羽流漂移出固定监测域时，基线方法无法维持跟踪，而D²C框架成功适应演化分布。

Conclusion: D²C是动态环境中高效且鲁棒的大气监测解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate and timely air pollution monitoring and tracking are critical for protecting public health and supporting effective environmental management. This paper presents an adaptive Density-Driven Control (D$^2$C) framework for coordinating multiple unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to track and map dynamically evolving pollutant distributions. The proposed framework extends the D$^2$C strategy to guide UAV trajectories based on predictive pollutant propagation and dynamically updated reference densities. A sensor-driven surrogate plume update mechanism is employed, in which reference samples are propagated using locally sensed concentration gradients and wind estimates through gradient attraction, wind advection, inter-sample repulsion, and stochastic diffusion, enabling UAVs to maintain an accurate spatio-temporal representation of the pollution field. The framework utilizes a coordinated structure where individual UAVs share their local measurements with a central coordinator to maintain a unified set of reference samples, facilitating systematic target allocation and trajectory planning. Simulation results under both mild and strong wind conditions demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Sinkhorn distance. Specifically, in scenarios where the plume drifts beyond a fixed monitoring domain under high-velocity, time-varying wind fields, the tested baseline methods fail to maintain tracking, whereas the proposed D$^2$C framework successfully adapts to the evolving distribution. These findings highlight D$^2$C as an efficient and resilient solution for atmospheric monitoring in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [58] [Physics-Grounded STL Monitoring from Image Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19688)
*Xinpei Ni,Glen Chou,Samuel Coogan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多视角图像学习共享潜在表示并解码物理间距的STL视觉监控方法，联合训练谓词解码器将认证率从58.7%提升至96.3%，并用共形预测校准区间STL界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作任务需满足STL的时序与条件约束，但STL鲁棒性依赖距离、间隙等无法从图像直接观测的物理间距，且为每个公式单独训练视觉监控器代价高。

Method: 从多视角图像学习共享潜在表示，联合训练编码器与谓词解码器以输出各谓词的物理间距，再用STL语义组合成任意有界公式的鲁棒性估计而无需重训；对所选STL子句的解码误差用共形预测校准，生成双侧区间并通过区间STL传播到由这些子句组成的任意公式。

Result: 联合训练使认证率从58.7%升至96.3%（谓词符号准确率几乎不变）；在LIBERO仿真厨房任务中，对18个未见公式的4662次评估正确率98.9%；对长达17分钟的任务，校准界在96.9%任务上无错误地判定满足或违反；单次校准对八个子句的全部255个合取覆盖率达91.0%，支持新公式免重校准。

Conclusion: 共享潜在表示加共形校准区间STL可实现无需逐公式训练、可泛化到新公式且带可靠认证保证的机器人任务STL监控。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation missions must satisfy timed and conditional requirements that can be expressed in signal temporal logic (STL). While a practical means of monitoring these requirements is from camera images, this is challenging because STL robustness depends on physical margins, such as distances and clearances, that are not directly observed. Rather than learning a separate visual monitor for each formula, we learn a shared latent representation from multi-view images and decode physically meaningful margins for individual predicates. Co-training the encoder with predicate decoders increases the certification rate from 58.7% to 96.3% compared with a frozen encoder, despite nearly unchanged predicate sign accuracy. STL semantics then compose these margins into robustness estimates for any bounded formula over the trained predicates, without retraining. We further calibrate the decoding errors of selected STL clauses using conformal prediction, yielding two-sided intervals that certify both satisfaction and violation and propagate through interval STL to any formula composed from the clauses. In simulated kitchen missions (LIBERO), our monitor correctly predicts satisfaction or violation in 98.9% of 4662 evaluations across 18 unseen formulas. For missions lasting up to 17 min, calibrated bounds establish satisfaction or violation for 96.9% of missions without incorrect conclusions. A single calibration also provides simultaneous 91.0% coverage across all 255 conjunctions of eight clauses, allowing new formulas without recalibration.

</details>


### [59] [Early Readability of Trajectory Quality in Diffusion Driving Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19684)
*Xiang Zhang,Ziyang Leng,Yuxin Bao,Zhihao Zhao,Bolei Zhou,Xugui Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 扩散式驾驶规划器在去噪中途的内部状态已足以预测候选轨迹质量，首个去噪评估后即可筛选候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究能否在去噪完成前利用扩散规划器的中间状态预判候选轨迹质量，从而提前筛选并节省计算。

Method: 在Alpamayo、ReCogDrive-IL和HDP三个预训练规划器上，用线性探针从中间状态预测最终轨迹质量，并在留出场景上评估候选排序与选择；提出首个去噪评估后即选择并仅完成所选候选的早选方法。

Result: 三个规划器的首个去噪评估状态即可支持有效选择，进一步去噪未稳定提升选择质量；早选方法相比随机选择提升开环轨迹质量，并将Alpamayo单相机与四相机的平均位移误差分别降低17.8%和13.4%，闭环驾驶得分与进度也更高。

Conclusion: 扩散规划器的早期内部状态具有选择价值，早选可在减少计算的同时提升轨迹质量与闭环表现。

Abstract: Diffusion-based driving planners can generate multiple trajectories for the same scene, but these candidates vary in quality. Can their internal states reveal which candidate will be better before denoising is complete? We systematically study this question in three pretrained planners: Alpamayo, ReCogDrive-IL, and HDP, using linear probes to predict final trajectory quality from intermediate states and evaluating candidate ranking and selection on held-out scenes. Across all three planners, we observe that states from the first denoiser evaluation already support informative selection, while further denoising does not consistently improve selection quality. Further analyses show that readout depends on the representation and reader, and that selection can coexist with limited overall ranking accuracy. These findings motivate an early-selection method that selects after the first denoiser evaluation and completes only the chosen candidate. This reduces computation relative to fully denoising every candidate and improves open-loop trajectory quality over random selection across all three planners. In particular, it reduces Alpamayo's average displacement error by 17.8% and 13.4% in one- and four-camera configurations, respectively. Closed-loop evaluations also show higher mean driving scores and progress.

</details>


### [60] [PSK: A Matrix-Free Single-Loop Method for K-Level Physics-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19660)
*Zherong Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PSK uses only gradients and projection to solve K-level physics-based control in O(ε^-3) iterations, avoiding Hessian/KKT solves; smoothing constraints into constant-width bands preserves complexity without CQ, but shrinking bands introduce K-dependent lower bounds.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Differentiating through implicit physics steps typically requires Hessian or KKT linear solves, which are costly; the paper asks whether simple gradient steps suffice for control with many contact/collision constraints.

Method: Reformulates K-step control as a K-level optimization with strongly convex lower levels and recasts it as a min-max problem; proposes Penalized Single-loop K-level method (PSK) using only gradients and a projection, updating all levels in parallel. For contact-rich constraints violating CQ, replaces each constraint by a smooth wall acting on a constant-width band, proving feasibility and banded Fritz-John under mild CQ-free assumptions.

Result: PSK reaches ε-stationary control in O(ε^-3) iterations with K-independent exponent but K-dependent constant; shrinking band width ε yields a lower bound of Ω(ε^-(K+3)/4) calls for deterministic zero-respecting methods with fixed K≥2 on a hard instance. Assumptions are proven for smoothed kinematic and dynamic multi-robot navigation among segment obstacles; experiments show PSK with sixteen levels reaches every goal and, at equal time, achieves lower loss than tuned unrolling in 17/18 cases, while implicit differentiation is lower in 10.

Conclusion: Gradient-only single-loop optimization (PSK) is a viable alternative to Hessian/KKT-based implicit differentiation for physics-based control, with provable complexity under constant-width constraint smoothing; however, shrinking bands lose the K-independent exponent, revealing a fundamental limitation.

Abstract: Physics-based control is central to robot learning, but differentiating through implicit physics steps typically requires Hessian or Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) linear solves; we ask whether gradient steps suffice. Each simulation step minimizes an energy under many contact and collision constraints. Without constraints, we view control over $K$ steps as $K$-level optimization with strongly convex lower levels, recast as one min--max problem. Our Penalized Single-loop $K$-level method (PSK) uses only gradients and a projection, updates all levels in parallel, and reaches an $\epsilon$-stationary control in $O(\epsilon^{-3})$ iterations, with a $K$-independent exponent but a $K$-dependent constant. Contact-rich constraints, however, typically violate a constraint qualification (CQ). We replace each constraint by a smooth wall that acts on a band of constant width. Under mild assumptions that need no CQ, every exact solution of the banded lower levels is then feasible and banded Fritz--John, and PSK keeps the $O(\epsilon^{-3})$ complexity. Without a CQ that is uniform in the band width, however, a shrinking band loses the $K$-independent exponent: with width $\epsilon$ and fixed $K\geq2$, deterministic zero-respecting methods need $\Omega(\epsilon^{-(K+3)/4})$ calls on a hard instance whose control dimension grows with $1/\epsilon$. We prove our assumptions for smoothed kinematic and dynamic multi-robot navigation among segment obstacles. In navigation experiments, PSK, tuned per case, reaches every goal with sixteen levels and, at equal time, reaches a lower loss than tuned unrolling in 17 of 18 cases, while implicit differentiation is lower in 10.

</details>


### [61] [Towards Probabilistically Safe Reinforcement Learning: Theory and Deployment on Humanoid and Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19616)
*Pol Mestres,Varun Gabbita,Jason H. K. Siu,Sergio A. Esteban,Hampus Serneke,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RSGF-RL扩展框架，支持一般轨迹级安全约束（含机会约束），允许有偏估计，可与PPO集成，并实现零样本仿真到现实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习不保证安全约束，现有安全RL方法约束类别受限且需无偏估计，无法与PPO等先进算法直接集成。

Method: 扩展鲁棒安全梯度流强化学习（RSGF-RL）框架，容纳一般轨迹级安全约束与有偏值和梯度估计，分析训练中约束违反界及收敛到近似KKT点。

Result: 训练中约束违反以高概率被估计偏差加随rollout数消失的项所界定，初始策略不安全时衰减至该水平；迭代收敛至近似KKT点；PPO风格估计实现Unitree G1全身控制和Crazyflie无碰撞导航的零样本sim-to-real部署。

Conclusion: RSGF-RL扩展克服了现有安全RL的限制，提供理论保证并与PPO兼容，验证了实际安全关键场景部署可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) enables robots to acquire complex skills, but standard RL formulations do not guarantee that learned policies satisfy safety constraints, which limits their use in safety-critical settings. Existing safe RL methods with provable guarantees typically support only restricted classes of constraints and require unbiased estimators of the objective and constraint functions, which prevents their direct integration with state-of-the-art algorithms such as proximal policy optimization (PPO). In this paper, we extend the recently introduced robust safe gradient flow-based reinforcement learning (RSGF-RL) framework to overcome these limitations. Our formulation accommodates a general class of trajectory-level safety constraints, including chance constraints such as collision avoidance with a prescribed probability, and our analysis allows for biased value and gradient estimators. We show that, with high probability, the constraint violation throughout training is bounded by the estimator bias plus a term that vanishes as the number of rollouts grows, and decays to this level if the initial policy is unsafe. We also establish the convergence properties of the iterates to approximate KKT points of the original safe RL problem. Finally, we show that RSGF-RL implemented with PPO-style estimates enables zero-shot sim-to-real deployment of whole-body control policies on a Unitree G1 humanoid and collision-free waypoint navigation on a Crazyflie drone.

</details>


### [62] [ResWAM: Learning What Changes, Not What Stays, for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19559)
*Chongyu Fan,Gaowen Liu,Ramana Rao Kompella,Jason Corso,Sijia Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出视觉残差学习和ResWAM模型，仅监督冻结DINOv3特征空间中当前帧到未来帧的变化，以低计算成本提升视觉-语言-动作策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型监督完整未来观测，但动作块内观测变化小，损失被静态背景复制主导，难以学习任务相关的机器人与物体运动；且依赖大型视频生成模型或独立世界模型，训练与推理计算开销大，阻碍闭环部署。

Method: 提出视觉残差学习，在冻结DINOv3空间中仅监督当前帧到未来帧的特征变化；并构建ResWAM，总参数2.3B，含0.5B双分支DiT，通过条件流匹配联合训练动作分支与残差分支。

Result: 无机器人预训练下，LIBERO-Plus平均成功率81.1%，RoboTwin 2.0为93.8%，RoboDojo成功率9.40%、得分13.41，训练计算2.7-4.0 TFLOPs、推理计算1.0-1.4 TFLOPs均为最低；真实机器人Open、Put、Stack平均80.0%，较π0.5提升10.0%，延迟92.7 ms。

Conclusion: 视觉残差学习作为监督目标比完整未来特征具有更高特征秩和更丰富的场景变化表示，并空间聚焦于机械臂与操作物体；ResWAM在低计算成本下取得领先性能，验证了该方法的有效性与部署可行性。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) augment vision-language-action policies (VLAs) with future prediction so that the policy can anticipate how its actions will affect the physical world. Existing WAMs supervise complete future observations. However, observations typically change little over an action chunk, so the future loss is dominated by copying static background content rather than learning task-relevant robot and object motion, limiting policy learning. Moreover, existing WAMs rely on large video generation models or separate world models, incurring heavy training and inference compute that hinders closed-loop deployment. To improve policy performance, we propose visual residual learning, supervising only the feature change from the current frame to the future frame in a frozen DINOv3 space. Compared with complete future features, this residual learning target yields a higher feature rank with richer and more diverse representations of scene changes, and spatially concentrates on the robot arm and manipulated objects rather than the background. To further improve computational efficiency, we present ResWAM, a residual world-action model with 2.3B parameters in total, including a lightweight 0.5B dual-branch DiT that jointly trains an action branch and a residual branch via conditional flow matching. Without robot pretraining (i.e., pretraining on external robot action trajectories beyond the benchmark demonstrations), ResWAM achieves top average success rates on LIBERO-Plus (81.1%), RoboTwin 2.0 (93.8%), and RoboDojo (9.40% success, 13.41 score) at the lowest training (2.7-4.0 TFLOPs) and inference compute (1.0-1.4 TFLOPs), and leads real-robot Open, Put, and Stack (80.0% average, +10.0% over $\pi_{0.5}$) at 92.7 ms latency.

</details>


### [63] [Cooperative Gradient Estimation for Multi-Robot Source Seeking with Heteroskedastic Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19535)
*Alessandro Renzaglia,Lara Briñón-Arranz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于异构移动机器人团队的3D标量场源定位方法，结合加权质心Voronoi划分编队与加权最小二乘梯度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源定位方法多假设传感器同质且忽略测量噪声差异，导致梯度估计不鲁棒；需在编队设计与估计中显式处理异构传感器精度。

Method: 在球面上定义由传感器噪声方差参数化的加权质心Voronoi划分编队策略，结合加权最小二乘估计器计算编队中心场梯度；推导梯度估计协方差闭式解作为球面任意编队的方差下界，并证明所提划分渐近满足达到该下界的条件；利用估计梯度通过梯度上升策略引导团队趋向源。

Result: 仿真表明该方法较替代策略更有效，并能在线重构以响应机器人故障，增强适应性。

Conclusion: 显式考虑传感器异构性的编队与估计框架可实现近最优的鲁棒梯度估计，并支持在线自适应源定位。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel approach for localizing the source of a three-dimensional scalar field using a team of heterogeneous cooperative mobile robots. Each robot measures the field intensity at its location and shares the information within the team. Measurement noise is explicitly considered, and heterogeneity arises from robot-dependent sensing accuracies, leading to heteroskedastic data. The proposed framework combines a weighted centroidal Voronoi tessellation-based formation strategy, defined on a spherical surface and parameterized by sensor noise variances, with a Weighted Least Squares estimator to compute the field gradient at the formation center. By explicitly accounting for sensor heterogeneity in both formation design and estimation, the method achieves robust gradient estimation under noisy measurements. In particular, we show analytically that this construction is nearly optimal. The covariance of the gradient estimate is derived in closed form, providing a lower bound on the attainable estimation variance for any formation on the sphere, and the proposed tessellation is shown to asymptotically satisfy the conditions under which this bound is attained. The estimated gradient is then exploited within a gradient-ascent strategy to guide the team toward the source location. Simulation results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach compared to alternative strategies and highlight its intrinsic ability to reconfigure online in response to robot failures, thereby enhancing its adaptability.

</details>


### [64] [Depth-Aware Path Planning and Collision Avoidance for Robotic Nursing Assistants Using Real-Time 3D Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19534)
*Peeyush Thakur,Dharmendra Sharma,Archit Sharma,Sandeep Gupta,Anuj Nandanwar,Laxmidhar Behera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合RGB-D深度感知、规则避障与NavfnROS全局规划器的深度感知路径规划与避障框架，用于护理机器人安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR导航对透明或低矮障碍物检测不足，视觉系统易受噪声和遮挡影响，需要面向杂乱环境的可靠导航方案。

Method: 集成RGB-D深度传感、基于规则的避障和NavfnROS全局路径规划器；在配备Kinova Gen3机械臂的Husky A200机器人上实现，采用ROS-Gazebo中面向全局规划、感知融合与实时控制的多层架构。

Result: 仿真杂乱环境中成功率最高提升40%，最小间隙改善35%；真实环境实验验证了其计算高效且安全感知的导航性能。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为自主医疗机器人计算高效且安全感知的导航解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a depth-aware path planning and collision avoidance framework for the safe navigation of robotic nursing assistants in cluttered environments with low-height obstacles. Conventional LiDAR-based navigation systems, while accurate for mapping and localization, fail to detect transparent or low-height obstacles, whereas vision-based systems are prone to noise and occlusion. The proposed framework integrates RGB-D depth sensing, rule-based obstacle avoidance, and the NavfnROS global path planner to drive the robot from an initial point to a goal point. The proposed approach is implemented on the Husky A200 robot, equipped with a Kinova Gen3 arm, to form a complete setup for a robotic nursing assistant. This system features a multi-layer architecture for global planning, perception fusion, and real-time control in the ROS-Gazebo environment. Experimental evaluations in a simulated cluttered environment demonstrate up to 40\% higher success rate and 35\% improvement in minimum clearance compared with single-sensor baselines. Furthermore, real-world experimental results validate the proposed approach as a computationally efficient and safety-aware navigation solution for autonomous healthcare robots.

</details>


### [65] [Long-Horizon Causal Inertial Odometry in Multistory Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19387)
*Tianzi Zhang,Yan Wang,Hao Qiao,Jian Kuang,Xiaoji Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出长时程惯性里程计架构LTCA，将1秒窗口压缩为1Hz运动令牌并利用60秒因果缓存维持运动状态，并引入气压计分支提供高度约束，显著降低多层建筑室内3D定位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多层建筑室内3D定位中，电梯或自动扶梯先加速数秒后长时间匀速巡航，步态激励消失、比力退化为恒定重力反作用，1-2秒窗口内瞬时速度不可观测，导致短窗口学习式惯性里程计输出退化至训练集条件均值、垂直位移被系统性低估。

Method: 提出Local-Token Causal Attention（LTCA）长时程架构，将每个1秒惯性窗口压缩为1Hz运动令牌，并在有界60秒因果缓存上维持起始事件所建立的运动状态；引入可插拔气压计分支，在线时通过局部差分高度观测为垂直通道提供直接高程约束，并采用在线/回退成对条件训练使气压计在训练中正则化两种模式共享的表示。

Result: 在自建九栋建筑基准MSIO上，气压计在线时RoNIN和TLIO实例在Seen/Unseen划分的ATE-3D分别为2.83/4.18 m和2.44/5.12 m；推理时禁用气压计，相同权重回退至纯惯性推理，误差为3.24/4.28 m和2.64/5.23 m，仍低于所有原生短窗口基线；并在公开RoNIN和TLIO基准上进一步验证。

Conclusion: LTCA通过长时程因果注意力与可插拔气压计条件训练，有效解决了匀速运动下短窗口惯性里程计退化的问题，在多层建筑室内3D定位中优于短窗口基线，且具备纯惯性回退能力。

Abstract: In indoor 3D localization inside multistory buildings, an elevator or an escalator accelerates for a few seconds and then cruises at constant velocity for tens of seconds. Gait excitation disappears, the specific force degenerates into a constant gravity reaction, and instantaneous velocity becomes unobservable within a 1-2 s window. The output of short-window learned inertial odometry consequently degrades toward the conditional mean of the training set, and vertical displacement is systematically underestimated. We propose Local-Token Causal Attention (LTCA), a long-horizon architecture that compresses each 1 s inertial window into a 1 Hz motion token and sustains, over a bounded 60 s causal cache, the motion state established at the onset event. We further introduce a pluggable barometer branch that, when online, supplies a direct elevation constraint to the vertical channel through local differential height observations, and we train it with paired online/fallback conditional training so that the barometer also regularizes the representation shared by the two modes during training. On MSIO, our benchmark of nine buildings, the RoNIN and TLIO instances reach 3D trajectory errors (ATE-3D) of 2.83/4.18 m and 2.44/5.12 m on the Seen and Unseen splits, respectively, with the barometer online. With the barometer disabled at inference, the same weights fall back to pure inertial inference and reach 3.24/4.28 m and 2.64/5.23 m, still below every native short-window baseline. We further validate the framework on the public RoNIN and TLIO benchmarks.

</details>


### [66] [MARS: Dynamic Routing of Reinforcement-Learned Atomic Skills for Multi-Stage Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19324)
*Zequan Zeng,Junqin Yang,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MARS提出一种模块化原子技能动态路由框架，将冻结的通用VLA策略与多个独立训练的强化学习原子技能策略结合，通过共享RL Token状态表示和基于Mamba的时序语义路由器，提升多阶段机器人操作中通用与专用策略的协调性与执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在多任务机器人操作中表现良好，但在抓取、对齐、插入等关键操作阶段存在控制精度与执行稳定性的不足；需要将通用操作能力与任务特定的细粒度控制结合，并在多阶段任务中有效协调通用策略与专用策略。

Method: MARS由冻结的通用VLA策略和多个独立训练的原子技能策略组成；以共享RL Token状态表示和VLA参考动作作为条件，对关键操作阶段的专用控制策略进行强化学习优化；设计动态路由器，采用Mamba循环编码与历史特征窗口捕捉状态演化，将候选策略的技能描述编码为语义查询，通过注意力机制在技能语义与时序状态特征之间建立对应关系，实现高层策略选择。

Result: 在固定轨迹评估下，MARS在四个LIBERO任务套件上平均Macro-F1为90.47%，较MLP路由器提升7.08个百分点；在仿真Tube Transport任务中，全任务成功率从20.04%提升至85.67%。

Conclusion: 实验表明，阶段特定的强化学习与时序状态感知的动态策略路由能够改善多策略协调，并提升多阶段机器人操作的整体任务执行性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in multi-task robotic manipulation. However, they still face limitations in control precision and execution stability during critical manipulation stages, such as object grasping, alignment and insertion. To combine general-purpose manipulation capabilities with task-specific fine-grained control, while enabling effective coordination between generalist and specialized policies throughout multi-stage manipulation tasks, this paper proposes MARS (Modular Atomic-skill Routing System), a dynamic routing framework for reinforcement learning-based atomic skills. MARS integrates a frozen generalist VLA policy with multiple independently trained atomic skill policies. Using a shared RL Token state representation and the reference action of VLA, specialized control policies are optimized through reinforcement learning for different cr`itical manipulation stages. For high-level policy selection, we develop a dynamic router that integrates temporal context and skill semantics. The router employs Mamba-based recurrent encoding and a historical feature window to capture state evolution during manipulation. Skill descriptions of candidate policies are encoded into semantic queries, and attention mechanisms are used to establish correspondences between skill semantics and temporal state features. Experimental results show that MARS achieves an average Macro-F1 score of 90.47% across four LIBERO task suites under fixed-trajectory evaluation, outperforming an MLP-based router by 7.08 percentage points. In the simulated Tube Transport task, the full-task success rate increases from 20.04% to 85.67%. These results demonstrate that stage-specific reinforcement learning and temporal state-aware dynamic policy routing can improve multi-policy coordination and overall task execution performance in multi-stage robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [67] [Residual Reinforcement Learning for Visual Servo Control with Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19318)
*Mahdi Nobar,Alisa Rupenyan,John Lygeros,Jürg Keller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合延迟补偿卡尔曼滤波与Soft Actor-Critic残差策略的逆雅可比PI控制器，在传送带上实现机械臂移动目标跟踪，将跟踪误差降低至理论下界以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生不精确、测量误差与噪声导致基于模型的控制器性能受限，需要一种能在建模与测量不确定性下提升跟踪精度的方法。

Method: 采用环状逆雅可比PI控制器，并利用Soft Actor-Critic在数字孪生中学习残差策略；使用延迟补偿卡尔曼滤波器融合乱序相机测量以估计移动目标位置；通过频域灵敏度分析推导一类基于模型控制器的最小可达误差下界。

Result: 在传送带速度27.9-41.8 mm/s范围内，方法将平均跟踪误差从基线逆雅可比PI的1.1-1.4 mm降低至0.3-0.7 mm，且低于理论下界；仿真与硬件实验验证了可靠的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: 在有限保真度仿真器中学习残差修正并结合延迟补偿，能显著改善模型不确定性下的基于模型控制性能，同时保持可靠的仿真到现实迁移。

Abstract: We address tracking a moving object on a conveyor with a Franka Emika Panda robot arm, despite an imprecise digital twin, measurement errors, and noise. Our novel approach augments a loop-shaped inverse-Jacobian PI controller with a residual policy learned via Soft Actor-Critic in the digital twin. A delay-compensated Kalman filter fuses out-of-sequence camera measurements to provide latency-aware moving target position estimates. We compare the achieved performance to a performance bound for a class of model-based controllers derived from a frequency-domain sensitivity analysis, quantifying the minimum achievable error imposed by uncertain kinematic model mismatch. Simulation and hardware experiments indicate that our residual policy drives tracking error below the bound. Across conveyor speeds (27.9-41.8 mm/s), our method reduces mean tracking errors from 1.1-1.4 mm (baseline inverse-Jacobian PI) to 0.3-0.7 mm. These results show that learning residual corrections in a limited-fidelity simulator, combined with delay compensation, can substantially improve model-based control under modeling and measurement uncertainties while maintaining reliable sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [68] [Physics-Informed Causal Reasoning for Nonstationary Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19251)
*Jungjin Lee,Se Jin Kim,Sung Kwon On,Insoon Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PICR结合上下文推理与物理信息神经网络，仅从交互中推断上下文以调制固定物理网络，使机器人无需重训练即可适应变化的物理条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统在快照中看似相同却因隐藏物理条件对同一动作响应不同；上下文模型未说明上下文如何改变运动方程，物理信息网络仅描述单一系统且无法识别系统变化。

Method: 提出Physics-Informed Causal Reasoning (PICR)，从近期交互重新推断上下文，用该上下文条件化固定物理信息网络以确定机器人惯性与势能；在NVIDIA Isaac Sim的Franka Panda机械臂上测试。

Result: 在变化和未见负载下预测运动，未见负载误差较基线降低1.6-3.8倍；支持替代力矩下的反事实查询；上下文编码负载物理属性并跟踪变化，诱导的重力与惯性项与模拟器在拉格朗日分量级别一致。

Conclusion: PICR弥合上下文模型与物理信息网络，仅从交互推断的上下文即可编码物理属性并控制机械臂响应，无需重训练或手工模型，且诱导物理项具有物理有效性。

Abstract: Reasoning about what an action will cause requires internalizing how a system behaves. As conditions seldom stay fixed, this must often happen within a few interactions. Yet systems that look identical in a snapshot can respond differently to the same action, depending on physical conditions the snapshot hides. Context-based models capture this separation by summarizing recent transitions into a latent context, but do not specify how that context should change the equations of motion. Physics-informed neural networks build those equations into the model, but describe only a single system and cannot recognize when it has changed. We present Physics-Informed Causal Reasoning (PICR), which bridges the two. Only a context, re-inferred from recent interactions, is updated; it conditions a fixed physics-informed network that determines the robot's inertia and potential energy, so the robot adapts to new physical conditions from its own experience without retraining or a hand-specified robot model. On a Franka Panda arm in NVIDIA Isaac Sim, PICR predicts motion under changing and unseen payloads, reducing error on the latter by 1.6-3.8x over baselines, and extends to counterfactual queries under alternative torques. Our analysis shows that the context, inferred solely from interaction, encodes payload physical properties, tracks their changes, and governs the arm's response. The gravity and inertia terms induced by the context further demonstrate its physical validity, each agreeing with the simulator at the level of individual Lagrangian components.

</details>


### [69] [Continuous Lower-Limb Joint-Angle Estimation from sEMG and IMU: The Conditional Value of Cross-Activity and Cross-Subject Data under Update-Matched Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19238)
*Jiaying Xu,Guangyuan Chen,Buyun Gao,Zhen Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将数据来源对意图驱动外骨骼关节角度估计的影响分解为信息量与优化更新量，揭示数据价值具有层次性、状态依赖性和方向性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将借用用户其他活动数据或其他用户同活动数据简单视为“更多数据”，混淆了信息量与优化更新带来的效应，并可能掩盖负迁移。

Method: 在公开数据集十名受试者六种运动活动上，使用高密度sEMG和IMU估计髋、膝、踝角度；冻结紧凑残差因果TCN-GRU解码器，采用更新匹配回放控制，将每个新增数据源与相同更新次数的用户自身数据对比。

Result: 用户自身其他活动使R²从0.722升至0.788，其中+0.048来自回放之外的信息；下楼梯是可靠有害供体，坐-站-走是可靠有益供体；其他用户同活动数据在仅有目标活动时增益G_T=+0.053，在已有自身其他活动数据时减半为G_W=+0.028，完全校准后供体价值在一到两条目标记录后崩溃；两来源的逐目标增益相关，留一受试者门控仅在低校准下恢复大部分跨受试者价值。

Conclusion: 数据价值是分层的、状态依赖的和有方向的；瓶颈在于无测试标签时决定何时迁移。

Abstract: Intent-driven exoskeleton control needs a continuous, causal estimate of the wearer's joint kinematics and an answer to a second question: whose data should train the estimator for a new user? Borrowing the user's recordings of other activities, or other users' recordings of the same activity, is usually evaluated as "more data" versus "less data," which confounds the information a source carries with the optimizer updates it adds and hides negative transfer behind averages. We separate these effects for hip, knee, and ankle angle estimation from high-density sEMG and an IMU across six locomotion activities on all ten participants of a public dataset. A compact residual causal TCN-GRU decoder is frozen, and an update-matched replay control prices every added source against the same number of updates on the user's own data. A user's own other activities raise $R^2$ from 0.722 to 0.788, of which +0.048 is information beyond replay; stair descent is a reliably harmful donor and sit-stand-walk a reliably beneficial one. Other users' same-activity data help while the user has only the target activity ($G_\mathrm{T} = +0.053$) and half as much once own other-activity data exist ($G_\mathrm{W} = +0.028$); at full calibration, donor value collapses after one to two target records. Per-target gains from the two sources correlate, and leave-one-participant-out gates recover most of the cross-subject value only at low calibration. Data value is hierarchical, state-dependent, and directional; the bottleneck is deciding, without test labels, when to transfer.

</details>


### [70] [Self-Calibrating Vision-Agent-Action: Deploying Pick-and-Place to New Robot Cells without Demonstrations or Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19198)
*Jae Hun Hwang,Yechan Seo,Hong-In Won*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VAA是一种无需机器人演示或微调的仿真视觉-智能体-动作系统，通过自标定和测量实现机器人单元中的拾放任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）在机器人硬件、相机几何、外观和基座框架变化时性能显著下降，需要无需演示和微调的鲁棒方案。

Method: VAA利用预训练视觉-语言和语言模型进行解释，从自身运动和指尖测量命令映射与相机几何，估计镜头畸变，并基于测量物体深度规划抓取；通过受控研究比较8个LIBERO微调VLA在18个变化单元中的表现，并进行配对消融实验。

Result: 在18个变化单元中，冻结VAA成功230/360（64%），精调VAA成功268/360（74%），最强公开VLA仅76/360（21%）；两步LIBERO-Goal链中VAA完成54/60，而四个VLA均未完成。

Conclusion: VAA通过自标定和测量深度流程，在硬件、相机和框架变化下显著优于现有VLA，且无需演示或微调。

Abstract: In simulation, Vision-Agent-Action (VAA) performs pick-and-place in robot cells with gripper or base-frame changes without robot demonstrations or fine-tuning. It uses pre-trained vision-language and language models for interpretation, measures the command map and camera geometry from its own motion and fingertips, estimates lens distortion, and plans grasps from measured object depth. A controlled study of eight public LIBERO-fine-tuned vision-language-action models (VLAs) varies robot hardware, camera geometry, appearance, and base frame. Gripper and frame changes dominate their losses in the tested fractional design. Across 18 cells with these changes, frozen VAA achieves 230/360 successes (64%), the refined VAA 268/360 (74%), and the strongest released VLA on this subset 76/360 (21%). Paired ablations support the self-calibration bundle, lens estimation under compounded changes, and the measured-depth pipeline. On a two-step LIBERO-Goal chain with object state carried between instructions, VAA completes 54/60 chains in changed cells, while four evaluated VLAs complete none.

</details>


### [71] [Ego2Sim2Humanoid: Agentic Real-to-Sim for Learning Loco-Manipulation VLAs without Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19196)
*Chen Liu,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种方法，将人类第一视角眼镜拍摄的演示数据转换为经过物理验证的机器人训练轨迹，从而在无需真实机器人演示的情况下，仅用转换后的数据集微调Groot N1.7，即可让Unitree G1自主完成移动操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略的模仿学习通常依赖遥操作机器人演示，成本高昂；而第一视角眼镜虽能低成本采集人类演示，却只跟踪头部和视野内手部，且因人体与机器人身材、臂展、手部形态不同，直接复制人类动作无法产生可行接触。

Method: 该方法包含：agentic real-to-sim阶段从RGB-D和头部流重建碰撞与纹理资产、补全未见表面并预测物体上的目标接触带；运动适配阶段用抓取规划器将接触带转为可行机器人手腕位姿与手指闭合，用双层站姿优化规划无碰撞且保持手部可达的站立姿态，用全身运动生成器合成下肢运动与视野外手臂运动；两阶段运动校正先在运动学上混合可见性转换，再在物理仿真中基于agent设计的语义奖励训练强化学习跟踪策略；最后通过机器人机载相机渲染重建场景，生成同步的机器人视角图像、本体感受和验证过的动作标签。

Result: 仅在该转换数据集上微调、不使用真实机器人演示，GR00T N1.7在Unitree G1上自主执行了移动操作任务。

Conclusion: 第一视角人类演示经物理验证的real-to-sim转换后，可作为有效的机器人模仿学习训练数据，替代昂贵的遥操作机器人演示。

Abstract: Imitation learning for visuomotor policies typically relies on teleoperated robot demonstrations, which are costly because every episode requires a trained operator controlling a physical robot in real time through teleoperation interfaces and wearable body-tracking hardware. Egocentric glasses capture human demonstrations without a robot or handheld grippers, yet they track only the head and in-view human hands, and directly copying human motion to a robot with different stature, arm reach and hand morphology fails to produce feasible contacts. \method{} converts egocentric glasses recordings into physically verified robot training trajectories. An agentic real-to-sim stage reconstructs collision and textured visual assets from the recorded RGB-D and head streams, completes unseen surfaces when needed, and predicts target contact bands on the manipulated objects. During motion adaptation, a grasp planner turns each contact band into a feasible robot wrist pose and finger closure, bilevel stance optimization plans collision-free standing postures that keep hand targets reachable, and a whole-body motion generator synthesizes lower-body locomotion and out-of-view arm motion. Two-stage motion correction then blends visibility transitions kinematically and trains a reinforcement-learning tracking policy in physics simulation under agent-designed semantic rewards, completing manipulation phases even when the object leaves the camera view. Rendering the reconstructed scene through the robot's onboard camera along the optimized rollout yields synchronized robot-view images, proprioception and verified action labels. Fine-tuned exclusively on this converted dataset, without real-robot demonstrations, GR00T N1.7 performed loco-manipulation tasks autonomously on a Unitree G1. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [72] [Distilling Safety Filters for Filter-free Multi-agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19190)
*Srikar Babu Gadipudi,Somayeh Sojoudi,Claire J. Tomlin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在训练时利用安全过滤器生成安全数据并蒸馏到策略中，使多机器人强化学习策略在无过滤器部署时实现零碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人强化学习训练中试错导致碰撞，而集中式控制障碍函数（CBF）过滤器的约束数随团队规模二次增长；且过滤器掩盖不安全动作成本、阻止近碰撞状态进入训练数据，导致策略学不到真正安全行为。

Method: 仅从执行的安全动作中用隐式Q学习训练；在合成的近碰撞状态下将CBF过滤器蒸馏到策略中；证明蒸馏损失界定了策略约束违反程度，并在蒸馏状态覆盖部署状态时界定每步碰撞概率。

Result: 无过滤器、仅用局部观测时，1,800个多智能体测试回合零碰撞；3智能体训练的策略零样本迁移到20智能体团队，单智能体计算量恒定，最多8智能体保持无碰撞；在Crazyflie四旋翼上最多5架同时飞行验证。

Conclusion: 将安全过滤器蒸馏进策略可消除部署时对集中式过滤器的依赖，在保持可扩展性的同时实现多机器人无碰撞部署。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can teach robot teams to navigate shared spaces, but trial and error causes collisions that real robots cannot afford. Control barrier function (CBF) safety filters prevent these collisions by correcting unsafe actions, yet in a team the filter becomes a centralized optimization whose number of constraints grows quadratically with the team size. We propose an approach that builds the filter's safety into the RL policy and examine how such a system behaves during filter-free deployment. However, the filter that keeps training safe also hides what the policy must learn: it masks the cost of unsafe actions, which actor-critic methods then overvalue, and it keeps near-collision states out of the training data. Our approach therefore learns only from executed safe actions with implicit Q-learning and distills the filter into the policy at synthetic near-collision states. We prove that filter distillation loss bounds the policy's constraint violation and hence how far agents can cross their safety margin, so errors never accumulate. When distillation states cover deployed states, the loss also bounds the collision probability at every step. Without a filter and acting only on local observations, our policies have no collisions in 1,800 test episodes with multiple agents. A policy trained with three agents transfers zero-shot to teams of twenty at constant per-agent computation and stays collision-free with up to eight agents. We test our policies on Crazyflie quadrotors with up to five flying at the same time.

</details>


### [73] [ImpedanceMimic: Learned Impedance as Action Space for Force-Accurate Motion Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19153)
*Matthias Jammot,Pembe Gizem Özdil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ImpedanceMimic，一种强化学习策略，从运动与力测量中学习逐关节刚度、阻尼和位置设定点，推理时仅需运动信息即可高保真地同时实现运动与力跟踪，并零样本迁移到不同人体和人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从运动反推力是病态问题：不同控制器可产生相同运动却不同接触力。基于物理的运动模仿依赖固定增益PD控制，而接触与摆动阶段对关节刚度和阻尼需求冲突，固定增益导致运动跟踪与力跟踪之间的权衡。

Method: 使用强化学习策略ImpedanceMimic，根据运动和力测量动态调节每个关节的刚度、阻尼和位置设定点，但推理阶段仅需运动输入；将增益表示为惯性缩放先验的倍数，使单一策略可控制受试者特异的生物力学模型而无需显式人体测量输入。

Result: 在129名参与者和六项活动中，ImpedanceMimic在运动学与力保真度上优于匹配的固定增益PD（所有集成方案）、直接力矩控制、肌肉驱动控制及现有模仿控制器，接近专用的运动到力回归器；可零样本迁移到未见人体和人形机器人；学到的阻抗构成跨身体共享的低维运动状态表示，刚度随负载缩放、阻尼预判卸载。

Conclusion: 学习式可变阻抗模仿能突破固定增益PD的运动-力跟踪权衡，获得可迁移、低维且具有生物力学解释性的运动状态表示。

Abstract: Recovering the forces behind a motion is ill-posed: different controllers can reproduce the same movement while generating different contact forces. Physics-based motion imitation resolves this ambiguity through simulation, but relies on fixed-gain proportional-derivative (PD) control. We show that fixed gains impose a trade-off between motion and force tracking, as contact and swing place conflicting demands on joint stiffness and damping. We introduce ImpedanceMimic, a reinforcement-learning policy that modulates per-joint stiffness, damping, and position setpoints from motion and force measurements, but requires only motion at inference. Across 129 participants and six activities, ImpedanceMimic outperforms matched fixed-gain PD under every integration scheme, direct-torque, muscle-actuated and existing imitation controllers in kinematic and force fidelity, approaching a dedicated motion-to-force regressor. By expressing gains as multiples of inertia-scaled priors, a single policy controls subject-specific biomechanical models without explicit anthropometric input and transfers zero-shot to unseen humans and a humanoid robot. The learned impedance forms a low-dimensional representation of motor state, shared across bodies, that generalizes to withheld activities: stiffness scales with load, and damping anticipates unloading.

</details>


### [74] [SuperGlance: Driving at a Glance with a Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19138)
*Jieyuan Pei,Xin Yan,Mingwei Xu,Haiyi Zhang,Siyu Zou,Zhangyi Hu,Zhuoxuan Li,Yuguang Yang,Rongjunchen Zhang,Cheng Chi,Hui Xiong,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuperGlance利用VLM第一个token前的隐藏状态作为编码器式驾驶策略，线性头对5223条候选轨迹打分，单次前向即可规划，耗时84ms，比Qwen-Drive-1.0快6倍且性能不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA自回归逐步解码规划耗时超1秒，而人类驾驶依赖快速的System 1；已有快速方案把System 1置于VLM之外，丢失了VLM能力。作者认为VLM写首个token前已把场景读入一个隐藏状态，该状态足以驱动和问答。

Method: 用decoder-only VLM的初始隐藏状态接线性头，对固定候选轨迹集打分；奖励离线计算，支持奖励加权监督和精确期望奖励的强化学习；训练前后用多选问答（双选项顺序防捷径）保持视觉能力，并用KL项防漂移。

Result: 视觉多选问答与基座VLM解码相当；规划仅84ms GPU时间，比Qwen-Drive-1.0快6倍，在NAVSIM、PAI-AV、WOD-E2E上至少不差；AlpaSim闭环中两者在300ms延迟内km/责任事故保持，计入GPU时间后领先从2.8倍增至3.9倍；延迟1.2s时本模型低于Qwen-Drive-1.0在其518ms时的表现。

Conclusion: 单次前向读取隐藏状态为VLA提供更可行的System 1路径，显著加速规划并保持能力，但延迟鲁棒性仍受限于计入的GPU时间。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is turning to vision-language-action models (VLAs). They answer and plan by decoding, one step at a time. Each step is another forward pass, so a plan can take over a second of GPU time. Embodied AI often pairs such a slow, reasoning System 2 with a fast System 1 that acts. Human drivers plan at a glance, with System 1. Faster designs usually put System 1 outside the vision-language model (VLM), in a planner without its abilities. Yet before a VLM writes its first token, it has already read the scene into one hidden state. We show that, once trained, this hidden state is enough to drive and to answer questions about what the model sees. SuperGlance reads it to turn a decoder-only VLM into an encoder-style driving policy. Its linear head scores a fixed set of 5,273 candidate trajectories. With the set fixed, rewards are computed offline, so supervised targets can be reward-weighted and reinforcement learning maximizes the exact expected reward. Before and during policy training, multiple-choice training keeps the VLM's visual abilities in one pass, so the policy still answers questions about driving scenes. Training questions are built against shortcuts. Each must be answered right in two option orders. Against drift, a KL term keeps each stage near its starting model.
