One pass offers VLAs a more plausible path to System 1. SuperGlance answers visual multiple-choice questions as well as its base VLM does by decoding. It plans in 84 ms of GPU time, six times faster than Qwen-Drive-1.0, and at least as well on NAVSIM, PAI-AV and WOD-E2E. In AlpaSim closed loop, km per at-fault incident holds for both planners up to a 300-ms delay and drops beyond. With GPU time charged as delay, SuperGlance's lead over Qwen-Drive-1.0 grows from 2.8 to 3.9 times. Delayed by 1.2 s, our model falls below Qwen-Drive-1.0 at its own 518 ms.

</details>


### [75] [Wireless Edge-Assisted Adaptive Latent Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Models in Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19131)
*Fatih Temiz,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WEALR无线边缘辅助框架，通过联合选择执行位置与潜在推理收敛阈值，在CMDP下用TRPO-Lag平衡任务性能与资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型计算需求大，难以在资源受限的端侧实现实时控制；潜在推理的测试时缩放存在任务成功、推理延迟与能耗之间的权衡；需在真实通信场景下实现高效协同推理。

Method: 构建无线边缘辅助的自适应潜在推理框架，决策智能体联合选择执行位置和潜在推理收敛阈值，建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，采用拉格朗日约束深度强化学习TRPO-Lag求解。

Result: 基于LIBERO操作任务、实测VLA推理开销和射线追踪无线信道评估，TRPO-Lag的可行性加权任务成功率比基准CDRL方法最高提升23%。

Conclusion: 所提框架能在变化无线条件和任务需求下有效平衡任务性能与资源消耗，实现VLA模型的高效边缘协同推理。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable embodied artificial intelligence (AI) by translating visual observations and language instructions into robotic actions. Emerging use cases in physical AI are expected to drive the need for efficient inference and communication, as the computational demands of the VLA models pose challenges for real-time control with limited onboard resources. Test-time scaling through latent reasoning allows these models to refine hidden representations without generating explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens, but introduces a trade-off between task success, inference latency, and energy consumption. In this paper, we address the efficient collaborative inference under a realistic communication scenario. We propose a wireless edge-assisted framework for adaptive latent reasoning (WEALR) that augments onboard computation with edge computing. A decision-making agent jointly selects the execution location and latent-reasoning convergence threshold. We formulate this joint adaptation as a constrained Markov decision process (CMDP). A Lagrangian-constrained deep reinforcement learning (CDRL) approach, namely trust region policy optimization Lagrangian (TRPO-Lag), learns to balance task performance and resource consumption under varying wireless conditions and task requirements. We evaluate the framework using LIBERO manipulation tasks, profiled VLA inference costs, and ray-traced wireless channels. Experimental results demonstrate that TRPO-Lag achieves up to 23% higher feasibility-weighted task success rate compared to benchmark CDRL methods.

</details>


### [76] [Leaning Over to Reach Far-Away Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19100)
*Allen Devaraj,William Xie,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究通过支撑环境稳定来扩展人形机器人工作空间的全身模型预测控制方法，使用采样MPC实现倾斜、支撑、伸展和恢复，将可达范围扩展约50厘米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人形机器人全身控制旨在避免或适应接触，但要将物体放置在正常可达范围之外的表面上，明确需要利用接触支撑。研究旨在通过支撑环境来扩大机器人的工作空间。

Method: 采用基于采样的全身模型预测控制（MPC），仅使用局部感知和基准标记，在Unitree H1-2人形机器人上实现倾斜、支撑、伸展和恢复，控制器在笔记本上以34 Hz实时运行。

Result: 控制器实现仿真到现实迁移，将可达范围扩展约50厘米，维持负载姿态超过四分钟；在54次试验（6个目标、3种桌面高度）中，89%成功到达目标，81%完成完整伸展与恢复循环；时间持续性和探索尺度强烈影响成功率，CEM在规划速率和模型失配下提供最宽的操作区域。

Conclusion: 这些结果建立了在线、接触丰富的人形机器人操作的真实世界基线，并表明较小的实时采样预算足以实现该任务。

Abstract: We study supported reaching to extend a humanoid robot's workspace by bracing against the environment for stabilization using a straightforward yet capable whole-body model predictive control (MPC) approach, exclusively with local sensing and fiducial markers. While typical solutions for whole body humanoid control aim at avoiding or accommodating contact, contact is explicitly required to reach objects placed on surfaces far out of normal reach. We employ sampling-based MPC for whole-body leaning, bracing, reaching, and recovery on a Unitree H1-2 humanoid. The controller runs in real-time at 34 Hz on a laptop, transfers sim-to-real, extends reach by about 50 cm beyond the unbraced standing workspace, and sustains the loaded posture for over four minutes, before pose estimation drifts. The controller reaches the commanded target in 89% of trials and completes the full reach-and-recovery cycle in 81%, across 54 trials spanning six targets and three table heights. We further characterize why such a small real-time sampling budget is sufficient, finding that temporal persistence of sampled actions and exploration scale strongly govern success, while CEM provides the widest operating region over planning rate and model mismatch. These results establish a real-world baseline for online, contact-rich humanoid manipulation. Code, videos, and full hardware details are available at this https URL

</details>


### [77] [RoboCoEvo: Expanding Robot Capabilities through Agent-Policy Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19081)
*Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Dawei Wang,Zhiyuan Li,Jinbang Huang,Xuan Zhao,Yintao Ma,Yangzheng Wu,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboCoEvo，一种失败引导的协同进化框架，通过代码即策略的方式让MLLM智能体与机器人基础模型相互促进、迭代提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM智能体与机器人基础模型的集成系统改进是解耦的，一方进步未必惠及另一方，限制了整体能力；需要二者协同进化。

Method: RoboCoEvo以失败引导为核心：智能体通过代码即策略编写并改进可执行的失败检测与恢复程序，产生有针对性的纠正经验；再用后训练将这些纠正行为固化到VLA等机器人模型的端到端策略中；更新后策略的失败分布反过来指导智能体程序的下一轮修订。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1K、RoboCasa和RoboTwin上的实验表明，随着协同进化轮次推进，自主VLA任务性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 智能体显式可执行知识与机器人模型参数学习的迭代耦合能够实现协同进化，从而有效提升集成系统的自主任务执行能力。

Abstract: Integrating MLLM-based agents with robotic foundation models has attracted increasing attention as a means of enhancing robotic capabilities. However, the improvement of such integrated systems remains largely decoupled across their agent and model components, with advances in one component not necessarily benefiting the other, substantially limiting the capabilities of the combined system. As both MLLMs and robotic foundation models become increasingly capable, fully realizing their combined potential calls for co-evolution, an iterative process in which improvements in agents and robotic foundation models reinforce one another. We propose RoboCoEvo, a failure-guided co-evolution framework that couples the refinement of the agent's explicit and reusable executable knowledge with parameter learning in robotic foundation models, such as vision-language-action (VLA) models. Through a code-as-policy formulation, the agent develops and refines executable failure-detection and recovery programs, generating targeted corrective experience. Post-training consolidates the resulting corrective behaviors into the robotic model's end-to-end policy, improving autonomous task execution. Changes in the updated policy's failure distribution, in turn, guide subsequent revisions to the agent's programs. Experiments on BEHAVIOR-1K, RoboCasa, and RoboTwin demonstrate sustained improvements in autonomous VLA task performance across successive co-evolution rounds.

</details>


### [78] [Certify, Then Select: Viability-Aware Runtime Steering of Frozen Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19047)
*Angelo Moroncelli,Loris Roveda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAST 在测试时从冻结扩散策略采样多个动作块，用障碍条件认证安全性并学习价值集成排序选取执行块，并引入可行性（CAST-V/VL）避免死端，在多种机器人任务上优于安全过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略部署时面临演示中未包含的约束，逐一重训练不现实；现有安全过滤器修改动作时忽略任务，大幅修改会使策略无法继续执行。

Method: 提出 CAST：从冻结策略采样若干动作块，用障碍条件逐一认证，执行通过认证且被学习价值集成排名最高的块；证明认证步骤安全性、与可行条件策略的 KL 偏差界及闭环增益分解；进一步提出 CAST-V 以策略条件可行性保持安全控制权威，可用模型有限样本保证估计或从控制器日志学习（CAST-VL）。

Result: 在 robomimic 操作、平面四旋翼、MuJoCo 人形和独轮车上，冻结策略下 CAST 在几乎所有设置中联合任务与约束成功率高于输出过滤器；过滤器修改幅度大时先认证后排序尤为关键，可行性机制消除了一步认证看不到的大部分死端。

Conclusion: 先认证后排序的测试时动作选择能有效提升冻结扩散策略在约束下的任务成功率，可行性建模对避免死端至关重要。

Abstract: Diffusion policies, which generate short action sequences by denoising random noise, are a leading way to learn robot skills from demonstrations. Once deployed, they meet constraints the demonstrations never contained, and retraining for each is impractical. A safety filter edits the policy's action to satisfy them, but the edit ignores the task, and a large edit can leave the policy where it cannot continue. We propose CAST (Certified Action Selection at Test time): draw a few action chunks from the frozen policy, certify each with a barrier condition, and execute the certified chunk that a learned value ensemble ranks highest. We prove that a certified step is safe whatever the critic does; that, whenever a certified sample exists, the executed chunk stays within log N nats of the policy conditioned on feasibility, while a safety-filter projection can be singular to it; and that an exact closed-loop identity splits the gain of ranking into availability, spread and the critic's target. Certifying the next chunk is not enough: a generative controller can have safe actions available now and none later. We characterise the safe control authority of a frozen policy under finite sampling, and CAST-V preserves it through a policy-conditioned viability, estimated with a model under a finite-sample guarantee or learned from the controller's own logs (CAST-VL). On robomimic manipulation, a planar quadrotor, a MuJoCo humanoid and a unicycle, with frozen policies, CAST raises joint task-and-constraint success above the output filter in nearly every setting; certifying before ranking is decisive where a filter's edit is large, and viability removes most dead ends that one-step certification cannot see. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [79] [TMA: Sim-to-Real Forceful Humanoid Manipulation via Tactile-Driven Motor Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18976)
*Yiting Chen,Kelin Yu,Sarthak Kaingade,Bryan Trinh,Kaiyu Hang,Roberto Martin-martin,Nima Fazeli,X. Alice Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TMA方法及全身系统，实现零样本仿真到真实的有力人形操作，以圆形门把手开启为基准，仅用仿真训练即在真实人形机器人上显著优于RMA和反应式触觉策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形操作研究多聚焦几何主导交互，而有力多指操作需应对真实接触与交互动力学不确定性；圆形门把手开启需稳定多指摩擦抓握、无滑移扭矩、力调节推压和全身稳定，是理想挑战基准。

Method: TMA以触觉可观测交互组织策略学习，用锥几何作为多指接触表示归纳偏置，基于触觉历史在线调整运动行为；上半身操作策略与预训练通用运动控制器联合训练以协调灵巧交互与全身稳定；全程仿真训练后零样本迁移到Unitree G1人形机器人及BrainCo Revo2多指手。

Result: 在真实世界四个商用圆形门把手上，TMA显著优于标准RMA和反应式触觉策略基线。

Conclusion: 触觉驱动的运动适应结合全身协调，可实现零样本仿真到真实的有力人形多指操作，圆形门把手开启验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent humanoid loco-manipulation in articulated environments has largely focused on geometry-dominant interactions, where success is primarily determined by geometric alignment and motion execution. In contrast, forceful multi-finger manipulation requires the robot to adapt to real-world uncertainties in contact and interaction dynamics. We present Tactile-Driven Motor Adaptation (TMA) and an integrated whole-body system for zero-shot sim-to-real forceful humanoid manipulation, and study round-doorknob opening as a challenging benchmark requiring stable multi-finger frictional grasping, sufficient torque generation without slip, force-regulated pushing, and whole-body stability. TMA structures policy learning around interactions observable through tactile feedback, uses cone geometry as an inductive bias for multi-finger contact representation, and adapts motor behavior online from tactile history. An upper-body manipulation policy is trained alongside a pretrained general locomotion controller to coordinate dexterous interaction and whole-body stabilization. Trained entirely in simulation, TMA transfers zero-shot to a Unitree G1 humanoid with BrainCo Revo2 multi-fingered hands and substantially outperforms standard Rapid Motor Adaptation (RMA) and reactive tactile policy baselines across four commercial round doorknobs in the real world.

</details>


### [80] [Optimal Operating-Point Selection for Single-Linearization Trajectory Planning of Nonlinear Robot Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18935)
*Bhanuka Dayawansa,Rohan Munasinghe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种离线性能驱动的单点线性化位置选择方法，通过优化变量化线性化点并网格搜索加有界细化，显著降低非线性轨迹目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单点线性化计算高效，但规划性能依赖所选线性化点；现有方法通常固定选择初始点、中点或终点，缺乏系统性优化。

Method: 从三次种子轨迹采样候选工作点，对每个仿射模型求解相同的约束有限时域二次规划，将控制序列作用于原非线性动力学，以最小化非线性轨迹目标为准则，通过网格搜索和有界细化选择最优工作点。

Result: 在三自由度UR5机械臂的四个点到点运动上，最优归一化工作点位于0.325至0.935之间，体现轨迹依赖性；相比最佳常规初始/中点/终点线性化，非线性轨迹目标分别降低17-18%、21-22%、37-38%和7-8%；相同PID反馈修正下性能优势在代表性任务中仍保持。

Conclusion: 系统化选择工作点可在保持固定仿射规划模型的同时显著提升单点线性化轨迹规划性能。

Abstract: Single-point linearization provides a computationally efficient basis for optimization-based trajectory planning of nonlinear robot manipulators. However, planning performance depends on the selected linearization point. This paper presents an offline performance-driven approach that treats the location of a single linearization as an optimization variable. Candidate operating points are sampled from a common cubic seed trajectory, and each corresponding affine model is used to solve an identical constrained finite-horizon quadratic program (QP). The resulting control sequences are executed on the original nonlinear dynamics, and the operating point minimizing the nonlinear trajectory objective is selected through grid search and bounded refinement. Evaluation on four point-to-point motions of a three-degree-of-freedom UR5 manipulator yields optimal normalized operating-point locations between 0.325 and 0.935, demonstrating trajectory-dependent selection. Compared with the best-performing conventional initial, midpoint, or terminal linearization, the proposed approach reduces the nonlinear trajectory objective by 17-18%, 21-22%, 37-38%, and 7-8% across the four tasks, respectively. Additional nonlinear execution with identical PID feedback correction confirms that the performance advantage persists for a representative task. These results demonstrate that systematic operating-point selection can substantially improve single-linearization trajectory planning while preserving a fixed affine planning model.

</details>


### [81] [Correcting Persistent Failures in Point-Cloud Grasping Policies using Human Demonstrations in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18919)
*Achyuthan Unni Krishnan,Karthik Rengarajan,Bharath Narasimhan,Chris von Csefalvay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种人在环失败循环方法：自动挖掘强化学习策略持续失败的抓取场景，由人在虚拟现实中用手抓取目标物体并返回两个抓取点作为示范，仅指定抓取位置而由机器人自行选择接近方式，从而结合RL与少量人类示范提升点云抓取策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭、仓库和农场中的机器人需要抓取从未见过的物体，通常没有3D模型或类别标签；基于深度传感器点云的抓取策略仅依赖观测到的几何信息，可适用于任意新物体。Contact-GraspNet等离线抓取生成器按学习到的合理性排序而非按抓取是否真正抬起物体排序；RL可直接优化抓取成功，但会持续在特定抓取上失败。

Method: 提出人在环失败循环：自动挖掘RL策略反复失败的抓取情况，发送到VR应用中由人工用手抓取物体，并以两个抓取点的形式返回训练；人只贡献“抓哪里”，机器人自行决定接近方式。

Result: 在仿真中，RL将未见物体上的成功率从Contact-GraspNet的55%提升到83%；加入58个人类示范后，RL持续失败的困难案例成功率从24%提升到45%，几乎翻倍，并在未示范的类似案例上恢复RL单独会损失的性能，同时不降低未见物体上的成功率。

Conclusion: 少量针对性人类示范可有效弥补RL在困难抓取上的持续失败，且不损害泛化性能，说明人在环失败循环是提升点云抓取策略的实用补充手段。

Abstract: Robots in homes, warehouses and farms must pick up objects they have never seen, usually without a 3D model or a category label. A grasping policy that works directly on the point cloud from a depth sensor needs only the geometry the robot observes, so it applies to any new object. Offline grasp generators such as Contact-GraspNet work this way, but they rank grasps by learned plausibility rather than by whether the grasp actually lifts the object. Reinforcement learning (RL) can optimize for that directly and make a point-cloud grasping policy much better on average, yet it leaves specific grasps persistently unsolved. We present a human-in-the-loop failure loop that targets those grasps: situations an RL policy keeps failing are mined automatically, sent to a virtual-reality application where a person picks the object up with their own hands, and returned to training as two grasp points. The person contributes only where to grasp; the robot chooses its own approach. In simulation, RL raises success on unseen objects from 55% with Contact-GraspNet to 83%. Adding 58 human demonstrations nearly doubles success on the hard cases RL kept failing, from 24% to 45%, and recovers performance that RL alone loses on similar cases that were never demonstrated, without reducing success on unseen objects.

</details>


### [82] [Passage-Focused Soft Coordination for Hierarchical Multi-Robot Navigation in Bottleneck Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18877)
*Haoze Dong,Stepan Dergachev,Meng Guo,Konstantin Yakovlev,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种以通道为中心的软全局协调与灵活局部执行框架，在多机器人导航中实现高成功率与更优的完工时间和流时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性全局调度对执行偏差敏感，局部方法缺乏长时程协调，需兼顾长时程合作与灵活执行。

Method: 构建通道诱导的凸集图，进行优先级排序的两级软协调：通道感知路径选择考虑通道使用与通行方向，路线受限的连续时空规划惩罚时间冲突；生成的参考轨迹指导模型预测路径积分控制器在线避碰，并采用自适应时间与几何跟踪和选择性重规划应对执行偏差。

Result: 在4种环境、5-30个机器人的1200个实例中达到接近完美的成功率，完工时间和流时间优于基线；8机器人硬件实验全部无碰撞完成。

Conclusion: 以通道为中心的协调能有效平衡长时程合作与灵活执行。

Abstract: In multi-robot navigation, rigid global schedules are sensitive to execution deviations, while local methods lack long-horizon coordination. We propose a passage-focused framework combining soft global coordination with flexible local execution. A passage-induced graph of convex sets supports priority-ordered planning with two levels of soft coordination. Passage-aware route selection accounts for passage usage and traversal directions, while route-restricted continuous space-time planning penalizes temporal conflicts. The resulting references guide a model predictive path integral controller that avoids collisions online. Adaptive temporal and geometric tracking accommodates execution deviations, while selective replanning maintains coordination without rigid trajectory reservations. Across 1,200 instances in 4 different environments with 5-30 robots, the framework achieves a near-perfect success rate and outperforms evaluated baselines in makespan and flowtime. Hardware experiments with 8 robots demonstrate successful completion of all trials without collisions. These results highlight the effectiveness of passage-focused coordination in balancing long-horizon cooperation with flexible execution.

</details>


### [83] [Lazy-MOSAIC: Accelerating Skill-Centric Manipulation Planning with Learned Skill-Effect World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21517)
*Itamar Mishani,Mukund Krishnakumar,Oren Salzman,Maxim Likhachev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Lazy-MOSAIC 是一个基于惰性规划的机器人长时程操作规划框架，通过廉价的学习技能效果模型预测技能可行性，将昂贵仿真集中在有希望的候选技能上，并在假设失效时仅修复受影响部分，从而显著加速规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作任务需要在庞大的技能序列与参数混合空间中高效搜索，而现有 MOSAIC 方法对每个参数化技能都进行高保真仿真，计算成本过高。

Method: 引入惰性规划框架，先由学习得到的技能效果世界模型在亚毫秒级预测技能的可行性与产生的世界状态，据此引导仿真聚焦于有希望的候选技能；当预测假设被推翻时仅局部修复搜索，并保证最终返回计划中每个技能均经仿真验证。

Result: 在难度递增的杂乱货架取物任务上评估并在实体机器人上演示，Lazy-MOSAIC 规划速度显著快于基线、仿真查询次数更少，且在每种设置下成功率匹配或超过基线。

Conclusion: 用廉价预测替代大量仿真查询的惰性规划策略，能在保证计划有效性的同时大幅提升长时程操作任务的规划效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation tasks require a robot to perform a sequence of physical interactions with its environment, typically combining several different skills. Planning such behaviors efficiently is challenging: given a library of parameterized skills, solutions lie in a vast hybrid space of skill sequences and their parameters, and evaluating each candidate skill's feasibility and effect with a high-fidelity world model such as a physics simulator is computationally expensive. MOSAIC, a recent skill-centric approach, lets the skills guide a non-sequential search toward the regions of this space where they are likely to succeed; however, it pays the full cost of simulation for every parameterized skill it considers. In this work, we introduce Lazy-MOSAIC, a lazy planning framework that instead searches over hypothesized skill outcomes. A cheap learned skill-effect world model predicts, in a fraction of a millisecond, a skill's feasibility and the world state it produces. Lazy-MOSAIC then uses these predictions to focus expensive simulation on promising candidate skills and, when a hypothesis is invalidated, repairs only the affected part of the search, while ensuring that every skill in a returned plan is validated in simulation. We evaluate Lazy-MOSAIC on cluttered-shelf retrieval tasks of increasing difficulty and demonstrate it on a physical robot, showing that it plans substantially faster than baselines with fewer simulator queries, while matching or exceeding their success rate in every setting. Visit this http URL .

</details>


### [84] [Multi-CONDOR: Learning Multistable Motion Primitives from Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21059)
*Gabriele Giuseppe Di Marzo,Mariano Ramírez Montero,Maximilian Stölzle,Rodrigo Pérez-Dattari,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出multi-CONDOR框架，可学习具有多个离散吸引子或连续吸引目标轮廓的稳定运动基元，并在仿真与机械臂抓取实验中验证其优于单目标基元与StableNODE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于动力系统的运动策略多针对单一目标，但许多操作任务存在多个离散目标或连续可接受结果集合，需要在统一框架中同时保持这些目标结构且保证连续自主动态与收敛性。

Method: 结合模仿学习与鼓励学习动力学和编码期望吸引结构的潜在参考系统一致的损失函数，扩展CONDOR为multi-CONDOR，支持多离散吸引子或连续吸引目标轮廓，并给出基于Lyapunov的收敛性分析。

Result: 在离散目标仿真中，相比由状态机选择的分开学习的单目标基元，轨迹RMSE降低约72%；在双目标和三目标任务中，将StableNODE的初始状态超出目标容差比例0.31%和7.29%均降至0%；Franka Emika机械臂实验成功从演示学习实现由容器任一 handles 抓取。

Conclusion: multi-CONDOR将运动复现与收敛到多个有效结果的灵活性相结合，可统一处理离散多目标和连续目标轮廓的运动基元学习。

Abstract: Dynamical-system-based motion policies provide a framework for learning reactive robot motions with stability and convergence properties. Many manipulation tasks, however, admit several distinct goals or a continuous set of acceptable outcomes, requiring motion primitives that preserve these alternatives. Accommodating both goal structures within a unified framework with continuous, autonomous dynamics remains an open challenge. We propose a CONDOR-based framework, multi-CONDOR, for learning motion primitives with either multiple discrete attractors or a continuous attracting goal contour. The approach combines imitation learning with losses that encourage agreement between the learned task dynamics and latent reference systems encoding the desired attraction structure. We provide a Lyapunov-based analysis of the convergence properties of the learned motion dynamics. We evaluate the framework on simulated benchmarks with discrete goals, qualitative examples with continuous goal contours, and robotic grasping experiments. In simulation, multi-CONDOR reduces trajectory RMSE by approximately 72% compared with separately learned single-goal motion primitives selected by a state machine. For tasks with two and three goals, respectively, it reduces the fraction of evaluated initial states ending outside the goal tolerance from StableNODE's 0.31% and 7.29% to 0% in both settings. Experiments with a Franka Emika robot arm demonstrate successful grasping of a container by either of its two handles using motions learned from demonstrations. Together, these results show multi-CONDOR combines motion reproduction with the flexibility to converge to alternative valid outcomes.

</details>


### [85] [ReRead: Wrong Predictions, Useful Representations - Correcting Frozen Robot Policies by Re-reading Their Own Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20465)
*Xu Su,Shunlong Xiao,Qintao Yi,Mingkuan Zhao,Xinmiao Yu,Yizhe Li,Zihan Wang,Bokui Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReRead模块：冻结主干，用只读缓存（当前及前两步）的校正器预测动作残差，在RoboTwin 2.0上显著提升多个策略的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略失败时常被归因于表征不足而重训主干或加视觉/记忆模块，作者认为错误预测不等于错误表征，策略可能已保留所需信息但未能正确读出动作。

Method: 冻结主干，训练一个不接收图像、仅基于离线演示的校正器，读取当前及前两步的缓存来预测动作残差，并加入原动作；仅增加4.17%延迟。

Result: 在11个最难任务上，LingBot-VLA、π0.5、Fast-WAM成功率分别提升23.45、13.09、8.00个百分点；50任务配对评估中Fast-WAM从90.73%升至92.66%；Flexiv双臂机器人模糊阶段任务从0%升至36%。消融显示四种外部视觉编码器无额外增益，替换为其他轨迹的过去缓存会显著增大动作误差。

Conclusion: 校正依赖主干自身表征而非外部视觉信息，说明失败主要源于读出而非表征，ReRead是一种低成本有效的策略校正方案。

Abstract: When a pretrained robot policy fails on challenging tasks, common remedies include retraining the backbone, adding an external visual encoder, or introducing a memory module. We argue that wrong predictions do not imply wrong representations: a policy can retain the required information yet fail to read it out as the correct action. To test this view, we propose ReRead: with the backbone frozen, a corrector re-reads the current and two previous caches to predict an action residual. The corrector sees no images, is trained solely on offline demonstrations from all 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, and adds only 4.17% latency. Ablations and interventions show that the correction relies on the backbone's own representations: none of four external visual encoders adds further gain, and swapping in past caches from other trajectories sharply increases action error. On the 11 hardest RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, ReRead improves the success rates of LingBot-VLA, $\pi_{0.5}$, and Fast-WAM by 23.45, 13.09, and 8.00 percentage points, respectively. In paired evaluation on all 50 tasks, it raises Fast-WAM's average success rate from 90.73% to 92.66%. On a Flexiv dual-arm robot, it raises success on a phase-ambiguous task from 0% to 36%.

</details>


### [86] [Making Koopman Control Guarantees Hold on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20402)
*Abulikemu Abuduweili,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Koopman控制器在潜空间线性模型上稳定，却可能无法稳定非线性对象；本文提出Tangent-Invariant Koopman (TIK)以保证一阶匹配，从而将稳定性传递到非线性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的潜空间线性模型能稳定，但部署控制器反复重编码物理状态，导致名义闭环稳定性不能保证非线性对象的稳定性。

Method: 证明当学习闭环动态与真实闭环动态在目标点嵌入切空间上一阶一致时，稳定性可传递；推导近似匹配的鲁棒裕度；提出TIK，利用离线转移估计局部线性化并强制一阶匹配，结合双线性预测器捕捉大机动下的状态相关驱动。

Result: 在四个不稳定基准上，40个标准Koopman控制器潜闭环稳定但部署非线性系统局部不稳定；40个TIK控制器均局部稳定非线性对象；离线曲率测试在倒立摆上识别稳定TIK，高噪声下不确定；在模型误差与曲率界下，不变终端集保证约束满足；TIK在0.25 ms规划预算下实现88%约束倒立摆起摆成功，七关节臂每步1-5 ms。

Conclusion: 一阶切空间匹配是Koopman控制器稳定非线性对象的关键；TIK通过强制该匹配实现可靠稳定与实时控制。

Abstract: A Koopman controller can stabilize its learned latent-space linear model yet fail to stabilize the nonlinear plant. The learned model propagates predictions in latent space, while the deployed controller repeatedly re-encodes the measured physical state. We show that nominal closed-loop stability transfers to the nonlinear plant when the learned and true closed-loop dynamics agree to first order on the embedding's tangent space at the target. We derive a robustness margin for approximate matching and show that a stable open-loop latent model cannot exactly match an unstable equilibrium. We introduce Tangent-Invariant Koopman (TIK), which estimates a local plant linearization from offline transitions and enforces first-order matching to this estimate. TIK recovers the fitted LQR gain at the target, while a bilinear predictor captures state-dependent actuation during large maneuvers. Across four simulated unstable benchmarks, all 40 Standard Koopman controllers have stable latent closed loops, but their deployed nonlinear systems are locally unstable. In contrast, all 40 TIK controllers locally stabilize their respective nonlinear plants. An offline stability test using empirical curvature estimates identifies stable TIK controllers on two cart-pole systems, but is inconclusive under high noise. Under explicit model-error and curvature bounds, an invariant terminal set guarantees constraint satisfaction after entry under local feedback. TIK achieves 88\% constrained cart-pole swing-up success with a 0.25 ms planning budget and controls simulated seven-joint arms within 1-5 ms per step.

</details>


### [87] [NF-VLA: A Normalizing-Flow Baseline for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20314)
*Ruichen Deng,Qinyu Zhao,Yong-Hyun Park,Mutian Tong,Liang Zheng,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NF-VLA将条件自回归归一化流与基于分数的动作精炼结合，通过单一最大似然目标训练视觉-语言-动作策略，在LIBERO基准上达到98.35%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自回归归一化流能否仅凭精确最大似然训练出与主流方法竞争的VLA策略，并统一策略训练、动作生成与精炼。

Method: 采用深层-浅层架构：深层块集中处理视觉、语言和本体感受前缀条件，浅层无条件块提供共享可逆动作变换；视觉-语言骨干与流在经高斯平滑的动作块上联合精确最大似然训练，推理时用同一学习密度做单次基于分数的校正。

Result: 在LIBERO四个标准套件上成功率98.35%，LIBERO-Plus上72.6%（同一检查点无需微调）；消融显示仅深层前缀条件与基于分数的精炼均提升闭环成功率。

Conclusion: 精心结构的自回归流是VLA控制的有竞争力方法，单一学习密度即可支持策略训练、动作生成与精炼。

Abstract: Autoregressive normalizing flows (NFs) provide a tractable density for learning and sampling continuous robot actions. We show that a single maximum-likelihood objective can train vision--language--action (VLA) policies competitive with leading methods on manipulation benchmarks. We present NF-VLA, which combines conditional autoregressive normalizing flows with score-based action refinement. Its deep--shallow architecture concentrates visual, language, and proprioceptive prefix conditioning in a high-capacity deep block, while unconditioned shallow blocks provide a shared invertible action transformation. The vision--language backbone and flow are jointly trained by exact maximum likelihood on Gaussian-smoothed action chunks. The same learned density supplies a single score-based correction at inference, refining generated actions to account for training-time smoothing without a separately trained denoiser. Trained on LIBERO from a pretrained vision--language backbone without robot pretraining, NF-VLA achieves 98.35\% success across the four standard suites and 72.6\% on LIBERO-Plus using the same checkpoint without further fine-tuning. Ablations show that deep-only prefix conditioning and score-based refinement both improve closed-loop success. Together, these results establish carefully structured autoregressive flows as a competitive approach to VLA control, with one learned density supporting policy training, action generation, and refinement.

</details>


### [88] [Right Goal, Wrong Body: Re-grounding Frozen VLAs under Actuation Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20244)
*Umer Siddique,Peilang Li,Yongcan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReGround是一种推理时方法，通过冻结VLA模型并从与任务无关的电机唤醒中学习部署机器人动力学，在检测到驱动偏移时重新接地其预期运动，从而在延迟等变化下恢复任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在理想环境下能产生期望的机器人行为，但部署时驱动变化（如重新安装、机械磨损或命令延迟）会导致任务失败和不安全行为，因此需要解决不可避免的驱动偏移问题。

Method: ReGround保持VLA模型冻结，从与任务无关的电机唤醒中学习部署机器人的动力学，然后跟踪策略的预期运动并保持接近策略自身的计划；仅在检测到偏移时激活，无需奖励、演示或重新训练。

Result: 通过两个VLA模型、八个LIBERO任务和五种偏移设置验证，ReGround能恢复接近最优的成功率，例如在400毫秒延迟下将π_{0.5}的成功率从0.16提升到0.90，同时比线性校准基线更安全。

Conclusion: ReGround是一种有效的推理时方法，能够在驱动偏移下恢复VLA模型的任务成功率并保持安全性，且无需额外训练或演示数据。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are capable of producing desired robotic behaviors in ideal settings. However, they often fail when the robot's actuation changes at deployment. For example, remounting, mechanical wear, or command latency changes can lead to failed tasks and unsafe behaviors. To address the inevitable actuation shift, we propose ReGround, an inference-time method that keeps the VLA frozen and re-grounds its intended motion. ReGround learns the deployed robot's dynamics from task-agnostic motor babbling, and then tracks the policy's intended motion while staying close to the policy's own plan. ReGround activates only upon detecting a shift, and needs no rewards, demonstrations, or retraining. We show via two VLAs, eight LIBERO tasks, and five shift settings that ReGround can restore near-oracle success, such as raising $\pi_{0.5}$'s success from 0.16 to 0.90 under 400 ms latency, while remaining safer than a linear-calibration baseline. Project website and open-source code: this https URL .

</details>


### [89] [RoboKit: Accelerating Parallel Differentiable Optimization for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20185)
*Jianglong Ye,Xuanbin Peng,Conor Mc Gartoll,Changwei Jing,Lai Wei,Jiali Chen,Xueyan Zou,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboKit 是一个基于 GPU 并行多种子求解器的高性能通用机器人可微优化工具包，集成了几何、渲染与物理功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人优化工具在性能、灵活性和与学习的集成方面存在不足，需要一种通用且高效的并行可微优化工具包。

Method: 基于三个原则构建：GPU 并行多种子求解器结合采样与优化器；混合和由粗到细的表示加速碰撞、可微渲染和仿真；代价可微且仿真并行，支持将优化蒸馏为策略并训练强化学习。

Result: 在七个任务上比任务专用基线快 2-60 倍；2.97 秒优化 4096 个抓取；蒸馏策略在精度相当下快 9-240 倍；强化学习在同一模拟器上达到 GPU 基线分数的时间最多提前 2.1 倍。

Conclusion: RoboKit 通过并行可微优化、高效几何表示和学习集成，显著提升了机器人优化任务的性能与灵活性。

Abstract: We present RoboKit, a high-performance, general-purpose toolkit for parallel differentiable optimization in robotics, built on three principles: (i) Performance and flexibility: GPU-parallel multi-seed solvers pair sampling with versatile optimizers, running 2-60$\times$ faster than task-specific baselines across seven tasks; (ii) Geometry, rendering and physics: hybrid and coarse-to-fine representations accelerate collision, differentiable rendering and simulation, optimizing 4096 grasps in 2.97 s; (iii) Integration with learning: costs are differentiable and simulation is parallel, so RoboKit both distills optimization into policies and trains RL; distilled policies run 9-240$\times$ faster at comparable accuracy, and RL reaches a GPU baseline's scores up to 2.1$\times$ sooner on the same simulator.

</details>


### [90] [SAFER: Navigating the Exploration-Exploitation Dilemma in World-Model-Based Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20107)
*Yansong Qu,Zilin Huang,Zihao Sheng,Sikai Chen,Samuel Labi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFER 是一个结合离线失败学习与在线持续优化的框架，利用专家标注与分块隐世界模型提升自动驾驶在安全关键场景下的规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要预判行为后果，尤其在罕见且时间紧迫的事故场景；世界模型受限于训练数据覆盖范围，导致探索-利用困境：覆盖内规划难以超越数据，覆盖外模型误差会误导规划。

Method: SAFER 离线阶段通过部分训练智能体与可恢复脚本扰动制造失败，并由专家为访问状态标注动作与价值；规划时使用分块隐世界模型快速预见后果，并用风险感知采样规划器以回报减有界碰撞惩罚评估候选机动；在线阶段持续在离线数据覆盖外精炼模型。

Result: 闭环评估中，SAFER 在 CARLA Leaderboard 路线取得最高路线完成率0.95与每步奖励0.77（特权鸟瞰视角），在 MetaDrive 取得最高回合奖励，常规53.4、安全关键交通49.0。

Conclusion: SAFER 通过离线学习他人失败并在线上自我改进，有效扩展世界模型覆盖并提升自动驾驶规划的安全性与性能。

Abstract: An autonomous vehicle must foresee the consequences of its actions, especially in the rare, time-critical situations where crashes happen. A world model offers this foresight, but only within the coverage of its training data, which poses an exploration-exploitation dilemma. Within this coverage a planner can hardly improve on the data, and beyond it the model's errors mislead it. Logs of competent driving rarely contain the failures that would widen the coverage, and agents that learn from interaction gain them only by failing themselves, without being shown what to do instead. We propose SAFER, a framework that learns offline from failures that others make, judged by an expert, and keeps learning online from its own driving. Offline, partially trained agents and scripted, recoverable perturbations Stage failures in simulation, and an expert Annotates every visited state with its action and value, so that much of what the planner explores is covered before it drives. In planning, a chunked latent world model Foresees the consequences fast enough for real-time use, and a risk-aware sampling-based planner Evaluates candidate maneuvers by their return minus a bounded collision penalty. Online, a loop continuously Refines the model wherever this driving leaves the coverage of the offline data. In closed-loop evaluation against the compared methods, SAFER reaches the highest route completion and reward per step on the CARLA Leaderboard routes, 0.95 and 0.77 from a privileged bird's-eye view, and the highest episode reward in MetaDrive, 53.4 in regular and 49.0 in safety-critical traffic. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [91] [Value and Policy Calibration from Execution Feedback for Socially Compatible Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19969)
*Sen Shen,Ning Liu,Xiangrui Kong,Yingwei Li,Dongkun Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合想象策略改进与真实交互校准的世界模型强化学习框架，使自动驾驶车辆在无信号交叉口兼顾自身通行效率与人类驾驶车辆的交互负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在交叉口的决策会影响周围人类驾驶车辆的延误、制动与行驶平顺性，需要学习社会兼容行为，并将想象交互结果与实际执行反馈相连接。

Method: 构建交互感知潜在世界模型支持想象，采用离策略校正与回放更新，使观察到的奖励和状态转移同时监督评论家与演员，实现基于最终交互的直接价值与策略校准。

Result: 无信号交叉口仿真实现100%成功率，相比PIWM和DreamerV3分别将后车HDV加速度变化降低65.5%和44.0%；消融实验表明价值校准降低HDV负担、策略校准提升通行效率；分布外交通偏移下相比PIWM在相当通行时间下具有更低自车加加速度和HDV加速度变化。

Conclusion: 想象策略改进与真实交互校准相结合的世界模型强化学习框架能够有效平衡自动驾驶车辆通行效率与人类驾驶车辆交互负担，并在分布外交通条件下保持较好的平顺性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicle (AV) decisions at intersections affect the delay, braking, and motion smoothness of surrounding human-driven vehicles (HDVs). Learning socially compatible behavior requires connecting imagined interaction outcomes with feedback from executed actions. We propose a world-model reinforcement learning framework combines imagined policy improvement with direct value and policy calibration from finalized interactions, balancing AV progress with HDV interaction burden. An interaction-aware latent model supports imagination, while off-policy correction and replay updates enable observed rewards and transitions to supervise both the critic and actor. Simulation experiments at an unsignalized intersection achieve a 100% success and reductions in following-HDV acceleration variation of 65.5% and 44.0% relative to PIWM and DreamerV3, respectively. Ablations identify complementary roles for value calibration in reducing HDV burden and policy calibration in improving passage efficiency. Tests under out-of-distribution traffic shifts further show lower ego jerk and HDV acceleration variation than PIWM at comparable passage times.

</details>


### [92] [AgenticJevNav: Agentic Object Search and Navigation via Event-Driven Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19912)
*Shuning Zhang,Shuli Zhang,Yunheng Wang,Wei Lu,Jiaxi Zhang,Yihang Kang,Tao Wang,Xiangyu Chen,Yixiao Feng,Kai Sheng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgenticJevNav 是一种面向智能体导航的分层架构，通过目标导向规划、证据记忆、事件触发验证与有界执行，在仿真和真实四足机器人上取得优于基线方法的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体导航需要在证据不完整、执行失败和推理延迟的条件下维持目标并调整动作，现有方法难以兼顾目标保持与自适应执行。

Method: 提出 AgenticJevNav，将目标导向规划、证据记忆、事件触发验证和有界执行相连接；Agent 基于 RGB、里程计、记忆和反馈规划，保留目标所有权并授权有界动作集；Jev 在执行受阻/失败或连续两段低进展后审查结构化证据，并以版本、新鲜度和一次性检查约束决策应用；单一执行器与独立 RGB 反射约束运动，目标修订时撤销过期请求与动作后再重新规划。

Result: 在六类仿真任务族共 60 次试验中达到 73.33% 成功率，比次优的仅 Agent 聚合对照高 21.67 个百分点；在真实 GENISOM L1 四足机器人上 30 次试验成功率为 70.0%，并包含有界搜索报告。

Conclusion: 仿真与真实机器人结果验证了所提架构的有效性，剩余失败案例指出了在 grounding、接近规划和运行时方面仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Agentic navigation must maintain goals and adapt actions from incomplete evidence despite execution failures and delayed inference. We present AgenticJevNav, which links goal-directed planning, evidence memory, event-triggered verification, and bounded execution. The Agent plans from RGB, odometry, memory, and feedback, retaining goal ownership while authorizing a bounded action set. Jev reviews structured evidence after blocked/failed execution or two consecutive low-progress segments. Version, freshness, and once-only checks govern decision application. A single executor and independent RGB reflex constrain motion. Goal revisions revoke obsolete requests and actions before replanning. Across six simulated task families, AgenticJevNav achieves 73.33% SR over 60 trials, 21.67 percentage points above the next-best Agent-only aggregate control under differing historical protocols. A real-world GENISOM L1 quadruped deployment achieves 70.0% success over 30 trials, including bounded search reporting. Together, the simulation and physical-robot results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed architecture, while remaining failures highlight opportunities to improve grounding, approach planning, and runtime.

</details>


### [93] [HospitalBench: Diagnosing Where and Why Embodied Agents Fail on Long Hospital Task Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19274)
*Gaytri Jena,Kapil Wanaskar,Aman Chadha,Vasu Sharma,Vinija Jain,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HospitalBench是一个在NVIDIA Isaac Sim中诊断长时程具身任务的基准，包含十项医院杂务，每项由五步因果链子任务组成；首轮15次运行中仅2次通过全部子任务，失败调用后多数尝试不同调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程具身任务的失败模式无法通过最终任务成功率识别，现有基准将子任务并排放置，缺乏步骤间的因果依赖，难以诊断链条断裂位置及失败后的行为。

Method: 构建HospitalBench：十项医院杂务，每项含两步不计分预热和五步计分因果链子任务，在单一连续仿真中运行；视觉语言智能体通过技能级工具从相机图像行动；用仅执行模型可调用动作的脚本机器人验证可解性；从轨迹读取链条断裂点、失败调用后的动作及位置估计漂移。

Result: 首轮报告15/60次计划运行（三个模型高努力，T1和T10各两个起始状态、T3一个），脚本机器人在三项杂务上验证；15次中2次通过全部五子任务；子任务1-5后仍运行的次数为15、8、4、2、2；189次失败调用中168次后续调用不同、111次后续调用未失败。

Conclusion: 首轮结果仅为初步，非完整基准；将发布场景、任务链、脚本机器人、评估框架和轨迹。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied tasks fail in ways that end-task success cannot see. HospitalBench is a diagnostic benchmark of ten hospital chores in NVIDIA Isaac Sim, each two unscored warm-up steps followed by a causal chain of five scored sub-tasks in one continuous simulation: the state one step leaves (an object pose, a choice, a fact seen once) determines what the next step must do, unlike benchmarks that place sub-tasks side by side. Vision-language agents act through skill-level tools from camera images. A scripted robot that makes only the calls a model gets is the solvability check of a chore. From each trajectory the benchmark reads where a chain breaks, what the agent did after a failed call, and how far its own position estimate drifts from the true one. This version reports first results only: 15 of 60 planned runs (three models at high effort, one run each on chores T1 and T10 from both start states and T3 from one), with scripted-robot proofs on three of the ten chores. Of the 15 runs, 2 passed all five sub-tasks. Runs still going after sub-tasks 1 to 5 were 15, 8, 4, 2, 2. Of 189 failed calls, 168 were followed by a different call and 111 by a call that did not fail. These are first results and not a full benchmark. We will release the scenes, task chains, scripted robot, evaluation harness and trajectories.

</details>


### [94] [Refinement vs. Replanning: Inference Compute Allocation in Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19034)
*Alba Maria Tellez Fernandez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 冻结分块机器人策略可在每次策略调用内增加去噪精化工作量或提高重规划频率两侧分配推理算力；在LIBERO仿真中，h=2、d=2比h=2、d=5成功率更高且更省算力，h=5在成功率相近时大幅降低单次尝试算力，但未确立等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时可用的推理算力有限，需在不更新模型权重的前提下，决定算力应更多用于单次调用内的动作分块精化，还是更频繁地基于新观测重规划，而两者相互耦合、需联合评估。

Method: 冻结单个SmolVLA检查点，在同步LIBERO仿真中进行受控评测：以去噪步数d作为精化控制、固定分块长度K=50，用名义算力速率与完整尝试成本两种口径报告成功率，在发现任务上选择比较、在留出任务上检验预设比较，并分别用算术量和主机时延刻画成本。

Result: d=10时82.1%的算术量与去噪无关，固定输入主机时间仿射拟合的截距占17.7%；h=2、d=2成功率139/160，高于d=5的122/160（+10.6个百分点，任务条件95%区间+4.4至+16.9），且每控制步算术量少5.9%；d=2时h=5为138/160、与h=2的139/160相近，但单次尝试算术量低60.7%，未确立等价。

Conclusion: 部署自适应必须联合评估生成精化工作量与反馈频率，算力口径（算术量与时延）会给出不同成本画像；该案例仅说明方法，不给出通用最优配置。

Abstract: A frozen chunked robot policy can spend deployment-time inference compute in two coupled ways: more work within each policy call to refine a predicted action chunk, or more frequent policy calls to replan from fresh observations. We study this allocation without updating model weights. Our evaluation procedure reports success with both nominal compute rate and complete attempt cost, selects comparisons on discovery tasks, and tests prespecified comparisons on held-out tasks. Comparisons added after inspecting the held-out results only summarize observed patterns. We demonstrate the procedure with one frozen SmolVLA checkpoint in synchronous LIBERO simulation, using denoising count as the refinement control and fixing chunk length at K=50. Arithmetic and latency characterize cost differently: at d=10, 82.1% of counted arithmetic is independent of denoising, whereas a descriptive affine fit to fixed-input host time assigns 17.7% to its intercept. At h=2, d=2 succeeds on 139/160 attempts versus 122/160 for d=5 (+10.6 percentage points; task-conditional 95% interval: +4.4 to +16.9), while using 5.9% less arithmetic per control step. Executing h=5 rather than h=2 at d=2 yields 138/160 versus 139/160 successes and 60.7% lower arithmetic per attempt; equivalence is not established. The case study shows why deployment adaptation must evaluate generation effort and feedback frequency jointly; it does not define a universal setting.

</details>


### [95] [Robotic Reward Models in the Foundation-Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20741)
*Guoqiang Liang,Yiqi Lin,Shangchen Zhou,Wenzheng Zeng,Yanzhe Chen,Zechen Bai,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述将114种机器人奖励模型按输出回答的问题分为五类，并发布RRM-Bench-1K基准与RRM-Hub工具，发现最强评估器能排序整体执行但密集信用更依赖时间进程而非任务状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人奖励模型（RRM）虽用基础模型替代逐任务奖励工程，但同名方法输出回答的问题不同，且现有评估（与演示顺序一致性、下游策略收益）无法判断奖励是否追踪任务完成度。

Method: 按原生输出回答的问题将114种方法归为Outcome、Absolute Progress、Relative、Temporal Value、Hybrid五类；分析模型设计、监督数据来源与评估实践；构建含1,117段真实机器人视频及任务状态和相机证据标签的RRM-Bench-1K，设计四个对比套件分离被评条件与常见线索；对27个评估器进行统一研究。

Result: 最强最终分数足以用于整体执行排序与筛选；但密集信用更贴近执行进程而非任务状态，且没有评估器能在决定性视角缺失时发出信号。

Conclusion: 奖励模型所需保真度取决于其驱动的决策；未来方向包括真实失败监督和区分任务状态与时序的指标，并开源RRM-Hub以统一应用14种现有RRM并测试新评估器。

Abstract: Robotic reward models (RRMs) use foundation models to replace per-task reward engineering with reusable, task-conditioned evaluation. Yet under one name, their outputs answer different questions, from whether a task succeeded to which execution is better. This survey organizes 114 methods into five semantic families by the question their native outputs answer: Outcome, Absolute Progress, Relative, Temporal Value, and Hybrid. Within this taxonomy, we review how model design determines what an evaluator can judge, how raw experience becomes supervision, and how existing evaluation practice tests the result. The review exposes a gap: agreement with demonstration order and downstream policy gains do not show whether a reward tracks task fulfillment. We therefore introduce RRM-Bench-1K, 1,117 real-robot videos of closed-loop executions with task-state and camera-evidence labels, and four contrast suites that separate the credited condition from its usual cues. A unified study of 27 evaluators shows that the strongest final scores rank whole executions well enough for selection and filtering. Dense credit, however, follows the progression of an execution more closely than its task state, and no evaluator signals when the decisive view is missing. How much fidelity a reward model needs therefore depends on the decision it drives. We outline directions from real-failure supervision to metrics that separate task state from chronology, and open-source RRM-Hub, a toolbox in which 14 existing RRMs can be readily applied and new evaluators tested on the same capabilities.

</details>


### [96] [Selective Sensor Acquisition for Multimodal Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20730)
*Margaux Edwards,Tobias Fischer,Michael Milford*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出选择性可控的传感器采集方法，仅在主传感器性能估计较差时才获取第二传感器，并通过在线阈值调整实现用户指定的第二传感器使用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态位置识别系统通常对每个查询都刚性读取并处理所有传感器，即使主传感器已足够，这会增加能耗、影响第二传感器更新率，并在被动隐身等场景中带来问题。

Method: 设计选择性、可控的传感器采集机制：用户指定第二传感器使用率，系统根据主传感器近期性能在线调整阈值，仅当主传感器估计性能足够差时才采集额外传感器；该方法只需PR方法产生匹配分数，适用于单帧和序列PR。

Result: 在多个数据集、PR描述子和传感器组合上，使用第二传感器仅25%查询时恢复55%的可用PR性能增益，使用50%查询时恢复81%；并分析了替代采集决策机制、使用率控制方法，以及第二传感器强于、相当或弱于主传感器时的性能。

Conclusion: 选择性传感器采集可在显著降低第二传感器使用率的同时保留大部分多模态PR性能增益，且无需针对传感器模态、PR方法或环境进行校准。

Abstract: Multimodal Place Recognition (PR) can improve robustness, but most systems rigidly read and process every sensor for every query, even when the primary sensor is sufficient, with implications for energy consumption, second sensor update rate, and domain-specific considerations such as passive stealth. We introduce selective, controllable sensor acquisition, where a user specifies a desired second sensor usage rate and the system acquires the additional sensor only when the primary sensor's estimated performance is sufficiently poor. An online threshold adjustment achieves the requested usage rate by adapting to recent primary sensor performance, directing additional sensing towards queries where fusion is most likely to help without calibration specific to sensor modality, PR method, or environment. The approach requires only a PR method that produces a match score and applies to both single-frame and sequence-based PR. Our system, across multiple datasets, PR descriptors, and sensor combinations, recovers 55% of the available PR performance gain while using the second sensor on only 25% of queries, and 81% while using it on 50%. We further analyse alternative acquisition decision mechanisms and usage-rate control methods, and examine performance when the second sensor is stronger than, comparable to, or weaker than the primary sensor.

</details>


### [97] [OrthoMark: Place Recognition with Point-Anchored Orthographic Height Mark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20570)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的LiDAR地点识别方法，用BEV极坐标傅里叶谱检索、相位相关估计位姿，并用点锚定高度列验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Z轴视角下重力对齐扫描是场景正射图，重访导致平面刚体运动，极坐标谱对此不变而相位相关可恢复；但垂直视场随距离截断近处结构，使高度在全局检索中误导、在对齐后逐格比较时才具决定性。

Method: 基于“两扫描只能通过同一几何表面计算的feature比较”原则：用BEV的平移旋转不变极坐标谱检索候选（每关键帧1.9KB）；用相位相关估计平面位姿；为每个BEV单元保留其回波高度的16位掩码，作为点锚定高度列评分，并按两扫描距离计算传感范围期望重叠加权。

Result: 在KITTI开发序列上，无3D配准的免训练方法中AP最高；跨机器人全部多机器人序列上匹配或超越RING++，描述子大小仅为其1/350；所有测试中闭合的机器人间回环比学习型BEVPlace++更多。

Conclusion: 垂直高度信息在验证阶段有益、在检索阶段有害；所提免训练方法在单机器人与多机器人场景均表现优异且描述子极紧凑。

Abstract: LiDAR place recognition provides the loop closures of Simultaneous Localization and Mapping(SLAM). Perspective from Z-axis, a gravity-aligned scan is an orthographic map of the scene, and a revisit from another position and heading moves this map rigidly in the plane, a motion that a polar Fourier spectrum ignores and phase correlation recovers. Along the vertical, the heights of the returns describe the structures, but which heights are observed depends on the range, because the sensor's vertical field of view cuts nearby structures low; height thus misleads a global key, yet is decisive once two scans are aligned and compared cell by cell where they overlap. We present \method, a training-free method built on one principle: two LiDAR scans can only be compared through features computed from the same geometric surfaces in both scans. \method retrieves candidates with a translation- and rotation-invariant polar spectrum of the bird's-eye view (BEV), 1.9,KB per keyframe, and verifies each candidate by estimating its planar pose through phase correlation and scoring it with \emph{point-anchored height columns}: every BEV cell keeps a 16-bit mask of the heights of its own returns; within a session, the score is weighted by the expected overlap of the two scans' sensing ranges, computed from their distance. We show that this vertical link pays off in verification but hurts in retrieval, where what a height slice contains depends on the range to the structure. For single robot, \method has the highest AP of the training-free methods without 3D registration on the KITTI development sequences; across robots, on all multi-robot sequences, it matches or exceeds RING++ at 1/350 of its descriptor size and closes more inter-robot loops than the learned BEVPlace++ on all tests.

</details>


### [98] [Vision-Language Grounded Semantic Navigation for Autonomous Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20558)
*Ravindran Vasoor Saravanan,Chittin Varunav Ravi,Madhumita Singh,Thirulok Sundar Mohan Rasu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一个基于ROS2的端到端视觉语言导航系统，在仿真中结合前沿探索、Grounding DINO开放词汇检测和语义建图，成功执行自然语言导航指令，但受限于检测和定位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人需要理解自然语言指令并在非结构化环境中导航，但将开放词汇视觉感知与经典机器人建图导航流程集成仍具挑战。

Method: 构建完整的ROS2系统，集成基于前沿的探索、使用Grounding DINO的开放词汇目标检测、基于深度的三维定位和语义建图；系统接收自然语言指令，从RGB图像检测候选目标并估计世界坐标，生成导航目标由Nav2执行；在Gazebo室内环境中用TurtleBot3机器人测试。

Result: 系统自主探索房间，检测到5个目标物体中的3个，并成功执行了两条自然语言导航指令，且无需任务特定训练；性能受检测不确定性、深度定位误差和探索覆盖不完整限制。

Conclusion: 开放词汇接地模型可集成到经典导航流程中，展示了轻量级纯CPU架构下视觉语言接地导航系统的可行性与当前局限。

Abstract: Language-guided navigation enables mobile robots to interpret natural language commands and navigate toward user-specified targets in unstructured environments. However, integrating open-vocabulary visual perception with classical robotics pipelines for mapping and navigation remains a challenging problem. In this work, we present a complete ROS2-based system that combines frontier-based exploration, open-vocabulary object detection using Grounding DINO, depth-based 3D localization, and semantic mapping to enable end-to-end vision-language grounded navigation in simulation. The system accepts natural language commands such as ``go to the yellow box,'' detects candidate objects from RGB images using text prompts, estimates their positions in the world frame, and generates navigation goals executed by the Nav2 stack. In a Gazebo-based indoor environment with a TurtleBot3 robot, the system autonomously explored the room, detected three of the five target objects, and successfully executed both natural language navigation commands tested, without task-specific training. The results indicate that open-vocabulary grounding models can be integrated into classical navigation pipelines, though performance is limited by detection uncertainty, depth-based localization error, and incomplete exploration coverage. These findings highlight both the feasibility and current limitations of deploying vision-language grounded navigation systems using lightweight, CPU-only architectures.

</details>


### [99] [AsymEEF: Bimanual Manipulation with Asymmetric End Effectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20557)
*Xiaokang Sun,Ningyue Wang,Kunqi Xu,Guangqi Jiang,Jianglong Ye,Yong Wu,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AsymEEF提出一种跨末端执行器演示生成与统一双臂策略学习框架，可将少量源数据迁移至异构双臂末端执行器组合，无需额外遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作演示采集成本高且难以在不同运动学与动作空间的末端执行器间复用，双臂操作中每对末端执行器往往需重新采集数据。

Method: 利用运动学教师锚点提供几何监督，稀疏真实世界X-Calib锚点校正抓取执行差异；共享编码器将腕部位姿与执行器状态映射到形态无关潜空间，形态条件解码器重建目标指令；生成轨迹经回放过滤保证任务级可执行性，并支持跨多末端器对的统一双臂视觉运动策略训练。

Result: 在四个仿真与六个真实任务中，回放成功率优于适配迁移基线，仿真中平均回放有效扩展增益达16.6倍，并在真实机器人上通过下游策略训练进行评估。

Conclusion: AsymEEF能有效生成可执行的跨末端执行器演示并支持统一双臂策略学习，显著降低双臂数据采集负担并提升演示复用性。

Abstract: Teleoperated demonstrations are costly to collect and rarely reusable across end-effectors with different kinematics and action spaces. This burden grows in bimanual manipulation, where each end-effector pair may require new data. We present AsymEEF, a framework for executable cross-end-effector demonstration generation and unified bimanual policy learning. Given a small source dataset, AsymEEF transfers demonstrations to heterogeneous combinations of two-, three-, and five-fingered end-effectors without additional teleoperation. Kinematic teacher anchors provide geometric supervision, while sparse real-world X-Calib anchors correct discrepancies in grasp execution. A shared encoder maps wrist poses and actuator states to a morphology-agnostic latent space, and a morphology-conditioned decoder reconstructs target commands. Generated trajectories undergo replay filtering for task-level executability. The representation also supports a unified bimanual visuomotor policy trained across multiple pairs. Across four simulated and six real-world tasks, AsymEEF improves replay success over adapted transfer baselines, achieves a mean replay-valid expansion gain of 16.6 times in simulation, and is evaluated through downstream policy training on real robots. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [100] [Prompt Re-Specification and Target-Mask Availability in Mask-Conditioned Visual Reinforcement Learning for Moving-Platform Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20075)
*Ho Tim Tsui,Jongyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究检验二值目标掩码与文本提示驱动的SAM 3检测器能否让固定权重视觉强化学习策略适应外观不同的着陆平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉强化学习无人机着陆策略在训练与测试平台外观不一致时性能急剧下降；二值目标掩码可去除大部分像素级外观信息而保留目标空间表示，并允许用文本提示指定目标。

Method: 在仿真中，用冻结权重的基于掩码的DrQ-v2策略，仅通过修改其微调SAM 3检测器的文本提示来应对新平台；另设使用真值掩码训练的策略实验，比较补全缺失掩码与掩码质心、面积修正的效果，并跟踪平台随时间变化。

Result: 香蕉主题平台使着陆成功率从75.6%降至12.0%，原提示下SAM 3几乎检测不到；修改提示后无需更新权重即恢复至68.4%。补全平台可见时缺失的掩码比质心和面积修正更能提升着陆；时间跟踪提供掩码更频繁，甚至在平台被确认不在渲染图像中后仍继续产生掩码，但未确立着陆成功率的可靠提升。

Conclusion: 能否向策略提供可用掩码似乎是该方法的重要限制因素；仅靠提示调整可在不更新权重下显著恢复性能，但掩码供给的可靠性仍是关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Visual reinforcement learning (RL) policies that land a drone on a moving platform can fail when the landing pad looks different from the one used in training. Using binary target masks as the visual input removes most pixel-level appearance information while preserving the spatial representation of the target, and lets a text prompt specify the target. In simulation, we test whether a frozen (fixed-weight) mask-based DrQ-v2 policy can handle a new pad when only the prompt of its fine-tuned SAM 3 detector changes. A banana-themed pad lowered landing success from 75.6% to 12.0%, and SAM 3 rarely detected it under the original prompt. A revised prompt, chosen after testing several candidates, restored success to 68.4% with no weight updates. In a separate experiment with a policy trained on ground-truth masks, filling in masks that were missing while the pad was visible helped landing far more than the tested mask-centroid and area corrections. Tracking the pad over time supplied masks more often and also kept producing them after the pad was confirmed absent from the rendered image, but it did not establish a reliable improvement in landing success. Whether a usable mask reaches the policy at all thus appears to be an important limiting factor for this approach.

</details>


### [101] [PlanDiv: Test-Time Scaling with Latent Plans for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20010)
*Hanbo Zhang,Lianlei Shan,Liuhaichen Yang,Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PlanDiv通过引入潜规划假设使测试时动作候选在“计划”层面多样化，而非仅噪声重采样，显著提升VLA策略在长程任务与多解任务上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时缩放通过采样多个动作候选并选优来改进VLA策略，但作者发现从单一解码器重采样时收益迅速递减：候选间差异主要来自噪声而非计划差异，在CALVIN上候选数从4增至8仅提升0.06-0.07个完成任务数。

Method: 提出PlanDiv动作头：轻量规划器生成K个潜规划假设，共享的掩码扩散执行器在一次骨干前向中为每个规划解码一个动作块，策略执行自身似然排名最高的动作块；规划仅从示范中学习，无需规划标签或奖励。

Result: 在固定骨干、数据和训练配方下，CALVIN ABC→D五任务链成功率从56.9%提升至69.9%，而自回归、无规划或同规划重采样的8候选最多达64.9%；LIBERO-Long上4个规划消除40%的单候选失败（匹配重采样最多21%）；真实Franka机械臂上单解任务与单输出OpenVLA-OFT+基线持平，双解任务54次试验成功51次对40次。

Conclusion: 候选多样性应来自计划层面的差异而非噪声重采样；PlanDiv以无监督方式学习潜规划，在长程多任务和多解任务上带来实质增益，且收益源于规划本身而非更多采样。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves vision-language-action (VLA) policies by drawing several action candidates and executing the best one. We find that its returns diminish quickly when the candidates are resampled from one decoder: they differ by noise rather than by plan, and for each of three resampling sources, going from four to eight candidates adds only 0.06-0.07 to the average number of completed tasks on CALVIN. We introduce PlanDiv, an action head that makes the candidates differ by plan: a lightweight planner proposes K latent plan hypotheses, a shared masked-diffusion executor decodes one action chunk per plan from a single backbone pass, and the policy executes the chunk that its own likelihood ranks highest. The plans are learned from demonstrations alone, without plan labels or rewards. With the backbone, data and training recipe held fixed, eight plan-conditioned candidates raise five-task chain success on CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D from 56.9% to 69.9%, while eight candidates from autoregressive, plan-free or same-plan resampling reach at most 64.9%. The same-plan baseline is PlanDiv's own checkpoint with the plan held fixed, so this gap comes from the plans, not from drawing more samples. On LIBERO-Long, four plans remove 40% of single-candidate failures, against at most 21% for matched resampling. On a real Franka arm the gain appears where a task admits more than one solution: PlanDiv is on par with a single-output OpenVLA-OFT+ baseline on a one-solution task and succeeds in 51 of 54 trials against 40 of 54 on two tasks with two solutions.

</details>


### [102] [Lao Siji: Adapting openpilot to Closed-Loop End-to-End Driving in Neural Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19922)
*Chengzhi Gao,Yongzhi Liu,Jinchang Xu,Weichao Zhuang,Guodong Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出Lao Siji端到端驾驶策略，在2026年NVIDIA AlpaSim E2E挑战赛中获得第三名，通过微调openpilot生产模型而非从头训练基础模型实现，训练仅需1 GPU小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶方案多基于通用视觉基础模型从头训练，成本高；作者希望验证能否以极低训练成本基于开源L2生产模型适配出有竞争力的驾驶策略。

Method: 冻结openpilot视觉编码器，用自车状态适配器和路线记忆微调策略层；在神经重建场景的闭环推演中，使用特权地图教师监督横向自我修正、车身范围损失保证道路与障碍物净空，并蒸馏基座模型其余输出以保留跟车能力；纵向安全依赖基座模型前车估计加规则速度层。

Result: 在AlpaSim挑战赛排名第三，优于多数基础模型方案；消融显示自我修正样本贡献主要增益，净空损失可在无教师处替代；更长训练、模仿人类日志、人类日志速度目标无增益或有害；无需额外训练在HUGSIM和Odyssey上亦居前列；训练仅1 GPU小时，权重相对发布模型偏移3.9%。

Conclusion: 基于开源生产模型进行轻量微调是构建高性能端到端驾驶策略的高效路径，自我修正监督与净空损失是关键，而模仿人类日志与更长训练并非必要。

Abstract: We present Lao Siji, an end-to-end driving policy that ranks third in the 2026 NVIDIA AlpaSim E2E Driving Challenge, ahead of most entries built on foundation models. Instead of training from scratch on a general-purpose vision backbone, we adapt openpilot, an open-source L2 production model. We freeze its vision encoder and fine-tune the policy layers with an ego-state adapter and a route memory. On closed-loop rollouts in neural-reconstructed scenes, we supervise the policy with a privileged map teacher for lateral self-correction and a body-extent loss for road and obstacle clearance, while distilling the base model's remaining outputs to retain car following. Ablations show that self-correction samples carry the gain, while the clearance loss substitutes for them where no teacher exists. Longitudinal safety instead rests on the base model's lead-vehicle estimate behind a rule-based speed layer. Longer training, imitating human logs, and human-log speed targets yield either no measurable gain or adverse effects. Without further training, Lao Siji also places among the leaders on HUGSIM and Odyssey. The final training takes only one GPU-hour and shifts the weights by 3.9% from the shipped model. Code will be released after review.

</details>


### [103] [JEPA4R: Embodied JEPA World Models with 4D Point Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19771)
*Jiarui Dong,Zhiheng Liang,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Heejeong Nam,Haoyu Chen,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JEPA4R 是一个基于 JEPA 的世界模型，它直接观察单深度相机的点云，在隐空间中预测和规划，并通过策略头作用于冻结的隐表示（称为 4D 点规划）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 JEPA 世界模型存在两个问题：二维图像使操作所依赖的度量几何隐含且与外观纠缠；模型想象的内容不可见，且多仅在仿真中用短期隐规划评估。

Method: 提出 JEPA4R：以单深度相机点云为输入，在点云隐空间中预测和规划；在冻结的世界模型隐表示上训练策略头；并用解码器将想象结果可视化为 4D 点云。

Result: 解码器展示的 4D 点云在 200 步内平均点误差小于 1.1 厘米；在 OGBench 的 Cube-Single、Cube-Double、Cube-Triple 和 Scene 上优于像素 JEPA 模型；在 MetaWorld 和 Adroit Relocate 上分别以 98.4% 和 88.0% 排名第一，超过第二名 R3M 的 96.0% 和 65.3%；仅在数字孪生中训练，JEPA4R 及其策略头即可在真实机械臂上零样本运行，兼容夹爪和灵巧手。

Conclusion: JEPA4R 通过在点云隐空间中进行 4D 点规划，解决了度量几何与外观纠缠及想象不可见的问题，并在多个基准和真实机器人上取得领先性能。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) world models predict future embeddings instead of pixels and plan in latent space. Two challenges remain: 2D images leave the metric geometry that manipulation acts on implicit and entangled with appearance, and what these models imagine stays hidden, as they are mostly judged in simulation by short-horizon latent planning. We present JEPA4R, a JEPA world model that observes single-depth-camera point clouds, predicts and plans in their latent space, and acts through a policy head on its frozen latents; we call this 4D point planning. A decoder on the frozen world model shows what it imagines as 4D point clouds, with a mean point error within 1.1 cm over 200 steps. Under the policy-learning protocols of OGBench and CortexBench, JEPA4R leads pixel JEPA models on OGBench Cube-Single, Cube-Double, Cube-Triple and Scene, and ranks first on MetaWorld and Adroit Relocate with 98.4% and 88.0%, above the runner-up, R3M, at 96.0% and 65.3%. Trained only in a digital twin, JEPA4R and its policy heads run zero-shot on the real arm with both a gripper and a dexterous hand.

</details>


### [104] [Cache or Denoise Less? Residual Reuse in a Flow-Matching World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19709)
*Abhinav Gupta,Nissan Pow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在6B参数的世界动作模型Fast-WAM上比较跨周期缓存与减少去噪步数，发现两步无缓存即可匹敌十步成功率（500个初始状态上差距≤2个百分点），缓存提速空间很小（最多节省11–14%单次调用时间），且缓存策略对采样器与噪声设置高度敏感、容易失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配机器人策略每次动作块生成都需要多次网络评估，跨周期缓存（如C³ache）试图复用之前重规划调用的计算，但其是否优于简单减少去噪步数尚不明确。

Method: 在LIBERO-Goal任务上使用6B参数的Fast-WAM世界动作模型，对比无缓存少步数与C³ache缓存策略；测量RTX A6000上PyTorch默认compile模式下的重规划调用耗时，并建立成本模型分析缓存上限；进一步考察缓存步数覆盖流路径比例与成功率的关系，以及每次调用重采样噪声的影响。

Result: 两步无缓存达到十步的成功率（500个初始状态非劣效，差距在2个百分点内，针对单一检查点与噪声种子）；重规划调用从202 ms降至92 ms；保留两步新鲜步的缓存不可能快于两步无缓存；每四次调用刷新第一步的缓存按成本模型最多节省11–14%；在Fast-WAM当前时间偏移下C³ache八步缓存覆盖80%路径（而非44%），成功率63%对95%；覆盖≥60%路径的缓存在每次调用重采样噪声时损失14–40个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果支持以更少步数作为基线，并强调必须在实际部署的采样器与噪声设置下测试复用策略；作者发布了评估框架、日志与检查清单。

Abstract: Flow-matching robot policies generate each action chunk through repeated network evaluations, one per denoising step. Cross-cycle caches such as C$^3$ache reuse computation from earlier replanning calls, but whether they beat simply taking fewer steps is unclear. We compare the two in Fast-WAM, a 6B-parameter world action model, on LIBERO-Goal. Two uncached steps keep the success of ten: on all 500 initial states they are non-inferior within two percentage points, for one checkpoint and noise seed. Under PyTorch's default compile mode, they also cut a replanning call on an RTX A6000 from 202 to 92 ms. This leaves little room for caching. A cache that keeps two fresh steps cannot be faster than two uncached steps: every call still encodes the images, runs the video expert and computes those two steps. Caching the first of two steps, refreshed every fourth call, saves at most 11--14% of a call by our cost model. Cached policies are also fragile. Their losses tracked the share of the flow path the cached steps cover: under Fast-WAM's current time shift, C$^3$ache's eight cached steps cover 80% of the path instead of 44%, and the cached policy succeeds on 63% of states against 95% uncached. Resampling the noise on every call cost caches covering 60% or more of the path 14--40 points. These results argue for fewer-step baselines and for testing reuse under the deployed sampler and noise settings. We release our evaluation harness, logs and a checklist.

</details>


### [105] [VLN-Flash: Parallel Action Decoding with Adaptive Execution for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19287)
*Ruizhe Xie,Honghua Chen,Binglin Wang,Tong Guo,Xu Tang,Puti Yan,Shaoxiong Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLN-Flash是一种免训练解码器，通过单次前向传播并行评估完整动作前缀树，在保持条件动作分布的同时加速视觉语言导航并提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归动作解码中机器人需等待一连串依赖预测，速度慢；而动作词表小，使并行评分所有短时域规划成为可能。

Method: 提出免训练解码器，利用分支特定注意力和匹配的token位置，在精确算术下保持原策略对每个前缀的条件动作分布；以逐步贪心前缀与各深度最强替代方案间的分数差决定执行动作数，并执行所选深度联合分数最高的规划后再观测。

Result: 在Uni-NaVid和StreamVLN上动作解码较原生自回归加速近3倍；在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上成功率和SPL均提升，成功率最高提升2.7个百分点；在Unitree GO2上提升导航成功率，扣除网络传输时间后平均动作间等待减少约160毫秒。

Conclusion: VLN-Flash在不需训练的情况下同时实现解码加速与导航性能提升，适用于真实机器人部署。

Abstract: When a vision-and-language navigation policy generates an action sequence autoregressively, the robot waits for a chain of dependent predictions. A small discrete action vocabulary makes it possible to score all short-horizon plans in parallel. We introduce VLN-Flash, a training-free decoder that evaluates the complete action-prefix tree in a single forward pass. Branch-specific attention and matched token positions preserve the original policy's conditional action distribution for each prefix in exact arithmetic. We use the score margin between the stepwise-greedy prefix and its strongest alternative at each depth to decide how many actions to execute. The robot then executes the plan with the highest joint score at the chosen depth before observing again. On matched inputs, VLN-Flash achieves a nearly $3\times$ speedup in action decoding over native autoregression on Uni-NaVid and StreamVLN. Compared with the native autoregressive decoders, it improves success rate and success weighted by path length (SPL) on R2R-CE and RxR-CE, with success gains up to 2.7 percentage points. On a Unitree GO2, VLN-Flash improves navigation success and cuts the mean wait between actions by about 160 ms after subtracting network transmission time.

</details>


### [106] [Technical Report of Auriga's 1:10 Scale Autonomous Vehicle Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18855)
*Marya Sabouri Dodaran,Ali Taheri Garakani,Mandana Shabanzadeh Nasrollah-Abadi,Yousef Ashrafi,Mohammad Mahdi Azimi,Erfan Nekouei Ghahfarokhi,Amirhossein Kheiri Holighi,Seyyed Sobhan Hosseini Hajibakandeh,Matin Jamshidi,Amirhossein Gholizadeh Behbahani,Mohammad Hossein Moaiyeri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该报告介绍了Shahid Beheshti大学机器人实验室开发的1:10比例自动驾驶车辆Auriga Robotics的设计与实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个模块化研究平台，用于未来自动驾驶研究，并在类城市场景中验证自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Method: 平台集成定制机械底盘、基于ESP32的分布式嵌入式系统（负责传感器接口、实时数据采集和执行器控制）以及Jetson Orin NX模块（负责基于深度学习的车道、标志和交通灯检测、决策与轨迹控制）；系统通过结构化UART链路通信，软件栈包括ROS、模块化固件以及Stanley、PID和Pure Pursuit控制算法。

Result: 该车辆在结构化驾驶场景中实现了稳定的自主导航，并在类城市环境中展现出鲁棒的性能和可靠性。

Conclusion: 该系统可作为未来自动驾驶研究的模块化研究平台。

Abstract: This report presents the design and implementation of Auriga Robotics' 1:10 scale autonomous vehicle, developed at Shahid Beheshti University's Robotics and Intelligent Automation Laboratory. The platform integrates a custom mechanical chassis, a distributed embedded system based on ESP32 microcontrollers, and a Jetson Orin NX module for high-level perception and control. The embedded layer manages sensor interfacing, real-time data acquisition, and actuator control, while the Jetson handles environment perception through deep-learning--based lane, sign, and traffic light detection, as well as decision-making and trajectory control. System communication is achieved via structured UART links, ensuring synchronized operation between perception and control subsystems. The vehicle's software stack incorporates ROS, modular firmware design, and multiple control algorithms---Stanley, PID, and Pure Pursuit---to achieve stable autonomous navigation in structured driving scenarios. The overall system demonstrates robust performance and reliability in urban-like environments, serving as a modular research platform for future autonomous driving studies.

</details>


### [107] [Robustness as a Transferable Skill: Cross-Task Adversarial Defense for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18702)
*Jinquan Zhang,Run Yang,Dongfu Yin,Qiang Lai,Yufeng Yan,Pengshuai Yin,F. Richard Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究跨任务的可迁移对抗鲁棒性：源操作任务上学到的防御更新无需目标任务侧对抗训练即可保护独立专门化的VLA策略，并提出SN-TRV向量组合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略易受对抗视觉扰动攻击，而现有防御通常需要针对特定任务进行对抗微调，成本高且难以迁移，因此作者探究源任务上学到的鲁棒性能否迁移到独立训练的目标策略上。

Method: 首先验证单个源防御更新即可提升留出任务的鲁棒性，确立跨任务防御复用；随后提出Source-Norm Transferable Robustness Vector（SN-TRV），在保留源更新均值方向的同时用源更新范数校准其幅度，并将该更新直接应用于目标视觉编码器，无需目标对抗数据、目标侧梯度优化或额外推理模块。

Result: 在三种VLA骨干网络、固定与自适应攻击以及不同源防御方案下进行评估，结果表明该方法优于朴素平均，并揭示了攻击覆盖范围的局限；在物理PiPER试验中，面对打印对抗补丁的平均成功率从20.3%提升至45.8%。

Conclusion: 源任务学到的防御可以跨任务复用并提升目标VLA策略的对抗鲁棒性，SN-TRV提供了一种无需目标侧对抗训练的有效组合与迁移方案，但攻击覆盖仍存在局限。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies are vulnerable to adversarial visual perturbations, while defenses often require task-specific adversarial fine-tuning. We investigate whether robustness learned on source manipulation tasks can protect independently specialized target policies without target-side adversarial training. We first show that even a single source defense update improves robustness on held-out tasks, establishing cross-task defense reuse independently of multi-source aggregation. To combine source updates, we introduce Source-Norm Transferable Robustness Vector (SN-TRV), which retains the direction of their mean while calibrating its magnitude using source-update norms. The resulting update is applied to the target visual encoder without target adversarial data, target-side gradient optimization, or additional inference modules. Evaluations across three VLA backbones, fixed and adaptive attacks, and different source defense recipes show improved robustness over naive averaging and identify limits in attack coverage. Physical PiPER trials increase average success under printed adversarial patches from 20.3% to 45.8%.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [A Matrix with Steps: An Episodic and Procedural Memory Method for Tool-Using Agents and Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20230)
*Jürgen Paulhart,Peter Schüller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种为生产级智能体平台构建的记忆系统，通过外部验证确保只从确认成功的结果中学习，并用关联矩阵与有序步骤记录分离存储，解决智能体重复失败和误信假成功的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体调用工具和机器人执行任务时存在两个缺陷：重复失败过的操作，以及从自认为成功但实际未成功的运行中学习，导致不可靠的行为。

Method: 将每次运行记录为情节，仅当结果经外部检查（测试、工具结果或人工）确认时才视为成功，智能体自述不算。学习内容存入双部分记忆：用delta规则写入的关联矩阵，用于根据情境找到适用过程；以及有序、可寻址的步骤记录，回答给定步骤之后是什么（步骤是智能体目标而非动作）。矩阵求和无顺序，仅作查找器；顺序、结果计数和删除由记录管理。还描述了结果存储、情节召回用于解题以及记忆的文件格式。

Result: 仿真中，自判成功并自认完成即停止的机器人报告96%任务完成但仅解决35%；采用外部验证后解决79%。在10万条合成情节（磁盘457MB）的存储中，最旧情节仍最先被找到，未返回他人记忆中的情节，仅由模型担保的成功从未被召回；查找中位耗时笔记本16ms、服务器节点40ms。任务结果测量至约1万条确认记录，精确记录使每次重访首次尝试即解决。

Conclusion: 通过外部验证和分离式记忆结构，智能体可避免从假成功中学习并可靠召回经过确认的解题过程，实现高效且可扩展的任务复用。

Abstract: Agents that call tools and robots that execute tasks are challenged by two shortcomings: they repeat what failed before, and they learn from runs that they only believe succeeded. We describe the construction of a memory that addresses both, built for a production agent platform. Every run is recorded as an episode, and only an outcome confirmed by an external check, a test, a tool result or a person counts; the agent's own claim never does. What is learned from episodes is kept in a memory with two parts: an associative matrix, written with the delta rule, which finds the procedure that applies to a situation; and a section of ordered, addressable step records that answers what comes after a given step, where a step is a goal of the agent, not a motion. Because the matrix sums its associations, it keeps no order and cannot return or delete a single one; it serves as the finder, and order, outcome counts and deletion live in the records. We describe how execution outcomes are stored, how episodes are recalled and used for solving tasks, and the file format that stores the memory. In simulation, a robot that judged its own success and stopped when it believed it was done reported $96\%$ of its tasks as done and solved $35\%$; with external verification it solved $79%$. In a store of $100000$ synthetic episodes ($457$MB on disk), the oldest episode was still found first, no episode from another person's memory was returned, and a success vouched for only by the model was never recalled; a lookup took $16$ms on a laptop and $40$ms on a server node (median). Task outcomes were measured up to about $10000$ confirmed records, where the exact records solved every revisit at the first attempt.

</details>


### [109] [NI-ACT: A Neural Intent Interface for Brain-Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19332)
*Yinghao Li,WeiHsien Lee,Zeyu Zhang,Canyang Zhao,Ke Fang,Xing Li,Bing Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出分层脑-具身框架NI-ACT，将运动皮层群体活动作为持久意图令牌，配合视觉-本体感知策略闭环抓取，验证了意图表示、读取位置与时机对具身控制效果的决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统皮层内脑机接口让用户始终处于快速感觉运动闭环中，每个接近、修正和抓取动作都需神经即时指定；研究探索皮层群体活动能否作为学习型具身策略的任务级条件，摆脱符号指令与手工技能库。

Method: 构建NI-ACT分层脑-具身框架：一次性读取短暂运动皮层信号作为持久意图，再由视觉运动策略基于视觉与本体感知闭合感知-动作环；采用反事实抓取任务（场景无法指定目标），比较完整群体潜变量意图令牌与one-hot解码方向；在双阵列记录中对比PMd与M1意图（匹配读取窗口），并测试仅用go cue前PMd预备活动驱动抓取。

Result: 完整群体潜变量意图令牌将132个留出场景的抓取完成率从69%提升至81%，而仅连续方向未优于one-hot标签；双阵列会话中PMd意图完成率77%高于M1的53%，传统post-cue M1读取为42%；中心-外任务中仅用go cue前PMd预备活动即可驱动完整抓取，单次记录中完成率甚至高于同一动物post-cue读取。

Conclusion: 神经信号的表示方式、读取位置与读取时机共同决定其向具身智能体传递运动目标的有效性；持久意图令牌加具身策略的范式可行且优于传统即时闭环解码。

Abstract: Intracortical brain-computer interfaces can drive robotic arms, but they keep the user inside the fast sensorimotor loop: every approach, correction, and grasp must be neurally specified. Whether intracortical population activity can instead serve as a task-level condition for a learned embodied policy, without symbolic commands or hand-designed skill libraries, remains unknown. NI-ACT addresses this question with a hierarchical brain-embodied framework: a brief motor-cortical signal is read once as a persistent intent, and a visuomotor policy then closes the perception-action loop from vision and proprioception. In a counterfactual grasping task where the scene cannot specify the target, the neural intent alone determined which object the robot grasped; replacing the intent changed the behavior accordingly. Passing the full population latent as a dedicated intent token, rather than a one-hot decoded direction, raised grasp completion from 69% to 81% over 132 held-out scenes, whereas a continuous direction alone did not improve on a one-hot label. Where the signal was read also mattered: in one dual-array session, with the readout window matched, premotor (PMd) intent completed more grasps than primary motor (M1) intent (77% vs. 53%), and the conventional post-cue M1 readout reached 42%. Preparatory activity was itself sufficient: in a center-out task, intent read only from PMd before the go cue (that is, before any movement) drove complete grasps, and in one recording it completed more grasps than the same animal's post-cue readout. These results show that how a neural signal is represented, and where and when it is read, jointly determine how effectively it conveys movement goals to an embodied agent.

</details>


### [110] [Bring Back Language to Actions: Restoring VLM capabilities in VLA Models via Parameter Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18891)
*Mohamed Salim Aissi,Olivier Sigaud,Laure Soulier,Nicolas Thome*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过SVD分解任务向量并学习标量混合系数，无需重训练即可合并VLM与其VLA衍生模型的参数，同时保留动作预测和视觉语言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人示范数据上微调预训练视觉语言模型得到的VLA模型会出现灾难性遗忘，严重削弱原VLM的语义和视觉 grounding 能力。

Method: 提取VLA与VLM权重之差（任务向量），用奇异值分解将其分解；对每个分量学习一个标量混合系数，控制保留多少VLA适配，并以联合保持动作预测和视觉语言能力为目标优化这些系数。

Result: 合并后的模型在动作预测和视觉语言两个维度上同时表现良好，持续优于已有的模型合并基线方法。

Conclusion: 无需重训练任一模型，仅通过分解任务向量并学习混合系数进行参数合并，即可有效缓解VLA微调导致的灾难性遗忘，兼顾动作预测与视觉语言能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) are obtained by fine-tuning pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on robot demonstration data, enabling action prediction from visual observations and language instructions. However, this fine-tuning induces catastrophic forgetting, substantially degrading the semantic and visual grounding capabilities of the original VLM. We address this by merging the parameters of a VLM and its VLA derivative without retraining either model. Concretely, we extract the task vector (the difference between VLA and VLM weights) and decompose it via Singular Value Decomposition. For each component, we learn a scalar mixing coefficient controlling how much of the VLA adaptation is retained, optimised to jointly preserve action prediction and vision-language capabilities. Experiments show that our merged model performs well on both axes simultaneously, consistently outperforming established merging baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Successive Convexification in Learned World Models for Safe Offline Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19466)
*Samuel Younger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将基于学习世界模型的安全规划建模为潜空间中的逐次凸规划（SCP），并在每个规划步施加学习到的离散时间控制障碍函数约束，同时通过平滑MaxMin激活和谱归一化层保证动力学光滑性，以约束线性化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的规划器在使用世界模型时只能通过打分或拒绝采样动作序列来处理安全约束，缺乏对可行性和最优性的显式保证，因此需要一种能提供可行性认证的安全规划方法。

Method: 在学习世界模型的潜空间中，将安全规划表述为SCP，并在整个规划时域的每一步将学习到的离散时间控制障碍函数作为约束；为使SCP适用，采用平滑MaxMin激活和谱归一化层构建网络，并用Lipschitz界和平滑宽度界定线性化误差；由于SCP是局部方法，从采样得到的初始计划出发进行求解。

Result: 在两个Safety-Gymnasium导航任务上，该规划器在相同实际耗时下比使用128倍样本的MPPI和CEM违反约束更少（但差异不显著），在更长时域下获得更高回报，且比无采样起点的SCP安全得多；SCP在Lipschitz有界模型上收敛更频繁，并能逐步报告计划是否为模型中规划问题的可行一阶最优KKT点，或未找到附近可行计划。

Conclusion: 将安全规划表述为潜空间SCP并显式施加控制障碍函数约束，可在保持竞争力的安全性的同时提供可行性/最优性认证，且从采样计划初始化对SCP的安全性和收敛性至关重要。

Abstract: World models learned from offline data allow an agent to plan without further interaction with the environment, but the sampling-based planners used with them handle safety constraints only by scoring or rejecting sampled action sequences.
