We instead pose safe planning in the latent space of a learned world model as a successive convex program (SCP), in which a learned discrete-time control barrier function is imposed as a constraint at every step of the horizon. SCP requires smooth dynamics, so we build the networks from smoothed MaxMin activations and spectrally normalised layers, and bound the resulting linearisation error in terms of the Lipschitz bound and the smoothing width. As SCP is a local method, we start it from a sampled plan. On two Safety-Gymnasium navigation tasks with offline data, this planner has fewer violations than MPPI and CEM drawing 128 times as many samples at equal wall-clock time in our main comparison, though not significantly, and earns greater return at longer horizons, and is much safer than SCP without a sampled start. SCP converged more often on Lipschitz-bounded models. Unlike sampling, it also reports at each step whether its plan is a feasible, first-order optimal KKT point of the planning problem in the model, or that no feasible plan was found nearby. Our implementation is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [112] [Fuzzy Explainable Continual Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20427)
*Peng Qin,Tao Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FECHRL方法，通过两阶段行为克隆与Takagi-Sugeno模糊评论家，在单一强化学习框架中统一持续学习稳定性与策略可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续机器人导航需策略持续适应新环境并保留旧知识，而可解释强化学习需通过策略交互学习有意义的行为表示；两者目标冲突：持续学习强调策略稳定性，可解释行为学习需要自适应探索，现有方法很少同时兼顾。

Method: 提出模糊可解释持续分层强化学习（FECHRL）：引入两阶段行为克隆策略，从强模仿引导逐步过渡到自主强化学习，以平衡快速策略适应与可解释行为演化；并设计基于Takagi-Sugeno模糊推理系统的模糊评论家，缓解模仿引导学习与自主探索切换时的值外推误差，提升持续训练稳定性。

Result: 在序列多场景导航任务上的实验表明，该方法在单一强化学习框架内有效统一了持续学习能力与策略可解释性，实现了稳定的知识保留和有意义的行为解释。

Conclusion: FECHRL能够同时兼顾持续学习中的策略稳定性与可解释行为学习所需的自适应探索，验证了统一持续学习与策略可解释性的可行性。

Abstract: Continual robot navigation requires reinforcement learning policies to continuously adapt to new environments while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Meanwhile, explainable reinforcement learning aims to learn meaningful behavioral representations through policy interaction. However, these objectives are inherently conflicting, as continual learning emphasizes policy stability whereas explainable behavior learning requires adaptive exploration. Existing methods rarely address both simultaneously. To resolve this issue, this paper proposes a Fuzzy Explainable Continual Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (FECHRL) method. A two-stage behavior cloning strategy is introduced to balance rapid policy adaptation and explainable behavior evolution by progressively transitioning from strong imitation guidance to autonomous reinforcement learning. Furthermore, a fuzzy critic based on a Takagi--Sugeno fuzzy inference system is proposed to alleviate value extrapolation errors during the transition between imitation-guided learning and autonomous exploration, thereby improving continual training stability. Experimental results on sequential multi-scenario navigation tasks demonstrate that the proposed method effectively unifies continual learning capability and policy explainability within a single reinforcement learning framework, achieving stable knowledge retention and meaningful behavioral explanation.

</details>


### [113] [QA-CMIF:Accurate and Lightweight Cross-Modal Latent Imputation and Fusion for Fault-Tolerant sEMG-Inertial Hand Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20124)
*Xinyu Tao,Shuang Huang,Yeyun Cai,Fang Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QA-CMIF框架，通过逐通道编码、软门控可靠性评分、置信加权残差旁路和跨注意力潜在空间修复，解决sEMG-惯性融合中部分通道损坏污染整体表征的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常佩戴中电极微位移、脱落和断线会使未知子集的sEMG通道出现突发伪迹、冲击或断路；现有融合网络在首层卷积混合所有通道，少数坏电极会污染整体潜在表征，而仅用门控衰减又会丢失区分细粒度手势所需的共激活线索。

Method: QA-CMIF对每个sEMG通道独立编码，用软门控评分其可靠性，并通过置信加权残差旁路保护完好通道；跨注意力修复器在潜在空间利用惯性流和剩余可靠sEMG通道修复退化通道，并采用坏通道对齐损失。

Result: 在Ninapro DB3、DB2、DB5共29名受试者的被试内评估中（17-52类手势），50%通道损坏下QA-CMIF较仅门控基线在三个17类队列提升1.29-2.65个百分点（配对t检验Holm校正p<0.002），在40类和52类任务上提升扩大至2.59-4.15个百分点，并超过损坏增强的常规融合4.61-19.85个百分点；参数量仅0.175-0.185M，2000 Hz和200 Hz设置下MAC为2.45-2.63M，较近期基线节省46%-55%参数和72%-81%计算量，单线程CPU 99百分位延迟1.44 ms。

Conclusion: QA-CMIF在通道损坏条件下显著优于门控基线和常规融合方法，且以更低参数量和计算量实现低延迟，适用于肌电假肢控制与可穿戴人机接口。

Abstract: Fusing surface electromyography (sEMG) with inertial measurements is widely used for myoelectric prosthesis control and wearable human-machine interfaces. In daily wear, however, socket micro-displacement, electrode lift-off, and wire failures often corrupt an unknown subset of sEMG channels through burst artifacts, lift-off shocks, or open-circuit dropouts. Existing fusion networks mix all channels in the first convolutional layer, so a few corrupted electrodes contaminate the entire latent representation, whereas gate-only attenuation of corrupted channels discards the co-activation cues needed to separate fine-grained gestures. We propose QA-CMIF, a quality-aware cross-modal latent imputation and fusion framework that encodes each sEMG channel independently, scores its reliability with a soft gate, and protects intact channels via a confidence-weighted residual bypass, while a cross-attention imputer repairs degraded channels in latent space using the inertial stream and remaining reliable sEMG channels under a bad-channel alignment loss. Across within-subject evaluations on 29 participants from Ninapro DB3, DB2, and DB5 spanning 17-52 gesture classes, QA-CMIF outperformed the gate-only baseline under 50% channel corruption by 1.29-2.65 percentage points, denoted pp, across all three 17-class cohorts with paired-t Holm-adjusted p<0.002, widened this margin to 2.59-4.15 pp on the 40- and 52-class tasks, and surpassed corruption-augmented conventional fusion by 4.61-19.85 pp. Requiring only 0.175-0.185M parameters and 2.45-2.63M multiply-accumulate operations across the 2000 Hz and 200 Hz setups, QA-CMIF saves 46%-55% in parameters and 72%-81% in compute over recent baselines with a 1.44 ms 99th-percentile single-thread CPU latency.

</details>


### [114] [Infrastructure-Based Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning with Attention for Highway Ramp Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20078)
*Chenhao Tong,Steffen Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer的多智能体强化学习匝道合流控制模型，通过路侧控制器收集全局CAV状态并用注意力编码器筛选相关车辆信息，在SUMO中实现最高车流、安全性和平滑度，并在动态需求与通信误差下保持无碰撞和高吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速公路匝道合流存在安全与通行效率问题；无协调时人类驾驶员和自利自动驾驶车辆易产生危险操作与拥堵；CAV与MARL虽可训练协同，但密集交通下有效观测困难：仅观测邻近车辆易局部最优，观测全局则引入大量无关信息。

Method: 提出Transformer-based MARL车辆控制模型：路侧基础设施控制器经V2I接收所有CAV状态并回传控制指令；设计基于注意力的观测编码器，使每辆车从全局交通状态中过滤相关车辆信息；在SUMO中与三个基线（仅邻近观测、全局信息编码、规则模型）对比评估。

Result: 所提模型取得最高交通流、安全性和平滑度；在四小时动态交通需求仿真中成功避免拥堵，其他基线在高流量下失败；存在人类驾驶车辆时仍有效；在理想通信条件下训练后，面对真实V2I通信误差仍保持无碰撞并维持吞吐；可视化多交通场景下自车对其他车辆分配的相关性权重。

Conclusion: 注意力机制可有效从全局交通状态中提取相关观测，使MARL匝道合流控制在效率、安全和平滑性上优于基线，并具备对混合交通与通信误差的鲁棒性。

Abstract: On-ramp merging is a typical safety and throughput problem on highways. Without proper coordination, human drivers and self-interested autonomous vehicles may make risky manoeuvres and cause accidents or congestion. With recent advances in connected autonomous vehicles (CAVs) and multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), vehicles can be trained to coordinate for safe and efficient merging. However, in dense traffic, forming an effective observation of the traffic state is challenging. For example, observing only the neighbouring vehicles may lead to locally optimal decisions, while observing the entire traffic can introduce much irrelevant information. In this paper, we propose a transformer-based MARL vehicle control model to address these problems. A roadside infrastructure controller receives the states of all CAVs via vehicle-to-infrastructure (V2I) communication and sends control commands back to them. An attention-based observation encoder is designed where each vehicle can filter the relevant vehicle information from the global traffic state. The proposed model is evaluated in SUMO against three baselines: a model that observes only the neighbouring vehicles, a model that encodes the global traffic information, and the rule-based SUMO model. The results show that our model achieves the highest traffic flow, safety and smoothness. Furthermore, in a four-hour simulation with dynamic traffic demand, our model successfully avoids traffic jams, while other baseline models fail at high traffic flow. We also show that our model remains effective when human-driven vehicles are present. We further show that our model, trained in ideal communication conditions, can still remain collision-free and maintain throughput under realistic V2I communication errors. Finally, we visualise the relevance weights that an ego vehicle assigns to other vehicles in multiple traffic situations.

</details>


### [115] [Foundation Pareto Flow Policy for Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19825)
*Zhanjiang Yang,Lijun Sun,Yueming Li,Meng Li,Yijun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FP2——基于多模态扩散Transformer与流匹配损失的离线多目标强化学习基础策略，通过预训练-中训练-后训练三阶段流程实现帕累托权衡策略的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标强化学习方法（在线或离线）通常局限于任务或目标特定的策略学习，无法将学到的权衡泛化到未见任务/目标，因此需要能泛化帕累托最优策略的方法。

Method: 将FP2实例化为配备无似然流匹配损失的多模态扩散Transformer，统一、可扩展且可指令地学习多样权衡轨迹；并设计三阶段训练：大规模MORL数据集预训练、精选高质量数据集中训练、新任务少样本轨迹后训练。

Result: 在D4MORL基准多数任务上优于先前最先进方法，在复杂帕累托流形上实现更优零样本插值与外推，并能高效少样本适配新连续控制任务。

Conclusion: FP2借助基础模型式预-中-后训练范式，有效实现离线多目标强化学习中权衡策略的泛化与少样本适配。

Abstract: The overarching goal of reinforcement learning (RL) is to efficiently optimize the agent's policy for various environments, tasks, dynamics, and even user preferences, which often consist of multiple and potentially conflicting rewards/objectives. This necessitates the learning of Pareto-optimal policies that can navigate different trade-offs among objectives. However, existing multi-objective RL (MORL) methods, whether trained online or offline, are typically limited to task- or objective-specific policy learning and do not address generalization of learned trade-offs to unseen tasks/objectives. In this work, we propose the Foundation Pareto Flow Policy (FP2) for offline MORL in continuous robotic control, inspired by the pre-mid-post-training paradigm underlying recent foundation models. Specifically, we instantiate FP2 as a multi-modal diffusion transformer equipped with a likelihood-free flow matching loss, enabling unified, scalable, and instructable learning of diverse trade-off trajectories. We further develop a three-stage training pipeline to gradually optimize the policy: (1) pre-training on a large-scale MORL dataset, (2) mid-training on a carefully curated high-quality dataset, and (3) post-training on few collected trajectory data from new tasks and trade-offs. Empirical results demonstrate that FP2 outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods on most tasks of the D4MORL benchmark, achieving superior zero-shot interpolation and extrapolation on complex Pareto manifolds as well as efficient few-shot adaptation of the learned trade-off prior to new continuous-control tasks. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [116] [Conditional Optimal Transport and Triangular Maps for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19815)
*Mohammad Al-Jarrah,Yuhang Mei,Karen Leung,Bamdad Hosseini,Amirhossein Taghvaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将模仿学习统一表述为条件采样问题，通过三角传输求解，并提出OTP、FMP、SIP三种策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Policy中参考分布固定为高斯分布，作者认为参考分布应作为设计变量，从而利用示范动作块的经验分布提升性能。

Method: 将策略定义为三角映射的第二块，把参考分布传输到给定观测历史下动作块的条件分布；参考分布取示范动作块的经验分布，并据此提出最优传输策略OTP、流匹配策略FMP和随机插值策略SIP。

Result: 在Push-T和robomimic Square任务上，三种策略性能与Diffusion Policy和Consistency Policy相当，但网络更小、训练更短；在少量网络评估时，信息丰富的参考分布贡献显著。OTP仅需一次评估最小网络（Push-T上0.23M参数）即可生成动作块，SIP在更高成本下取得最高分数。

Conclusion: 三种实例各有取舍而非单一最优：OTP适合对推理时间和模型规模敏感的场景，SIP以更高成本获得最佳性能。

Abstract: We introduce a unified formulation of imitation learning from demonstrations as a conditional sampling problem solved by triangular transport: a policy is the second block of a triangular map that transports a reference distribution to the conditional distribution of the action chunk given the observation history. Unlike in Diffusion Policy, where it is a fixed Gaussian, the reference becomes a design variable; we take it to be the empirical distribution of the demonstrated action chunks and introduce three instances of the formulation: an optimal transport policy (OTP), a flow matching policy (FMP), and a stochastic interpolant policy (SIP). On the Push-T and robomimic Square tasks, the three policies are competitive in task performance with Diffusion Policy and Consistency Policy while using considerably smaller networks and shorter training, and the informative reference accounts for a substantial part of their performance when few network evaluations are used. The three instances offer different trade-offs rather than a single best method: OTP generates an action chunk with a single evaluation of the smallest network (0.23 million parameters on Push-T) and is suitable for settings in which inference time and model size matter, whereas SIP attains the highest scores among the proposed policies at a higher cost.

</details>


### [117] [The Sense Paradigm for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19796)
*Haidong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sense提出用单一交叉熵损失替代防坍缩机制，使嵌入空间距离具有可逆性语义，并在导航与不可逆转移检测上优于LeWorldModel。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型为防止嵌入坍缩需引入目标网络、方差惩罚或分布惩罚，虽避免坍缩却使嵌入距离失去意义，无法区分可逆与不可逆转移。

Method: 提出Sense，用基于有向距离 d(x→y)=Σ_i ReLU(y_i−x_i) 的单一交叉熵损失替代原有防坍缩机制；该损失自身避免坍缩，编码器无需预测器，预测器随后在固定嵌入上用普通回归拟合。

Result: 在四个像素环境中，Sense学到的返回距离检测不可逆转移的AUC为0.95–1.00，LeWorldModel为0.39–0.59；在TwoRoom导航基准中，Sense规划成功率100%，LeWorldModel为91%；目标变远时所有基线失败率上升，而Sense在Cup、Cliff、Sokoban中失败率接近零，在Pendulum中为所有方法最低。

Conclusion: Sense通过有向距离交叉熵损失同时实现防坍缩与距离语义化，显著提升不可逆转移检测与长距离规划鲁棒性，优于LeWorldModel等基线。

Abstract: Latent world models predict the future in an embedding space rather than in pixels. An encoder trained only to predict collapses and maps every observation to the same point, so existing models add a mechanism against collapse, such as a target network, a variance penalty or a distribution penalty. These mechanisms keep the embedding from collapsing, but they leave its distances without meaning, and the model cannot tell a reversible transition from an irreversible one. We propose Sense, a single cross-entropy loss over the directed distance $d(x\to y)=\sum_i \mathrm{ReLU}(y_i-x_i)$ that replaces these mechanisms. The loss avoids collapse on its own, and since the encoder no longer needs the predictor, the predictor is fitted afterwards to fixed embeddings by plain regression. On four pixel environments, the return distance learned by Sense detects irreversible transitions with an AUC between 0.95 and 1.00, where LeWorldModel stays between 0.39 and 0.59. On TwoRoom, the navigation benchmark of LeWorldModel, planning with Sense reaches the goal in 100% of episodes against 91% for LeWorldModel. As the goal moves farther away, the planning failure rate of every baseline grows, while that of Sense stays near zero in Cup, Cliff and Sokoban and is the lowest of all methods in Pendulum.

</details>


### [118] [RIFT: Reward Improvement Field Transfer for Cross-Domain Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19681)
*Jinhee Won,Mardavij Roozbehani,Munther Dahleh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出奖励改进场迁移（RIFT）方法，通过迁移源域中低奖励到高奖励过渡的结构来增强目标域离线强化学习数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域离线强化学习中目标数据有限，源数据虽多但动力学差异导致直接复用不可靠，需利用域间共享的奖励提升结构。

Method: 在共享潜在空间编码源和目标过渡，用最优传输配对源域低/高奖励过渡并拟合仿射场，将场应用于目标低奖励锚点并解码以增强目标数据集。

Result: 在重力、形态和摩擦偏移的密集奖励运动任务上，聚合性能优于现有基线；理论上界定了合成误差条件并推导与下游回报的条件联系。

Conclusion: RIFT能在动力学不同的相关域间有效迁移低到高奖励结构，为目标域数据增强提供可靠方法。

Abstract: Learning from limited target data is a central challenge in cross-domain offline reinforcement learning. Abundant source data can help, but dynamics differences can make direct transition reuse unreliable. We propose Reward Improvement Field Transfer (RIFT), which instead learns how low- and high-reward source transitions differ. RIFT encodes source and target transitions in a shared latent space, pairs low- and high-reward source transitions using optimal transport, and fits an affine field to their displacements. It applies this field to low-reward target anchors and decodes the results to augment the target dataset. The approach builds on the hypothesis that related domains can share useful low-to-high reward structure despite different dynamics. We bound synthesis error under shared class geometry, identify conditions favoring target anchoring over source-high transplant, and derive a conditional connection to downstream return. Experiments on dense-reward locomotion tasks under gravity, morphology, and friction shifts show improved aggregate performance over existing baselines.

</details>


### [119] [Test-Time Constraint-Aware Diffusion Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19380)
*Braghadeesh Lakshminarayanan,Ojas Phadake,Qi Chen,Dominik Baumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TCA-DPC，通过指数倾斜和Metropolis调整朗之万算法在预训练扩散控制器采样中在线适配新安全约束，无需重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散预测控制器生成的序列可能违反部署时新增的安全约束（如障碍物变化），重训练成本高且限制实用性。

Method: 将预训练模型作为有限时域序列先验，基于约束违反惩罚定义指数倾斜的适配分布，利用生成序列中违反约束的比例在线调整倾斜强度，并在预训练采样器的高斯噪声空间中使用Metropolis调整朗之万算法近似采样。

Result: 摘要未提供定量实验结果。

Conclusion: TCA-DPC能够在不重训练的情况下使预训练扩散预测控制器适配新引入的安全约束。

Abstract: Diffusion models can learn multimodal distributions over finite-horizon state-input sequences, providing a way to generate candidates for predictive control. However, sequences generated by a pretrained model may violate safety constraints introduced at deployment, for example when obstacles are added or repositioned. Retraining whenever these constraints change limits the usefulness of learned predictive controllers. In this paper, we propose Test-Time Constraint-Aware Diffusion Predictive Control (TCA-DPC), an algorithm for adapting the sampling distribution of a pretrained diffusion controller to newly introduced safety constraints without retraining. We treat the pretrained model as a prior over finite-horizon sequences and define an adapted distribution through exponential tilting based on a constraint-violation penalty. The tilt strength is adjusted online using the fraction of generated sequences that fail constraint verification. We approximately sample the adapted distribution using the Metropolis-adjusted Langevin algorithm in the Gaussian noise space of the pretrained diffusion sampler.

</details>


### [120] [Recovered or Memorized? Investigating Dynamical Consistency and Faithfulness in World Model Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18807)
*Jerome Francisco,Kyle Deng,George Flint,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出锚定恢复认证（ARC）方法，用离散Fréchet距离在仿射规范下评估世界模型与真实动力学的一致性；在TD-MPC2上测试表明k=10时最稳定但因果潜变量与模拟器的相对误差仍较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的内部表征虽被证明有意义且具因果性，但尚不清楚其是否忠实于环境的支配动力学，因此需要一种量化动态一致性的评估程序。

Method: 引入ARC程序，结合离散Fréchet距离与真值，在仿射规范内评估候选世界模型的动态一致性；在TD-MPC2（无解码器世界模型）上学习不同超参数下的单摆摆动，取k=5、10、25步，并在PCA导出的干预子空间内引导模型，用相对误差与真值模拟器比较。

Result: ARC充分性在k=10时对TD-MPC2最一致，七项约束中六项经Holm-Bonferroni校正后统计显著；但模拟器与因果潜变量之间的相对误差仍然较大。

Conclusion: TD-MPC2的内部表征在中期步长下表现出一定的动态忠实性，但整体误差仍显著；后续可用DreamerV3等带解码器（重建损失）的世界模型进一步研究结果是否不同。

Abstract: World models develop internal simulators that can learn the structure and dynamics of an environment. It has been demonstrated that their internal representations are both meaningful and causal, but are these representations faithful to the governing dynamics of the environment they are learning? We introduce Anchored Recovery Certification (ARC), a procedure that utilizes a discrete Fréchet distance calculation with ground truth to evaluate dynamical consistency on candidate world models up to an affine gauge. We use the procedure on TD-MPC2 (decoder-free world model) that has learned the pendulum swing on an observation lift under varying hyperparameters at k = 5, 10, 25 steps. The model is then steered within a PCA-derived intervention subspace and compared against a ground-truth simulator using relative error to investigate dynamical faithfulness. ARC sufficiency held for TD-MPC2 most consistently at k = 10, with six of seven constraints statistically significant after Holm-Bonferroni correction, while the relative error between the simulator and the causal latent remained substantial. Further research could be conducted with decoder world models such as DreamerV3 (reconstruction loss) to investigate whether the results differ.

</details>


### [121] [D'BEAST: Logged Bad Experiences as Explanation for Autonomous Driving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20019)
*Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan,Nur Ahmad Khatim,Mansur Arief*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究表格型强化学习智能体的决策能否由其“坏经验”（与安全或任务事件相关的训练转移）解释，并通过重放固定训练日志进行消融实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习智能体从错误中学习，但其决策难以追溯到这些经验；作者希望验证坏经验是否携带对决策至关重要的信息。

Method: 固定训练日志在相同初始Q表下重放三种条件：FULL保留所有更新，SKIP省略坏经验更新，NEUTRALIZE保留更新但去除事件惩罚；在六个经典MDP和CARLA闭环驾驶中评估，并做局部消融分析。

Result: 完整驾驶任务中FULL从40个测试起点中的33个到达目标，SKIP和NEUTRALIZE均无一到达；在SKIP或NEUTRALIZE失败前的47个局部决策中，移除或中和中位数为1的小组坏经验后，智能体在46个案例中选择了失败策略的动作。

Conclusion: 对该智能体而言，结果表明其决策可以由坏经验解释，坏经验携带决策相关信息。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents learn from mistakes, yet their decisions can be difficult to trace back to those experiences. We investigate whether a tabular agent's decisions can be explained by its bad experiences, defined as the training transitions associated with safety or task events. A fixed training log is replayed from the same initial Q-table under three conditions. FULL retains all updates, SKIP omits bad-experience updates, and NEUTRALIZE retains those updates without their event penalties. We illustrate the method on six classic Markov decision processes and evaluate it in closed-loop driving in CARLA. On the full driving task, FULL reached the goal from 33 of 40 test starts, whereas SKIP and NEUTRALIZE reached it from none, indicating that the bad experiences carry decision-relevant information. At the local level, for 47 decisions shortly before a failure of SKIP or NEUTRALIZE, removing or neutralizing a small group of bad experiences, with a median size of one, caused the trained agent to select the failing policy's action in 46 cases. For this agent, the results indicate that its decisions can be explained by its bad experiences.

</details>


### [122] [Minari: Infrastructure for Collaborative Reinforcement Learning Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18644)
*Omar G. Younis,Rodrigo Perez-Vicente,John U. Balis,Alex Davey,Will Dudley,Jordan K. Terry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Minari是一个开源Python库，用于创建、共享和加载离线强化学习数据集，解决数据集格式不一、难以复现的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习依赖共享数据集，但现有数据集格式各异、文档稀少，且环境代码更新后多数数据集无法重新生成，阻碍了数据共享与复现。

Method: 提出Minari库，API遵循Gymnasium，通过包装环境并正常步进即可记录数据集；支持元数据附加、过滤、合并、打乱，并通过HuggingFace Hub一键发布或下载；每个数据集存储环境规格、种子、包装器及固定包依赖以支持重建和再生成。

Result: TorchRL、AgileRL和d3rlpy已原生支持加载Minari数据集；Minari托管338个可复现数据集（35万条轨迹、5500万步），覆盖机器人运动与操作、迷宫与网格导航、语言条件指令跟随、Atari和网页导航，并包含D4RL的完整再生成及公开生成脚本；报告了六种离线RL算法在再生成的D4RL和MuJoCo数据集上的基线分数。

Conclusion: Minari通过标准化格式、元数据管理和可复现性设计，显著降低了离线RL数据集的共享与复用门槛，并获得主流库的原生支持。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) depends on shared datasets, but sharing them is still hard. Each benchmark ships its own format, documentation is thin, and most datasets cannot be regenerated once the environment code moves on. We introduce Minari, an open-source Python library for creating, sharing, and loading RL datasets. Its API follows Gymnasium, so recording a dataset means wrapping an environment and stepping through it as usual. From there, users can attach metadata, filter, combine, and shuffle datasets, and publish or download them with one command through the HuggingFace Hub. Every dataset stores the environment specification, seeds, applied wrappers, and pinned package requirements needed to rebuild the environment and regenerate the data. TorchRL, AgileRL, and d3rlpy already load Minari datasets natively, so switching datasets in those libraries takes one line of code. Minari also hosts 338 reproducible datasets (350K trajectories, 55M steps) covering robotic locomotion and manipulation, maze and grid navigation, language-conditioned instruction following, Atari, and web navigation, including a full regeneration of D4RL with public generation scripts. We report baseline scores for six offline RL algorithms on the regenerated D4RL and MuJoCo datasets.

</details>


### [123] [UNITE: Consolidating Embodied VLM Specialists via Shared-Anchor One-Bit Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20767)
*Tao Tao,Anke Tang,Enneng Yang,Zhi Hou,Xiaogang Wang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UNITE 提出一种共享骨干混合专家框架，将多个具身视觉语言专家压缩合并为一个紧凑模型，存储仅约单个专家的1.16倍，并在多数基准上接近甚至超过原始专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身能力（视觉定位、空间推理、导航等）通常由独立微调的专家模型分别实现，分开存储导致存储成本成倍增长；而直接合并为一个稠密模型又会因参数共享而产生任务更新冲突，损失各专家性能。

Method: 保留每个专家的前馈块作为可路由专家，用共享锚点加一比特分解（共享浮点锚点+专家特定行缩放二值矩阵）表示专家库，通过闭式交替更新联合优化锚点、缩放与符号，无需校准数据；随后在冻结骨干与专家的情况下，仅用不到0.6M参数（<0.01%的8.8B激活参数）进行token级专家选择适配。

Result: 由四个Qwen3-VL-8B专家构建的UNITE模型占用20.3GB，为单个稠密专家的1.16倍、比未压缩MoE低59.6%；在22个基准和R2R Val-Unseen上，平均最佳专家性能保留率与六种静态合并方法持平，在Scan2Cap、ScanQA及全部四项导航指标上领先，并在全部五个3D场景理解基准（Scan2Cap最高+19.1）和R2R导航上超过所有源专家；相对未压缩MoE，22个基准中21个几乎无损，唯一例外是VSI-Bench，帧数实验表明其跨帧信息利用受损。

Conclusion: UNITE能以接近单模型的存储代价整合互补的具身能力，分解压缩近乎无损，是一种高效的专家合并方案。

Abstract: Embodied capabilities such as visual grounding, spatial reasoning, navigation, and driving understanding are often developed in separate vision-language specialists fine-tuned from a common pretrained this http URL the specialists separately multiplies storage, whereas merging them into one dense model forces conflicting task updates to share parameters and loses part of each specialist's this http URL introduce UNITE, a framework that consolidates such specialists into a compact shared-backbone mixture of experts. UNITE keeps the feed-forward block of each specialist as a routable expert and represents the expert bank through a shared-anchor one-bit factorization: a shared floating-point anchor plus expert-specific, row-scaled binary this http URL anchor is optimized jointly with the scales and signs through closed-form alternating updates, using only source weights and no calibration this http URL adaptation then adjusts token-level expert selection with the backbone and experts frozen, updating fewer than $0.6$M parameters ($<0.01\%$ of the $8.8$B active parameters). Built from four Qwen3-VL-8B specialists, the complete UNITE model occupies $20.3$ GB, $1.16\times$ the storage of one dense specialist and $59.6\%$ below the uncompressed MoE. Across $22$ benchmarks and R2R Val-Unseen, UNITE matches six static merging methods in mean retention of best-specialist performance, leads all of them on Scan2Cap, ScanQA, and all four navigation metrics, and surpasses every source specialist on all five 3D scene understanding benchmarks (up to $+19.1$ on Scan2Cap) and on R2R navigation. Relative to the uncompressed MoE, factorization is nearly lossless on $21$ of $22$ benchmarks; the exception is VSI-Bench, where frame-count experiments suggest impaired use of cross-frame this http URL thus consolidates complementary embodied capabilities near single-model storage.

</details>


### [124] [Spectral Steering: Local Inverse Geometry for Frozen Diffusion Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19572)
*Angelo Moroncelli,Alessandro Leanza,Loris Roveda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究冻结扩散生成器在结构化编辑请求下的引导忠实性与局部逆校正的作用，提出 Spectral steering 方法，并通过理论分析与多领域实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结扩散生成器常需在保留部分输出的同时修改多个属性，但仅靠条件化不足以保证引导方向正确，需理解请求与目标特定剩余采样器雅可比之间的交互。

Method: 提出 Spectral steering，将请求特定的余弦增益预测器与阻尼逆校正及固定时间重线性化结合用于有限编辑；使用标量零空间和匹配运动控制检验预测机制，并在图像、音频、机器人、控制与规划及复杂角色运动生成等实验中评估。

Result: 在回溯识别的局部区域内 8,923 个实例中，预测增益与实际增益的 Spearman 相关系数为 0.79，条件数为 -0.11；50 对图像修复案例中，四次校正使未观测像素 PSNR 较 Adam 在相同总导数遍历下提升 3.25 dB。

Conclusion: 分析与控制实验将请求相关几何与方向性增益联系起来，并明确了这些增益在何种条件下可转化为下游性能提升。

Abstract: A frozen diffusion generator may need to change several output properties while preserving others. We study when guidance faithfully realizes such a structured request and whether local inverse correction improves its direction. Our account links this benefit to the interaction between the request and the objective-specific remaining-sampler Jacobian: conditioning alone is insufficient. Spectral steering combines a request-specific cosine-gain predictor with damped inverse correction and fixed-time relinearization for finite edits. Scalar nulls and matched-movement controls test the predicted mechanism. Across 8,923 instances in a retrospectively identified local regime, predicted and realized gains correlate at Spearman 0.79, versus -0.11 for condition number. Experiments span images, audio, robotics, and control and planning, including complex character motion generation. On 50 paired inpainting cases, four corrections improve unobserved-pixel PSNR over Adam by 3.25 dB at equal total derivative traversals. Together, the analysis and controls connect request-dependent geometry to directional gains and establish where these gains translate into downstream performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [ARC-VLN: Adaptive Recursive Computing-based Visual-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18696)
*Xiang Zhang,Xiangyu Dong,Yang Lu,Xiaoguang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARC-VLN是一个基于共享参数语言主干的token类型感知递归计算框架，用于高效空中视觉语言导航，通过为视觉token自适应选择退出深度来降低存储和推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中视觉语言导航需要在保持指令条件动作预测的同时，降低模型存储和推理成本。

Method: 在共享参数语言主干上构建token类型感知递归计算框架，文本和动作token保留全深度计算，视觉token自适应选择退出深度；保留提前退出视觉token的表示，并通过活动token压缩缩短后续递归块处理的序列；设计专门学习策略缓解路由坍缩到单一退出深度的问题。

Result: 在OpenFly的Test-Seen和Test-Unseen子集上，ARC-VLN在所有对比方法中取得最低导航误差、最高成功率和最高路径长度加权成功率，同时减少每条轨迹的平均决策步数、总浮点运算量、平均模型推理时间和推理期间的平均GPU内存占用。

Conclusion: ARC-VLN具有作为轻量级应用VLN主干的较大潜力。

Abstract: Efficient aerial visual-language navigation (VLN) requires reducing model storage and inference costs while maintaining instruction-conditioned action prediction. We present ARC-VLN, a token-type-aware recursive computation framework built on a language backbone with shared parameters. ARC-VLN retains full-depth computation for text and action tokens while adaptively selecting exit depths for visual tokens. Representations of early-exiting visual tokens are retained, and active-token compaction shortens the sequences processed by subsequent recursive blocks. To mitigate routing collapse to a single exit depth, we design a dedicated learning strategy that encourages the model to distribute visual tokens across multiple exit this http URL OpenFly's Test-Seen and Test-Unseen subsets, ARC-VLN achieves the lowest navigation error and the highest success rate and success weighted by path length among all compared methods. Meanwhile, it reduces the average number of decision steps per trajectory, total floating-point operations, mean model inference time per trajectory, and average GPU memory usage during inference, indicating its great potential as a VLN backbone for lightweight applications.

</details>


### [126] [DuoFlow: Towards Synchronized Speech and Motion Generation with Fine-Grained Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21530)
*Jun Dong,Ronghui Li,Zhongyuan Hu,Yunqi Zhao,Lijie Guo,Ruizhang Xu,Di Kang,Linchao Bao,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MotionSpeech基准和DuoFlow双流模型，实现可控的语音-动作联合生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音-动作联合生成模型主要依赖隐式语义或中间表示，缺乏对动作层次、身体部位和声音风格的显式控制。

Method: 构建包含文本、声音风格描述和两级动作条件的MotionSpeech基准；提出DuoFlow双流潜在流变换器，在共享噪声水平和统一秒级时间坐标下联合去噪语音与动作，使用模态特定残差连接器注入结构化条件、分层双向跨模态注意力协调生成，并通过单调音素路径结合归一化进度锚点和CTC监督强化语音时间建模。

Result: DuoFlow提供细粒度动作和声音风格控制的统一接口，能够合成高质量、同步且与指令一致的语音-动作序列。

Conclusion: MotionSpeech基准和DuoFlow模型有效桥接了可控语音-动作联合生成中的显式控制缺口。

Abstract: An embodied speaker's expressiveness depends not only on what is said, but also on how the voice sounds and how the body moves. However, existing joint speech-motion generation models are largely driven implicitly by transcript semantics or intermediate speech representations, offering little explicit control over motion hierarchy, body parts, or vocal style. To bridge this gap, we construct MotionSpeech, a structured benchmark for controllable joint speech-motion generation that includes transcripts, voice-style descriptions, and motion conditions at two hierarchical levels. Building on this benchmark, we propose DuoFlow, a dual-stream latent flow transformer that jointly denoises speech and motion under a shared noise level and a unified second-based temporal coordinate. Modality-specific residual connectors inject structured conditions into the corresponding streams, layer-wise bidirectional cross-modal attention coordinates their generation, and a monotonic phoneme pathway combines normalized progress anchors with CTC supervision to strengthen speech temporal modeling. DuoFlow provides a unified interface for fine-grained motion and vocal-style control and demonstrates the ability to synthesize high-quality, synchronized, and instruction-consistent speech-motion sequences.

</details>


### [127] [Lost in the Hallway: Benchmarking Persistent Spatial Memory Across Revisits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21485)
*Henry Zheng,Weibo Gong,Yang Yue,Rui Huang,Yang Zhang,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReSpaceBench 基准，用于评估具身智能体跨多次访问的持久空间理解（重访地点识别、同类房间区分与状态更新），发现现有模型远逊于人类，并发布训练集 RS-30K 以提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估未显式体现跨分离访问的物理地点同一性，导致智能体在重复环境中可靠运行所需的持久空间理解能力缺乏系统评测。

Method: 构建 ReSpaceBench：涵盖 590 个合成与真实家庭场景的 3,244 道题，组织为房间关联、持久状态追踪与下游推理共 9 项任务；通过受控漫游以物理进入房间定义访问，并刻意加入同类型房间干扰与跨会话物体变化；评测 32 个模型与 3 个智能体式视频理解框架，并分析执行轨迹；同时提供无重叠的合成训练集 RS-30K，微调 Qwen3-VL-8B。

Result: 最强零样本模型总体准确率 50.5%（450 题子集 52.2%），人类为 90.1%；智能体框架较同基座直接推理无整体提升；主要失败模式为证据遗漏与跨访问房间关联错误；识别熟悉房间与定位正确访问房间存在差距；微调 Qwen3-VL-8B 将准确率从 35.5% 提升至 60.4%，关联任务增益最大且可迁移至真实场景。

Conclusion: 迭代式证据检索本身不足以保证一致的空间记忆；需显式建模跨访问地点同一性并保留中间访问与转换，RS-30K 微调可显著缓解该缺陷。

Abstract: Persistent spatial understanding is essential for embodied agents to operate reliably in environments they repeatedly encounter. It requires recognizing revisited places despite viewpoint changes, distinguishing them from similar-looking rooms, and updating memories as environments change. Yet existing evaluations often leave physical place identity across separated visits implicit. We introduce ReSpaceBench, a benchmark comprising 3,244 questions across 590 synthetic and real-home scenes, organized into 9 tasks spanning room association, persistent state tracking, and downstream reasoning. Controlled walkthroughs define visits by physical room entry and deliberately include same-type room distractors and cross-session object changes. Our evaluation of 32 models and 3 agentic video-understanding frameworks reveals substantial limitations: the strongest evaluated zero-shot model achieves 50.5% overall accuracy and 52.2% on a 450-question subset, compared with 90.1% for human annotators. The evaluated agentic frameworks offer no overall improvement over direct inference with the same base model. Analysis of agent execution traces reveals evidence omissions and incorrect cross-visit room associations as major failure modes, suggesting that iterative evidence seeking alone does not ensure consistent spatial memory. Further analyses expose a gap between recognizing familiar rooms and identifying the correct visited room, while annotation-guided sampling highlights the importance of preserving intervening visits and transitions. We also provide RS-30K, a separate training dataset from synthetic sources with no template, scene, route, or video overlap with the benchmark. Fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-8B raises overall accuracy from 35.5% to 60.4%, with the largest gains in association and improvements extending to real-life scenes.

</details>


### [128] [Déjà-Events: Multi-view 3D Geometry with Event Cameras via Sparsity-Aware Adaptive Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21302)
*Arda Cinar Demirtas,Pablo Varela Bernal,Marc Pollefeys,Sunghwan Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Déjà-Events 是一个仅用事件相机的前馈模型，单次前向即可从多视角预测深度、相机位姿和点图，通过从 RGB 教师模型蒸馏训练，仅需约 1% 参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机在快速或弱光场景中能保留结构信息，适合 3D 感知，但稠密 3D 真值的事件数据集远小于图像数据集，难以直接训练前馈 3D 重建模型。

Method: 提出 Déjà-Events，仅用事件输入，单次前向预测深度、位姿和点图；在配对 RGB 与事件记录上，从冻结的 RGB 教师模型 Déjà View 蒸馏最终 token，无需深度或位姿真值，仅训练约 1% 参数；并引入稀疏感知自适应门控，按事件密度逐块调整共享 transformer 块的门控，以在增加细化步数时保持精度；仅在模拟数据上训练。

Result: 在 EventScape 上取得所评估事件方法中最低点图误差，并在真实 MVSEC 和 DSEC 上零样本评估；在 DSEC 上将最强基线误差减半（0.15 vs. 0.30 AbsRel），参数量约为其十分之一；在 EventScape 和 DSEC 上深度误差最低，在 EventScape 上轨迹误差最低。

Conclusion: 通过从 RGB 前馈模型蒸馏并结合稀疏感知自适应门控，Déjà-Events 在无需 3D 真值且仅训练少量参数的情况下，实现了事件相机 3D 感知的领先精度，并具备对真实数据的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Event cameras keep capturing structure where conventional cameras blur or saturate, which makes them attractive for 3D perception in fast or poorly lit scenes. Event datasets with dense 3D ground truth, however, are far smaller and less diverse than the image collections behind feed-forward 3D reconstruction models. We present Déjà-Events, an event-only model that predicts depth, camera pose and pointmaps from multiple views in a single forward pass. Rather than relying on scarce 3D labels, we distil it from Déjà View, a feed-forward RGB model that refines its tokens by repeatedly applying one shared transformer block. On paired RGB and event recordings, the event student learns to reproduce the frozen teacher's final tokens, which requires no depth or pose ground truth and trains only about 1% of its parameters. Because events are dense at edges and moving texture but nearly absent in textureless regions, we further introduce sparsity-aware adaptive gating, which adapts the shared block's gates to events and adjusts them per patch by event density, so that accuracy holds as refinement steps are added. Trained only on simulated data, Déjà-Events achieves the lowest pointmap error among the event-based methods we evaluate, on EventScape and zero-shot on the real MVSEC and DSEC datasets. On DSEC it halves the error of the strongest baseline (0.15 vs. 0.30 AbsRel) with about a tenth of its parameters. It also has the lowest depth error on EventScape and DSEC and the lowest trajectory error on EventScape.

</details>


### [129] [DRaGAN: Dynamic Graph Attention Network for Multi-Radar Point Cloud Encoding in Automotive Radar Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21162)
*Christof Leitgeb,Thomas Puchleitner,Max Peter Ronecker,Daniel Watzenig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRaGAN，一种面向多雷达点云的动态图注意力网络，通过传感器相对傅里叶编码、门控远近程枢纽节点和注意力引导动态重连，在TruckScenes上显著提升性能并大幅降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达对雾、雨、雪具有穿透能力且分辨率不断提升，适合作为鲁棒感知模态；但原始雷达数据处理开销大，现有工作多聚焦于类LiDAR的稀疏点云，而LiDAR导向的编码器难以适配雷达点云的稀疏性和传感器相对各向异性。

Method: DRaGAN引入传感器相对的angle与range傅里叶编码以刻画固定孔径雷达特性；沿每个传感器视线设置门控短程与长程枢纽节点，实现高效的局部-全局消息传递；采用注意力引导的动态重连，剪枝弱边并仅在两跳邻域内加入有限数量的潜在连接，从而在深层网络中减少处理边数同时保持表示质量。

Result: 在TruckScenes上，DRaGAN相比所评估的RadarGNN基线测试NDS提升10.10分，报告模型FLOPs降低63.7%；代价是在所评估实现上模型延迟从40 ms增至53.16 ms，可训练参数从1.2M增至1.7M。

Conclusion: DRaGAN通过针对雷达特性的图注意力设计，在显著提升多雷达点云感知精度的同时大幅降低计算量，尽管延迟和参数量有所增加，仍为雷达点云处理提供了有效的权衡方案。

Abstract: Radar represents a key modality for weather-robust perception. Unlike optical sensors such as camera and LiDAR, radar can penetrate fog, rain and snow due to its longer wavelength, and recent hardware advances have steadily improved its spatial resolution, thus making it increasingly relevant for autonomous systems. While processing raw radar data requires substantial memory computation, most existing work has focused on preprocessed point-cloud data similar to LiDAR. However, the sparse radar points and sensor-relative anisotropy make LiDAR-oriented encoders suboptimal. To address this problem, we present DRaGAN, a dynamic graph attention network for multi-radar point clouds. DRaGAN introduces a sensor-relative Fourier encoding of angle and range to capture fixed-aperture radar characteristics, gated short- and long-range hub nodes along each sensor's line of sight for efficient local-global message passing, and attention-guided dynamic rewiring that prunes weak edges and adds a bounded number of latent connections restricted to two-hop neighborhoods. This reduces the number of processed edges in deeper layers while preserving representation quality. On TruckScenes, DRaGAN improves test NDS by 10.10 points over the evaluated RadarGNN baseline and reduces the reported model FLOP count by 63.7%. This trade-off includes an increase in model latency from 40 to 53.16 ms and in trainable parameters from 1.2M to 1.7M on the evaluated implementations. Code and pretrained models are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [130] [IGRecon4D: Dynamic-Object-Aware Feed-Forward Reconstruction of Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21126)
*Yifei Tong,Ziyang Huang,Yizhao Han,Xiaoxiao Long,Hao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IGRecon4D，一个前馈4D驾驶场景重建框架，通过训练时实例先验蒸馏和LiDAR锚定的高斯定位，解决语义泄漏与几何漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈4D驾驶场景重建方法存在两个互补的失败模式：交通参与者语义泄漏到静态表示中，以及高斯位置在跨视角下的几何漂移，影响重建质量。

Method: 采用实例级语义分支提供潜在动态参与者先验，通过实例-动态一致性约束蒸馏到动态掩码学习；同时利用稀疏LiDAR测量引导稠密深度估计，并将深度反投影以锚定高斯位置，提升多视角几何一致性。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset上全图像重建性能提升，在nuScenes零样本跨数据集评估中交通参与者区域重建更强。

Conclusion: IGRecon4D有效缓解语义泄漏和几何漂移，提升动态驾驶场景的前馈4D重建质量，并展现出跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 4D driving-scene reconstruction enables efficient modeling of dynamic autonomous-driving scenes, but current methods suffer from two complementary failure modes: semantic leakage of traffic participants into the static representation and geometric drift of Gaussian locations across viewpoints. We propose \textbf{IGRecon4D}, a feed-forward dynamic scene reconstruction framework that addresses these failures with training-time instance-prior distillation and LiDAR-anchored Gaussian positioning. Specifically, an instance-level semantic branch provides a potentially dynamic participant prior, which is distilled into dynamic-mask learning through the Instance-Dynamic Consistency Constraint to encourage high-recall localization of traffic-participant regions prone to dynamic-static leakage. In parallel, sparse LiDAR measurements guide dense depth estimation, and the resulting depths are back-projected to anchor Gaussian positions for improved multi-view geometric consistency. Experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset and zero-shot evaluation on nuScenes demonstrate improved full-image reconstruction on the main benchmark and stronger traffic-participant-region reconstruction under cross-dataset evaluation.

</details>


### [131] [GRAZE: Text-Free Grasp Synthesis for Unseen Objects from Human Gaze](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21104)
*Jeongho Lee,Boeun Kim,Hyung Jin Chang,Jungchan Cho,Sang-Il Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAZE 用记录的眼动（gaze）而非物体或部位名称来生成手-物抓取动作，在未见物体上抓取目标部位更频繁、成功率更高，并能捕捉文本提示难以表达的接近侧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件的手-物交互生成虽提供直观语义控制，但语言难以指定交互应发生在物体的哪个位置：命名抓取部位需要物体专用词汇，命名接近侧则需要提示所缺乏的参考系。

Method: 提出 GRAZE（GRAsp from gaZE），从记录的眼动生成手-物抓取运动；其 Geometry-Aware Gaze Encoding（GeoGaze）将眼动射线映射为物体几何上的线索，并通过交叉注意力以这些线索条件化扩散 Transformer；同时构建基于 HOT3D 录制的抓取中心基准 HOT3D-Grasp，含物体不相交的划分。

Result: 在未见物体上，GRAZE 抓取目标部位的频率高于最强文本基线（49.3% vs. 43.6%），抓取成功率最高（66.3% vs. 46.6%），并零微调迁移到 GazeHOI（45.3% vs. 33.8%）；眼动旋转绕物体时接近侧随之改变的比例为 59.1%，而用时钟词重训的文本模型为 20.5%；同一骨干下文本提示命名部位更好，但眼动补充接近侧；同时提供两者时 GRAZE 保持提示的部位准确率并跟随眼动。

Conclusion: 眼动能够传达文本提示难以指定的接近侧信息；GRAZE 表明以眼动为条件的生成在抓取部位定位与成功率上优于文本条件方法，且可与文本提示互补。

Abstract: Text-conditioned hand-object interaction (HOI) generation provides intuitive semantic control, but language is an indirect way to specify where an interaction should occur on an object. Naming the part to grasp requires object-specific words, and naming the approach side requires a reference frame that a prompt lacks. We propose GRAZE (GRAsp from gaZE), which generates hand-object grasp motion from recorded gaze instead of object and part names. Its Geometry-Aware Gaze Encoding (GeoGaze) maps gaze rays to cues on object geometry, and cross-attention conditions a diffusion Transformer on these cues. We also introduce HOT3D-Grasp, a grasp-centered benchmark with object-disjoint folds built on HOT3D recordings. On unseen objects, GRAZE grasps the target part more often than the strongest text-conditioned baseline (49.3% vs. 43.6%), achieves the highest grasp success (66.3% vs. 46.6%), and transfers without fine-tuning to GazeHOI (45.3% vs. 33.8%). Gaze conveys the approach side: when gaze is rotated around the object, the approach turns with it in 59.1% of cases, against 20.5% for a text model retrained with clock words. On the same backbone, a text prompt names parts better, but gaze adds the approach side. Given both, GRAZE keeps the prompt's part accuracy and follows gaze.

</details>


### [132] [MetricHand: Geometry-Aware Flow Matching for 4D Hand Reconstruction in Metric World Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21060)
*Yibo Bai,Likui Zhang,Jinglei Zhang,Zhengbin Pang,Xingshen Song,Xiaoqing Yin,Jinsheng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MetricHand，一种几何感知的流匹配框架，用于在度量世界空间中实现4D手部重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将弱透视下的逐帧估计提升到世界坐标，忽略了近距离下关节相关的深度变化，导致平移、朝向和关节运动误差耦合，且在遮挡时恶化。

Method: MetricHand将手-物体交互区域的深度先验编码为关节关联表示，保留局部空间结构和度量尺度；与视觉特征融合后指导粗度量估计，并作为时间生成每一步的条件；联合恢复世界空间平移、全局朝向、MANO关节运动和形状。

Result: 相比HaWoR，相机空间MPJPE降低49.3%和52.9%，绝对世界空间MPJPE降低28.7%和21.7%，腕部定位误差降低48.5%和37.9%，世界空间加速度误差降低44.2%和64.3%。

Conclusion: MetricHand在提高绝对定位精度的同时改善了时间保真度，验证了几何感知流匹配在度量世界空间4D手部重建中的有效性。

Abstract: Egocentric hand motion provides physical motion supervision for dexterous manipulation and vision-language-action pretraining. Reliable use of this supervision requires accurate and temporally coherent 4D hand reconstruction in metric world space. Existing methods typically lift framewise estimates produced under weak perspective into world coordinates. By ignoring joint-dependent depth variation at close range, this approximation couples errors in translation, orientation, and articulation that persist after world lifting and worsen under occlusion. We present MetricHand, a geometry-aware flow matching framework for 4D hand reconstruction in metric world space. MetricHand encodes depth priors from hand-object interaction regions into joint-associated representations that preserve local spatial structure and metric scale. Fused with visual features, this geometry guides coarse metric estimation and conditions every step of temporal generation. The model jointly recovers world-space translation, global orientation, MANO articulation, and shape, while structural priors and temporal context support estimation under partial occlusion. MetricHand reduces camera-space MPJPE by 49.3% and 52.9%, absolute world-space MPJPE by 28.7% and 21.7%, and wrist localization error by 48.5% and 37.9% relative to HaWoR, respectively. World-space acceleration error decreases by 44.2% and 64.3%, demonstrating improved temporal fidelity alongside more accurate absolute localization.

</details>


### [133] [POKE: Probe Offline, Keep Efficiently for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21033)
*Jaehyuk Lee,Yeajin Lee,Suhyun Bae,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: POKE 通过离线计算全图动作敏感度并蒸馏到轻量学生网络，在线无反向传播地评分并采用多样性选择保留视觉 token，在 LIBERO 上以 32/512 token 实现高成功率与加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉 token 剪枝可降低 VLA 推理成本，但基于注意力的评分不直接衡量 token 对预测动作的影响；在线计算动作敏感度成本高，而在剪枝图上评分会导致评分图锁定：被移除 token 失去后续层的直接路径，得分偏低而难以回归。

Method: 定义动作敏感度为 token 被替换为其相机均值 token 时一阶动作变化的幅度，用一次反向传播估计所有 token；提出 POKE，离线在全图上计算动作敏感度并蒸馏到参数少于 0.3M 的学生网络，推理时学生网络对当前观测评分，无需反向传播或历史掩码，并用分数引导的多样性选择保留 token。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上保留 512 个视觉 token 中的 32 个，OpenVLA-OFT 成功率达 95.2%、π0.5 达 90.8%，分别获得 1.85× 和 1.26× 加速；优于注意力、多样性和蒸馏类剪枝器，在 LIBERO-Plus 上优势更大；重放观测上剪枝图评分随机掩码重选率达 59%（随机期望 6%）。

Conclusion: 离线全图敏感度蒸馏加在线轻量评分与多样性选择，可避免评分图锁定，在显著减少视觉 token 的同时保持高任务成功率并实现推理加速。

Abstract: Visual token pruning reduces the inference cost of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, but attention-based scores do not directly measure a token's effect on the predicted action. We define action sensitivity as the magnitude of the first-order action change when a token is replaced by its camera's mean token and estimate it for all tokens with a single backward pass. Computing it online is costly: scoring on the full (unpruned) graph needs an extra forward pass at every control step. Scoring on the pruned forward pass is cheaper but causes scoring-graph lock-in: removed tokens lose their direct paths through later layers and tend to receive lower scores, making those locations less likely to return. On replayed observations, even with a random conditioning mask, pruned-graph scoring re-selects 59% of the mask's tokens, versus 6% expected by chance; attention histories show a similar bias. POKE (Probe Offline, Keep Efficiently) computes full-graph action sensitivities offline and distills them into a student with under 0.3M parameters. At inference, the student scores the current observation without backpropagation or previous masks, and score-guided diversity selection retains the tokens. Keeping 32 of 512 visual tokens on LIBERO, POKE achieves 95.2% success on OpenVLA-OFT and 90.8% on $\pi_{0.5}$ at $1.85\times$ and $1.26\times$ speedups, outperforming attention-, diversity-, and distillation-based pruners, with larger margins on LIBERO-Plus.

</details>


### [134] [FAVR: Flow-Aware Visual Residual Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20971)
*Qixuan Wu,Xiaole Yang,Yuguang Yang,Canyu Chen,Linlin Yang,Haixia Pan,Guodong Guo,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAVR是一个轻量级框架，通过流感知视觉残差对齐将预训练视频表示集成到冻结的VLA策略中，仅需少量可训练参数即可显著提升机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频表示蕴含丰富的时序信息，但将其有效集成到现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中仍具挑战性，现有方法难以在不破坏冻结策略的前提下实现流感知的视觉条件化。

Method: FAVR通过线性测试时训练（TTT）维护紧凑的时序视觉状态，并在基于流的动作生成过程中检索动作条件化的视觉特征；利用冻结动作头的仿射结构显式刻画注入视觉特征对预测动作速度的影响，将流感知通道选择建模为箱约束优化问题，提出基于FISTA的联合门控优化求解器，并通过对角化与重缩放推导高效闭式近似。

Result: FAVR仅需1.74-2.19M可训练参数和5,000步适应，在RoboTwin 2.0上使LingBot-VLA在干净条件下从87.12%提升至90.04%，随机条件下从86.88%提升至89.38%；在五个RoboTwin任务上的消融实验表明，流感知门控将平均成功率从跨流步骤共享门控的72.0%提升至82.0%。

Conclusion: 流感知视觉条件化能在动作生成全过程中有效增强冻结VLA策略，且FAVR以极低参数量和适应成本实现了跨基准的稳定性能提升。

Abstract: Pretrained video representations provide rich temporal information for robotic manipulation, yet effectively integrating them into existing vision-language-action (VLA) policies remains challenging. We present Flow-Aware Visual Residual Alignment (FAVR), a lightweight framework for flow-aware visual conditioning of frozen VLA policies. FAVR maintains a compact temporal visual state through linear Test-Time Training (TTT) and retrieves action-conditioned visual features throughout flow-based action generation. By exploiting the affine structure of the frozen action head, we explicitly characterize how injected visual features influence predicted action velocities and formulate flow-aware channel selection as a box-constrained optimization problem. We develop a FISTA-based solver for joint gate optimization and derive an efficient closed-form approximation through diagonalization and rescaling. With only 1.74-2.19M trainable parameters and 5,000 adaptation steps, FAVR improves pretrained VLA policies across benchmarks. On RoboTwin 2.0, FAVR improves LingBot-VLA from 87.12% to 90.04% under clean conditions and from 86.88% to 89.38% under randomized conditions. A controlled ablation on five RoboTwin tasks further shows that flow-aware gating increases average success from 72.0% with a gate shared across flow steps to 82.0%, demonstrating the benefit of adapting visual conditioning throughout action generation.

</details>


### [135] [EviBridge: Encoding Predictive Visual Evidence for Proactive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20916)
*Zhichao Huang,Changheng Cai,Song Ren,Zhichen Zhao,Hongsen Liu,Chaoyang Zheng,Wenxi Wu,Yichen Long,Yuan Fang,Luya Wang,Gufeng Liu,Yuchen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviBridge将预测性视觉表征集成到视觉-语言交互模型中，用于工具交接中的联合状态与工具预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具交接交互需要在响应前进行准备：模型等待交互线索出现后，在准备的同时评估累积证据是否支持响应或需要取消。

Method: 提出EviBridge框架，通过可训练的证据接口将冻结的V-JEPA 2编码器特征映射到语言解码器，并与原生视觉输入结合，保留时间上下文且仅使用每个决策时刻可用的观测。包含两个实例：EviBridge-E区分近期与历史证据并编码每次观测的时间与空间位置；EviBridge-I保留池化V-JEPA 2特征与交互轮次的时间顺序并生成专用交互token。在FuGIBench流式视频基准上评估准备、响应与取消。

Result: EviBridge-E和EviBridge-I的最佳变体Overall得分分别为0.6448和0.5892，而各自任务适配的原生基线为0.2178和0.5327，提升覆盖目标交接、长时间等待和取消请求场景。

Conclusion: 预测性视觉证据对交互决策具有重要价值，EviBridge有效提升了工具交接辅助中的状态与工具预测性能。

Abstract: Tool-handover interaction calls for preparation before a response is warranted. The model waits until interaction cues appear, then prepares while assessing whether the accumulating evidence supports a response or calls for cancellation. We introduce EviBridge, a framework that integrates predictive visual representations into a vision-language interaction model for joint state and tool prediction. A trainable evidence interface maps features from a frozen V-JEPA 2 encoder into the language decoder alongside native visual inputs, preserving their temporal context and using only observations available at each decision time. The framework has two instantiations. EviBridge-E distinguishes recent from historical evidence and encodes the timing and spatial location of each observation. EviBridge-I preserves the temporal order of pooled V-JEPA 2 features and interaction turns and generates dedicated interaction tokens. We evaluate on FuGIBench, a streaming-video benchmark for preparation, response, and cancellation in tool-handover assistance. The best-performing variants of EviBridge-E and EviBridge-I achieve Overall scores of 0.6448 and 0.5892, compared with 0.2178 and 0.5327 for their respective task-adapted native baselines. These gains span target handovers, long waits, and cancelled requests, demonstrating the value of predictive visual evidence for interaction decisions.

</details>


### [136] [MVSTF: Multi-View Spatio-Temporal Fusion for LiDAR-Based Moving Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20872)
*Jiaoju Zhou,Chao Ye,Xuebo Yang,Weiyang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVSTF是一种端到端多视角时空融合网络，利用序列点云生成多视角图像和特征差异，通过多分支网络并行提取时空和维度特征，实现高精度运动目标分割，在SemanticKITTI和KITTI-Road基准上达到先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人在动态环境中识别物体运动属性面临挑战，现有方法受边界模糊、遮挡、视角约束和训练复杂度等限制。

Method: 利用序列点云生成多视角图像和特征差异；设计包含双下采样块(DDB)、空间注意力块(SAB)、多核卷积神经网络块(MKCNN)、双特征融合块(DFFB)和点融合(PF)模块的端到端多分支网络，从特征差异中提取运动特征，从多视角图像中提取几何和语义特征；引入辅助语义头提升鲁棒性和可解释性。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和KITTI-Road基准上验证，在SemanticKITTI MOS验证集和KITTI-Road验证集上取得最先进结果，在隐藏的SemanticKITTI-MOS测试集上取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: MVSTF网络有效解决了动态环境中运动目标分割的挑战，通过多视角时空融合和多分支特征提取实现了高精度分割，并展示了优越的性能。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots confront significant chal lenges in recognizing the motion properties of objects in dy namic environments. However, existing methods are hindered by limitations such as boundary ambiguity, occlusion, viewpoint constraints, and training complexity. In this paper, we propose an innovative end-to-end Multi-View Spatio-Temporal Fusion network, named MVSTF, which utilizes sequential point clouds to generate multi-view images and feature differences, and designs a multi-branch network to extract spatio-temporal and dimen sional features in parallel, and realizes high-precision moving object segmentation. First, sequential point clouds are utilized to generate multi-view images and feature differences. Then, an end-to-end multi-branch network including Dual Down-sampling Blocks (DDB), Spatial Attention Blocks (SAB), Multi-Kernel Convolutional Neural Network blocks (MKCNN), Dual Feature Fusion Blocks (DFFB), and the Point Fusion (PF) module, is designed to extract motion features from feature differences and geometric and semantic features from multi-view images. Finally, an auxiliary semantic head is introduced to further improve the algorithm's robustness and the model's interpretability. This work is validated on the SemanticKITTI and KITTI-Road bench marks, achieving state-of-the-art results on the SemanticKITTI MOS validation set and the KITTI-Road validation set, and competitive results on the hidden SemanticKITTI-MOS test set. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [137] [TRR: A Thermal Interaction Engine for Robot Simulation and RGBT Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20792)
*Biwen Yang,Zhe Cao,Jin Zhang,Haoyang Sun,Zonglu Hu,Jintao Zhao,Zhehao Zhang,Kaizheng Chen,Chengyi Liu,Chengyu Wei,Siyuan Wu,Ruiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出热机器人渲染器TRR，将深度分辨热状态附加到接触双方并模拟接触、传输与分离中的局部热交换，同步生成RGBT观测与事件级任务检查，并构建了2224对静态-动态配对（共4448条轨迹）的六任务基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作会改变后续交互的热条件（释放后保留的热量、随物体携带的热量或表面下储存的热量），即使当前温度相似也会影响后续接触结果；训练轨迹必须捕捉早期动作与后期观测及任务结果之间的这种热历史关联。

Method: TRR为两个接触伙伴附加空间与深度分辨的热状态，沿接触、运输和分离更新局部热交换，并在同一执行时钟上生成同步的RGB-热（RGBT）观测和事件局部任务检查；通过受控干预验证保留热、空间运输和内部储存对后续接触结果的影响，并从同一状态生成配对六任务基准；同时在MuJoCo和Isaac Sim上演示RGBT观测适配器。

Result: 在专用学习语料上，可见热历史将安全接触点选择从75.0%提升到91.7%；在新加热流程与共享反馈执行下，基于动态历史训练使模拟迁移完成率提升50个百分点；报告了十种机器人配置和六个相机视角的工程清单及开发者工作流。

Conclusion: TRR能够将热历史与动作、观测及任务结果关联起来并生成同步RGBT数据，显著改善安全接触选择与迁移完成率；其基准、工作流和平台资源构成计划公开发布的首批内容。

Abstract: A robot's actions change the thermal conditions of its next interaction: heat retained after release, carried with objects or stored beneath the surface can change a later contact's outcome despite similar current temperatures. Training trajectories must capture how these effects connect earlier actions to later observations and task outcomes. We present TRR, a \emph{Thermal Robot Renderer} that attaches spatial and depth-resolved thermal state to both contact partners, updates local heat exchange through contact, transport and separation, and generates synchronized RGB--thermal (RGBT) observations and event-local task checks on one execution clock. Controlled interventions show why retained heat, spatial transport and internal storage matter for subsequent contact outcomes. From the same state, TRR produces a paired six-task benchmark of 2,224 static--dynamic pairs, that is, 4,448 trajectories with synchronized dual-view observations, states and actions. Visible thermal history raises safe contact-site selection from 75.0\% to 91.7\% on a dedicated learning corpus, and training on dynamic histories raises simulated transfer completion by 50 percentage points under a new heating procedure with shared feedback execution. We demonstrate RGBT observation adapters on MuJoCo and Isaac Sim, report an engineering inventory of ten prepared robot configurations and six demonstrated camera viewpoints, and describe a developer workflow that carries an existing robot scene to exported RGBT records. The benchmark, the workflow and the platform resources described in Section 6 form the first items of a planned public release. Demonstrations and resource updates are available on the project page.

</details>


### [138] [Competent Autonomous Driving Through Fine Tuning Vision Language Action Models with Symbolic Traffic Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20648)
*Barkin Dagda,Vinal Asodia,Iman Sharifi,Peng Wei,Saber Fallah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出规则引导的VLA框架，将英国《公路法规》条款形式化为一阶逻辑规则并作为强化学习奖励信号，在CARLA仿真数据上提升了动作预测准确率与规则合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽能统一感知、推理与控制，但准确的动作预测并不等同于遵守交通法规，需将显式交通规则融入策略优化。

Method: 先用专家示范做监督微调，再用强化学习精调；用PyReason将限速适应、跟车距离、路权优先等规则形式化为可执行一阶逻辑；冻结的视觉语言模型提取场景谓词，结合车辆状态评估候选控制动作的预期后果；在CARLA留存数据上离线比较三种基于群体的RL方法。

Result: 相比监督基线，动作预测准确率与编码规则合规率均有提升；最佳变体将整体规则通过率由92.9%提升至97.6%，评估样本中未观察到编码路权检查违规。

Conclusion: 符号规则反馈可有效提升VLA策略与显式交通规则标准的一致性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models offer a unified approach to perception, reasoning, and control for autonomous driving, but accurate action prediction alone does not establish compliance with traffic regulations. This paper presents a rule-guided VLA framework that incorporates symbolic traffic-rule feedback into policy optimisation. The framework combines supervised fine-tuning on expert demonstrations with reinforcement learning (RL) refinement using a reward that encourages encoded-rule compliance while penalising deviations from expert actions. Selected provisions of the United Kingdom Highway Code are formalised as executable first-order logic rules using PyReason, covering speed adaptation, following distance, and road-user priority. A frozen vision-language model extracts scene predicates, which are combined with vehicle-state information to assess the projected consequences of candidate control actions. Offline evaluation on held-out data collected in the CARLA simulator compares three group-based RL methods. The results show improvements in both action prediction accuracy and encoded-rule compliance over the supervised baseline. The best-performing variant increases the overall rule pass rate from 92.9% to 97.6%, with zero observed violations of the encoded road-user-priority check in the evaluated samples. These findings support symbolic rule feedback as a means of improving VLA policy alignment with explicit traffic-rule criteria.

</details>


### [139] [Instructions Before Actions: Prospective and Retrospective Language Supervision for Driving Vision-Language-Action Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20631)
*Angel Martinez-Sanchez,Ross Greer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究区分了驾驶VLA模型中前瞻性指令与回顾性标签，开发ADVLAT-Engine同步语音指令、道路视频和轨迹，比较导航应用语言并评估微调效果，发现微调降低轨迹误差但指令影响不一致，反事实指令下响应性更强，回顾性标注分析待完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶VLA模型能接受导航指令，但预测轨迹不等于遵循指令；需区分动作前发出的前瞻性指令与观察后写的回顾性标签，以厘清监督收集与评估方式。

Method: 开发ADVLAT-Engine同步口语指令、道路场景视频和车辆轨迹；在48条重放路线上比较三个导航应用；对四个开源驾驶VLA在导航应用前瞻性驾驶数据微调前后进行评估；在真实路口用反事实指令测试响应性；另收集四人回顾性标注并描述受控比较。

Result: Google Maps与乘客共同播报的87个操作中，乘客使用更多场景依赖语言；微调使乘客驾驶轨迹误差降低23-59%，但指令效应不一致；真实路口反事实指令下，微调Alpamayo 2 Super在15米和5米分别提升31和18个准确率点，Qwen-Drive用离散命令比句子更可靠地遵循替代操作；回顾性标注的受控比较分析尚待完成。

Conclusion: 研究将操作预测与指令遵循分离，建立比较前瞻性与回顾性监督的框架，表明微调可降低轨迹误差但指令遵循需专门评估。

Abstract: Driving vision-language-action (VLA) models can accept navigation instructions, yet predicting a driver's trajectory does not establish that a model follows the instruction. We study how driving-language supervision is collected and evaluated, distinguishing prospective instructions issued before an action from retrospective labels written after observing it. We develop ADVLAT-Engine to synchronize spoken instructions, road-scene video, and vehicle trajectories, and compare three navigation apps on 48 replayed routes. On 87 maneuvers announced by both Google Maps and a passenger, the passenger uses more scene-dependent language. We evaluate four open driving VLAs before and after fine-tuning on prospective navigation-app drives. Fine-tuning reduces passenger-drive trajectory error by 23-59%, but instruction effects remain inconsistent. At real intersections, counterfactual instructions reveal stronger responsiveness: fine-tuned Alpamayo~2 Super gains 31 and 18 accuracy points at 15 and 5~m, while Qwen-Drive follows alternative maneuvers more reliably with a discrete command than with a sentence. We additionally collected retrospective annotations from four people for the driving recordings and describe controlled comparisons of annotation content, timing, and downstream learning; those analyses are pending. Our findings separate maneuver prediction from instruction-following and establish a framework for comparing prospective and retrospective supervision.

</details>


### [140] [VoxSAOR: Semantic-Aware Occupancy Robustification via Aleatoric Uncertainty Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20507)
*Mengfei Duan,Yuheng Zhang,Guoqiang Zhao,Yuanfan Zheng,Kailun Yang,Zhiyong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoxSAOR通过特征空间代理建模偶然不确定性，提升视觉3D占用预测的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉3D占用预测方法未显式建模遮挡、深度模糊和投影误差导致的体素特征空间变化可靠性，影响语义预测性能。

Method: 提出VoxSAOR框架：量化局部语义一致性(LSC)得到体素结构稳定性先验，用跨视角语义共识过滤不可靠对应；对每类构建稳定到模糊的特征转移，用类条件对角高斯分布建模；基于代理分布，AREL用递增尺度的特征扰动训练多个独立预测头，扰动强度由结构可靠性调制。

Result: 在多数据集和占用主干网络上实验表明，VoxSAOR提升了语义占用预测、长尾识别和预测校准性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模观测诱导的特征变化并集成扰动训练，可有效增强3D占用预测的鲁棒性和校准性，且推理时无需采样。

Abstract: Vision-based 3D occupancy prediction provides a dense semantic representation of scenes for autonomous driving and embodied perception, yet remains vulnerable to occlusion, depth ambiguity, and projection errors that induce spatially varying reliability in voxel features. Existing methods typically map voxel representations to semantic predictions without explicitly modeling such observation-induced feature variations. To address this limitation, we propose VoxSAOR, a semantic-aware occupancy robustification framework that models aleatoric uncertainty through feature-space proxies. It first quantifies Local Semantic Consistency (LSC) to derive a voxel-wise structural stability prior and uses cross-view semantic consensus to filter unreliable semantic correspondences. Within each semantic category, spatially matched stable and ambiguous voxels form stable-to-ambiguous feature transitions, which are modeled by a class-conditional diagonal Gaussian distribution, capturing both feature displacement and dispersion. Guided by these proxy distributions, Aleatoric Regime Ensemble Learning (AREL) trains independently parameterized prediction heads using feature perturbations at progressively increasing scales, with perturbation strength modulated by structural reliability. At inference, proxy modeling and sampling are disabled, and all heads process the same observed features; their output probabilities are then averaged to produce a deterministic consensus prediction without requiring sampling. Experiments across multiple datasets and occupancy backbones demonstrate the effectiveness of VoxSAOR in improving semantic occupancy prediction, long-tail recognition, and predictive calibration. The source code will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [141] [VISTA: A Framework for Vision-Informed Semantic Traffic Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20458)
*Murat Arda Onsu,Poonam Lohan,Burak Kantarci,Aisha Syed,Matthew Andrews,Sean Kennedy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VISTA框架，利用V-JEPA将路侧视频压缩为未来场景嵌入，经V2X传给车载TinyLlama进行语言推理，实现高效交通态势感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始交通视频经V2X传输带宽开销大，且在资源受限车辆上直接部署大型多模态模型不现实。

Method: 路侧单元用V-JEPA和注意力池化模块把视频片段转为紧凑未来场景嵌入，通过V2X发送至车辆；车载TinyLlama小语言模型配合自定义视觉适配器，结合自然语言查询解释嵌入。

Result: 在数字孪生城市交通环境中，未来碰撞预测准确率达97.77%、F1为89%，通信负载较原始RGB视频传输降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 基于嵌入的多模态智能可支撑低时延、通信高效且面向安全的IoT智能交通系统。

Abstract: The Internet of Things (IoT) is transforming intelligent transportation systems by connecting roadside cameras, edge computing units, vehicles, and traffic infrastructure into a shared sensing and decision-making ecosystem. However, transmitting raw traffic video over vehicle-to-everything (V2X) links is bandwidth-intensive, while deploying large multimodal models directly on resource-constrained vehicles is often impractical. To address these limitations, this article proposes VISTA (Vision-Informed Semantic Traffic Awareness), a multimodal vision-language edge-intelligence framework that combines predictive video representation learning with lightweight language-based reasoning to provide communication-efficient traffic situational awareness. In the proposed architecture, roadside surveillance units employ the Video Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) and an attentive-pooler module to convert traffic video clips into compact future-scene embeddings. These embeddings are transmitted through V2X links to vehicle-mounted systems, where a TinyLlama small language model, enhanced with a custom visual adapter, interprets the embeddings alongside natural-language (NL) traffic queries. VISTA enables vehicles to obtain interpretable traffic information, reason about upcoming scene dynamics, and identify potential collision risks without receiving raw video streams. Experimental results in a digital-twin urban traffic environment show that VISTA achieves 97.77% accuracy and an 89% F1 score for future collision prediction while reducing the communication payload by several orders of magnitude compared with raw RGB video transmission. These results demonstrate the feasibility of embedding-based multimodal intelligence for low-latency, communication-efficient, and safety-oriented IoT-enabled transportation systems.

</details>


### [142] [UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge 2026: Site-Calibrated Synthetic Data and Multi-Detector Fusion for Roadside 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20431)
*Arnav Kumar,Brijesh Soni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该报告总结了团队arnav007在UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR挑战赛（V2X-Real赛道）中的工作：仅用合成路边LiDAR数据训练3D检测器并生成逼真合成扫描，最终得分0.8947。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是仅使用合成的路边LiDAR数据训练车辆、行人和卡车的3D检测器，并生成与真实扫描难以区分的合成数据，以解决仿真到现实的域迁移问题。

Method: 开发并校准基于CARLA的流水线至固定传感器站点，利用无标签真实开发扫描精确建模距离图像（包括无回波光束）；提出组合推理检测器，使用仅在合成数据上训练的多个3D检测器（VoxelNeXt、PV-RCNN和SECOND），每帧扫描对齐到合成地平面并在多个地面高度处理，通过鸟瞰图聚类融合边界框，并进行类别特定后处理以细化朝向、框尺寸并过滤低置信度检测。

Result: 最终提交获得0.8947的综合得分；消融研究表明，逐扫描地平面校直使真实扫描的mAP提高了一倍以上，而跨不同高度和模型的边界框融合进一步提升了性能。

Conclusion: 逐扫描地平面校直和跨高度、跨模型的边界框融合是提升合成到真实LiDAR 3D检测性能的有效策略。

Abstract: This report summarizes the work submitted by team arnav007 (submission ID 1054222) for the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge (V2X-Real track) at the IEEE BigData Cup 2026. The primary objective is to train 3D detectors for vehicles, pedestrians, and trucks using only synthetic roadside LiDAR data, and to generate synthetic scans that are realistic enough to be indistinguishable from real ones. A CARLA based pipeline is developed and calibrated to the fixed sensor site, accurately modeling the range image, including no-return beams, based on unlabelled real development scans. A combined inference detector, using multiple 3D detectors (VoxelNeXt, PV-RCNN, and SECOND) trained exclusively on synthetic data, is also proposed. Each scan is aligned to the synthetic ground plane and processed through the detectors at multiple ground heights. The resulting bounding boxes are fused using bird's-eye view (BEV) clustering, followed by class-specific post-processing to refine heading, box size, and filter low-confidence detections. The final submission achieved a combined score of 0.8947. The ablation study shows that per-scan ground leveling more than doubled the real-scan mAP, while fusing boxes across different heights and models further improved performance.

</details>


### [143] [DominoBench: Evaluating Multi-Step Physical Reasoning from Still Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20385)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 我们测试了30个多模态模型，发现它们在无辅助情况下无法可靠预测2D刚性体场景的物理结果；构建了DominoBench基准（854项，427个最小对），并提供四阶提示阶梯；前沿模型在关键个体命运判断上最高正确率72%，配对双题全对最高34%；给出决定性计算及所需量后可达82-92%，但小型开源模型仍接近随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型能流畅描述场景并解教科书物理题，看似应能预测推球后坡道、摆锤、跷跷板和多米诺等场景的演化，但这一能力是否真实存在尚缺系统检验。

Method: 构建DominoBench基准：包含854张2D刚性体场景静态图，按427个最小对组织（1-3格变化决定结果），结果由物理模拟器计算；每项配四阶提示阶梯，最高阶给出决定结果的确切计算。测试来自10个家族的30个模型，比较无辅助、指向决定步骤、文本给出精确几何、以及写出决定计算及所需量（含静态图无法显示的速度）等条件。

Result: 无辅助时，Anthropic、Google、OpenAI的前沿模型在命运翻转个体上正确率至多72%，一对双题均对至多34%；指向决定步骤帮助很小，文本精确几何帮助有限；写出决定计算及所需量后，同批模型达82-92%，而小型开源模型仍接近随机。

Conclusion: 从静态图进行物理预测失败在定量步骤：最强模型在获得决定量时能使用它，但无法自行产生；流畅的场景描述不能作为正确预测的证据，规划或行动于物理世界的模型必须无提示地找到并计算决定量，DominoBench即检验此能力。

Abstract: Can a vision-language model look at a picture of a chain reaction and tell what will happen? Recent multimodal models describe scenes fluently and solve textbook physics problems, which suggests they should also predict how a scene of ramps, ledges, pendulums, seesaws and dominoes plays out once a ball is pushed. We tested this claim on 30 models from 10 families and reached a clear conclusion: unaided, no model predicts the outcome reliably. We built DominoBench, a benchmark of still images of 2D rigid-body scenes whose outcome a physics simulator computes. It holds 854 items in 427 minimal pairs, in which a change of one to three grid steps in one quantity decides whether one person survives, and every item comes with a four-rung hint ladder ending in the exact calculation that decides the outcome. Unaided, frontier models from Anthropic, Google and OpenAI are right about the person whose fate flips in at most 72% of items and answer both twins of a pair correctly in at most 34%. Pointing at the deciding step helps little, and exact geometry given as text barely helps, yet once the deciding calculation and the quantities it needs, including speeds that a still image cannot show, are written out, the same models reach 82-92% on the deciding person, while small open-weight models stay near chance. Physical prediction from a still image therefore fails at the quantitative step: the strongest models use the deciding quantity when they are given it, but do not produce it on their own. For models that plan or act in the physical world, a fluent account of a scene is thus no evidence of a correct prediction: such models must find and compute the deciding quantity unprompted, and DominoBench tests whether they do.

</details>


### [144] [Signs of Wear: Evaluating Intelligent Speed Assistance Under Synthetic Adverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20382)
*Johannes Fabian Hahn,Mao Shan,Julie Stephany Berrio Pere*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文构建了ISA-Wear数据集，通过生成式图像编辑在真实街景限速标志上添加雪、锈迹、植被、褪漆或泥污等磨损，并系统评估磨损对智能限速辅助（ISA）检测-读取流程的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ISA基准数据集未充分覆盖乡村道路常见的风化、脏污和植被遮挡的限速标志，导致对系统在真实磨损条件下的鲁棒性评估不足。

Method: 利用多模态图像编辑模型，依据文本指令对真实街景限速标志施加五类磨损（雪、锈、植被、褪漆、泥污），每类分三个严重程度，并适当调整周围场景且保持视角；生成配对的原图与编辑图，经几何检查与人工审核过滤（排除21%几何形变的编辑），以LPIPS距离度量严重程度，仅评估原图可被正确识别的帧。

Result: 在716张磨损帧上，检测-读取流程中磨损先破坏检测再破坏读取：检测器仅找到62%的磨损标志（原图全部找到），漏检占读取失败的78–87%，零样本检测器呈现相同模式；给定标注框时分类器仍能读取78%的磨损标志，而依赖检测器时仅56%。

Conclusion: 磨损对ISA系统的瓶颈在于检测阶段而非读取阶段，现有基准高估了系统在真实磨损条件下的性能，需重视检测环节的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Intelligent Speed Assistance (ISA) systems rely on cameras to recognize speed-limit signs, yet existing benchmarks underrepresent the weathered, dirty, and overgrown signs commonly encountered on rural roads. We use generative image editing to introduce different forms of wear into real street-level images of speed-limit signs. The resulting ISA-Wear dataset contains paired original and edited images. Guided by text instructions, a multimodal image-editing model adds snow, rust, vegetation, faded paint, or mud to the speed-limit sign in a street-level frame at three severity levels. Where appropriate, the surrounding scene is adapted as well, and the viewpoint is kept. Every edited frame has an unedited counterpart, and we only evaluate frames whose original is recognized correctly, so any failure can be attributed to the wear. Since the model alters the sign's geometry in 21\% of the edits, all edits are filtered by a geometric check and a manual review. Severity is measured as the LPIPS distance between the original and the edited sign plate. On 716 worn frames, we find that in detect-then-read pipelines, wear breaks detection before it breaks reading. The detector finds only 62\% of the worn signs, even though it finds every one of them in the original frames. Missed detections account for 78--87\% of failed reads, a pattern that a zero-shot detector shares. Given the annotated sign box, as in the crop-based benchmarks, a classifier still reads 78\% of the worn signs, but only 56\% when it has to rely on the detector

</details>


### [145] [Unreasonable Confidence from Simulator Misspecification: Directional Robustness on the SPEED+ Sim-to-Real Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20321)
*Nikolay Dolgov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了航天器位姿估计中合成到真实图像的域差距，发现模型在真实域上会过度自信，迁移效果不对称，且光度增强仅在特定照明下有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于真实在轨航天器的标注图像稀少，视觉导航系统主要依赖合成图像训练，但模拟器在辐射度上存在误差（如缺失高光、硬阴影和传感器饱和），导致合成到真实的域差距，这是此类系统的核心风险。

Method: 采用故意简化的单目位姿估计器，在SPEED+基准上进行2x2研究（骨干网络适应与光度增强），训练于合成图像，测试于两种真实硬件在环域（太阳灯和灯箱）。所有评审后实验均预注册。

Result: (1) 模型失败时不自知：合成数据上 conformal 校准至90%的置信区域，在太阳灯下仅覆盖9-15%的旋转误差，灯箱下21-38%。(2) 迁移不对称：扩展数据和特征将合成旋转误差从1.65降至1.03 rad，但太阳灯域仅改善0.05 rad（低于种子标准差的两倍）；而适应骨干网络最后阶段可将63-83%的额外合成增益迁移至两个真实域。(3) 骨干网络适应后，光度增强仅在其建模的方向上有效：光度堆栈在灯箱上从1.17改善至0.99 rad（每个种子），但太阳灯上变化不超过种子间变异；骨干冻结时则对太阳灯帮助更大。

Conclusion: 合成到真实的域差距导致模型过度自信，且迁移效果不对称；骨干网络最后阶段的适应是提升真实域性能的关键，光度增强的效果依赖于照明条件，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Vision-based navigation systems for orbit must be trained almost entirely on synthetic imagery, because real labelled images of a given spacecraft in flight rarely exist. Benchmarks like SPEED+ are built for that situation: the training set comes from a simulator, and the test sets are real images of a satellite mockup under two kinds of lighting. That simulator approximates how light reflects off mylar and aluminium, and like every scientific simulator it is misspecified. Its error is radiometric, since it misses the specular highlights, hard shadows and sensor saturation common in real images. We probe the resulting synthetic-to-real gap, the central risk for such systems, with a deliberately simple monocular pose estimator. In a 2x2 study of backbone adaptation and photometric augmentation, trained on synthetic images and tested on both real hardware-in-the-loop domains, we find three things. (1) The model does not know it is failing: regions conformally calibrated to 90% on synthetic data cover only 9-15% of rotation errors on sunlamp and 21-38% on lightbox, so a wrong pose carries the confidence of a correct one. (2) Transfer is asymmetric: scaling data and features improved synthetic rotation error from 1.65 to 1.03 rad but moved the harshly lit sunlamp domain by only 0.05 rad, under twice its seed s.d., whereas adapting the backbone's last stage carried 63-83% of its extra synthetic gain to both real domains. (3) Once the backbone adapts, augmentation helps only in the direction it models: our photometric stack improved the diffusely lit lightbox domain from 1.17 to 0.99 rad on every seed but moved sunlamp no more than seed-to-seed variation, while with the backbone frozen it helped sunlamp more. Every post-review experiment was pre-registered, and the code, per-image predictions and pre-registration record are at this https URL .

</details>


### [146] [EvoMem3D: Adaptive-Depth Temporal Representation Memory for Multi-Camera 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20241)
*Moshe Kimhi,Idan Kligvasser,Yotam Intrator,Regev Cohen,Ehud Rivlin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoMem3D 是一种自适应深度的时序框架，通过缓存全深度锚点特征并结合当前帧部分特征，以降低多相机3D检测的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多相机3D检测器依赖大型ViT图像编码器，重复全深度编码成为主要推理开销，而相邻驾驶帧高度冗余。

Method: 缓存最近的全深度检测器接口外观表示作为只读锚点，结合策略选择的当前帧部分特征；轻量级预编码控制器在图像处理前选择语义编码器出口；解耦时序外观与几何对齐，当前帧校准定义解码器几何，运动条件位置适配器处理锚点到当前帧的自车运动。

Result: 在nuScenes上，EvoMem3D达到61.50 NDS和52.76 mAP，端到端延迟从364.4 ms降至168.7 ms（x2.16）；与StreamPETR结合达到61.36 NDS和52.55 mAP，加速x1.92；控制实验分离了当前证据、时序重用、记忆校正和自适应路由的效果，审计确认策略能区分语义驾驶状态。

Conclusion: EvoMem3D通过自适应深度和时序重用有效降低了多相机3D检测的推理成本，同时保持高精度。

Abstract: Modern multi-camera 3D detectors increasingly rely on large Vision Transformer (ViT) image encoders, making repeated full-depth encoding a dominant inference cost even though adjacent driving frames are highly redundant. We present EvoMem3D, an adaptive-depth temporal framework that caches the most recent full-depth detector-interface appearance representation as a read-only anchor and combines it with policy-selected partial features from the current frame. A lightweight pre-encoding controller chooses among semantic encoder exits before image processing, allocating shallow computation to stable frames and deeper computation when the visual state changes.
