To reuse appearance without carrying stale geometry, we decouple temporal appearance from geometric alignment: current-frame calibration defines the decoder geometry at every timestamp, while a motion-conditioned positional adapter accounts for anchor-to-current ego motion. On nuScenes, EvoMem3D achieves 61.50 NDS and 52.76 mAP while reducing end-to-end latency from 364.4 to 168.7 ms (x2.16) relative to standard dense DETR3D. With StreamPETR, it reaches 61.36 NDS and 52.55 mAP at a x1.92 speedup. Controlled studies isolate the effects of current evidence, temporal reuse, memory correction, and adaptive routing, while our audit confirms that the policy learns to distinguish semantic driving regimes.

</details>


### [147] [Radar Panoptic Transformer: Semantic-Guided Mask Queries for Dynamic Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20203)
*Mayank Mayank,Bharanidhar Duraisamy,Rohit Mohan,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Radar Panoptic Transformer（RPT），一种端到端点云网络，融合雷达自适应特征提取与语义引导掩码预测，在RadarScenes上达到SOTA（71.6% mIoU，82.2% PQ），并在MAN TruckScenes长距全向场景中优于基线，实时推理31Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载雷达点云稀疏且非均匀采样，动态目标检测点极少且被静态背景主导，几何线索有限，难以分离实例，亟需针对雷达特性的全景分割方法。

Method: RPT包含：骨干网络通过分组向量注意力聚合位姿对齐的时序上下文，并基于距离、方位角和RCS学习逐点邻域半径；点级语义预测在前景候选处初始化查询，通过查询-点类别一致性引导掩码交叉注意力，无需几何聚类生成实例掩码；引入辅助RCS一致性损失匹配预测与标注实例的统计量。

Result: RadarScenes上71.6% mIoU、82.2% PQ，达SOTA；MAN TruckScenes（探测距离达230米的长距全向雷达）上优于所评估基线；RadarScenes上端到端推理31Hz，超过传感器帧率。

Conclusion: RPT有效应对雷达点云稀疏非均匀挑战，实现高精度全景分割与实时推理，并具备长距全向场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Automotive radar provides long-range sensing and direct Doppler velocity measurements, but its sparse and non-uniformly sampled point clouds pose significant challenges for panoptic segmentation. Dynamic objects often yield only a few detections amid a dominant static background, providing limiting the geometric cues for separating object instances. We propose \emph{Radar Panoptic Transformer (RPT)}, an end-to-end point-based network that incorporates radar-adaptive feature extraction with semantic-guided mask prediction. Its backbone aggregates pose-aligned temporal context through grouped vector attention and learns per-point neighborhood radii conditioned on range, azimuth, and radar cross section (RCS). Point-wise semantic predictions initialize queries at likely foreground detections and guide masked cross-attention through query-point class agreement, producing instance masks without geometric clustering. An auxiliary RCS-consistency loss matches instance-level statistics between predicted and annotated instances. RPT achieves state-of-the-art performance on RadarScenes with $71.6\%$ mIoU and $82.2\%$ PQ. We further evaluate RPT on MAN TruckScenes, where it outperforms the evaluated baselines in a long-range, full-surround radar setting with sensing ranges up to $230\,\mathrm{m}$. RPT achieves real-time end-to-end inference at $31$\,Hz on RadarScenes, exceeding the sensor frame rate.

</details>


### [148] [DoF-Query Transformer for Adaptive Fusion of Egocentric Vision and Forearm HD-sEMG in Continuous Hand Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20196)
*Jiahang Liu,Wenbo Zhang,Junsong Wang,Zhenzhi Ying,Siqi Hou,Naohiko Sugita,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出融合自我中心RGB与128通道前臂HD-sEMG的多模态框架，通过DoF查询Transformer实现20个手部关节角的连续回归，平均R²达0.88、MAE为2.80°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一传感模态在多变工况下难以可靠估计手部运动学，而精确的手部运动学估计对直观人机交互、细粒度操作示范采集、具身智能数据集构建及灵巧机器人学习均至关重要。

Method: 构建手指无束缚多模态框架：视觉端采用Vision Transformer编码器，肌电端采用Transformer编码器处理128通道HD-sEMG，融合端为自由度查询Transformer解码器；为每个自由度分配独立可学习查询，实现按DoF与上下文自适应的视觉-肌电融合。在10名受试者的同步多模态数据上评估。

Result: 融合模型平均R²=0.88、MAE=2.80°，优于仅视觉（0.82、3.21°）与仅肌电（0.68、5.13°）模型，并优于代表性单模态基线与替代融合设计；DoF分析显示视觉歧义关节与视角条件下融合增益更大，合成视觉遮挡下鲁棒性优于仅视觉估计。

Conclusion: 自我中心视觉与前臂HD-sEMG具有互补价值，可捕捉细粒度手部运动学，在人体示范采集与灵巧机器人学习方面具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Continuous and accurate estimation of human hand kinematics is important not only for intuitive human-machine interaction, but also for capturing fine-grained human manipulation demonstrations that can provide action supervision for embodied-intelligence datasets and dexterous robot learning. However, individual sensing modalities may be insufficient for reliable estimation under varying operating conditions. We present a finger-unencumbered multimodal framework for continuous regression of 20 hand joint angles from egocentric RGB images and 128-channel forearm high-density surface electromyography (HD-sEMG). The framework combines a Vision Transformer-based visual encoder, a Transformer-based EMG encoder, and a degree of freedom (DoF) query Transformer fusion decoder. To account for the varying informativeness of visual and EMG cues across joint DoFs and input conditions, the decoder assigns a separate learnable query to each DoF, enabling DoF-specific and context-adaptive fusion of the two modalities. The framework was evaluated on synchronized multimodal data from 10 participants. Averaged across participants, the proposed fusion model achieved a mean coefficient of determination ($R^2$) of $0.88$ and a mean absolute error (MAE) of $2.80^\circ$, compared with $0.82$ and $3.21^\circ$ for the vision-only model and $0.68$ and $5.13^\circ$ for the EMG-only model. It also outperformed the evaluated representative unimodal baselines and alternative fusion designs. DoF-wise analyses showed larger fusion gains for visually ambiguous joints and viewing conditions, while synthetic visual occlusion demonstrated greater robustness than vision-only estimation. These results highlight the complementary value of egocentric vision and forearm HD-sEMG for capturing fine-grained human hand kinematics, with potential applications in human demonstration acquisition and dexterous robot learning.

</details>


### [149] [Aerial Person Detection under Edge Constraints: Held-Out Evaluation and Edge-Ground Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20127)
*Raghu Venkat,Tricha Anjali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究在搜索无人机计算、通信和像素资源受限条件下的人员检测问题，预先声明主要评估方案，并将后续分析标记为探索性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索无人机中失踪人员仅占几个像素，机载计算能力有限且无线电链路带宽窄，需在严格资源约束下评估人员检测性能。

Method: 采用预注册评估框架；探索性对比按热成像帧标识划分记录块；使用46,000参数检测器并引入对比度线索门控；与YOLO11n在相同折上对比；用842,000参数地面模型对YOLO前16个候选裁剪重新评分，阈值在验证地点冻结。

Result: 按记录块划分使固定虚警率下召回率从32.7%降至12.3%；小模型门控检测召回10.4%对全图应用5.7%；YOLO11n召回54.5%但算力约11倍；在4K 117米视频中YOLO前16含行走人员帧比例最高92%对本方案74%；YOLO阈值跨地点虚警超标1.7至3.4倍且每帧耗4至24次标准推理；重评分将召回从44.9%提升至53.7%，事后配对分析增益+8.8点[+2.4,+12.5]，合并虚警从0.86降至0.64每帧但差异不显著；两种检测器对土色静止人员覆盖率均很低。

Conclusion: 资源受限的无人机人员检测需严格评估协议；小模型可低算力运行但召回有限，YOLO精度高但算力与跨地点阈值稳定性差；候选重评分可提升召回，静止低对比目标仍是难点。

Abstract: A search drone sees a missing person as a few pixels, carries a small computer, and shares a narrow radio link. We study person detection under those limits; the primary evaluations were declared before they ran, and later analyses are marked exploratory. In an exploratory comparison, holding out recording blocks inferred from thermal frame identifiers cut recall at a fixed false-alarm target from 32.7% to 12.3%. With nothing tuned on test blocks, a 46,000-parameter detector gated by a contrast cue finds 10.4% of people against 5.7% for the same network applied everywhere. A standard detector, YOLO11n, trained under the same folds finds 54.5% at a similar false-alarm rate for about eleven times the arithmetic, and on our 4K field footage at 117 m has a walking person among its top 16 detections in up to 92% of frames against 74% for our board. Run alone, YOLO11n's thresholds set at one location overshot their false-alarm target 1.7 to 3.4 times at new locations, and its best configuration at altitude cost 4 to 24 standard passes per frame. Re-scoring its 16 best crops (154 KB per frame) with an 842,000-parameter ground model, thresholds frozen on a validation location, raised recall from 44.9% to 53.7% against the same 16 crops with YOLO's own scores; in a post hoc paired analysis over 15 test locations the gain was +8.8 points [+2.4, +12.5]. Pooled false detections fell from 0.86 to 0.64 per frame, but that paired change was not distinguishable from zero. Coverage of a still person in earth tones was very low for both detectors in the clips we flew.

</details>


### [150] [Latent World Modeling for Point-Cloud Video Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20090)
*Zhuoyue Zhang,Jian Liu,Jihua Zhu,Ajmal Saeed Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointWM将点云视频掩码建模的补全任务转化为预测任务，通过仅使用历史上下文预测未来帧的特征和几何，消除了信息泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码建模范式（如MAE式重建或潜在特征预测）存在信息泄漏：查询目标自身位置会暴露几何运动信息；随机时空掩码使可见帧可插值而非预测，导致预训练任务退化为补全。

Method: 提出PointWM潜在世界模型：仅用最后可见位置和时间戳查询每个目标，从严格更早的上下文预测未来帧的特征与几何，并学习无标注的潜在动作以捕捉未来中过去无法确定的部分。

Result: 在HOI4D动作分割任务上，以P4Transformer为骨干，PointWM相比从头训练，F1@50离线提升24.9，严格因果推理下提升29.1。

Conclusion: 潜在世界模型可作为点云视频无泄漏预训练范式，实验结果验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Masked modeling is the dominant self-supervised paradigm for point-cloud videos. However, it leaks the information it is meant to predict, whether it reconstructs masked points as in MAE-style methods or predicts their latent features. Because point-cloud tokens are anchored at points sampled from each frame, querying a target at its own position reveals where its geometry has moved, in reconstruction and latent prediction alike. For latent predictors, random spatio-temporal masks introduce a further shortcut, as visible frames on both sides of each target allow interpolation rather than forecasting. The pretext task thus reduces to completion. Once the target positions are withheld, a masked latent predictor performs only slightly better than copying the last frame. To remove this leakage, we propose PointWM, a latent world model that turns completion into forecasting by predicting the features and geometry of future frames from strictly earlier context. Each target is queried only with its last-seen position and timestamp, while a latent action learned without annotations is meant to capture the part of the future that the past leaves undetermined. On HOI4D action segmentation with a P4Transformer backbone, PointWM improves F1@50 over training from scratch by 24.9 offline and 29.1 under strictly causal inference. These results show the promise of latent world models as a leakage-free pretraining paradigm for point-cloud this http URL code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [151] [ReactBench-AD: Do Driving VLAs and World-Action Models React to Imminent Hazards in Their Path?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20011)
*Nikos Theodoridis,Reenu Mohandas,Sushil Sharma,Ciarán Hogan,Ganesh Sistu,Anthony Scanlan,Tim Brophy,Ciarán Eising*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReactBench-AD基准，通过像素级反事实场景测试自动驾驶模型对路径中突然出现物体的反应，发现现有VLA和WAM模型反应微弱，但短时间微调可显著提升并泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）和世界-动作模型（WAM）声称能泛化到长尾场景，但主流基准以常规场景为主，无法验证这一能力，尤其缺乏对安全至关重要的长尾反应能力测试。

Method: 构建ReactBench-AD基准，基于真实NAVSIM和PhysicalAI-Autonomous-Vehicles场景，在所有摄像头视图和历史帧中一致地合成过街行人、站立行人或静止车辆，形成与原始场景像素级精确的反事实配对，以隔离插入物体对预测轨迹的因果效应；评估四个VLA和两个WAM，并对ReCogDrive进行短时间微调实验。

Result: 四个VLA和两个WAM对插入物体的反应微弱，往往几乎无反应；对ReCogDrive在编辑场景上短时间微调后，其紧急制动反应显著改善，且学到的行为能泛化到未见物体和完全真实场景，对常规驾驶性能影响极小。

Conclusion: 现有模型开箱即用的长尾能力存疑，但可作为基础通过少量额外训练获得长尾行为，凸显了展示长尾行为的真实或合成数据的价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) and World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged in autonomous driving with the promise of strong generalization to long-tail scenarios. Although these models achieve state-of-the-art results on autonomous driving benchmarks, most existing benchmarks are dominated by nominal scenarios and therefore cannot test this promise. To test one such long-tail capability that is critical for safety, we introduce ReactBench-AD, a benchmark that measures how driving models react to objects that appear in the ego vehicle's path. Built on real NAVSIM and PhysicalAI-Autonomous-Vehicles scenes, ReactBench-AD composites a crossing pedestrian, a standing pedestrian, or a stationary car consistently across all camera views and history frames. Each edited scene thus forms an exact, pixel-level counterfactual pair with its original, which allows us to isolate the causal effect of the inserted object on the predicted trajectory. Evaluating four VLAs and two WAMs, we find that they react weakly, often barely at all. Finally, we test how easily one of the evaluated VLAs, ReCogDrive, can be taught to brake hard for imminent hazards: a short fine-tuning stage on edited scenes substantially improves its reactions, with the learned behavior generalizing to unseen objects and fully real scenes, at minimal cost to nominal driving. These results call into question the out-of-the-box long-tail capabilities of these models, but suggest that they can be a strong foundation for acquiring them with little additional training, highlighting the value of real or synthetic data that demonstrates the long-tail behaviors nominal datasets rarely contain. Code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [152] [Remember, Replay, Adapt: Learning from Past Experience in Online Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19901)
*Chenhao Ding,Jizhou Han,Lixin Xiu,Muyao Wang,Songlin Dong,Hideki Nakayama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出R2A在线视觉语言导航自适应框架，通过成功-失败记忆和先验锚定适应持续复用历史反馈，在多个基准和骨干网络上取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反馈的在线VLN方法仅从当前回合结果学习，先前获得的反馈未被充分利用；受人类情景记忆启发，希望持续复用成功与失败的导航经验来指导策略学习。

Method: R2A包含两个模块：成功-失败记忆（SFM）保存带反馈的成功和失败导航回合，并基于新指令检索任务相关经验；先验锚定适应（PAA）相对于冻结的预训练策略，强化成功轨迹、抑制失败轨迹，同时正则化行为漂移，实现无需源域数据或专家监督的持续反馈复用与稳定策略自适应。

Result: 在REVERIE、R2R和R2R-CE三个基准及五种导航骨干网络上实验，性能一致提升；在REVERIE验证未见集上，DUET的SR从46.98%提升至69.84%，SPL从33.73%提升至50.98%。

Conclusion: R2A通过情景记忆式反馈复用和先验锚定适应，有效实现在线VLN的持续稳定策略自适应，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Existing feedback-based methods for online vision-and-language navigation (VLN) primarily learn from current episode outcomes, leaving previously acquired feedback underutilized. Inspired by human episodic memory, we propose R2A, an online VLN adaptation framework that continually reuses successful and failed navigation experiences for policy learning. Specifically, Success-Failure Memory (SFM) retains successful and failed navigation episodes with acquired feedback and retrieves task-relevant experiences based on incoming instructions, enabling historical feedback to guide subsequent navigation. Prior-Anchored Adaptation (PAA) learns from these experiences by reinforcing successful trajectories and suppressing failed ones relative to a frozen pretrained policy, while regularizing behavioral drift. Together, these modules enable continual feedback reuse and stable policy adaptation without source-domain data or expert supervision. Extensive experiments across three benchmarks, REVERIE, R2R, and R2R-CE, and five navigation backbones demonstrate consistent performance gains. On the REVERIE validation unseen split, R2A improves DUET's SR from 46.98% to 69.84% and SPL from 33.73% to 50.98%.

</details>


### [153] [PanDWM: Panoramic 4D Driving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19857)
*Jiajun Sun,Zeyu Zhang,Zeyu Ren,Guangfeng Jiang,Yang Zhao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanDWM将六路针孔相机视频拼接为360度全景带，把多视角一致性转化为图像连续性，用因果学生模型逐窗口生成并蒸馏自教师模型，经高斯泼溅解码为4D场景，在nuScenes上以8-46倍速度超越多相机基线且FID/FVD更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶世界模型通常基于六路独立光心、重叠小的针孔相机生成视频，需额外学习跨视角一致性，带来建模负担与不一致问题。

Method: 将六相机画面拼接成单一360度全景带，使相机边界位于图像内部；以布局为条件，因果学生模型分四步逐窗口生成，蒸馏自因果教师并在自身rollout上训练；前馈解码器沿相机射线将每帧提升为3D高斯，结合相邻帧静态高斯补全为4D场景。

Result: 在nuScenes上以拼接的单一360度视角评测，PanDWM的FID和FVD低于多相机基线，速度为其8-46倍，并保持50步教师模型的FID，同时将FVD降低22-25%。

Conclusion: 将多相机视角拼接为全景带可简化一致性建模，配合蒸馏与高斯泼溅解码，PanDWM在质量与效率上均优于现有多相机驾驶世界模型。

Abstract: Driving world models usually generate videos from six pinhole cameras with separate optical centres and small overlaps, and must learn to keep these views consistent. We present PanDWM, which stitches them into one $360^\circ$ panoramic band. Camera boundaries then fall inside one image, so keeping the views consistent means keeping the image continuous, and each pixel still records its camera ray. Conditioned on layouts, a causal student generates the band window by window in four steps; it is distilled from a causal teacher and trained on its own rollouts. A feed-forward decoder lifts each frame along these rays into 3D Gaussians, and static Gaussians from neighbouring frames complete them into 4D scenes. On nuScenes, scored in one stitched $360^\circ$ view, PanDWM has lower FID and FVD than the multi-camera baselines at $8$-$46\times$ their speed, and keeps its 50-step teacher's FID while lowering FVD by 22-25%.

</details>


### [154] [Beyond Vehicle-Centric Data: Scaling Autonomous Driving through Roadside-to-Vehicle Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19830)
*Yichen Zhang,Zihan Wang,Hao Chen,Runyi Huang,Ruixue Zong,Jing Wang,Yunong Mao,Lei He,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyR2V 将路侧多模态观测转化为多车视角的七摄像头环视视频，并证明生成数据可有效扩充自动驾驶训练集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶训练数据主要依赖车队采集，成本高；路侧基础设施记录的多车交互数据因视角差异难以直接用于车载模型训练。

Method: 提出 AnyR2V 框架：聚合序列路侧观测为几何与道路使用者运动的共享场景表示，沿目标车辆轨迹渲染外观、深度与布局条件，生成七摄像头环视视频；利用时空对齐的路侧-车载数据对监督，并将路侧3D框转换到目标车坐标系与生成图像配对；构建 R2V360 数据集（89条城市路口真实交通序列）。

Result: 在图像保真度及深度、语义一致性上优于六种场景重建方法；将相当于真实训练帧数50%的生成帧加入训练，在三个3D目标检测器上使真实车载测试集 mAP 提升3.3–3.4 AP点。

Conclusion: 路侧交通记录可转化为多车辆视角的训练数据，为自动驾驶提供新的数据来源。

Abstract: Training autonomous driving models requires large and diverse datasets, collected primarily by vehicles. Scaling them entails investment in fleet operation, data processing and annotation. Road infrastructure records traffic and multi-vehicle interactions, yet the viewpoint gap between roadside and onboard sensors limits its direct use for training. Here we introduce AnyR2V, a framework that transforms multimodal roadside observations into seven-camera surround-view videos for different vehicle perspectives. It aggregates sequential observations into a shared scene representation of geometry and road-user motion, then renders appearance, depth and layout conditions along target-vehicle trajectories. Supervised by spatiotemporally aligned roadside-vehicle pairs, AnyR2V generates surround-view videos guided by scene structure and road-user motion. Existing roadside 3D bounding boxes are transformed into target-vehicle coordinates and paired with generated images, allowing one traffic recording and its annotations to yield training data from multiple vehicle perspectives. We construct R2V360, a dataset of 89 real traffic sequences at an urban intersection, with aligned multimodal roadside data and onboard surround-view videos. AnyR2V outperforms six scene reconstruction methods in image fidelity and in depth and semantic agreement between generated and real onboard images. We further evaluate generated videos along other vehicles' trajectories by adding them to real training data. Adding generated frames equal to 50% of the original real training frame count improves mean average precision (mAP) by 3.3-3.4 AP-points across three 3D object detectors on the real onboard test set, relative to real-data-only baselines. These results demonstrate that roadside traffic records can provide training data from multiple vehicle perspectives, offering a new data source for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [155] [The Flip Test: Video World Models Fail to Learn the World State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19822)
*Mi Luo,Lijun Yu,Adams Wei Yu,Ang Cao,Baochen Sun,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Nan Hua,Yuan Hao,Alex Dimakis,Ya Xu,Ed H. Chi,Xuezhi Wang,Yinxiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlipTest基准，通过反事实测试揭示11个视频世界模型均无法准确追踪世界状态，最佳准确率仅18.3%，低于随机猜测；而视觉语言模型和编码代理能捕捉边界信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型声称能预测场景未来，但当前模型是否真正追踪世界状态存疑。作者通过微小改变当前状态应翻转未来的反事实测试来检验模型。

Method: 构建FlipTest，包含基于10个模拟和15个真实任务共1680帧的三级递进测试：视觉最小对（检验预测是否跨越边界变化）、匹配控制（检验是否仅在真值变化处变化）、密集扫描（精确定位变化位置）。评估11个视频世界模型，并与视觉语言模型及编码代理对比。

Result: 所有11个视频世界模型均未达标，最佳平均对准确率18.3%（真实与模拟场景），低于随机猜测的25%；模型在不变未来处与变化未来处翻转频率相同，扫描响应平坦。视觉语言模型具选择性，边界附近呈近S形曲线；编码代理接近真实边界。

Conclusion: 决定信息存在于帧中，视频模型仅遗漏了它。应重新思考将世界建模等同于纯视频预测，下一帧预测不仅需绘制合理像素，还需识别控制未来动态的因果状态。

Abstract: Video world models promise to predict the future of a scene from its present. Do current models keep this promise? We probe this question counterfactually. When a small change to the present flips the future, such as a box resting a few millimeters before or past a table edge, a model that tracks the state of the world must flip its prediction too. We build FlipTest, a three-level hierarchy of increasingly strict tests over 1,680 initial frames from 10 simulated and 15 real-world tasks. At the base, visual minimal pairs, two nearly identical frames whose true futures differ, test whether predictions change across the boundary; matched controls go further and test whether they change only where the future does; and dense sweeps, the strictest level, locate exactly where they change. All 11 video world models we evaluate fall short. The best averages 18.3% pair accuracy over real and simulated scenes, below the 25% of random guessing. Models flip as often on steps that leave the future unchanged as on steps that change it, and their response stays flat across the sweeps. On the same frames, a vision-language model is selective and traces a near S-shaped curve near the boundary, and a coding agent that measures the image comes close to the true boundary. This implies that the deciding information is in the frame, and video models just miss it. We therefore urge the community to rethink world modeling as pure video prediction. Next-frame prediction must do more than draw plausible pixels; it must also identify the causal states that control future dynamics.

</details>


### [156] [Pros4D: Modeling Evolving 4D Worlds in Latent Space for Dynamic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19803)
*Rongxin Gao,Dongxuan Liu,Chu Li,Chengyang Ji,Jialing Huang,Runyu Chen,Kairun Wen,Chenxin Li,Jingrui Ye,Haosong Zhen,Xinghao Ding,Yue Huang,Yuzhi Huang,Xiaodong Tu,Jingyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Pros4D框架，在潜在空间建模动态4D世界，统一单目视频的回顾性动态理解与前瞻性未来动态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM仅理解已观测动态，而动态环境中的智能体还需预测观测边界之外的未来动态；外部几何处理与文本描述会增加推理复杂度或丢失连续动态信息，因此需要一种可保留观测动态并具备预测能力的共享内部状态。

Method: 构建Pros4D-Engine生成几何监督并构造两项任务的配对样本；提出Pros4D-Bench在涵盖动态相机、物体与场景的八类空间任务上评测；模型将观测动态压缩为潜在观测状态并自回归滚动为潜在未来状态，采用4D Latent Alignment结合视觉-语义与几何监督进行对齐，并用联合语言监督连接任务推理。

Result: 在多个基准上，两项能力均相较强基线取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 在潜在空间统一建模演化4D世界可有效兼顾已观测动态理解与未来动态预测，为物理世界理解与具身智能提供支持。

Abstract: Dynamic spatial reasoning from monocular video enables vision-language models (VLMs) to understand how 3D environments evolve over time, supporting physical world understanding and embodied intelligence. Existing efforts primarily address observed-dynamics understanding, whereas agents in dynamic environments also require future-dynamics prediction beyond the observation boundary. Moreover, external geometric processing and textual descriptions can increase inference complexity or lose information when representing continuous dynamics. These limitations motivate a shared internal state that preserves observed dynamics and remains predictive beyond the input video. Accordingly, we present Pros4D, a framework for modeling evolving 4D worlds in latent space that unifies retrospective understanding of observed-dynamics with prospective reasoning for future-dynamics prediction. Specifically, Pros4D-Engine derives geometry-grounded supervision and constructs paired examples for both tasks, while Pros4D-Bench evaluates them across eight spatial task categories spanning dynamic cameras, objects, and scenes. At the model level, Pros4D compresses observed dynamics into latent observed states and autoregressively rolls them forward into latent future states. 4D Latent Alignment (4DLA) grounds these states through complementary visual-semantic and geometric supervision, while joint language supervision connects them to task reasoning. Experiments across benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong baselines in both capabilities.

</details>


### [157] [When Backdoors Can Be Switched Off: Suppression-Aware Backdoor Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19774)
*Nur Afsa Syeda,Mohamed Elmahallawy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究VLA模型中的抑制感知后门攻击，通过合取触发激活与指令条件抑制相结合，提出三种变体，在SmolVLA和OpenVLA上显著降低检测率并保持攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA后门攻击主要关注基于触发的激活和隐蔽动作操纵，而对恶意行为的条件抑制（conditional suppression）研究不足，这一维度可能影响后门威胁的评估与防御。

Method: 设计抑制感知后门，将合取触发激活与指令条件抑制结合，实例化为三种变体：Cross-Modal Trigger（机器人状态与空间分离视觉触发）、Self-Suppressing Trigger（状态触发加[DISRUPT]令牌激活，[RESTORE]令牌抑制攻击）、Multi-Trigger（额外要求视觉触发并在物理触发持续时仍保持抑制）；在SmolVLA上使用两个SO-100操作数据集，与State Backdoor、BadVLA、TabVLA、SilentDrift四个基线对比。

Result: Self-Suppressing和Multi-Trigger将Isolation Forest检测率从96–98%降至1–18%，动作空间l散度降低10–14倍，攻击成功率达63–71%，且干净任务性能完美；在OpenVLA与LIBERO上实验表明攻击可迁移至7B参数策略，并规避STRIP和动作分布偏移检测。

Conclusion: 条件抑制是评估VLA策略后门威胁的重要维度，抑制感知后门能有效降低检测率并保持攻击效果，且具有跨模型迁移能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map visual observations, robot states, and natural-language instructions to robotic actions, but their multimodal interfaces introduce new security risks. Existing VLA backdoor attacks primarily study trigger-based activation and stealthy action manipulation, leaving conditional suppression of malicious behavior underexplored. We investigate suppression-aware backdoors, which combine conjunctive trigger activation with instruction-conditioned suppression. We instantiate this design through three variants: Cross-Modal Trigger, combining robot-state and spatially separated visual triggers; Self-Suppressing Trigger, requiring a state trigger and a [DISRUPT] instruction token for activation, with [RESTORE] token suppressing the attack; and Multi-Trigger, additionally requiring a visual trigger while retaining suppression even when physical triggers persist. We evaluate these attacks using SmolVLA on two SO-100 manipulation datasets against four baselines: State Backdoor, BadVLA, TabVLA, and SilentDrift. Self-Suppressing and Multi-Trigger reduce Isolation Forest detection from 96--98% to 1--18% and action-space l divergence by 10--14X, achieving 63--71% attack success rates with perfect clean-task performance. Experiments with OpenVLA on LIBERO further show that the attacks transfer to a 7B-parameter policy, evading STRIP and action-distribution-shift detection. Our findings establish conditional suppression as an important dimension for evaluating backdoor threats in VLA policies.

</details>


### [158] [Toward Planning Representations That Separate Task Structure from Visual Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19621)
*Aarav Srivastava,Vishwas Sathish,Rajesh P. N. Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在ProcTHOR程序化生成房屋中对ObjectNav进行受控视觉泛化评估，发现表面材质变化是导航性能下降的主因，而全局图像统计偏移不能可靠预测性能；基于DINOv2冻结特征规划的模型在完全偏移下成功率最高（0.83），但仍对表面外观敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉泛化评估常同时改变多个因素，难以定位失败来源；有效规划需要保留任务相关几何与物体身份、忽略无关视觉变化的表示。

Method: 在ProcTHOR房屋中构建受控ObjectNav评估：训练与评估房屋共享平面图、物体位置、目标和起始状态，仅单独或组合改变视觉因素；比较无模型RL、基于模型RL和使用冻结预训练视觉表示的智能体。

Result: 外观变化损害导航且效应因因素而异：表面材质变化贡献了成功率平均变化的大部分，杂乱和光照影响很小；全局图像统计偏移幅度不能可靠预测性能，仅改变目标物体外观即可显著损害端到端智能体；基于DINOv2冻结特征规划的模型在完全偏移下成功率最高（0.83），但仍对表面外观敏感。

Conclusion: 规划表示必须将不变的空间任务结构与外观分离，这是视觉泛化导航的关键要求。

Abstract: Effective planning requires representations that preserve task-relevant geometry and object identity while ignoring visual variation that does not alter the task. Existing evaluations of visual generalization often change several factors simultaneously, making the source of a failure difficult to identify. We introduce a controlled evaluation of object-goal navigation (ObjectNav), the task of reaching an instance of a named object category, in procedurally generated ProcTHOR houses. Training and evaluation houses share floor plans, object locations, goals, and start states, while visual factors are changed individually or in combination. Across model-free reinforcement learning (RL), model-based RL, and agents using frozen pretrained visual representations, our results show that appearance changes impair navigation, but their effects differ sharply by factor. Surface material changes account for most of the average change in success, whereas clutter and lighting have little effect. The magnitude of a global image statistics shift does not reliably predict performance: changing only the goal object's appearance can substantially impair an end-to-end agent despite a small image-level change. We found that a model based agent that plans over frozen features from the pretrained DINOv2 image encoder achieves the highest success (0.83) under the full shift, but remains sensitive to surface appearance. Our experiments reveal an important requirement for planning representations: they must separate invariant spatial task structure from appearance.

</details>


### [159] [PALIN: Pixel-Aligned LiDAR-NIR Multimodal 3D Object Detection with Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19485)
*Yanan Zhang,Amit Vishnia,Chengbo Ai,Eleni Christofa,Runan Cheng,Siyuan Meng,Yuanchang Xie,Yu-Min,Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建像素对齐的LiDAR-NIR多模态数据集PALIN，并提出CMambaFusion融合框架提升3D目标检测精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR纯几何表示在稀疏观测和遮挡下存在歧义，跨传感器多模态融合受标定和同步误差影响。

Method: 利用Ouster LiDAR采集约8000帧像素对齐的LiDAR-NIR数据（静态城市路口与移动郊区场景），并提出含四方向Cross-Mamba双向跨模态交互模块与Gated Concat-Mamba联合特征细化模块的CMambaFusion框架。

Result: PALIN支持有效的多模态学习，CMambaFusion在精度-效率权衡上优于纯LiDAR和代表性融合基线。

Conclusion: 像素对齐的LiDAR-NIR多模态数据与CMambaFusion框架可有效提升3D目标检测性能与效率。

Abstract: 3D object detection is critical for intelligent transportation systems. LiDAR has been widely adopted for accurate geometric measurements; however, purely geometric representations remain ambiguous under sparse observations and occlusions, while cross-sensor multimodal fusion suffers from calibration and synchronization errors. To overcome these challenges, we leverage an Ouster LiDAR to construct PALIN (Pixel-Aligned LiDAR-NIR), a large-scale intrinsically pixel-aligned multimodal dataset jointly capturing geometry and NIR intensity, comprising approximately 8,000 annotated frames from static urban intersections and diverse mobile suburban scenes. Building upon this dataset, we propose CMambaFusion, a novel fusion framework consisting of a four-directional Cross-Mamba module for bidirectional cross-modal interaction and a Gated Concat-Mamba module for joint feature refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PALIN enables effective multimodal learning, and CMambaFusion achieves superior accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over both LiDAR-only and representative fusion baselines.

</details>


### [160] [Detecting Small Space Objects for Space Situational Awareness with Collaborative Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19394)
*Xingyu Qu,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于多视角空间目标检测的协同Edge AI框架，并对深度学习模型与融合策略进行了系统基准测试，揭示了图像分辨率、融合方式与星间链路带宽之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近地空间日益拥挤导致航天器碰撞与空间碎片风险上升，亟需低成本、高效率的星载空间目标检测与避碰系统；现有基于深度学习的Edge AI方案仍面临数据集有限、小目标检测困难及性能评估不全面等挑战。

Method: 构建面向多视角空间目标检测的协同Edge AI框架，系统地对深度学习模型及图像级、特征级和基于注意力的融合策略进行基准测试，并评估不同星间链路带宽下的检测精度与通信加推理时延。

Result: 更高图像分辨率显著提升小目标检测性能；在多视角场景下，图像级融合相比特征级和注意力融合，在评估的星间链路带宽范围内平均降低30.13%和32.80%的通信与推理联合时延；对于更大视角集合，特征级与注意力融合以感知-通信流水线分析开销为代价获得更高检测精度；在未超出接收容量时，多视角协作在星间链路约束下引入的时延开销极小。

Conclusion: 研究为未来协同卫星网络设计提供了有价值的设计洞见，可支撑新兴的空间态势感知与空间交通管理需求。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of space activities, near-Earth space is becoming increasingly congested, leading to a higher risk of spacecraft collisions and space debris generation. Recent close-proximity encounters and collision incidents necessitate efficient and cost-effective onboard systems for space object detection (SOD) and collision avoidance. While deep learning (DL) enabled Edge AI using low-cost vision sensors on single or multiple satellites has shown promises for SOD, significant challenges remain, including limited datasets, small SOD, and the lack of comprehensive performance assessments. This paper presents a collaborative Edge AI framework for multiview SOD and systematically benchmarks DL models and fusion strategies. Results show that higher image resolution significantly improves small SOD. For multiple views, image-level fusion reduces the combined communication and inference latency by 30.13% and 32.80% on average compared with feature-level and attention-based fusion across the evaluated inter-satellite link (ISL) bandwidth. For larger view sets, feature-level and attention-based fusion achieve better detection accuracy at the cost of analysis in the sensing-communication pipeline, revealing that multiview collaboration introduces minimal latency overhead under ISL constraints when reception capacity is not exceeded. Our work provides valuable design insights for future collaborative satellite network design, supporting the emerging space situational awareness and space traffic management requirements.

</details>


### [161] [AcousticBEV: Acoustic BEV Object Localization for Intelligent Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19381)
*Ziang Liu,Shiming Wang,Joseph Adeola,Julian F.P. Kooij*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文设计车载水平麦克风阵列作为车辆“耳朵”，构建了包含同步声学、LiDAR和3D标注的数据集AcousticScenes，并提出AcousticBEV方法，从声学信号估计BEV下运动车辆的方位和距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摄像头、雷达和LiDAR在环境感知中各有局限（如光照、恶劣天气），声学传感可提供互补感知能力，但此前在自动驾驶中探索不足。

Method: 设计水平麦克风阵列采集空间声学信号，采集真实驾驶数据集AcousticScenes（2,117静态和2,863动态样本，含同步LiDAR点云与3D包围框）；提出基于学习的AcousticBEV，以自车速度为条件，从麦克风阵列信号估计运动交通参与者的BEV中心位置。

Result: AcousticBEV优于经典信号处理基线和其它学习方法，在“Easy”场景F1为47.7±1.2%，在“Difficult”场景F1为45.1±1.4%。

Conclusion: 低成本车载麦克风阵列的声学信号能够在方位和距离上定位附近运动车辆，验证了声学感知用于自动驾驶的可行性。

Abstract: Modern autonomous driving and driver assistance systems rely predominantly on cameras, radars and LiDARs for environmental perception. However, these modalities face different environmental limitations, including poor illumination for cameras and adverse weather for LiDAR. Acoustic sensing, particularly using microphone arrays, could provide complementary perception capabilities for autonomous driving, but has remained largely underexplored. In this work, we design a horizontally arranged microphone array that functions as the "ears" of a vehicle, enabling localization of nearby moving vehicles in a bird's-eye view (BEV) representation that captures both their relative direction and distance. Using this array, we collect a real-world driving dataset, AcousticScenes, which contains 2,117 static and 2,863 dynamic samples with synchronized spatial acoustic signals, LiDAR point clouds and 3D bounding box annotations. Building on this dataset, we propose AcousticBEV, a learning-based method for acoustic BEV object localization, which estimates the center locations of moving traffic participants in BEV from the microphone-array signals, conditioned on the ego vehicle's own speed. Experimental results demonstrate that AcousticBEV outperforms both classical signal-processing baselines and other learning-based methods, achieving F1 scores of $47.7 \pm 1.2$% on "Easy" scenarios and $45.1 \pm 1.4$% on "Difficult" cases. Our findings demonstrate that acoustic signals from a low-cost, vehicle-mounted microphone array can localize nearby moving vehicles in both azimuth and distance.

</details>


### [162] [RETR: A Structure-Preserving RGB-Event Transformer for Robust 3D Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19358)
*Jingtao Dong,Hao Zhuang,Hao Yang,Liyuan Pan,Wei Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文首次将事件相机引入3D车道检测，构建了两个多模态基准数据集，并提出结构保持Transformer模型RETR，在挑战性光照和复杂道路几何场景下取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于RGB的3D车道检测在恶劣光照、运动模糊和远距离透视压缩下表现脆弱，而事件相机具有高时间分辨率和高动态范围的结构线索，但其稀疏、运动依赖的响应难以直接融合为一致的3D几何，因此需要系统研究事件相机在3D车道检测中的应用。

Method: 构建两个带度量3D车道标注的多模态基准：DSEC-3DLD（真实世界序列）和Ev-OpenLane（大规模仿真序列）；提出RETR结构保持Transformer，包含互惠上下文流融合对齐模态一致证据、不确定性感知结构巩固保留细薄不确定车道结构、以及几何状态解码器（提议条件初始化、参考条件查询演化、多样化几何查询）解码有序车道假设。

Result: RETR在两个基准上均达到最先进性能，尤其在挑战性光照和复杂道路几何场景中表现出显著提升。

Conclusion: 事件相机可为3D车道检测提供鲁棒补充线索，RETR能有效融合RGB与事件观测并保持车道结构，验证了事件相机在3D车道检测中的潜力。

Abstract: Robust 3D lane detection requires accurate metric road geometry recovery from visual inputs, yet conventional RGB-based approaches suffer from fragile lane evidence under adverse illumination, motion blur, and long-range perspective compression. Event cameras offer complementary high-temporal-resolution, high dynamic-range structural cues that are robust to these challenges, but their sparse, motion-dependent responses cannot be directly fused into consistent 3D geometry. In this paper, we present the first attempt to introduce event cameras into 3D lane detection. To enable systematic research, we first build two multimodal benchmarks with metric 3D lane annotations: DSEC-3DLD (real-world sequences) and Ev-OpenLane (large-scale simulated sequences). We further propose RETR, a structure preserving transformer that converts complementary RGB and event observations into coherent 3D lane geometry. RETR first aligns modality consistent evidence through Reciprocal Context Flow Fusion, then preserves thin and uncertain lane structures with Uncertainty Aware Structural Consolidation, and finally decodes ordered lane hypotheses using a Geometry State Decoder with proposal conditioned initialization, reference conditioned query evolution, and diversified geometric inquiry. Extensive experiments show that RETR achieves state-of-the-art performance on both benchmarks, especially showing strong improvements in challenging lighting and complex road geometry scenarios. Code and dataset will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [163] [Calibrating the Uncertainty of Self-Supervised Monocular Depth without Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19188)
*Jun Seok Kim,Rui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 自监督单目深度网络预测的逐像素不确定性在量级上普遍校准失准，现有仅评估排序的指标无法反映这一点；MonoCal通过立体对估计的单一常数校正尺度，并重训练不确定性以预测与第二自监督网络的深度分歧来校正形状，在KITTI上优于使用真值重缩放的方法，且推理时仅需单张图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要衡量不确定性对像素误差的排序能力，对不确定性的量级不敏感，因此不确定性即使只有实际误差的一半也能获得相同评分；作者希望检验预测不确定性是否在数值上与深度误差相当，包括平均量级（尺度）以及在不同置信度和近远像素间的变化（形状）。

Method: 在KITTI上评估多种不确定性估计的校准情况；提出MonoCal：尺度校准通过立体图像对测得一个常数乘子对不确定性进行缩放，无需重训练，且该常数与用真值拟合的常数高度接近；由于常数无法改变形状，进一步重训练不确定性网络，使其预测目标网络与第二个自监督网络深度不一致的位置，从而校正形状。

Result: 在KITTI上，所有评估的不确定性估计尺度均校准失准，MonoProb的误差几乎达到其预测不确定性的两倍；MonoCal在不同不确定性水平和深度范围内均取得比使用真值重缩放更好的校准效果，同时推理阶段仍只需单张图像。

Conclusion: 仅靠排序指标会掩盖不确定性的量级失准问题，MonoCal无需真值深度即可同时校正不确定性的尺度与形状，实现更可靠的逐像素不确定性估计。

Abstract: Self-supervised monocular depth networks can predict a per-pixel uncertainty, which is mainly evaluated by how well it ranks pixels according to their error. Such metrics are insensitive to the magnitude of the uncertainty, so an uncertainty half as large as the actual error obtains the same score. In this paper, we investigate whether the predicted uncertainty is as large as the depth error, both on average (scale) and across confident and uncertain, near and far pixels (shape). On KITTI, the scale is miscalibrated for every uncertainty estimate we evaluate, and the error of MonoProb is nearly twice its predicted uncertainty. We propose MonoCal, which corrects both the scale and the shape without ground-truth depth. Scale calibration multiplies the uncertainty by a single constant measured from stereo pairs, requiring no retraining, and this constant closely matches the one fitted with ground truth. Since a constant cannot change the shape, we further retrain the uncertainty to predict where the depth of the network disagrees with that of a second self-supervised network. Experiments on KITTI show that MonoCal achieves better calibration across uncertainty levels and depth ranges than rescaling with ground truth, while still using a single image at inference. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [164] [Do VLMs Really Look at the Road? A Diagnostic Evaluation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19184)
*Shankar Gangisetty,Muskan Singh,C V Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vision-language models for autonomous driving show high benchmark accuracy but lack visually grounded reasoning in safety-critical decisions, even without images.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing VLM evaluation for autonomous driving relies on question-answering benchmarks where high accuracy may not reflect true visual grounding, leaving safety-critical failure modes hidden.

Method: Proposes a diagnostic framework with two task categories (Adverse Weather; Junctions & Intersections) plus a nuScenes spatial-reasoning extension, evaluating open-source general, driving, and spatial-reasoning VLMs via four protocols: image vs. no-image, counterfactual weather sensitivity, linguistic robustness under paraphrasing and negation, and spatial reasoning; labels are programmatically derived from BDD100K and DriveLM.

Result: All models show systematic failures: strong affirmation bias, limited sensitivity to counterfactual scene changes, near-chance accuracy on negated questions despite high paraphrase consistency, and confident driving decisions even without visual input, all masked by competitive aggregate accuracy.

Conclusion: Current open-source VLMs are insufficiently grounded for safety-critical autonomous driving; evaluation must go beyond aggregate accuracy toward diagnostic, visually grounded assessment.

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being evaluated for autonomous driving through question-answering benchmarks, yet high benchmark accuracy does not necessarily reflect visually grounded reasoning. We present a diagnostic evaluation framework that systematically probes whether VLMs base safety-critical driving decisions on visual evidence or instead rely on language priors, answer biases, and dataset regularities. Our benchmark comprises two task categories, Adverse Weather and Junctions \& Intersections, with a nuScenes spatial reasoning extension, and evaluates open-source (general, driving, and spatial-reasoning) VLMs along four diagnostic protocols: image versus no-image evaluation, counterfactual weather sensitivity, linguistic robustness under paraphrasing and negation, and spatial reasoning. All ground-truth labels are derived programmatically from BDD100K and DriveLM annotations, enabling reproducible evaluation without manual labeling. Across all models, we find that competitive overall accuracy conceals systematic failure modes, including strong affirmation bias, limited sensitivity to counterfactual scene changes, near-chance performance on negated questions despite high paraphrase consistency, and confident driving decisions even without visual input. These results demonstrate that current open-source VLMs remain insufficiently grounded for safety-critical autonomous driving and underscore the need for diagnostic evaluation beyond aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [165] [WA-JEPA: Goal-Aware Predictive Feedback Action Refinement for a World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19183)
*Tao An,Weihang Liang,Tianqi Wang,Chuqing Cao,Wenming Tan,Kai Zhang,Haibo Lei,Tao Xie,Lijun Zhao,Ruifeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WA-JEPA是一个世界-动作模型，将动作生成、动作条件潜在预测和目标感知反馈耦合起来，无需候选动作搜索即可利用潜在预测指导动作生成，在保持模型紧凑和低在线计算成本的同时取得有竞争力的任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于JEPA的世界模型虽能在潜在空间预测动作后果，但主流规划方法依赖候选动作搜索，需反复评估动作序列，在线计算成本高；而直接动作生成虽避免搜索，却难以利用潜在预测结合任务目标来指导动作生成，这是将学习到的物理动态转化为有效决策的关键挑战。

Method: 提出WA-JEPA模型：首先生成动作提案并预测未来场景与本体状态，再以任务目标为参考通过两条互补反馈路径精炼提案——目标差异残差反馈（GDRF）利用预测场景与任务目标的潜在表示差异指导分块动作精炼，未来场景上下文反馈（FSCF）保留细粒度预测场景表示指导后续动作精炼；基于离线示范联合学习，在执行前迭代更新同一计划，使未来预测直接参与动作生成而无需候选动作搜索。

Result: 在短时域目标到达、长时域任务和完整任务执行上评估，结果显示目标感知预测反馈在保持紧凑模型和低在线计算成本的同时支持有竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: WA-JEPA通过目标感知的预测反馈将潜在预测直接用于动作生成，避免了候选动作搜索的高在线成本，验证了该范式在多种任务设置下的有效性。

Abstract: JEPA-based world models predict action consequences in latent space, but representative planning approaches rely on candidate-action search, requiring repeated evaluation of action sequences and incurring substantial online computational cost. Direct action generation avoids this search, yet how to leverage latent predictions to guide action generation given task goals remains a key challenge in translating learned physical dynamics into effective decisions. We introduce WA-JEPA, a world-action model that couples action generation, action-conditioned latent prediction, and goal-aware feedback. The model first generates an action proposal and predicts the resulting future scenes and proprioceptive states, then refines the proposal through two complementary feedback paths, using the task goal as a reference. Goal-discrepancy residual feedback (GDRF) uses differences between the latent representations of predicted scenes and the task goal to guide chunk-wise action refinement. Future-scene context feedback (FSCF) preserves fine-grained predicted scene representations to guide subsequent action refinement. Jointly learned from offline demonstrations, this process iteratively updates the same plan before execution, making future predictions directly participate in action generation without candidate-action search. We evaluate short-horizon goal reaching, long-horizon tasks, and full-task execution. The results show that goal-aware predictive feedback supports competitive task performance while maintaining a compact model and low online computational cost. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [166] [DEFDVO: Deformable Deep Visual Odometry for Equirectangular Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19095)
*Burak Sevsay,Egor Bondarev,Erkut Akdag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEFDVO是一种直接在等距柱状投影（ERP）全景图像上运行的基于图块的深度视觉里程计方法，通过球面初始化可变形特征提取、端到端接缝感知估计和球面梯度图块选择，解决ERP的纬度畸变、接缝和极区采样偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全向相机能捕捉完整场景，但ERP投影带来纬度相关畸变、图像边界人工接缝和向极点采样偏差，现有360°视觉里程计方法仅部分解决这些挑战。

Method: 提出DEFDVO：在ERP图像上直接操作的基于图块的深度VO；可变形特征提取器结合球面几何先验与学习采样偏移；接缝感知处理保持ERP连续性；球面梯度图块选择减少极区采样偏差；仅在合成TartanAirV2 ERP数据上训练。

Result: 在360DVO定义的TartanAirV2评测集上，最佳先验平均绝对轨迹误差（ATE）从0.201 m降至0.042 m，提升4.8倍；在真实360DVO基准的两个划分上均取得最低平均ATE。

Conclusion: DEFDVO有效弥补了现有360° VO方法对ERP特有挑战处理不充分的空白，显著提升轨迹精度，并在真实基准上达到最优性能。

Abstract: Omnidirectional ($360^\circ$) cameras capture the full surrounding scene in every frame, providing a strong advantage for visual odometry (VO) over pinhole sensors with a limited field of view. However, their equirectangular projection (ERP) introduces latitude-dependent distortion, an artificial seam at the image border, and a sampling bias toward the poles. Existing $360^\circ$ VO methods address these challenges only partially. DEFDVO closes this gap with a patch-based deep VO method that operates directly on ERP imagery, combining spherically initialized deformable feature extraction, end-to-end seam-aware estimation, and spherical-gradient patch selection. The deformable feature extractor combines spherical geometric priors with learned sampling offsets, while seam-aware processing preserves ERP continuity, and spherical-gradient selection reduces polar sampling bias. Trained exclusively on synthetic TartanAirV2 ERP imagery, DEFDVO improves the best prior average absolute trajectory error (ATE) by $4.8\times$ (0.201 m to 0.042 m) on the TartanAirV2 evaluation set defined by 360DVO and achieves the lowest average ATE on both splits of the real-world 360DVO benchmark. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [167] [Third-Place Solution for the UCF UrbanTwin V2X-Real Track at ECCV 2026: Synthetic Point Cloud Generation and Sim-to-Real 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18778)
*Antong Deng,Yuxin Zhang,Zhongxiang Xing,Zhenzhen Lin,Jiong Mu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UrbanTwin V2X-Real Sim2Real LiDAR挑战赛的完整技术方案：数据生成采用逐轴分位数校准，检测采用两阶段Voxel R-CNN，最终组合得分为0.6512。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Sim2Real LiDAR挑战中合成点云与真实点云分布不一致以及3D目标检测性能不足的问题。

Method: 数据生成使用逐轴分位数校准：以公开V2X-Real训练数据构建真实参考分布，对基础合成点云的x、y、z轴分别估计独立一维分位数映射，校准后按固定随机种子重采样并裁剪至有效空间范围，生成50帧、每帧2000个float32 XYZ点。检测使用两阶段Voxel R-CNN，训练数据包括RangeLDM增强的合成点云、每帧下采样至5万点的点云以及重建的行人数据；在4块RTX 4090上训练，全局批大小16，共40轮。测试时直接使用原始VoxelRCNN单模型推理。

Result: 官方评测服务器上，归一化Realism得分为0.7905，归一化Detection得分为0.5583；mAP@0.5为0.3908，BEV mAP@0.5为0.4219，mAP@0.7为0.1047。按官方加权规则，最终组合得分为0.6×0.5583+0.4×0.7905=0.6512。

Conclusion: 该方案完整覆盖数据构建、模型训练、推理、提交格式与官方评测，验证了逐轴分位数校准与Voxel R-CNN结合在Sim2Real LiDAR挑战中的有效性，最终组合得分为0.6512。

Abstract: This report presents a complete technical solution for the UrbanTwin V2X-Real Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge, covering both synthetic point cloud generation and 3D object detection. For data generation, we adopt per-axis quantile calibration. Public V2X-Real training data are used to construct the real reference distribution, and independent one-dimensional quantile mappings are estimated for the x, y, and z axes of the base synthetic point clouds. The calibrated point clouds are then resampled using a fixed random seed and clipped to the valid spatial range, producing 50 frames with 2,000 float32 XYZ points per frame. For detection, we use a two-stage Voxel R-CNN (VoxelRCNN) detector trained with RangeLDM-enhanced synthetic point-cloud data, point clouds downsampled to 50k points per frame, and reconstructed pedestrian data. Training is performed on four NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090 GPUs with a global batch size of 16 for 40 epochs. Final testing uses direct single-model inference with the original VoxelRCNN model. On the official evaluation server, the normalized Realism score is 0.7905 and the normalized Detection score is 0.5583, with mAP@0.5 of 0.3908, BEV mAP@0.5 of 0.4219, and mAP@0.7 of 0.1047. According to the official weighting rule, the final combined score is 0.6 x 0.5583 + 0.4 x 0.7905 = 0.6512. This report documents the complete implementation details of data construction, model training, inference, submission formatting, and official evaluation.

</details>


### [168] [AerialOcc: Unified 3D Aerial Perception without Task-Specific Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18770)
*Fan Liu,Xiang Gu,Baoyu Lv,Chuanyi Zhang,Liang Yao,Yuye Ma,Yuhui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于占据表示的统一无人机感知模型AerialOcc及大规模多类占据数据集AerialOcc-20K，单次推理支持多种感知任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机感知方案与特定任务输出绑定，检测、分割等目标通常由独立模型或共享主干加多任务头学习，模型未意识到任务间的共同结构；作者想探究单一感知表示能否在不针对特定任务设计的情况下支持不同层次理解。

Method: 选取占据作为任务无关表示，通过建模环境的空间存在与语义连接不同感知粒度；构建统一占据感知模型AerialOcc，无需任务特定预测头即可单次推理完成多任务；并构建大规模多类航空占据数据集AerialOcc-20K用于训练与评测。

Result: 大量实验表明AerialOcc在多种感知任务上取得有竞争力的性能，同时保持统一架构；模型与数据已公开。

Conclusion: 占据可作为通用感知接口，为自主无人机提供统一3D场景表示，迈向灵活可扩展的视觉智能。

Abstract: Aerial perception systems are increasingly expected to support diverse tasks, yet existing solutions remain fundamentally tied to task-specific outputs. Detection, segmentation, and other perception objectives are usually learned either by separate models or by sharing a backbone with multiple task heads, leaving the model itself unaware of the common structure underlying these tasks. We are interested in asking: can a single perception representation support different levels of understanding without being designed for any specific task? We notice that occupancy is a promising task-agnostic representation for drone perception. By modeling the spatial existence and semantics of the environment, it naturally bridges different perception granularities, from coarse scene understanding to fine-grained object localization. Therefore, we introduce AerialOcc, a unified occupancy-based perception model that enables multiple drone perception tasks from a single inference process, without maintaining task-specific prediction heads. To facilitate this direction, we construct AerialOcc-20K, a large-scale multi-class occupancy dataset for aerial environments. Extensive experiments show that AerialOcc achieves competitive performance across diverse perception tasks while preserving a unified architecture. Our findings demonstrate that occupancy can serve as a universal perception interface, offering a new perspective toward flexible and scalable visual intelligence for autonomous drones from a common 3D scene representation. Models and data are publicly available at this https URL

</details>


### [169] [Frame First, Decode Once: Object-Centric Coordinates and Single-Pass Decoding for System-1 World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18761)
*Abu Anas Ibn Samad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在物体初始位姿的SE(2)坐标系下表达输入输出并使用单次前向解码器，可显著降低系统1世界模型的决策遗憾并提升速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单次前向原始解码器作为系统1世界模型（快速动作条件预测器）时，哪些因素对按预测结果排序候选推动动作最关键。

Method: 在ManiSkill3 PushT-v1（512个留出测试族，8192次rollout）和lerobot/pusht人类演示（2384个测试窗口）上，用匹配检查点、分层自助法置信区间和Holm校正，比较不同解码器、训练损失、刚体投影和坐标系。

Result: 两个因素量级相近且占主导：使用物体初始位姿的SE(2)坐标系使五种解码器的决策遗憾降低1.9-2.2倍，物体坐标系模型用128族训练即可匹配世界坐标系用512族训练；单次解码优于迭代解码，物体坐标系下单次头遗憾0.020-0.021、每决策1.6-1.8 ms，而10步DDIM扩散为0.046（10.3 ms）、KV缓存自回归为0.059（12.9 ms）；单次头之间无显著差异；软token期望加端到端2D Kabsch投影轨迹误差最低（ManiSkill3 11.35 px，lerobot/pusht 3.78 px），具有精确刚体一致性，在4至50步DDIM预算下遗憾均低于扩散，延迟低3-29倍。

Conclusion: SE(2)物体坐标系和单次前向解码是系统1世界模型性能与速度的关键因素，软token期望加Kabsch投影在精度、一致性和延迟上综合最优。

Abstract: Single-pass primitive decoders such as LocateAnything and VFlash predict all quantized coordinate tokens of a geometric primitive in one forward pass, enabling fast, System-1 visual planning. We study what matters when such a decoder serves as a System-1 world model: a fast action-conditioned predictor that ranks candidate pushes by their predicted outcome. On ManiSkill3 PushT-v1 (512 held-out test families, 8,192 rollouts) and on human demonstrations from lerobot/pusht (2,384 test windows), we compare decoders, training losses, rigid-body projections and coordinate frames, using matched checkpoints, hierarchical bootstrap confidence intervals and Holm correction. Two factors of similar size dominate. First, expressing inputs and outputs in the SE(2) frame of the object's initial pose lowers decision regret by 1.9-2.2x for each of five decoders, and the object-frame model trained on 128 families matches the world-frame model trained on 512. Second, single-pass decoding outperforms iterative decoding: in the object frame, single-pass heads reach 0.020-0.021 regret at 1.6-1.8 ms per decision, against 0.046 for 10-step DDIM diffusion and 0.059 for KV-cached autoregression at 10.3 and 12.9 ms. The choice among single-pass heads has no measurable effect on regret, and our registered hypothesis that the frame effect exceeds the decoder spread is not supported. Soft-token expectation with an end-to-end 2D Kabsch projection gives the lowest trajectory error (11.35 px on ManiSkill3, 3.78 px on lerobot/pusht), exact rigid-body consistency, and lower regret than diffusion at every budget from 4 to 50 DDIM steps, at 3-29x lower latency.

</details>


### [170] [Is It Still There? Place-Registered Verification Makes VLMs Reliable Scene-Change Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18734)
*Jinjie Xie,Baihua Li,Qinggang Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STILL将变化检测重构为配准位置上的物体验证，利用3D几何配准和VLM判断物体是否仍在原位置，在SceneDiff协议上显著提升obj/sc AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人重访空间时需识别物体新增、移除或移动，但两次访问的轨迹、视角和光照不同，现有多图视觉语言模型因无法定位物体原位置而失败，只能报告单次捕获中出现的物体，漏检大部分移除和移动物体。

Method: 提出STILL，将变化检测重构为配准位置处的物体验证：用3D几何将每个变化假设的位置配准到另一次捕获中，再由一个VLM基于场景视图和局部位置视图回答物体是否仍在原处，并以相同形式监督训练。

Result: 在官方SceneDiff协议下，主指标obj/sc AP从24.0提升至37.8；在无厨房训练数据的自我中心厨房子集上从11.6提升至20.6；相比在同一标注上微调的VLM，F1提升超过21个点，运行时仅增加约11%。

Conclusion: 通过3D配准加VLM物体验证的范式，STILL有效解决了多图VLM在变化检测中无法定位物体的问题，在显著提升检测性能的同时保持较低计算开销。

Abstract: A robot that returns to a space must tell which objects were added, removed or moved since its last visit, while its trajectory, viewpoints and lighting differ between the two visits. We find that multi-image vision-language models (VLMs) fail at this task mainly because they cannot find the place where an object was. A VLM recognises what each frame shows but cannot check whether an object is still at its place in the other capture. It therefore reports objects that only one capture shows and misses most removed and moved objects. We propose STILL, which reformulates change detection as object verification at a registered place. 3D geometry registers the place of each change hypothesis in the other capture, and one VLM answers, from a scene view and a local place view, whether the object is still at that place, trained with supervision of the same form. Under the official SceneDiff protocol, STILL raises the main metric obj/sc AP from 24.0 to 37.8, and from 11.6 to 20.6 on the egocentric kitchen subset without any kitchen training data. It improves F1 by more than 21 points over a VLM fine-tuned on the same annotations and adds only about 11% to the runtime.

</details>


### [171] [LiNO: Lightweight Network for Occupancy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18733)
*Ruan Bispo,Tim Brophy,Reenu Mohandas,Anthony Scanlan,Ciaran Eising*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种实时轻量级占用栅格估计架构，融合相机与雷达传感器，结合残差多阶段头与任务特定数据增强，在保持高精度的同时显著降低计算与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有占用估计方法依赖大规模计算密集型架构，难以满足实时和实际应用需求；多传感器高维数据融合需要大量计算和内存。

Method: 设计轻量级架构，利用相机与雷达融合替代纯相机重模块，采用残差多阶段头进行占用估计，并引入任务特定数据增强以提升数据多样性和泛化能力。

Result: 在Occ3D上达到45.7 mIoU，在OpenOcc上达到41.9 RayIoU，运行速度17.2 FPS，内存仅5.3 GB；对比STCOcc的4.1 FPS和7.7 GB。

Conclusion: 该方法在占用估计任务上实现SOTA性能，同时具备实时性和低内存消耗，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Occupancy estimation for automated vehicles is a crucial task that builds a 3D map using vehicle sensors. This map allows the vehicle to identify drivable areas and various obstacle classes. The task involves complex subtasks such as depth estimation, object classification, and segmentation to align semantic information with the 3D grid. Moreover, producing these estimations from multiple sensors demands significant computational power and memory, as high-dimensional data fusion requires complex feature extraction and processing. Consequently, most existing methods rely on large and computationally intensive architectures that are not suitable for real-time or real-world applications. We present a real-time, lightweight architecture that leverages camera and radar sensors through a residual multi-stage head. Unlike prior methods that rely on heavy camera-only blocks, our architecture employs camera-radar fusion and lightweight components. Additionally, we introduce a task-specific data augmentation strategy that enhances data diversity and improves generalisation. Our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, attaining 45.7 mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) on Occ3D and 41.9 Ray-level Intersection over Union (RayIoU) (41.9) on OpenOcc datasets, running at 17.2 frames per second (FPS) and using only 5.3 GB of memory, compared with 4.1 FPS and 7.7 GB for STCOcc.

</details>


### [172] [Detection Is Not the Finish Line: Multiscale Decoding and Constraint-Aware Global Association for Multi-Camera 3D Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18725)
*Abdullah Naeem,Anav Katwal,Ayon Day,Md Tamjidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 1中，冻结检测器后通过类级尺度选择、几何到外观的ReID桥接和约束感知合并三阶段后处理，将3D HOTA提升至62.3947，排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于查询的多视角3D检测器在新相机网络中虽泛化良好，但输出轨迹严重碎片化：官方冠军的冻结Sparse4D检测器将每个人身份中位数分成8段，半数间隙超过因果跟踪器视野，表明系统剩余提升空间主要在检测之后。

Method: 保持检测器冻结，增加三个模块：(i) 两类输入尺度间按类别源选择，从0.75x尺度恢复小而远的人；(ii) 几何到外观桥接，将每个3D框投影到所有标定视角、匹配2D人体检测并聚合鲁棒轨迹级ReID描述子；(iii) 约束感知合并，结合因果短间隙拼接与离线质心连接聚类，施加时间排斥和度量3D可达性两个硬约束，并对刚性物体做类感知长间隙拼接；所有关联阶段仅改变对象身份。

Result: 在挑战赛评估服务器上取得62.3947 3D HOTA（DetA 57.4501、AssA 56.8131、LocA 80.1272），位列2026年10月30日总排行榜第一，比官方冠军复现高5.63分；仅约束关联阶段在DetA不变下通过AssA提升5.49带来+3.73 HOTA。

Conclusion: 冻结检测器后，通过尺度选择、ReID桥接和约束感知轨迹合并可显著减少轨迹碎片化、提升关联质量，证明该类系统的主要剩余增益在检测后处理阶段。

Abstract: A query-based multi-view 3D detector can transfer well to a new calibrated camera network and still produce badly fragmented tracks: on the AI City Challenge 2026 Track 1 validation set, the frozen Sparse4D detector behind the official challenge winner splits each Person identity into a median of eight fragments, and half of the gaps between fragments exceed the horizon of a causal tracker. We show that most of the remaining headroom of such a system lies after detection. Keeping the public detector frozen, we add (i) class-wise source selection between two input scales, which recovers small and distant people from a 0.75x pass while leaving large objects at the scale the detector was trained for; (ii) a geometry-to-appearance bridge that projects every 3D box into all calibrated views, matches it to 2D person detections, and aggregates robust track-level re-identification descriptors; and (iii) constraint-aware consolidation, which combines a causal short-gap stitch with offline centroid-linkage clustering under two hard constraints, temporal exclusion and metric 3D reachability, plus class-aware long-gap stitching for rigid objects. Every association stage changes object identities only. On the challenge evaluation server the system scores 62.3947 3D HOTA (DetA 57.4501, AssA 56.8131, LocA 80.1272), first on the general leaderboard snapshot of 30 October 2026 (second at the time of writing, after a competitor's detector update) and 5.63 points above our reproduction of the official winner; the constrained association stage alone contributes +3.73 HOTA through a +5.49 AssA gain at unchanged DetA. Controlled fixed-box validation studies, a constraint ablation, sensitivity curves, and a catalogue of negative results support the design.

</details>
